### [55] [DualClusterNet: Personalized Pattern-Space Composition for Next-POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03726)
*Ziyang Su,Guanjie Zheng,Jilin Hu,Linghe Kong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DualClusterNet通过组合群体共享的行为模式与空间原型，并保留用户个体偏好，预测用户下一步可能访问的兴趣点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同用户的移动行为可能遵循相似的规律，但这些规律会因用户偏好而体现于不同地点。仅依赖群体共性可能忽略个体差异，而只建模个人历史则可能难以充分利用跨用户的重复模式，因此需要兼顾共享规律与个性化变化的序列推荐方法。

Method: 提出因果序列推荐模型DualClusterNet。模型使用两组群体共享的原型字典，分别表示行为模式与空间信息，并通过软路由器从用户已观察到的序列前缀中提取相应的加权组合；随后以分解式乘性交互耦合两类信息，并通过逐特征仿射调制适配共享表示。同时，模型设置独立的用户条件分支，并以加性—投影门控保留个体差异。预测头将该表示与学习得到的一阶转移项结合，在完整的训练集兴趣点词表上进行排序。

Result: 在纽约市和东京数据集上的单次随机种子实验中，模型在完整候选集排序任务上表现具有竞争力。受控分析支持组合式共享分支和个性化分支均有贡献。评估采用仅由训练数据构建的词表，并保留原始最终目标：对未知兴趣点不进行跨越，也不以较早的目标替代。

Conclusion: 摘要提出了一种将跨用户共享的模式与空间原型、用户个体差异共同用于下一兴趣点推荐的方法。现有结果初步显示其有效性，但由于报告的是单次随机种子结果，结论的稳健性仍需通过多次重复实验及更广泛的评估进一步验证。

Abstract: Next point-of-interest (POI) recommendation supports personalized place discovery on location-based Web platforms. Mobility routines can recur across users while being realized at different places and adapted to individual preferences. We present DualClusterNet, a causal sequential recommender that composes two population-shared prototype dictionaries. At each observed prefix, soft routers retrieve pattern and spatial mixtures; a factorized multiplicative interaction couples them, feature-wise affine modulation adapts the shared representation, and a separate user-conditioned branch with an additive--projective gate retains individual variation. The prediction head combines this representation with a learned first-order transition term and ranks the complete training POI vocabulary. We evaluate on NYC and TKY using a training-only vocabulary and an original-final-target protocol that neither bridges unknown POIs nor substitutes earlier targets. The available single-seed results show competitive full-candidate ranking, while controlled analyses support the contribution of the compositional and personalized branches. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DualClusterNet-F4EE/.

</details>


### [56] [EvoMemState: Evolving Memory State for Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03743)
*Zhishang Xiang,Yunbo Tang,Zhongqiao Liu,Junkai Chen,Zerui Chen,Qingguo Hu,Yuxuan Zhang,William Grant,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以状态为中心的长期记忆框架，使智能体不仅保存用户事实，还能表示事实之间的关系及其对当前请求的适用方式，从而提升记忆检索完整性与回答准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统通常将记忆组织为彼此独立的事实，难以表达新事实如何更新、限制或冲突于旧事实，也未明确说明事实的适用范围、作用和当前优先级。这可能导致检索遗漏，或在回答时错误使用记忆。

Method: 框架包含三个部分：一是本体引导的记忆状态构建，从交互中提取持久事实，并建立描述事实有效性、适用范围及相互关系的关系状态；二是基于强化学习的状态演化，将记忆使用经验融入策略，为具体请求生成推理状态；三是状态感知推理，将相关事实、关系状态与推理状态结合，用于指导个性化回答。

Result: 在长期对话和记忆使用基准上的实验显示，该框架相较现有记忆系统取得了稳定提升，具体体现在检索到的记忆更加完整，以及回答更准确地依据记忆内容。

Conclusion: 论文表明，长期记忆不应仅保存事实，还应维护事实之间随时间演化的关系，并根据当前请求明确其使用方式。将关系状态与请求特定的推理状态结合，有望增强长期个性化辅助的可靠性。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to provide continuous personalized assistance by retaining user information across interactions. Existing systems support this process through fact extraction, organization, updating, and retrieval, but largely maintain memories as independent facts. This fact-centric paradigm may overlook how new facts update, constrain, or conflict with earlier ones, while leaving their applicability, role, and priority for the current request implicit. In this paper, we introduce EvoMemState, a state-centric framework that augments persistent facts with two complementary states: Relational States that capture how facts remain valid and relate to one another as memory evolves, and a Reasoning State that determines how these facts should be applied to the current request. Specifically, EvoMemState consists of three key components: (i) Ontology-Guided Memory State Induction extracts Persistent Facts and constructs Relational States that maintain their validity, scope, and evolving relations. (ii) RL-Based State Evolution internalizes memory-use experience into a policy through reinforcement learning to generate a request-specific Reasoning State. (iii) State-Aware Reasoning then combines the relevant facts with their Relational States and the generated Reasoning State to guide the personal agent. Extensive experiments on long-term dialogue and memory-use benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing memory systems in retrieval completeness and memory-grounded response accuracy. Our code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/EvoMemState.git.

</details>


### [57] [Enhancing Stock Market Information Retrieval with Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03747)
*Huan Phong Thai,Thu Anh Ngoc Le,Nhu Huynh Thi Le,Nhan Huu Tran,Lenh Cong Phan Pham,Hong Nhung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建面向股市信息问答的检索增强生成系统，并在一百条查询上评估文本匹配度与响应速度，结果显示系统具有较好的参考文本一致性和较快响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自然语言处理系统在动态金融环境中可能难以维持上下文准确性，也难以生成与用户问题相关的回答。研究旨在借助检索与生成相结合的方式，提升股市信息回答的准确性和相关性。

Method: 构建面向股市信息的检索增强生成系统，结合检索式方法与生成式方法。使用一百条股市相关查询进行评估，以一元词、二元词及最长公共子序列等文本重合指标衡量生成文本与参考文本的相似度，并测量系统响应延迟。

Result: 实验显示，系统取得较高的文本重合指标，表明生成内容与参考文档较为一致；平均响应时间为一点一六秒。摘要未提供具体指标分数、对照系统或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果初步表明，该系统能够较及时地生成与参考资料相符的股市信息回答，具有用于金融信息检索的潜力。不过，现有证据主要来自规模有限的数据集和文本相似度指标，尚不足以全面证明事实准确性或实际金融决策价值。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has been widely applied in financial domains, enabling automated analysis and retrieval of stock market information. However, traditional NLP-based systems often struggle with maintaining contextual accuracy and generating relevant responses in dynamic financial environments. To address these challenges, Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) models have emerged as a powerful solution, combining retrieval-based methods with generative capabilities to enhance response quality. In this study, we present a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system designed to generate responses related to stock market information. The system integrates retrieval-based and generative approaches to enhance the accuracy and relevance of financial text generation. To evaluate its effectiveness, we employ the ROUGE metric, specifically ROUGE-1, ROUGE-2, and ROUGE-L, to measure lexical and structural similarity between generated and reference texts. Experimental results on a dataset of 100 stock market-related queries demonstrate high ROUGE scores, indicating strong alignment with reference documents. Additionally, we assess latency to evaluate the system responsiveness, achieving an average response time of 1.16 seconds. These findings confirm that our RAG model is capable of providing accurate and timely stock market insights, making it a promising approach for financial information retrieval.

</details>


### [58] [Modular and Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search with Vectorium and kANNolo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03927)
*Silvio Martinico,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一套模块化向量检索工具链，将数据管理与索引构建分离，统一支持稠密、稀疏及多向量表示，并在多项基准测试中取得有竞争力的速度、准确率与内存表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似最近邻检索是现代信息检索系统的重要基础，但现有工具往往固定了向量表示、相似度函数、量化方案与索引结构的组合，或只对其中一部分进行抽象，且无法全面覆盖稠密、稀疏和多向量数据。因此，研究需要一种能灵活组合各项设计、同时不牺牲运行效率的通用工具链。

Method: 论文提出两个开源的锈语言库：一个负责向量、距离函数、量化器与数据集的独立抽象，并提供从三十二位浮点数到亚位编码等十一种编码器；另一个在此基础上实现分层可导航小世界图索引、倒排向量文件索引，以及可将任意首阶段索引与重评分数据集组合的两阶段索引。索引可按表示形式、编码器和度量方式进行组合，并通过编译期特化避免额外运行时开销。

Result: 实验表明，乘积量化在准确率上达到与法伊斯相当的水平，速度也具竞争力；拉比特量化和分层可导航小世界图索引达到或超过其参考实现，倒排向量文件索引则优于法伊斯。在七个公开数据集上与六种稠密系统及五种稀疏系统比较时，稠密数据的十二个数据集—准确率组合中有九个取得最佳内存—吞吐量权衡，且对分布外查询表现良好；稀疏数据上的三个数据集均由基于该工具链的系统领先，其中震荡与震荡波系统速度最快，而新索引更紧凑。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了将向量数据管理与索引层解耦的可行性：在覆盖多种向量表示和索引组合的同时，不必付出运行时性能代价。实验结果支持其在稠密与稀疏检索中的实用价值，尤其体现出良好的准确率、速度和内存效率。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbors (ANN) search over neural embeddings powers modern retrieval systems such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation. Embeddings come in three representations (dense, sparse and multi-vector), and efficient search combines an index structure, a similarity function and a quantization scheme. Existing libraries fix one combination, hard-code each one separately, or factor only one side of the problem, and none covers all three representations. We present Vectorium and kANNolo, two open-source Rust libraries that split this design space into a data-management layer and an indexing layer. Vectorium abstracts the vector, the distance function, the quantizer and the dataset independently, and provides 11 encoders, from 32-bit floats to sub-bit codes. On top of it, kANNolo implements Hierarchical Navigable Small-World (HNSW) graph and Inverted Vector File (IVF) indexes that are generic over representation, encoder and metric, and a two-stage index that composes any first-stage index with any rescoring dataset. Since every combination is specialized at compile time, this modularity adds no run-time overhead, and the resulting indexes reach state-of-the-art performance: our Product Quantization matches Faiss in accuracy at competitive speed, our RaBitQ and HNSW match or outperform their reference implementations, and our IVF outperforms Faiss. Against six dense and five sparse systems on seven public collections, our indexes give the best memory-throughput trade-off on dense data in 9 of 12 collection-accuracy pairs and perform well on out-of-distribution queries. On sparse data, the systems built on Vectorium lead on all three collections, with Seismic and SeismicWave the fastest and kANNolo's indexes the most compact.

</details>


### [59] [Characterizing Adversarial GEO via Implicit Brand Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04005)
*Shobhnik Kriplani,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种通过在网页文章中自然插入单次品牌提及，影响生成式搜索检索与回答的框架，并显示该方法能提升品牌曝光及回答中的提及率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式搜索会依据检索到的网页内容生成答案，因此修改网页内容可能影响哪些品牌进入答案。研究聚焦一种隐蔽操纵方式：在文章中加入预先指定的品牌，同时保持语境自然、避免明显广告痕迹，并确保内容仍具检索相关性。此类操作也带来生成式搜索内容操纵的安全风险。

Method: 研究提出“隐式品牌注入”多智能体框架，依次识别适合插入的位置、改写相关句子、核验编辑质量，并从候选结果中选出最终版本。研究还构建评测集，包含一千篇网页文章、六十个合成品牌和十二个领域，覆盖领域相关及非相关的品牌插入。实验在相同资源预算下，将该方法与直接提示及带排序的多候选生成方法比较，并评估插入质量、目标文本块的检索效果，以及品牌在生成答案中的传播情况。

Result: 相较基线方法，该框架更能保留原文语义，并较少增加文本困惑度；在所有评估的截断值、查询设置和插入场景中，目标文本块命中率均有所提高。最高结果包括：前十项命中率为百分之八十二点二，前十项平均倒数排名为零点七四；品牌提及率最高为百分之六十二点七，条件传播率最高为百分之七十五点九。

Conclusion: 即使只在文章中加入一次品牌提及，也可能同时提高内容被检索的机会，以及品牌出现在生成式搜索回答中的概率。研究揭示了隐式内容操纵的有效性，并指出生成式搜索系统需要发展相应的检测与防御机制。

Abstract: Generative search engines increasingly shape how users discover products, services, and commercial entities by generating answers from retrieved web content. This creates an opportunity to influence which brands appear in these answers by modifying the content available to the retrieval system. In this work, we study an adversarial generative engine optimization (GEO) setting in which a predefined brand is added to an article through a single contextually integrated mention. The challenge is to make the mention fit the surrounding article without making the content overtly promotional, while keeping it relevant enough to be retrieved and to influence the generated answer. To study this, we introduce Implicit Brand Injection (IBI), a multi-agent framework that identifies insertion locations, rewrites the selected sentence, verifies the edit, and selects the final candidate. To evaluate IBI at scale, we construct IBIBench, which contains 1,000 web articles and 60 synthetic brands across 12 diverse domains, with both endemic and non-endemic brand placements. We compare IBI with Direct Prompting and Best-of-N with ranking under an equivalent budget, evaluating insertion quality, target-chunk retrieval, and downstream brand propagation. Compared with the baselines, IBI produces insertions that better preserve semantics and disrupt perplexity less, while improving Hit@k across all evaluated cutoffs, query regimes, and placement settings. IBI achieves up to 82.2% Hit@10 and 0.74 MRR@10, while Brand Mention Rate (BMR) and Conditional Propagation (CP) reach up to 62.7% and 75.9%, respectively. These results show that a single brand mention can increase both retrieval exposure and brand visibility in generated responses, highlighting the need for defenses against implicit content manipulation in generative search.

</details>


### [60] [IDs That Mean Something: A Hands-On Tutorial on Semantic IDs for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04140)
*Roger Zhe Li,Aghiles Salah,Mete Sertkan,Changhong Jin,Yingjie Niu,Ruihai Dong,Barry Smyth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍推荐系统从无语义的整数编号转向语义标识符的发展、应用与实践，并讨论未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统依赖任意整数编号，模型只能从交互数据中学习物品表示，容易造成嵌入表规模膨胀，也难以适配不同应用场景。仅引入内容信息虽能缓解冷启动，却无法根本解决这些问题。语义标识符将内容、层级及关系信息编码进物品身份，为大规模语义信息的利用和跨任务统一提供了新途径。

Method: 本文以教程形式梳理从原始编号到语义标识符的演进，综述其在多模态推荐、冷启动和跨域推荐中的发展与应用，并通过实践练习指导参与者构建和评估语义标识符、训练模型及探索跨域迁移。

Result: 该教程系统呈现了语义标识符如何支持生成式与判别式推荐，并促进模态、领域和任务之间的统一。摘要未报告定量实验结果；其主要产出是综述内容、实践训练和对开放问题的讨论。

Conclusion: 语义标识符为推荐系统更有效地编码和利用大规模语义信息提供了可扩展框架，有望缓解传统编号方案的局限。未来仍需研究动态语义标识符，以及如何兼顾多方利益相关者的效用。

Abstract: Traditional recommender systems rely on arbitrary integer IDs to identify items, which carry no semantic information and force models to learn representations from interaction data. While leveraging content information can partially mitigate cold-start, this paradigm inherently suffers from exploding embedding tables and scenario-specific architectures. Recent years have seen the emergence of semantic IDs, which encode semantic information directly into the item identity, reflecting content, hierarchy, and relationships. This framework addresses the above limitations by offering a principled way to encode large-scale semantic information into item identity, benefiting both generative and discriminative applications and unlocking more native unification across modalities, domains, and tasks. This tutorial traces the evolution from raw IDs to semantic IDs as a scalable means of information utilization, offering a comprehensive review of their development and applications across multi-modality, cold-start, and cross-domain recommendation. Hands-on exercises guide attendees through building and evaluating semantic IDs, training models, and exploring cross-domain transfer. We conclude by discussing open challenges, including dynamic semantic IDs and multi-stakeholder utility. All materials of the tutorial could be found at the official website https://semantic-id-tutorial.github.io.

</details>


### [61] [IntentGEO: Intent-aware Generative Engine Optimization for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04225)
*Runlin Lei,Shiqi Zhang,Xiaokui Xiao,Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文指出，生成式引擎中的商品可见度会随用户意图变化；提出锚定式元优化，在保留明确推荐指令的基础上学习补充策略，提升不同需求与决策阶段中的引用排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式引擎优化研究多以通用查询为主，较少考察同一商品描述面对不同用户需求和决策目标时的表现。研究还发现，基于学习的提示词优化可能不如人工撰写的初始提示词，其更新过程会逐渐偏离明确的推荐指令，转向泛化的宣传话术。

Method: 研究提出意图导向的生成式引擎优化评估框架，考察需求具体程度和决策进程变化对商品可见度的影响，并比较既有方法在不同用户意图下的表现。在此基础上，提出锚定式元优化：将明确的推荐指令固定为核心提示词，再依据引用反馈学习追加策略，以减少优化漂移。

Result: 在四个领域、十种用户意图状态的实验中，锚定式元优化的平均引用排名增益比最强的既有学习型基线高百分之三十五，并在十种状态中的九种取得领先。消融实验显示，学习到的追加策略优于仅使用固定核心指令；该方法也优于以同一核心提示词为初始内容、但对完整提示词进行优化的方案。此外，它在智能体式推荐流程以及经单独适配的商业闭源在线助手上均取得最高增益。

Conclusion: 用户意图应成为生成式引擎优化的重要评估维度。对于推荐任务，固定核心推荐指令、仅对补充策略进行适应性学习，有助于兼顾不同需求与决策阶段，并避免优化过程退化为泛化宣传。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly shape recommendations across chatbots, e-commerce, and user-generated content (UGC) platforms, creating demand for Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) to improve item visibility in generated responses. Existing GEO studies are mainly content-centric, optimizing visibility under general-purpose queries. However, a shared item description usually serves users with different needs and decision goals, resulting in its visibility gains being dependent on the request. To investigate how changing intent affects visibility, we formulate IntentGEO to evaluate shared rewrites across need specificity and decision progression. We revisit existing GEO methods and find that their visibility gains vary across user intents. More critically, learning-based prompt optimization can underperform its handwritten seed on average. Tracing these updates reveals prompt drift from explicit recommendation instructions toward generic promotional tactics. To address this drift, we propose AMO (Anchored Meta-Optimization), which preserves recommendation instructions in a fixed core while learning appended strategies from citation feedback. Across four domains and ten intent states, AMO improves average citation-rank gain by 35% over the strongest prior learning-based baseline and leads on nine states. Ablations show that learned additions improve on the fixed core alone and that AMO outperforms full-prompt optimization initialized from that core. It also achieves the highest gains in an agentic recommendation pipeline and, after separate adaptation, on Doubao, a commercially deployed, closed-source online assistant. These findings highlight user intent as a key dimension of GEO evaluation and support anchored adaptation across recommendation needs and decision stages.

</details>


### [62] [ReuseGR: Offline--Online Decoupled Generative Retrieval via Adaptive Prefix Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04259)
*Xujing Wang,Yufeng Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Chao Wei,Cai Xu,Jie Zhao,Weiru Zhang,Wei Zhao,Ziyu Guan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种兼顾跨请求计算复用与个性化检索的方法，在提升召回效果的同时降低在线时延，并在真实线上测试中带来点击率和收入增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型生成式检索模型能够提升检索质量，但逐请求生成完整语义标识符会造成重复计算，难以满足低时延服务需求。另一方面，即使查询相同，不同用户的交互历史也要求检索结果保持个性化，因此需要在复用计算与个性化之间取得平衡。

Method: 方法将检索拆为查询级候选规划和请求级个性化补全：离线使用七十亿参数模型，为相同查询生成可共享的语义标识符前缀集合；在线使用五亿参数模型，依据用户历史选择前缀并生成后续标记。系统根据历史覆盖情况为每个查询选择缓存深度，以平衡计算复用和候选覆盖。为增强在线补全能力，研究通过路径级知识蒸馏，将大模型的后缀评分知识迁移至小模型；再通过预算截断策略，在固定分支预算下选择候选分支，并结合在线策略蒸馏和组相对策略优化加以训练。

Result: 在约二百万条查询—商品记录、包含三百三十八万个商品的工业搜索广告数据集上，与全程在线运行的五亿参数模型相比，该方法使百名命中率提升三点八三个百分点，在线平均时延降低百分之三十八点九；与全程在线运行的七十亿参数模型相比，其百名命中率相当，在线时延降低百分之五十七点七。为期十四天的用户级线上测试中，点击率相对提升百分之零点九三，每千次请求收入相对提升百分之一点零五。

Conclusion: 通过将共享的查询级规划与个性化的请求级补全相结合，并压缩候选分支的计算开销，该方法在保持强检索效果的同时显著降低在线时延；线上测试也表明，这种设计能够转化为实际业务收益。

Abstract: Larger generative retrieval models can improve retrieval quality, but their decoding cost makes low-latency serving difficult. When different users submit the same query, generating complete Semantic IDs (SIDs) for every request repeats computation, while their interaction histories call for different results. The challenge is to reuse large-model computation across requests while keeping retrieval personalized.
  We propose \textbf{ReuseGR}, which separates query-level candidate planning from request-level personalized completion. A 7B offline planner generates SID prefix sets shared across requests with the same query. A 0.5B online model uses each request's history to select prefixes and generate the remaining tokens. ReuseCut chooses a cache depth for each query based on historical coverage, balancing computation reuse and candidate coverage. To strengthen online completion, we transfer 7B suffix-scoring knowledge to 0.5B through path-level knowledge distillation (Path KD). BudgetCut then selects scored branches to improve final top-100 recall under a fixed branch budget. Its selection policy is initialized by on-policy distillation (OPD) and refined through group-relative policy optimization (GRPO).
  On an industrial search-advertising dataset with approximately 2M query--item records and 3.38M catalog items, ReuseGR improves query-level HR@100 by 3.83 percentage points and reduces mean online latency by 38.9\% relative to an all-online 0.5B baseline when prefixes are prepared. It achieves HR@100 comparable to an all-online 7B baseline with 57.7\% lower online latency. A 14-day user-level online A/B test against the 0.5B baseline shows relative gains of 0.93\% in click-through rate and 1.05\% in revenue per thousand requests.

</details>


### [63] [Do Retrieval Ranking Models Capture Event Order? A Study of Timeline-Constrained Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04343)
*Alon Eirew,Lidan Avisar,Kfir Bar,Ido Dagan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究检索模型能否识别查询事件的时间顺序。实验发现，常用语义检索器和神经重排序器对时间约束的处理有限，而显式考虑时间顺序能显著改善排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义检索旨在依据查询含义匹配文档，但模型是否真正编码事件之间的时间关系尚不明确。对于由有序事件组成的查询，相关文档不仅要涵盖事件，还应呈现与查询相容的时间进程，因此有必要评估现有模型对此类约束的处理能力。

Method: 构建一个聚焦于时间线约束文档检索的新评测集合，并在受控设置下比较常用稠密检索器、神经重排序器、词汇检索方法及一种时间线感知方法。任务要求系统根据自然语言事件序列，检索事件顺序相符的文档。

Result: 先进语义检索器和神经重排序器相较词汇检索仅有有限提升，表明其语义匹配能力并不能稳定捕捉时间约束。相比之下，时间线感知方法取得了显著的排序提升。

Conclusion: 在时间线约束检索中，事件顺序本身是重要的排序信号。显式建模时间关系能够改善检索效果，后续研究应进一步增强语义检索器的时间推理与时间关系建模能力。

Abstract: Semantic retrieval models aim to match documents to a query's "meaning", yet it remains unclear whether they successfully encode temporal relations between events in their ranking mechanisms. We investigate this question by studying timeline-constrained document retrieval, where a query is an ordered sequence of natural-language event statements while a relevant document should match those events in a compatible temporal progression. We then test whether widely used dense retrievers and neural rerankers capture such constraints, and whether explicitly preserving temporal order improves ranking, using a controlled setting over a novel focused evaluation collection. Experiments show that state-of-the-art semantic retrievers and rerankers provide only limited improvements over lexical retrieval, suggesting that their semantic matching capabilities do not reliably capture temporal constraints. We then compare a timeline-aware method with both semantic matching and standard rerankers and find that it yields substantial improvements. This demonstrates that considering temporal order can provide a strong ranking signal in this setting, suggesting future research on improving temporal modeling in semantic retrievers.

</details>


### [64] [DualEvolve: Dual-Level Self-Evolution of Mechanism and Orchestration for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04439)
*Tianqi Gao,Chenxu Wang,Wenxuan Yang,Yueqi Mou,Biao Tang,Ming Liu,Peng Yan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出双层协同演化框架，同时改进推荐模型及其研发流程；在全部六十四项设置与指标比较中，均优于三种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统的持续改进需要反复设计模型、优化训练并验证实验。现有以任务表现为导向的方法，主要依据候选方案的最终分数选择更新，却缺少对实验过程质量的独立优化信号。因此，智能体即使能改进模型，也未必能持续提升后续实验的质量。

Method: 提出双重演化框架。内层演化根据任务反馈修改推荐模型与训练代码，以提升推荐效果；外层演化评估内层实验过程，并结合评估分数和执行记录更新运行框架及技能规则，同时依据后续轮次的得分调整编排策略，从而改善之后实验的质量。

Result: 实验覆盖电影评分一百万数据集及三个亚马逊评论二〇二三数据子集，包含四种推荐方法和十六种设置。在六十四项设置与指标比较中，该框架的测试集均值均高于基础方法、开放演化方法和深度演化方法。相较深度演化方法，其宏平均相对提升为：前五位命中率百分之七点六，前五位归一化折损累计增益百分之九。

Conclusion: 结果表明，同时优化推荐机制与智能体研发流程，能够促进推荐系统的递归式自我改进，并在所测试的数据集、方法和设置中取得一致优势。

Abstract: Recommender systems are widely used in e-commerce and content platforms, where improvement requires repeated model design, training optimization, and experimental validation. Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate this development cycle. Sustained autonomous improvement requires agents to improve both recommendation models and their development workflows. However, task-performance-driven approaches select updates mainly by candidates' final scores, without a separate optimization signal for experimentation quality. We therefore present DualEvolve, a dual-level framework that co-evolves recommendation mechanisms and agent orchestration toward recursive self-improvement. Inner evolution revises model and training code using task feedback to improve recommendation performance. Outer evolution scores the inner-loop experimental process, updates harness and skill rules using these scores and execution traces, and adjusts orchestration choices based on next-round scores to improve the quality of subsequent experiments. Experiments on MovieLens-1M and three Amazon Reviews 2023 subsets cover four recommendation methods and 16 settings. DualEvolve achieves higher test means than Base, OpenEvolve, and DeepEvolve in all 64 setting-metric comparisons, with macro-averaged relative gains over DeepEvolve of 7.6% (HR@5) and 9.0% (NDCG@5).

</details>


### [65] [SourceFlow: Collaborative Source Coupling for Flow-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04455)
*Chenghao Wu,Yongqi Zhou,Jin Chen,Yifeng Zhou,Jinchang Zhu,Junwei Pan,Chao Zhou,Zhixiang Feng,Chengguo Yin,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将协同信息融入源分布的流式协同过滤方法，通过构造与用户目标交互历史相匹配的源向量，提升推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式推荐在去噪过程中可能扭曲原始偏好信号。现有流式协同过滤方法虽能以较少步骤完成推断，但通常依据全局物品频率独立采样源向量，使源向量缺乏针对目标用户历史的协同线索，不利于模型学习重建用户交互。

Method: 提出“源流”方法，将协同结构直接融入源分布，而非额外增加预测器的条件信息。邻域耦合通过用用户尚未交互、但具有协同关联的物品替换历史物品，构造偏移的二值源向量；协同共识利用与已观测物品共同出现的其他用户的交互证据，进一步调整替换概率；延展式恢复则在训练的后期流动时刻保留这些替换，为模型恢复原始交互提供更多机会。

Result: 在五个推荐数据集上的实验显示，该方法稳定优于有竞争力的基线；相较于表现最强的流式协同过滤基线，其十位召回率平均相对提升百分之十五点七。控制实验还表明，提升依赖源向量与目标历史的正确配对：将同一批源向量重新分配给不同用户后，效果反而下降。

Conclusion: 研究表明，流式推荐的源分布不应只反映全局交互频率，还应包含与目标用户历史相关的协同结构。通过构造并保留这种用户相关的协同源信息，可以显著改善交互历史的恢复与推荐效果。

Abstract: Diffusion-based recommenders learn user preferences by denoising interaction histories, but the added noise can distort the original preference signals. Flow-based collaborative filtering (CF) instead learns a discrete path from a sampled source to the user's observed training interactions (the target history), enabling inference in a few steps. Existing flow-based CF methods sample sources using global item frequencies to reflect the sparsity and popularity of observed interactions. Each source is sampled independently of its target history, leaving the predictor without user-specific source cues for learning to reconstruct that history. We propose SourceFlow, which brings collaborative structure into the source distribution itself rather than adding further conditioning to the predictor. Like inference from observed histories, training uses collaborative evidence to predict missing interactions. Specifically, Neighborhood Coupling constructs displaced binary sources by replacing historical items with unobserved collaborative alternatives. Collaborative Consensus further refines replacement probabilities using evidence from other interactions of users who share the observed item. Stretched Restoration retains these replacements at late flow times during training, providing more opportunities to recover the original interactions. Experiments on five recommendation datasets show consistent improvements over competitive baselines, including a mean relative Recall@10 gain of 15.7% over the strongest flow-based baseline. Controlled experiments confirm that these gains depend on correctly pairing collaborative sources with their target histories, as reassigning the same source vectors across users reverses the gains. Code will be publicly released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [66] [$τ$-Rec-Books: A Disclosure-Controlled Benchmark for Requirement Elicitation in Agentic Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04459)
*Bharath Sivaram Narasimhan,Karthik R. Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出“τ-荐书”基准，评估对话式推荐系统在用户逐步透露需求时发现偏好的能力；实验显示，需求越隐蔽，推荐成功率越低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式推荐虽能提供自然的交互方式，但系统必须先逐步弄清用户需求，才能推荐合适物品。现有评估需要区分用户何时透露需求，并检验系统能否通过推荐及用户反馈推断未明说的偏好。

Method: 研究构建“τ-荐书”基准：固定用户需求，只改变需求透露方式，分别设置用户一开始说出全部需求、先说一项且其余可询问，以及部分需求始终隐藏三种情形。隐藏需求只能通过提出书籍并根据用户接受或拒绝来推断。基准包含从维基数据整理的两万本书和四百五十项任务，采用规则模拟用户，并依据书籍元数据评分，不依赖大语言模型裁判；同时比较四种智能体与两种搜索接口。

Result: 各智能体和搜索接口的成功率均随需求更难获取而下降：全部需求预先提供时平均为百分之九十七点五，部分需求需询问时为百分之七十五点六，存在隐藏需求时仅为百分之二十八点三。部分需求任务的主要问题是智能体没有主动询问；明确要求询问后，各智能体成功率均至少达到百分之九十三点一。隐藏需求任务的多数失败，源于智能体推荐了此前未向用户提出过的书籍。要求先提出候选书后，智能体表现仅与“推荐热门书籍”的简单基线相当，明显弱于能利用拒绝反馈排除潜在需求的基线。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示，当前对话式推荐智能体在主动询问和利用用户反馈推断隐含偏好方面仍有明显不足。尤其在隐藏需求场景中，仅生成最终推荐并不够；系统需要先提出候选项，并系统地利用接受或拒绝信息缩小需求范围。

Abstract: While conversational recommendation systems provide a more natural user interface, they face the challenge of progressively discovering a user's needs before they can recommend an item that satisfies them. To evaluate the ability of AI agents at this elicitation process, we introduce $τ$-Rec-Books, a benchmark that holds each user request fixed while varying only when the user reveals their requirements: all at the start (volunteer), one at the start and the rest when asked (mixed), or some never (hidden). A hidden requirement can be found only by proposing books and inferring it from the user's accept or reject verdicts. We generate 450 tasks over a catalog of 20,000 books from Wikidata, develop a rule-based simulated user to answer the agent, and score recommendations against catalog metadata without an LLM judge. Across four agents and two search interfaces, success rates fall for every agent and interface, on average from 0.975 (volunteer) to 0.756 (mixed) and 0.283 (hidden). Mixed tasks fail mainly because agents do not ask, and when told to ask, every agent reaches at least 0.931. On hidden tasks, most failures come from agents recommending a book they never proposed to the user. When told to propose first, agents only match a simple baseline that proposes popular books, and stay well below one that uses rejection to rule out possible requirements. We release the catalog pipeline, task generator, simulated user, validators, reference agents, and all agent traces at https://github.com/nbharaths/tau-rec/tree/main/data/tracks/books-wikidata-v2.

</details>


### [67] [Collaborative Search Meets Large Language Models: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04619)
*Peiyuan Gong,Jiamian Li,Erhan Zhang,Fengming Liang,Kexin Liu,Yue Chen,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究比较了两类大语言模型协作搜索代理与传统搜索工具在协作搜索中的表现，发现智能代理能减轻负担、促进协作，但可能降低最终报告的丰富度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理正在进入共享沟通空间，支持多人协作搜寻信息，但它们如何影响用户的搜索体验、行为及协作成果，尚缺乏与传统搜索工具的系统比较。

Method: 研究招募八十名学生组成四十对。每对参与者分别在四种条件下完成四项协作搜索任务：依赖模型参数知识回答问题的问答代理；利用多人对话语境改写查询、检索网页并生成有来源依据答案，必要时提出澄清问题的协作搜索代理；在聊天中返回搜索结果列表的插件；以及手动使用谷歌搜索。每项任务后，参与者填写体验问卷并独立撰写报告，全部任务结束后再接受个人访谈。

Result: 研究发现，基于大语言模型的代理降低了用户感知到的工作负担，并有助于协作，同时改变了沟通方式和证据使用实践。代理也促使参与者更独立地改写信息，但报告内容的丰富度有所下降。

Conclusion: 大语言模型代理不仅是搜索工具，也会重塑协作搜索中的互动与信息处理方式。它们在减轻负担和支持协作方面具有优势，但可能带来成果内容不够丰富的代价；因此，评估此类工具时应同时考察用户体验、搜索行为与协作产出。

Abstract: Collaborative search enables users to address complex information needs jointly. Advances in large language models (LLMs) enable autonomous agents to support collaborative information seeking within shared communication spaces. However, little is known about how these agents shape search experiences, behaviors, and outcomes compared with conventional search tools. We conduct a user study with 80 students working in 40 pairs. Each pair completes four collaborative search tasks under four scenarios: QAAgent, which answers queries using an LLM's parametric knowledge; CoSearchAgent, which uses multi-user conversational context to reformulate queries, retrieve web information, and generate source-grounded answers, while asking clarifying questions when needed; an in-chat search plugin that returns result lists; and manual Google Search. After each task, participants complete several questionnaires assessing their search experience and independently write a report; after all tasks, they participate in individual interviews. Results show that LLM-based agents reduce perceived workload, support collaboration, and reshape communication and evidence-use practices. They also encourage more independent paraphrasing, albeit at the cost of report richness. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic empirical comparison of LLM-based agents and conventional tools for collaborative search across search experience, user behavior, and collaborative outcomes.

</details>


### [68] [QueryFlow: Multi-Hop Graph Retrieval with Adaptive Query Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04625)
*Manzong Huang,Chenyang Bu,Yi He,Jiapu Wang,Xindong Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于多跳检索增强生成的图检索方法，通过联合更新证据表示与查询条件，提升多跳证据召回和问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答需要从相关网页中找回彼此补充的证据，但若只依据原始问题独立评估证据，可能漏掉那些只有结合中间事实才显现相关性的内容。迭代检索虽能利用中间事实改写查询，图检索虽能显式表示证据关系，但现有固定查询的图检索方法未能根据累积的关系上下文调整查询条件。

Method: 论文将多跳证据恢复视为证据表示与查询条件的协同演化过程。所提方法循环整合由查询条件引导的图上下文摘要，更新共享查询状态；该状态进一步控制后续图传播中的边界输入和关系特征，使检索器既能更新对证据的表示，也能据累积上下文调整搜索方向，以寻找互补证据。

Result: 在四个具有代表性的多跳问答数据集上，该方法相较代表性基线，十项以内的宏平均召回率提高4.98个百分点，完整证据召回率提高8.40个百分点，大语言模型问答准确率提高8.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，多跳检索不应只更新证据表示，还应让查询条件随关系上下文共同演化。该方法通过查询与图上下文的循环交互，改善了多跳证据恢复，并在多个数据集上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) supports Web information seeking and fact verification by recovering complementary evidence across related pages within a limited retrieval budget. Scoring evidence independently against the original question can miss supporting units whose relevance emerges through intermediate facts. Iterative retrieval incorporates these facts into subsequent queries, while graph retrieval explicitly models relations among evidence units. Yet fixed-query graph retrievers update entity representations without adapting the query condition to accumulated relational context. We view multi-hop evidence recovery as the coupled evolution of evidence representations and query conditions, with accumulated context informing both what the retriever represents and how it searches for complementary evidence. QueryFlow realizes this perspective by recurrently integrating query-conditioned summaries of graph context into a shared query state, which controls boundary inputs and relation features for subsequent propagation. Across four representative multi-hop question answering (QA) datasets, QueryFlow achieves macro Recall@10 (R@10), Full Recall@10 (FR@10), and LLM-Acc gains of 4.98, 8.40, and 8.0 percentage points over representative baselines, respectively.

</details>


### [69] [SPADE: Spatiotemporal Preference Alignment and Diversity Enhancement for Local Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04662)
*Chenxu Wang,Wenxuan Yang,Yueqi Mou,Shuxian Bi,Biao Tang,Peng Yan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向本地生活应用首页搜索推荐的生成框架，通过改进长尾词生成、时空理解、偏好对齐和候选多样性，提升推荐质量；离线与线上实验均显示显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地生活应用需要从数百万词汇中生成搜索词，而不只是从固定候选集里挑选。推荐结果既要符合用户偏好，也要匹配细粒度时空需求、覆盖长尾意图，并在有限候选位中保持多样性。现有方法往往分别处理这些问题：常规分词不利于长尾词生成，粗粒度位置编码难以捕捉具体地域需求，通用偏好优化与线上曝光—点击分布不够一致，标准束搜索则容易产生相似结果。

Method: 提出端到端生成框架“时空偏好对齐与多样性增强”。其一，目标对齐子词合并方法将业务价值信号纳入分词合并过程，提高长尾搜索词的生成概率；其二，时空自适应表征以连续、多尺度编码和软门控替代粗粒度位置词元，强化精细时空定位；其三，多维自适应奖励采样偏好优化结合跨领域预训练与线上曝光—点击数据，通过复合奖励和自适应困难负样本采样实现偏好对齐；其四，采用基于逻辑函数的多样性束搜索，在保持召回能力的同时扩大意图覆盖。

Result: 大规模生产数据离线实验中，相较最强生成式基线，前五项命中率提升百分之五点六，前五项地理一致性指标降低百分之四十点三，前五项执行率提升百分之一点九。为期一周的线上对照实验中，模块点击率提升百分之一点八八，搜索量提升百分之零点一七七，搜索用户数提升百分之零点一零五。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将生成空间、时空语境、用户偏好分布和候选列表质量进行联合优化，能够改善本地生活应用的主动搜索词推荐，并带来离线指标与线上业务指标的同步提升。

Abstract: Proactive query recommendation aims to suggest relevant search queries to users on the homepage of local service apps. Unlike conventional recommendation over a fixed item set, this requires open-set generation from a vocabulary of millions, where outputs must simultaneously align with user preference, match fine-grained spatiotemporal context, cover long-tail intents, and provide diverse alternatives within a few candidate slots. Existing approaches address these axes in isolation: standard tokenization suppresses long-tail queries, coarse location encoding misses fine-grained geographic demand, generic preference optimization fails to align with production impression-click distributions, and standard beam search collapses to near-duplicate outputs. We present SPADE (Spatiotemporal Preference Alignment and Diversity Enhancement), an end-to-end generative framework that jointly optimizes the generation space, contextual representation, preference distribution, and output slate quality through four components: Objective-Aligned BPE (OA-BPE) aligns subword merging with business value signals to boost long-tail query generation probability; Spatio-Temporal Adaptive Representation (STAR) replaces coarse location tokens with continuous multi-scale encodings and soft gating for precise spatiotemporal grounding; MARS-DPO (Multi-dimensional Adaptive Reward Sampling DPO) combines cross-domain pre-training with preference alignment on production impression-click data via composite rewards and adaptive hard negative sampling; and Sigmoid Diversity Beam Search (SDBS) improves intent coverage without sacrificing recall. Offline experiments on large-scale production data show that SPADE outperforms the strongest generative baseline by +5.6% HR@5, -40.3% GeoC@5, and +1.9% Exec@5. A one-week online A/B test confirms production impact: +1.88% Module CTR, +0.177% Search Volume, and +0.105% Search DAU.

</details>


### [70] [OneTrans-GSR: A Generative Slate Ranking Paradigm Unifying Reranking and Mix-Ranking with One Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04720)
*Feng Cheng,Yu-Hsuan Huang,Fengyi Wang,Chaofan Zhou,Zhennan Zhu,Huan He,Bo Zhang,Ruoqi Zhang,Zhanfeng Mo,Ziwei Liu,Lanyi Li,Yifeng Pan,Junhua Long,Ziqiu Zhou,Yue Lin,Shaowei Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文将候选重排与异质内容混排统一建模为端到端策略生成问题。该方法在大规模线上系统中提升了交易、广告价值和复购表现，并降低排序链路延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统通常把大量计算用于逐候选评分，但最终曝光列表仍主要依赖人工规则或容量有限的专用模型。这些方案虽能编码业务知识，却难以充分利用更多数据与计算，限制了候选列表的组合空间，也使目标权衡依赖人工调参。论文希望建立一种可随计算资源增加而持续改进的列表生成方式。

Method: 论文把候选重排和混合排序统一为学习问题，并提出单一的解码式策略模型，基于既有推荐模型骨干端到端生成最终曝光列表。训练时，由奖励模型评价完整列表，并通过强化学习优化列表级及长期目标；生成时则显式执行业务约束。该策略可在统一列表中组合商品卡片、短视频、直播和广告等异质内容。

Result: 离线评估显示，随着奖励模型和策略模型投入的计算增加，列表质量持续提升。大规模线上对照实验中，每用户交易总额提升百分之二点六三，广告主价值提升百分之二点零六，复购率提升百分之二点零三；候选重排与混合排序的端到端延迟降低百分之十三。

Conclusion: 研究表明，以通用的学习策略替代人工启发式规则和专用低容量模型，可以实现可扩展的端到端曝光列表生成。该方向不仅能统一异质内容的排序，还提供了通过增加计算持续优化算法表现的路径。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems allocate substantial computation to pointwise candidate scoring, yet separate reranking and mix-ranking stages still assemble the final exposure slate using hand-crafted rules and specialized low-capacity models. Mix-ranking combines product cards, short videos, livestreams, and advertising cards supplied by distinct upstream business channels. Although these rules and models encode valuable domain knowledge, they are inherently limited in leveraging additional data and computation. They constrain the combinatorial space of candidate slates and determine objective trade-offs through manual tuning. To enable scalable and effective slate generation, we reformulate reranking and mix-ranking as a unified learning problem whose performance can improve with computation. Specifically, we instantiate this formulation across the reranking and mix-ranking stages and propose OneTrans-GSR, a single decoder-only policy that extends our deployed OneTrans backbone to generate the exposure slate end-to-end. During training, a reward model evaluates complete slates and guides reinforcement learning toward slate-level and long-term objectives. During decoding, business constraints are explicitly enforced. Offline evaluation shows that slate quality consistently improves as reward-model and policy compute increase. Deployed in a large-scale industrial recommendation system, OneTrans-GSR mixes heterogeneous content types within a unified slate. In online A/B tests, it improves GMV/u by 2.63%, advertiser value by 2.06%, and repurchase rate by 2.03%, while reducing end-to-end latency across the reranking and mix-ranking stages by 13%. These results mark a paradigm shift in slate generation: moving beyond human-engineered heuristics toward a general learned policy creates a scalable path for further algorithmic optimization and compute-driven improvement.

</details>


### [71] [Learning to Rank Explanations for Recommendation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04775)
*Ben Kabongo,Vincent Guigue*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于文本和对比学习的个性化解释语句排序方法，在三个领域的实验中取得领先表现，并验证了用户历史信息的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中的自然语言解释需要既有事实依据，又符合个人偏好，但现有大语言模型生成的解释难以评估。按语句排序、从评论中选取解释，便于使用成熟的排序指标进行细粒度且可复现的评测；然而，既有方法多依赖协同表示，语义增强效果有限，而面向个性化语句排序的语言模型适配研究不足。

Method: 提出一种完全基于文本的个性化解释排序框架，将任务建模为稠密检索。查询编码器把用户交互历史与目标物品元数据表示为向量，语句编码器将候选解释映射到同一向量空间；再通过多正例对比学习联合适配两个编码器，使其学习个性化相关性。

Result: 在亚马逊美妆、亚马逊玩具和 Yelp 三个领域，所提方法的前一位归一化折损累计增益均为最高，较对比方法高出 1.06 至 8.25 个百分点，相对提升为 11.8% 至 65.9%。在两个亚马逊数据集的前五位排序中也领先，但在 Yelp 上仍有方法表现更强。任务适配提升了五种受测语言模型骨干的效果，并可迁移至其他领域而无需目标领域微调。反事实分析进一步表明，用户专属历史对排序结果具有重要作用。

Conclusion: 将语言模型针对任务进行适配，并以稠密检索方式处理候选解释，有望提升个性化语句排序效果。用户历史是实现个性化的关键信息，跨领域迁移结果也显示该方法具有一定泛化潜力；不过，不同领域的表现并不完全一致。

Abstract: Recommender systems are ubiquitous on the Web, making understandable explanations essential for users. Although large language models (LLMs) increasingly generate natural-language justifications, evaluating their factual grounding and personalized relevance remains challenging. Statement-level ranking offers an alternative by selecting concise, review-derived explanations, enabling fine-grained and reproducible evaluation using established ranking metrics. However, existing statement ranking methods largely rely on collaborative representations, while semantic enhancements often fail to outperform simple user-history popularity baselines. Moreover, directly adapting language models for personalized statement ranking remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce LMStaR (Language Models for Statement Ranking), a fully text-based framework that formulates personalized explanation ranking as dense retrieval. A query encoder represents the user's interaction history and target-item metadata, while a statement encoder maps candidate explanations into a shared embedding space. Both encoders are jointly adapted through multi-positive contrastive learning to learn personalized relevance. Experiments on Amazon Beauty, Amazon Toys, and Yelp show that LMStaR achieves the highest NDCG@1 across all three domains, outperforming CLER by 1.06--8.25 points (+11.8--65.9%). It also leads top-five ranking on both Amazon datasets, although CLER remains stronger on Yelp. Task-specific adaptation improves all five evaluated backbones and transfers across domains without target-domain fine-tuning. Counterfactual analyses further reveal the importance of user-specific histories. These findings highlight the potential of task-adapted language models for personalized explanation ranking.

</details>


### [72] [Scaling Laws For Data-Constrained Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04979)
*Bowen Fu,Jingtao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究监督数据有限时，密集检索模型的最佳可达评测损失如何随模型规模和独特训练样本量变化，并提出更适合该场景的缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索模型通常依赖有限的高质量标注或交互日志进行微调，而这些数据会在多个训练轮次中重复使用。因此，数据充足场景下的常见缩放规律未必适用。论文希望厘清在固定模型规模与数据预算下，模型能够达到的最低评测损失如何变化。

Method: 将固定模型规模和数据预算条件下训练过程中达到的最低评测损失定义为最低可达评测损失。研究者先分析学习率对训练轨迹和最终损失的影响，并用表征相似性分析比较达到最低损失时的模型表示；随后在微软马可检索数据集的子集上微调三十五个双向编码器系列密集检索模型，拟合模型参数量与独特样本数对损失及达到该损失所需训练步数的缩放规律，同时考察权重衰减的最佳取值。

Result: 学习率显著影响优化过程，但对最终最低可达评测损失影响较小；按优化步数重新缩放后，不同学习率下的学习曲线趋于重合，达到最低损失的检查点也学到高度相似的表示。最低损失可由非嵌入参数量和独特样本数构成的乘法幂律准确描述，效果优于常见的加法缩放形式。研究还发现，权重衰减的最佳设置对应近似不变的指数移动平均时间尺度，并能带来更低损失及更强的域内、域外检索表现。综合损失与训练步数规律，在所研究范围内，增加预训练模型规模对降低损失的微调计算效率约为增加域内独特数据量的百分之一百四十六。

Conclusion: 在数据受限的密集检索微调场景中，模型规模与独特样本量的乘法幂律比传统加法形式更能描述最低可达性能。优化设置主要影响达到该性能的过程，而非最终上限；合理设置权重衰减可进一步改善表现。研究结果还表明，在实验覆盖的范围内，扩大预训练模型规模比增加独特域内数据更具计算效率，但这一结论不应直接外推到未研究的规模或数据条件。

Abstract: Dense retrieval models are typically obtained by fine-tuning pretrained language models on supervised retrieval data, where high-quality annotations or interaction logs are often limited and reused across many epochs. This data-constrained regime differs from the abundant-data setting assumed by standard scaling laws, making it unclear how the best attainable retrieval performance changes with model size and unique fine-tuning data. We study this question through the minimum achievable evaluation loss (MAEL), defined as the lowest evaluation loss reached during training under fixed model-size and data-budget constraints. We first show that learning-rate choices strongly affect optimization trajectories but have little effect on the final attainable MAEL: after rescaling the optimization-step axis, learning curves collapse across learning rates, and CKA analysis shows that checkpoints reaching MAEL learn highly similar representations. We then fine-tune 35 BERT-family dense retrievers on MS MARCO subsets and find that MAEL is accurately described by a simple multiplicative power law over non-embedding parameters and unique samples, outperforming commonly used additive scaling formulations. We further derive a scaling law for the training step at which MAEL is reached and show that optimal weight decay follows an approximately invariant EMA timescale, yielding lower MAEL and stronger retrieval performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks. Finally, combining loss and step scaling suggests that, in our observed regime, increasing pretrained model size is approximately 1.46 times as fine-tuning-compute-efficient as increasing unique in-domain data for reducing MAEL.

</details>


### [73] [When Category Means Move: A Paired-Support Audit of Recommendation Logs with Random Insertions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05006)
*Yu Zhang,Zhihan Wang,Yuhang Lu,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究发现，生产推荐日志中的类别均值受用户、视频及曝光支持集构成影响；用户去均值能显著降低绝对误差，但对相对类别差异和排序的改善有限。类别校准在后续测试中优于单一标量校准，但决策收益并不稳健，结论仅适用于相应重叠样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别仪表盘常用均值概括推荐表现，却忽略服务策略决定了哪些用户、视频和情境进入日志。因此，观测到的类别差异可能反映样本构成，而非类别本身的效果。本文旨在审计这些汇总指标的误差来源，并检验校准能否改善后续预测与决策。

Method: 研究使用快手随机插入数据，在两类日志共有的12,472名用户和5,486个视频上比较类别均值。评估标准均值、用户去均值并以随机总体均值锚定等方法的绝对误差、中心化平均绝对误差及排序相关性；通过完整支持集账本分解加权平方误差；在冻结的后续测试中比较类别校准与标量校准，并考察不同决策截断点的遗憾变化。此外，使用互不重叠的随机参考样本及已知真值压力测试检验稳健性。

Result: 标准均值的绝对误差和中心化平均绝对误差分别为0.1935和0.0491，排序相关性为0.7833。用户去均值加随机总体均值锚定使绝对误差降低80.0%，但中心化误差仅降低21.0%，且未能稳定改善排序。误差分解显示，视频内残差占72.41%，视频构成占23.90%，随机支持集仅占3.65%。后续测试中，类别校准的平均绝对误差为0.0251，优于标量校准的0.0531，二者直接差值为−0.0280，区间为[−0.0296，−0.0172]。决策效果则依截断点而异：前十名的名义遗憾变化为−0.0062，区间为[−0.0084，−0.0003]；前五名和前二十名的区间均包含零。对六项比较进行家族错误率调整后，前十名区间为[−0.0136，0.0013]，包含零；随机参考样本上的六项名义比较也均未排除零。已知真值压力测试表明，该方法对均值可加情形只能部分改善，在视频构成偏移及重叠较弱时则会失效。

Conclusion: 类别均值及其校正应被视为特定日志与重叠样本条件下的描述性结果，而非类别固有影响或普遍有效的修正。类别校准在预测误差上显示优势，但尚无稳健证据证明其能普遍改善推荐决策。所报告的不确定性刻画的是给定已实现视频目录和日历条件下的用户群波动，不能据此作更广泛的因果或普适性主张。

Abstract: Content-category dashboards summarize production recommendation logs, although the serving policy selects the users, items, and contexts behind each mean. Using KuaiRand random-insertion impressions, we audit these summaries on the same 12,472 users and 5,486 videos in both logs. Standard means have absolute/centered mean absolute error (MAE) 0.1935/0.0491 and rank correlation 0.7833. User demeaning plus a random grand-mean anchor reduces absolute MAE by 80.0%, but centered MAE by only 21.0%, without reliably improving rank. A complete-support ledger allocates weighted squared error mainly to within-video residual (72.41%) and video mix (23.90%), with 3.65% from random support. In a frozen later test, early category calibration reaches MAE 0.0251 versus 0.0531 for scalar calibration; their direct difference is -0.0280 [-0.0296, -0.0172]. Decision evidence is cutoff-specific: the nominal top-10 regret change for row-weighted demeaning versus naive is -0.0062 [-0.0084, -0.0003], while top-5 and top-20 intervals include zero. The six-contrast family-wise top-10 interval [-0.0136, 0.0013] includes zero. An outcome-free disjoint randomized-reference split also leaves all six nominal contrasts covering zero. A known-truth stress test shows incomplete improvement under a mean-additive positive control and failure under item mix and weak overlap. All decompositions are descriptive and each result is conditional on its overlap cohort. KuaiRand intervals quantify user-cluster variation conditional on the realized catalog and calendar; we claim neither intrinsic category effects nor a universally valid correction.

</details>


### [74] [Dataset Recommendation for Heterogeneous and Dynamic Scholarly Graphs via Multimodal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05071)
*Ornella Irrera,Matteo Lissandrini,Daniele Dell'Aglio,Gianmaria Silvello*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态学术注意力网络，用于从学术知识图谱中推荐与研究论文相关的数据集；实验表明，该方法优于现有先进方法，并能适应不同程度的图谱演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据资源日益增多，但研究者难以从各类仓储和学术出版物中发现相关数据集。现有学术知识图谱规模庞大、异构且持续变化，同时存在元数据缺失或错误、记录重复，以及数据集之间关联薄弱等问题，导致许多数据集难以被发现和复用。基于直接链接或流行度的检索方式也容易遗漏有价值的数据集，因此需要更可靠、适应性更强的推荐方法。

Method: 提出多模态学术注意力网络，一种面向异构学术知识图谱的多模态图表示学习方法。该方法结合感知图结构的表示与文本表示，使文本元数据有限甚至缺失时仍可进行推荐；并支持传导式、半归纳式和全归纳式场景，以适应持续演化的知识图谱，且无需重新训练。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法在数据集推荐任务中优于现有先进方法。摘要还指出，它能够在不同知识图谱演化场景下工作，并具有稳健性与可扩展性；但未提供具体数据集、指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该研究通过融合图结构信息与文本信息，缓解了学术知识图谱中数据集关联不足、元数据不完整带来的推荐困难，为提升数据集的可发现性和复用提供了一种可适应图谱变化的方案。

Abstract: Researchers struggle to find relevant datasets amid the growing volume of data across repositories and scholarly publications. Manual dataset retrieval is time-consuming and often fails to identify valuable resources that are not easily discoverable through direct links or popularity metrics. Dataset recommendation, which aims to identify datasets relevant to a paper, author, or query, has become essential for improving dataset discoverability and reuse. However, this task remains challenging because Scholarly Knowledge Graphs (SKGs) are large-scale, heterogeneous, dynamic, and incomplete. Although openly available SKGs organize scientific knowledge by linking scholarly outcomes, they often lack meaningful connections between datasets, leaving them isolated and underutilized. These graphs contain millions of evolving relations but still suffer from missing or inaccurate metadata, duplicate records, and weak dataset integration. This work presents the Multimodal Scholarly Attention Network (MM-SAN), a heterogeneous multimodal graph representation learning method for recommending datasets relevant to research publications within real-world SKGs. MM-SAN combines topology-aware and text-based embeddings, enabling effective recommendations even when textual metadata is limited or absent. It operates in transductive, semi-inductive, and fully inductive settings, adapting to evolving SKGs without requiring retraining. Experiments show that MM-SAN outperforms state-of-the-art methods, providing a robust and scalable solution for dataset recommendation.

</details>


### [75] [HARP: Training-Free Dual-Profile Agentic Communication for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05319)
*Zhen Tao,Xun Zhou,Xuhui Chen,Ziyue Qiao,Qingqiang Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文将大语言模型推荐重新表述为智能体间的通信过程，并提出一种无需训练的双侧画像框架，在四个公开数据集上显著提升首位命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的推荐存在用户与物品不对称的问题：只有用户拥有结构化画像，物品侧缺少对应的自我描述；画像信号仅进入上下文容量受限的重排序提示，未用于检索；用户画像在预热后被冻结，也缺乏合理的更新机制。这些缺陷限制了画像信息的利用与持续适应能力。

Method: 论文提出由四个阶段构成的通信视角：构建自我描述、沿协同图传递、理解对方信息、依据后续交互同步更新。基于此，构建一种冻结大语言模型、无需梯度训练的推荐框架：从规则中提取对称的物品画像，不增加大语言模型调用；在检索阶段利用可分解的画像同质性信号；并通过单次反思过程更新用户画像。

Result: 在四个公开基准上，该方法相较最强的已发表基线，将首位命中率提升百分之六点九至百分之二十四点零；各数据集经家族错误率校正后的显著性检验均小于十的负九次方。消融实验和逐案例归因分析表明，各组成操作带来互补收益，其中检索侧的画像门控贡献最大；反思更新过程也带来了相应权衡。

Conclusion: 论文提出了基于智能体通信的推荐问题表述，并以双侧画像、检索时画像匹配和反思式画像更新补齐现有方法的关键缺失。在不训练大语言模型的条件下，该框架取得显著效果，尤其说明将画像信号用于检索具有重要价值。

Abstract: In LLM-based recommendation, memory acts as a user-level agent in a memory-augmented backbone. Yet users and items remain asymmetric: only the user is given a structured profile; it enters a bandwidth-limited reranker prompt; and it is frozen after warmup. We recast LLM-based recommendation as an agentic-communication process in four stages: BUILD a self-description, TRANSMIT it along the collaborative graph, UNDERSTAND the other side, and SYNC it from the next interaction. This formulation reveals three unresolved communication challenges: the item side never produces a comparable self-description (one-sided BUILD); profile-level signals enter only the bandwidth-limited reranker prompt rather than retrieval, where they would not compete for context tokens (prompt-trapped UNDERSTAND); and the user's description is frozen after warmup with no principled update rule (absent SYNC). We instantiate the missing stages as HARP (Homophily-aware Agentic Recommendation via dual Profiles), a training-free, three-operator addition to a frozen LLM backbone: a rule-extracted symmetric item profile (zero extra LLM calls), a channel-decomposable profile-level homophily H(phi_u, phi_i) consumed at retrieval time rather than inside the reranker prompt, and a single-pass reflective update realising in-context profile evolution without any gradient step. On four public benchmarks (Yelp, Books, MovieTV, Goodreads), HARP lifts Hit@1 over the strongest published baseline by 6.9%-24.0%, with family-wise Holm-corrected p < 1e-9 on every dataset. Ablations and a per-case attribution study show that the operators contribute complementary gains, with the retrieval-side gate as the main source; we also report the trade-off introduced by the reflective pass. We formulate agentic communication and provide a training-free instantiation. Code is available at https://github.com/Jove-Zhen/HARP.

</details>

### [76] [Latent Cluster Separation as a Regularizer for Lifelong Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03652)
*Sabarish Radhakrishnan,Nathalie Japkowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文拆分研究持续异常检测中的潜在空间聚类正则化与基于距离的异常评分，发现聚类正则化本身可提升检测效果和对早期任务的保持能力，而基于距离的评分是方法不稳定性的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续异常检测系统需要在学习新任务时保留识别旧异常类型的能力。以往方法常同时采用可学习概念中心来组织潜在表示，并依据样本到中心的距离评估异常，因此难以判断性能提升究竟来自哪一项设计，也不清楚相关不稳定性由何而来。

Method: 作者在基于变分自编码器的持续异常检测框架中，将潜在空间聚类分离与基于距离的异常评分分别考察：一方面仅把聚类分离作为训练阶段的正则项，同时保持原有异常评分方式；另一方面单独分析切换到基于距离的评分所产生的影响及其机制。

Result: 实验显示，仅使用聚类分离正则化就可能提升异常检测质量和对先前任务的迁移保持效果，但其表现是否优于替代评分方式，取决于数据集本身的表示结构。研究还发现，此类方法常见的不稳定性主要源于基于距离的异常评分，并解释了其内在机制。

Conclusion: 聚类正则化与基于距离的异常评分并非不可分割的组合，应根据数据集的表示特性分别选择。聚类正则化可在不改变评分方式的情况下带来收益；而采用距离评分时需关注其可能引发的不稳定性。

Abstract: Lifelong (continual) anomaly detection systems must learn a sequence of tasks without forgetting how to recognize anomaly types seen earlier -- a property known as backward transfer. A common way to try to improve backward transfer is to reshape a model's internal representation around learnable concept centroids, typically coupled with a new way of scoring anomalies based on distance to those centroids. Because these two design choices are usually introduced together, it is unclear which one is actually responsible for any performance gain. In this paper, we unbundle the two mechanisms within a VAE-based lifelong anomaly detector and study them separately. We find that used purely as a training-time regularizer -- while leaving anomaly scoring unchanged -- latent cluster separation can improve detection quality and backward transfer, though whether it outperforms the alternative scoring approach depends on the dataset's underlying representation. Switching to distance-based scoring is instead where most of the instability commonly associated with this class of method actually originates; we identify and explain the underlying mechanism. This benefit depends on the dataset's underlying representation, and we report this as practical guidance for choosing between the two design choices, rather than as a list of caveats.

</details>


### [77] [Four Extensions of the Universal Law of Robustness: Log-Concave Covariates, Heterogeneous Mixtures, Vector-Valued Predictors, and Markovian Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03653)
*Tobias J. Peter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将拟合噪声样本所需的鲁棒性下界推广到多种更一般的数据分布、输出形式及样本依赖结构，并给出统一定理与显式常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的普适鲁棒性定律表明：在满足等周性质的协变量分布下，使用含有 p 个实参数的模型拟合 n 个低于噪声水平的样本，会迫使模型的利普希茨常数至少达到约 √(nd/p) 的量级。这揭示了平滑插值需要约 d 倍过参数化，但原有结论依赖高斯型集中、齐次分布、标量输出和独立样本。本文旨在放宽这些限制，厘清定律仍成立的条件及其代价。

Method: 作者建立一个涵盖多种情形的统一主定理，并分别处理：仅有指数型集中时所需的容量预热条件；由不同分布组成的异质混合及其典型组成条件；向量值预测在弗罗贝尼乌斯利普希茨尺度和欧几里得尺度下的界；以及平稳马尔可夫协变量。证明中显式追踪各项常数，并分析容量预热条件的必要性。

Result: 指数型集中已足以在相同速率和绝对常数下得到下界，但需满足容量预热条件；此条件的某种形式也是必要的。由此可将结论用于归一化对数凹协变量，其界因当前克拉姆斯—卡尼亚斯—利伯曼—西蒙斯猜想相关估计而多出对数维数损失。异质混合情形中，最坏分布分量的常数可由各分量常数的加权平均取代。向量输出的弗罗贝尼乌斯尺度界不依赖输出维数 K；欧几里得尺度仅损失 K 的负二分之一次方，而与协变量无关的噪声可消除该损失。平稳马尔可夫协变量保留独立同分布情形的速率，伪谱隙只影响样本量条件。

Conclusion: 拟合噪声样本所导致的鲁棒性下界并不局限于原先的狭窄设定：在适当集中、容量、混合典型性或样本量条件下，它可扩展至异质分布、向量输出和相关样本。统一定理同时刻画了这些推广的代价，并提供了明确的常数。

Abstract: Bubeck and Sellke's universal law of robustness says that fitting $n$ noisy samples below the noise level with a class of $p$ real parameters forces a Lipschitz constant of order $\sqrt{nd/p}$, when the covariate distribution on $\mathbb{R}^d$ is isoperimetric: smooth interpolation costs a factor $d$ of overparametrization. Its hypotheses are Gaussian-type concentration, a homogeneous covariate law, scalar outputs, and independent samples; we relax each one. Exponential concentration suffices, at the unchanged rate and absolute constant and at the price of a capacity burn-in, some form of which we show is necessary; this gives the law for normalized log-concave covariates, with a $\log d$ loss from the current KLS bound. For heterogeneous mixtures the worst component constant is replaced by the weighted average of the component constants, under a typicality condition on the mixture composition. For vector-valued predictors the bound at the Frobenius Lipschitz scale is free of the output dimension $K$, and the Euclidean bound loses only $K^{-1/2}$, a loss that covariate-independent noise removes. Stationary Markov covariates retain the i.i.d. rate, the pseudo-spectral gap entering only the sample-size conditions. One master theorem carries the shared argument, and constants are explicit throughout.

</details>


### [78] [Towards Automatic Heart Murmur Characterization from Multi-spot Phonocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03654)
*Marcelo Nogueira,Jorge H. Oliveira,Carlos G. Ferreira,Miguel T. Coimbra,Alípio M. Jorge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用四个听诊部位的心音，自动预测心杂音的时相、形态、音调、音质和等级。结果显示，多部位预测并进行后期融合表现最佳；联合输入或共享表示的模型也可完成预测，但准确表现较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动心音分析多停留在有无心杂音的二分类，未能提供临床听诊中常用的细致描述。研究旨在拓展自动分析能力，为筛查提供结构化的杂音表征。

Method: 研究使用包含735名患者、且具有主动脉瓣区、肺动脉瓣区、二尖瓣区和三尖瓣区完整录音的数据，设置五项患者级分类任务，分别预测杂音的时相、形态、音调、音质和等级。比较三种方案：各听诊部位独立分类后按最大严重程度融合；将各部位代表片段同步输入卷积神经网络，逐项预测；以及使用共享的多部位表示，同时预测全部五项属性。另评估通过加入少数类的全部片段来平衡训练数据的影响。

Result: 后期融合方案在五项任务上的非加权平均敏感度为85.56%至93.81%，相关类别的召回率均为80%至100%。多输入模型比后期融合低约7至16个百分点；多输出模型的表现为72.59%至79.40%，但可由一个共享表示同时估计五项属性。简单加入全部少数类片段反而降低患者级表现，可能因为其中包含不具代表性且噪声较大的片段，而评估时采用的聚类选段流程会筛除这类片段。

Conclusion: 基于多个听诊部位的心杂音细致表征具有可行性，既可通过部位级预测与融合实现，也可由联合学习模型完成。当前结果支持将自动系统从二元筛查推进到结构化报告；同时，训练数据的平衡方式需考虑片段代表性与患者级评估的一致性。

Abstract: Cardiac auscultation traditionally detects the presence of a heart murmur, but clinicians further characterize it by timing, shape, pitch, quality, and grade. Most automatic phonocardiogram analysis systems stop at binary detection and do not estimate this richer description. In this work, we formulate automatic murmur characterization as five patient-level classification tasks and compare three multi-spot strategies on recordings from the aortic, pulmonary, mitral, and tricuspid auscultation locations. First, classical and deep-learning classifiers are trained independently at each location, and predictions are combined via a maximum-severity late-fusion rule. Second, a synchronous multi-input CNN jointly processes one representative segment per location for a single task. Third, a unified multi-input, multi-output CNN predicts all five attributes from one shared multi-spot representation. Using 735 patients from the CirCor DigiScope dataset with complete four-spot recordings, the best late-fusion configurations reach an unweighted mean sensitivity between 85.56% and 93.81% across the five tasks, with class-specific recalls between 80.00% and 100.00% for every relevant category. The multi-input CNN trails late fusion by 7-16 points depending on the task, while the multi-output model reaches values between 72.59% and 79.40%, estimating all five attributes from one shared representation. We further show that naively balancing training data by adding all minority-class segments degrades patient-level performance under the maximum-severity rule, since it introduces non-representative, noisy segments not filtered by the clustering-based segment selection used for evaluation. These results indicate multi-spot murmur characterization is feasible with both fusion-based and jointly learned architectures, outlining a path toward automatic, structured murmur reports complementing binary screening.

</details>


### [79] [Mixture of Wide Experts: Sparse Routing for Interpretable Native Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03655)
*Tavish Mankash,Vardhaman Kalloli,Keshava Prasad,Deepan Muthirayan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 宽专家混合层让模型直接在固定、互不重叠的输出坐标上形成较易解释的特征；其预测表现优于稠密模型，坐标可读性也更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常把一个概念分散编码在许多内部维度中，因此难以直接解释。稀疏自编码器虽能在训练后提取较清晰的特征，但研究者希望模型能从一开始就在原生表示中形成可识别、可复用的坐标。

Method: 提出宽专家混合层：被选中的专家读取完整表示，但只能写入预先分配且彼此不重叠的输出坐标。研究在已知隐藏成因的受控任务中检验该设计，并以近似匹配的计算量训练三种架构；此外，通过语言模型评审坐标描述在新文本上的适用性，衡量原生坐标的可读性与特异性，并开展坐标干预案例分析。

Result: 在受控任务中，该设计较稠密模型和标准专家混合模型更清楚地定位隐藏成因。经过一百亿个词元训练后，宽专家混合模型的评测交叉熵为二点四一七，优于稠密模型的二点四五二，但不及标准专家混合模型的二点二二。原生坐标特异性得分分别为：宽专家混合模型零点五二三、稠密模型零点三七五、标准专家混合模型零点二六八。稀疏自编码器的得分为稠密模型零点四八九、宽专家混合模型零点五五六，说明宽专家混合模型的原生坐标可读性接近稠密模型经稀疏自编码器处理后的水平。案例还展示了复制特定角度相关坐标可改变下一词预测，以及危险请求检测坐标与拒绝引导坐标的功能。

Conclusion: 宽专家混合层将稀疏条件计算与固定输出坐标结合，使部分特征能在模型原生表示中被识别和直接干预。结果表明，这种可解释性提升并未牺牲相对稠密模型的预测表现；不过，标准专家混合模型在所报告的交叉熵指标上仍更优。

Abstract: Language models often spread a concept across many internal dimensions, making their features difficult to interpret. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) recover clearer features after training. We ask whether models can instead learn interpretable output coordinates directly. We introduce Mixture of Wide Experts (MoWE), a sparse feed-forward layer in which selected experts read the full representation but write to fixed, disjoint output coordinates. On a controlled task with known hidden causes, this design preserves predictive performance while localizing causes more clearly than dense and standard mixture-of-experts (MoE) models.
  At approximately matched compute, we train two 19-layer runs of each architecture for 10 billion tokens. MoWE achieves evaluation cross-entropy 2.417 versus 2.452 for dense, while standard MoE reaches 2.22.
  We measure native-coordinate readability by asking a language-model judge to describe each coordinate from passages where it is strongly active and test that description on new passages. Correcting for descriptions that also fit other coordinates gives a specificity score of 0.523 for MoWE, 0.375 for dense, and 0.268 for standard MoE. TopK SAEs trained on the same FFN updates score 0.489 for dense and 0.556 for MoWE, making native MoWE comparable to the dense SAE.
  In the first case study, we identify a four-coordinate causal path supporting the model's prediction of degrees after the prompt A right angle is 90. We then copy seven angle-related coordinate values into the model as it processes A rectangle has four, changing its next-token prediction from sides to degrees. In the second, one fixed coordinate set detects hazardous requests and another steers the next token toward refusal. These examples show that MoWE combines sparse conditional computation with coordinates that can be identified and reused directly.

</details>


### [80] [Stress-Testing the Robustness of LLM-Based Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03656)
*Ankitkumar Patel,Jigarkumar Patel,Amit Kumar,Supreetha Sreeram,Venkatesh Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统检验大型语言模型进行因果发现的能力，发现其普遍倾向于高估因果关系；考虑推断不确定性后，模型表现会明显下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现对科学研究至关重要，但大型语言模型可能依赖表面模式，而非真正的因果推理。现有研究尚未清楚揭示其失误发生的情境、原因及程度，因此需要全面评估其能力边界。

Method: 研究提出系统性压力测试框架，从模型规模、因果图复杂度、提示策略、元数据质量、数据质量和不确定性敏感性六个维度评估模型的因果发现表现，并考察传统评价指标在处理推断不确定性时的可靠性。

Result: 模型普遍表现出“乐观偏差”，倾向于判断存在过多因果联系，导致精确率偏低。推理能力较强的模型总体表现更好，但忽略不确定性时往往得到更高分；将不确定性纳入评价后，表现明显下降。扩大模型规模虽能改善结果，但元数据质量降低会使性能大幅退化。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型可作为因果发现流程的辅助工具，但其因果判断可靠性有限，且对元数据质量和不确定性处理较为敏感。研究者应审慎解读其输出，并采用能够反映不确定性的评价方法，以识别模型可能造成误导的情形。

Abstract: Why do some of the world's most powerful Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems still struggle to reason about cause and effect? Causal discovery, the ability to discern the underlying drivers of a system, is fundamental to scientific inquiry and has traditionally relied on structured data and statistical methods. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been explored as an alternative paradigm for causal inference, leveraging their broad knowledge and reasoning capabilities. Yet, despite their linguistic fluency, LLMs often rely on surface-level pattern recognition rather than genuine causal logic. While it is known that LLMs stumble in this domain, we lack a precise map of where, why, and how significantly these failures occur. In this paper, we propose a systematic stress-test framework across six critical dimensions: model scale, causal graph complexity, prompting strategy, metadata quality, data quality, and sensitivity to uncertainty. Our findings reveal a significant "optimism bias" in LLMs; models consistently overestimate causal links, leading to low precision across the board. Furthermore, we demonstrate that traditional evaluation metrics can be deceptive: while reasoning-heavy models lead in performance, the highest scores often occur when uncertainty in inference is overlooked. Once uncertainty is integrated into the evaluation, performance drops, providing a more honest reflection of causal reliability. We also find that, while scaling model size improves outcomes, these gains are fragile; performance degrades sharply as the quality of metadata decreases. This work serves as a practical guide to the blind spots of LLMs, offering a clear-eyed assessment of when these tools can be integrated into a causal discovery pipeline and when they are likely to lead researchers astray.

</details>


### [81] [Learning Directional Randers Geometry for Latent-Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03675)
*Théau Blanchard,Stéphanie Allassonnière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种非对称的芬斯勒—兰德斯度量学习框架，使潜在空间中的距离和轨迹能够体现方向性与单调变化约束，从而改善插值和外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络能从图像中提取有效的潜在表示，但潜在空间缺乏明确的几何结构，可能导致插值不准确、外推产生误导。现有插值方法通常假设转移代价对称，难以描述疾病进展等具有方向性、某些转变不可逆的过程。即使黎曼度量能够纳入曲率，距离仍然对称，因此仅考虑曲率不足以解决问题。

Method: 提出芬斯勒—兰德斯度量学习框架，在潜在空间中引入依赖方向的距离，使从一个状态到另一个状态的代价可以不同于反向转移。基于这一度量计算轨迹，以适配单调的序列约束，并用于潜在表示的插值与外推。

Result: 作者在受控的横断面数据集上展示了该方法用于插值和外推的潜力。摘要未提供具体数据集、量化指标或与基线方法的比较结果。

Conclusion: 对于存在方向性和不可逆变化的真实过程，显式建模距离的非对称性比单独引入曲率更能应对插值与外推问题。该框架为潜在空间中的方向性轨迹建模提供了方法，但其在真实纵向数据及实际应用中的效果仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Deep neural networks extract powerful latent representations from images, enabling fast and efficient downstream analysis. However, these latent spaces lack explicit geometric structure, leading to possibly poor interpolation fidelity and misleading extrapolation. Latent interpolation techniques assume symmetric transition costs which are often not the case for real world data. Their symmetric nature cannot capture directional processes, such as disease progression, where certain transitions are impossible (e.g. tumor growth dynamics or cognitive improvement in Alzheimer's disease). Although Riemannian metrics extend Euclidean geometry by incorporating curvature, they remain symmetric. We show that understanding asymmetry and not curvature alone directly addresses these problems. To do so we propose a new Finsler-Randers metric learning framework. It incorporates direction-dependent distances, thus enabling the computation of trajectories respecting monotonic sequential constraints. We demonstrate its potential for interpolation and extrapolation on controlled cross-sectional datasets.

</details>


### [82] [Does the Trust Boundary Hold over Time? A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Receiver-Directed Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03724)
*Ting Xu,Henry Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究联邦学习中接收方如何判断外部模型迁移是否有益，并指出判断基准会造成不同取舍：相对初始模型评估较容易识别有用迁移，却可能接纳损害当前模型的迁移；相对当前模型评估更安全，却会漏掉部分有用迁移。独立留出数据上的事后审计可筛除有害最终模型，并保留大部分收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接收方主导的联邦学习中，各参与站点需要自行决定是否接纳、拒绝或暂缓判断其他来源的模型组件。关键问题是这种信任边界能否在长期、多轮训练中持续可靠，尤其是评估基准不同是否会改变判断结果，以及能否在不改动训练阶段裁决的情况下识别后来失效的接纳决定。

Method: 作者构建包含十三个半合成站点的基准，提供每次迁移的精确效果判定，并采用配对随机种子、攻击协议和十四种方法进行六百轮评估；另在采用低秩适配的千问三一点七十亿参数模型站点上评估五百轮。研究比较以站点初始模型和当前模型为参照的门控机制，并提出两个命题解释其不同失效模式。此外，作者使用独立留出审计数据，在训练结束后决定部署最终模型或退回本地训练，同时报告已经失效的接纳；该审计保证不依赖此前训练历史。

Result: 在二十组配对的中央处理器实验种子中，以初始模型为参照的门控更善于识别相对初始模型有益的迁移，但有百分之二十九的迁移会损害站点当前模型，却仍被接纳。以当前模型为参照的门控，在该实验层级中仅接纳了百分之二对当前模型有害的迁移，但会漏掉许多相对初始模型有益的迁移。六百轮后，审计部署了百分之六十五至七十二的模型，未部署任何在审计目标上收益为负的模型，并保留百分之九十八的收益；实验中没有错误判断失效迁移收益方向的报告。使用两万条审计观测时，方差自适应界限检出了百分之九十九的、至少造成零点零一对数损失的失效迁移。针对共同目标的千问三比较也显示，类似取舍会持续至一百轮；在百分之三十八站点为对抗者的条件下，所评估方法中仅有两种在每个诚实站点组都取得正平均收益。

Conclusion: 长期联邦迁移中的门控表现取决于其评估对象：相对初始模型的收益与相对当前模型的安全性并非同一目标，不能由单一基准同时保证。将训练时门控与独立数据上的事后审计结合，可在保留多数收益的同时降低部署有害模型的风险，并识别已过时的接纳决定。

Abstract: In receiver-directed federated learning, each site draws a trust boundary. For every source and model component it judges, on its own data, whether the transfer helps (admit), harms (reject) or cannot yet be told (abstain). We ask whether this boundary holds over hundreds of rounds. Our benchmark has a federation of thirteen semi-synthetic sites, an exact oracle for every evaluated transfer, paired seeds, an attack protocol, fourteen methods over 600 rounds and Qwen3-1.7B LoRA sites over 500. The answer depends on which boundary is measured. Across 20 paired CPU seeds, a gate judging against a fixed reference better identified transfers useful relative to the site's starting model. Yet it admitted 29% of transfers harmful to the model the site now has. A gate judging against the current model admitted 2% of harmful transfers on that target in the CPU tier, but missed many transfers useful to the starting model. Two short propositions explain these distinct failures. We also evaluate a held out audit that leaves gate verdicts intact. After training, it decides whether each final model is deployed or the site falls back to local training, and reports admissions that have gone stale. With independent audit data its guarantees hold after any training history. At 600 rounds the audit deployed 65 to 72% of models, none with negative benefit on its target, and kept 98% of the gain. No stale report had the wrong sign in our runs. With 20,000 audit observations, a variance-adaptive bound found 99% of stale transfers that cost at least 0.01 in log loss. A common-target Qwen3 comparison on 60 selected transfers shows the same tension over 100 rounds. With 38% adversarial sites, FedFomo and CS-Gate were the only evaluated methods with positive mean gain in every honest group.

</details>


### [83] [Auditing a Model-Hub Benchmark: Data Quality, Training Budgets, and Side Information in HuggingBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03727)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文审计了一个用于推荐模型资源的基准，发现数据文件虽通过常规格式检查，资源与知识图谱实体却存在身份对应错误；修正映射和延长训练预算均能提升结果，而未被原研究使用的资源文本尤其有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型平台基准通常整合交互数据、知识图谱和资源文档，但各文件单独有效，并不代表它们描述的是同一对象。若资源与图谱实体的标识不一致，依赖该对应关系的推荐方法就会基于错误信息训练和评估。因此，论文将基准视为数据产品，检查其数据身份、来源关联及实验协议是否可靠。

Method: 作者对一个模型资源推荐基准进行系统审计：一是验证发布文件的数据格式、键值和取值范围；二是复现其报告的十一种推荐方法；三是使用三个训练随机种子开展受控实验，修正资源与知识图谱实体的对应关系，并比较不同图谱配置；四是检查固定训练轮数与早停设置的影响。此外，作者测试了未被原研究方法使用的资源文本，并检查其他推荐数据集发布版本中的标识兼容性。

Result: 四个外部知识图谱中有三个虽然通过键值和范围检查，却违反了图谱推荐所依赖的资源—实体身份约束。修正对应关系后，所有基于知识图谱的方法在每个随机种子下均有提升，十项召回率最高提升百分之七十三，并使二十七项图谱配置比较中的十项结论反转。训练预算也不足：二十九种经验证集选择的设置中，有七至八种在一百轮结束时尚未触发早停；在二十三个相关的设置—种子组合中，二十一个在延长训练后表现更好。使用资源文本和资源图谱的闭式线性模型，十项召回率超过原论文最佳报告结果百分之十八，且可在中央处理器上数分钟内完成拟合。对其他推荐数据集的检查也发现，同一数据集的不同发布版本可能使用互不兼容的标识。

Conclusion: 基准的可信度不仅取决于文件是否格式正确，还取决于跨数据源的对象身份和来源信息是否一致，以及实验预算是否充分。论文发布了修正文件、标识映射、验证工具和实验日志，并建议基准与模型资源库明确维护身份对应关系和数据来源，以提升推荐研究的可复现性与评估可靠性。

Abstract: Benchmarks built from model hubs combine interaction data, knowledge graphs (KGs) and resource documentation, but valid files do not ensure that these artifacts describe the same objects. Treating the release as a data product, we audit HuggingBench (SIGIR 2025), a benchmark for recommending Hugging Face resources, through data validation, a replication of its eleven recommendation methods and controlled experiments with three training seeds. In three of its four external KGs, the files pass key and range checks but violate the item-entity identity constraint on which KG recommenders rely. Correcting this join improves every KG-based method in every seed, by up to 73% in mean Recall@10, and consistently reverses ten of 27 comparisons between KG configurations. The protocol matters as well: the 100-epoch budget ends before early stopping in seven to eight of 29 validation-selected settings, and 21 of 23 such setting-seed pairs improve with a longer budget. The released item text, which no reported method uses, is the strongest signal: closed-form linear models with text and the item graph exceed the best reported Recall@10 by 18% while fitting in minutes on a CPU. Checks of other recommendation releases show that individually valid releases of one dataset can use incompatible identifiers. We release corrected files, mappings, validators and logs, and derive practices for preserving identity and provenance across the artifacts of benchmarks and model lakes.

</details>


### [84] [Minimal Superior Penalties: Characterising and Calibrating Decision-Aware Proper Scoring Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03734)
*Rouhollah Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文推导了布里尔分数和对数分数达到“正确预测始终优于错误预测”所需的最小惩罚，并指出经验结果不足以证明这一性质能提升模型选择表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练过程中，验证指标会决定保留哪个模型以及何时停止训练。理想的指标应满足优越性：任何正确预测的得分都优于任何错误预测，即使两者的预测概率非常接近。布里尔分数和对数分数单独使用都不满足这一要求；已有惩罚方案则根据正确预测得分的上界校准，可能施加过大的惩罚。

Method: 作者分别求出两种分数在正确预测和错误预测上的两个边界：正确预测得分的上确界与错误预测得分的下确界。两者之差构成无穷下确界惩罚，即恢复优越性所需惩罚的最大下界。作者还将已有惩罚和该下界纳入单参数惩罚族，并证明在采用单值化的平局处理规则时，族中每个成员仍严格适当。最后，在预先设定的两个数据集案例研究中考察惩罚与模型选择表现的关系。

Result: 对于布里尔分数，所推导的无穷下确界惩罚低于已有方案：类别数为三时，已有惩罚约为其四倍；类别数为二十时，约为其二点一一倍。案例研究中，十分类的若干实验设置显示，惩罚增大时所选模型的宏平均Ｆ一分数均值上升；但考虑随机种子与选择模式的嵌套后，这一趋势无法与噪声明确区分。另一个四十七分类设置甚至未呈现这一描述性趋势。此外，实验中的“无优越性”基线在代数上只是所研究惩罚族的重新缩放，因此无法将观察到的变化归因于优越性本身。

Conclusion: 若目标是保证优越性，惩罚只需严格大于推导出的无穷下确界值；更大的惩罚并非保证所必需，而是额外的校准取舍。论文给出了较紧的理论惩罚界限，但现有案例研究不能证明优越性会带来更好的模型选择表现。

Abstract: Training a classifier means letting a validation metric decide which model is kept and when training stops. A natural requirement on that metric is superiority: every correctly classified forecast should score better than every incorrectly classified one, however close the underlying probabilities are. Neither the Brier score nor the logarithmic score satisfies this on its own. We show that superiority is governed by the separation between two endpoints -- the supremum of the score on correct predictions and the infimum on incorrect predictions -- and derive both in closed form for each score. Their difference is the infimal penalty, the greatest lower bound on the penalty needed to restore superiority. Prior work restores superiority with a penalty calibrated from the first endpoint alone; for the Brier base, our infimal penalty is smaller than that published penalty by a factor ranging from 4.00 at three classes to 2.11 at twenty. We further embed the published and infimal penalties in a one-parameter family and show that, under a single-valued tie convention, every member of the family is strictly proper. In a pre-specified two-dataset case study, mean selected-model macro-F1 rises with the penalty in the reported ten-class regimes, though not distinguishably from noise once seed/selection-mode nesting is accounted for; a fifth regime at forty-seven classes does not reproduce even this descriptive pattern. The intended no-superiority baseline is algebraically a rescaling of the family under test, so the experiment cannot attribute the observed pattern specifically to superiority. When superiority itself is the objective, any penalty strictly above the infimal value suffices; choosing a larger penalty is a separate calibration trade-off rather than a requirement for the guarantee.

</details>


### [85] [SSPE: Robust Sequential Staypoint Extraction in Degraded Trajectory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03737)
*Yueyang Liu,Lance Kennedy,Andreas Züfle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种顺序式停留点提取算法，并配套无监督参数估计框架，以提升噪声和长时间信号缺失条件下的提取准确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从原始轨迹中提取有语义意义的停留点，是移动性分析的重要基础。但现实轨迹常含严重空间噪声和长时间信号缺失，传统滑动窗口方法对这些异常敏感，容易把连续停留片段错误拆分；同时，不同数据集适用的参数差异较大，依赖人工标注调参也存在局限。

Method: 提出顺序式停留点提取算法，采用由状态驱动的处理架构，以动态质心替代固定空间锚点，并利用向前查看多个步骤的启发式方法容忍短暂坐标漂移；通过时间桥接机制，减少长时间信号缺失造成的片段化。此外，提出基于共识的无监督参数估计框架，从原始轨迹中推导基准统计尺度，并通过最大化结构一致性选择参数，无需依赖标注真值。

Result: 在包含人工核验停留点真值的真实移动数据集上，与常用滑动窗口基线相比，该算法取得更高的准确性和F1值，同时计算效率也有所提升。

Conclusion: 该方法针对空间漂移与长时间数据缺失造成的停留点提取问题，结合动态状态处理、时间桥接和无监督参数选择，展现出优于标准基线的效果。摘要所述实验支持其在真实轨迹数据中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Extracting semantically meaningful staypoints from raw trajectory streams is a foundational prerequisite for mobility analytics. However, real-world trajectory data is frequently degraded by severe spatial noise and protracted temporal dropouts. Classic threshold-based extraction methods, such as the widely adopted sliding-window algorithm, are highly sensitive to these anomalies, routinely fracturing continuous stationary episodes into disjointed fragments. To overcome this limitation, we propose the Sequential Staypoint Extraction (SSPE) algorithm. SSPE employs a resilient, state-driven architecture that replaces static spatial anchors with a dynamic centroid and introduces an $n$-step lookahead heuristic to structurally tolerate transient coordinate drift. Concurrently, it utilizes temporal bridging mechanisms to prevent artificial fragmentation during prolonged signal loss. Since optimal extraction parameters vary significantly across datasets, we further introduce a fully unsupervised, consensus-based parameter estimation framework. This framework derives baseline statistical scales directly from the raw trajectory data and identifies the optimal parameter configuration by maximizing structural agreement, thereby eliminating the reliance on annotated ground truth. We evaluate SSPE on the ExtraSensory dataset, a real-world mobility corpus with manually verified ground-truth staypoints, against the sliding-window implementation of Trackintel, one of the most widely adopted extraction baselines in the field. Empirical results demonstrate that SSPE achieves superior accuracy, yielding higher F1-scores while simultaneously improving computational efficiency over this standard.

</details>


### [86] [RATSIFT: Resolution-Adaptive Transformer for Solar Irradiance Forecasting and Temporal Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03738)
*Jesto Peter,Timothy M. Hansen,Hossein Moradi Rekabdarkolaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合遗传算法调参的变换器模型，可利用不同时间分辨率的太阳辐照度数据，同时进行预测与时间分辨率下采样，并给出不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳辐照度的准确预测与时间分辨率转换对可再生能源并网和电网管理至关重要。传统方法难以捕捉辐照度的快速波动，因此需要能够融合不同时间尺度信息、适应多种输入与输出分辨率的预测方法。

Method: 构建变换器架构，同时利用全局与局部上下文信息，支持任意分辨率输入，并生成训练数据中涵盖的不同分辨率输出。为提高训练效率，采用遗传算法搜索合适的模型超参数，以减少穷举网格搜索带来的计算开销；模型还提供预测不确定性估计。

Result: 基于美国得克萨斯州达拉斯的真实数据进行评估，模型以五分钟分辨率生成预测时，输入为十五分钟、三十分钟、六十分钟及不规则间隔数据时，均方根误差分别为二十八点七八五、二十九点五四四、三十点一五七和三十二点一三二瓦每平方米。模型的不确定性估计能够反映输入分辨率与太阳辐照度波动的差异：高分辨率输入在波动较强时对应较宽的预测区间；较粗分辨率输入则具有较窄区间，同时训练时间和计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 该方法为多分辨率太阳辐照度预测与时间分辨率下采样提供了可扩展且高效的方案，并兼顾预测不确定性评估，具有服务实际能源预测和电网管理的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting and temporal downscaling of solar irradiance is critical for reliable renewable energy integration and grid management. Traditional methods often struggle to capture rapid irradiance variability. In this work, we develop a Transformer-based architecture for simultaneous forecasting and temporal downscaling. The model takes input data at arbitrary resolutions and generates outputs at any resolutions represented within the training data, leveraging both global and local contextual information.
