To improve training efficiency, a genetic algorithm-based optimization framework is implemented to identify suitable model hyperparameters, while reducing the computational cost of exhaustive grid search. The effectiveness of the proposed model is shown by analyzing real data in Dallas, Texas. The proposed model achieved root mean square error (RMSE) values of 28.785, 29.544, 30.157, 32.132~W/m\(^2\) for 15-, 30-, 60-minute, and irregularly spaced global input and producing forecast at 5-minutes resolution, respectively. Furthermore, this model produced reliable uncertainty estimates, exhibiting wider prediction intervals for high-resolution inputs during volatile solar conditions, whereas coarser-resolution inputs result in narrower intervals and significantly lower training time and computational cost. Our approach provides a scalable and efficient framework for multi-resolution solar irradiance forecasting and temporal downscaling, suitable for practical energy forecasting and grid management applications.

</details>


### [87] [CaRe-KT: Continuity-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning for Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03744)
*Mehrnoush Mohammadi,Kamal Berahmand,Mohammad Morsali,Shazia Sadiq,Wojtek Tomaszewski,Hassan Khosravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种查询自适应的检索增强知识追踪框架，通过构建与当前学习状态和预测情境相匹配的证据子图，并结合学习到的证据效用，提高知识状态估计效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪需要根据学习者的交互历史估计其不断变化的知识状态。检索增强方法虽能利用其他学习者的行为证据，但常将证据检索与后续预测分开处理，造成二者之间的对齐缺口：证据的相关性会随学习者状态和预测情境变化，而检索相关性也不必然等同于预测价值。

Method: 提出查询自适应框架，借助证据图缓存构建针对当前查询的证据子图。该方法结合题目层面的持久关系兼容性与交互层面的时间有效性，检索历史学习轨迹；再通过结果监督的对比学习，将这些轨迹与目标学习者的历史编码到共享表示空间。最后，利用注意力机制融合证据获取时的相关性和模型学习到的查询—证据兼容性，使相关性参与预测，但不直接决定证据的预测影响。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于强基线知识追踪模型，并且在学习历史稀疏和冷启动情形下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 研究支持将检索与推理之间的相关性连续性作为检索增强知识追踪的设计原则，同时强调检索相关性应为预测提供信息，而不应被视为证据预测效用的替代指标。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) estimates learners' evolving knowledge states from interaction histories to support adaptive learning. Retrieval-augmented KT improves these estimates using cross-learner behavioral evidence, but existing approaches typically separate evidence retrieval from downstream prediction. This creates a retrieval-inference alignment gap because evidence relevance varies with the learner's evolving state and prediction context. We propose CaRe-KT, a query-adaptive framework that maintains relevance continuity between evidence acquisition and inference without equating retrieval relevance with predictive utility. CaRe-KT introduces an Evidence-Graph Cache (EGC) that combines persistent question-level relational compatibility with interaction-level temporal validity to construct query-specific evidence subgraphs. Historical learning trajectories retrieved through these subgraphs are encoded alongside the target learner's history in a shared representation space using outcome-supervised contrastive learning. An attention mechanism combines acquisition relevance with learned query-evidence compatibility, allowing relevance to inform, rather than determine, each trajectory's predictive influence. Experiments across five benchmarks show that CaRe-KT outperforms strong KT baselines and remains robust under sparse-history and cold-start conditions. These findings support relevance continuity as a design principle for retrieval-augmented knowledge tracing.

</details>


### [88] [Honest Small Decision Models for Finance: Scale, Specialization Collapse, and Mixed-Corpus Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03755)
*Ajitabh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究面向金融工作流的小型决策模型：模型能快速给出带概率的判断，但在现实收益预测上未发现超越历史基准率的能力；混合训练可兼顾通用决策、规则判断与概率校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融决策不仅需要生成文本，还要求响应迅速、概率可信且过程可审计。论文旨在评估小型语言模型能否以单次前向计算完成结构化决策，并厘清其在规则明确的任务与真实结果预测任务中的能力边界，以及针对单一领域微调是否会损害通用决策能力。

Method: 研究在两个模型系列、从八亿到三百二十亿参数的九个规模上，采用统一且冻结的低秩适配微调方案。训练数据包括公开决策基准、带程序生成标准答案的政策规则语料，以及标注真实未来收益结果的金融语料；后者包含一万九千六百四十道滚动时间切分题目和一千三百六十道封存测试题。模型针对选择、评分或弃答类结构化问题，在一次前向计算中输出答案及概率。研究还比较窄领域微调与混合语料训练，并分析校准、领域温度设置及按置信区间分流复核的策略。

Result: 窄领域微调使公开通用决策能力下降九至四十五个百分点；这一数据混合效应随模型规模增大而减弱，并与窄领域梯度更新量相关。真实收益题上，所有训练规模的准确率均为零点三七至零点三八，与按预测期限划分的基准率相当；在控制条件下未发现额外预测优势。模型给出的概率较平坦且校准良好，能反映各期限的基准率；二十亿参数模型的校准误差最低，为零点零二六，而九十亿至三百二十亿参数模型在基准率漂移下升至零点零四三至零点零四四。混合语料模型以适度成本保留多项能力，在公开基准上达到或略超通用专家模型，同时维持政策规则任务的上限表现和接近收益专用模型的概率校准。

Conclusion: 小型模型适合快速处理答案由明确规则决定的金融任务，但现有训练并未使其在真实收益预测上超越条件基准率。概率平坦并非必然是缺陷：在缺少可验证预测优势时，诚实表达不确定性有助于将问题转交人工复核。将多个领域语料混合训练，有望在单一适配器中兼顾通用能力、规则判断与校准；部署时应按任务类型设置概率温度与分流机制。

Abstract: Financial workflows need fast, calibrated, auditable decisions, not just generated text. We train System-1 decision models, small language models fine-tuned to answer typed questions (choice/score/noul) with a probability in a single forward pass, across two model families (K2-Horizon and Qwen3.5/3.8) and nine rungs from 0.8B to 32B parameters, using one frozen LoRA recipe on a public decision benchmark and two new finance corpora: a programmatic-gold policy corpus (rule-reading with exact labels) and a reality-labeled outcome corpus (19,640 realized forward-return questions in walk-forward splits, plus a sealed 2026 test of 1,360 questions). Narrow fine-tuning costs general decision competence 9-45 percentage points, a data-mixture effect that shrinks with model scale and is associated with narrow-gradient volume. On reality-labeled questions, every trained rung from 0.8B to 32B lands at the horizon-conditioned prior baseline (accuracy 0.37-0.38, no detectable conditional edge under our controls); the models respond with flat, well-calibrated probabilities that track per-horizon base rates: naturally honest uncertainty, with a mid-scale calibration sweet spot (ECE 0.026 at 2B, degrading to 0.043-0.044 at 9-32B under prior drift). A mixed-corpus remediation carries all competencies in one adapter at modest cost: it matches or slightly exceeds the generalist specialist on the public benchmark while holding the policy ceiling and near-specialist outcome calibration, yielding a deployable artifact with per-domain temperature regimes and a band-dispatch escalation analysis: sharp, dispatchable bands where rules determine answers; flat, review-routing bands where reality does not.

</details>


### [89] [Language Models Can Benefit from Domain-Specific Inductive Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03756)
*Damien Teney,Liangze Jiang,Zachary Shinnick,Hemanth Saratchandran,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，针对不同数据领域调整的变换器结构可提升训练效率或泛化能力，但优势往往具有领域特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型通常采用少数通用架构处理多种预训练数据。本文探讨这类架构是否同样适合不同数据子集，以及针对特定领域调整学习机制能否改善效果。

Method: 研究者从头训练小型语言模型，分别使用网页文本、代码和数学等代表性数据子集。通过将标准激活函数替换为可学习、可针对数据集调整的样条函数，构造不同变体，并交叉组合架构与数据集，评估跨领域迁移表现。

Result: 在测试的七个数据集上，调整后的变换器变体均能提高训练效率或泛化能力，但收益通常依赖领域。例如，针对自然语言数据调整的架构在故事文本上表现较好，而针对数学调整的架构在自然语言任务上表现较差。研究还发现，较宽的损失盆地和更多可用的“彩票”子网络是性能提升的可能机制。

Conclusion: 不同预训练数据子集可能受益于不同的学习机制，单一通用架构存在局限。针对领域开发学习策略，有望提升数据效率，同时仍保留训练通用模型的能力。

Abstract: Language models are typically trained with a single choice of architecture on diverse domains and data sources. Modern transformers have been extensively refined for such general-purpose learning, but are they equally effective across different subsets of the pretraining data? We investigate this question by training small language models from scratch on representative subsets of pretraining data such as web text, code, and mathematics. We introduce variants of transformers in which standard activation functions are replaced with learned splines tuned on specific datasets, providing a simple way to modify their inductive biases. Using a mix-and-match of architectures and datasets, we evaluate cross-domain transfer, i.e. how well does an architecture tuned for one domain perform on data from another? We find that the tuned transformer variants improve training efficiency or generalization on all seven of our tested datasets, but the gains are often domain-specific. For example, architectures tuned on a natural language dataset such as FineWeb perform well on TinyStories, whereas those tuned on mathematics perform poorly on natural language. We identify several mechanisms underlying these improvements, including wider loss basins and more abundant "lottery tickets". Our results show that different subsets of pretraining data can benefit from different learning mechanisms, highlighting a limitation of the prevailing trend towards a small set of general-purpose architectures and optimizers. This points to an opportunity to improve data efficiency by developing domain-specific learning strategies that retain the ability to train general-purpose models.

</details>


### [90] [The role of injected noise in diffusion model generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03764)
*Vincent Emmerling,Felix Günther,David Kappel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，扩散模型在采样过程中注入的噪声会显著影响不同数据集模型生成样本的相似性，且影响最大时，样本的整体结构已形成、具体身份尚未确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不同数据集上训练的扩散模型，使用相同噪声输入时可能生成高度相似的样本，这暗示模型学到了具有泛化性的共同数据分布。论文旨在探究采样过程中注入的噪声如何影响这一现象，以及样本相似性对噪声变化有多敏感。

Method: 研究采样过程中注入噪声对跨数据集生成样本相似性的作用，并考察改变注入噪声时样本相似性的稳健程度；进一步分析这种影响在采样不同阶段的差异。

Result: 采样过程中注入的噪声对强泛化效应具有重要作用，可能增强或削弱不同模型生成样本的相似性。噪声变化的影响在特定采样阶段最明显：此时样本的宽泛结构已经形成，但具体样本身份尚未确定。

Conclusion: 跨数据集扩散模型生成样本的相似性并非只由训练数据或初始噪声决定，采样期间注入的噪声同样关键。尤其在从整体结构过渡到确定具体身份的阶段，噪声扰动会显著影响生成结果的相似性。

Abstract: Diffusion models trained on distinct datasets can produce highly similar samples when prompted with the same noise input, suggesting that they learn a generalized data distribution. We investigate the role of noise injected during the sampling process, and show that it plays an important role in making or breaking this strong generalization effect. Based on this finding we investigate the robustness of sample similarity to changes in the injected noise, and find that its impact is largest in the sampling regime where broad structure already exists but the precise identity of the sample is not yet determined.

</details>


### [91] [Quantifying out-of-distribution data severity with Wasserstein distances: benchmarking large language models for alloy property prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03767)
*YuJie Liu,Dezhen Xue,Jun Sun,Xiangdong Ding,Zhibin Gao,Turab Lookman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，通用大语言模型在多类材料性能预测中可优于专用材料语言模型；结合先验模型估值与高相似度数据，能进一步提升预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通用大语言模型推理能力增强，研究者希望明确其材料性能预测能力是否已能媲美或超越材料领域专用预训练模型，并探索如何以较低成本提升预测质量。

Method: 在钛、钢、高熵合金和铝等材料任务上，比较通用大语言模型与专用材料语言模型的预测表现。随后提出混合方法：先由已有模型生成数值估计，再将估计作为提示输入通用大语言模型，使其发挥推理修正作用。同时，利用相似度指标筛选参考数据，并考察数据量、数据质量与模型自报置信度的作用。

Result: 通用大语言模型的表现优于所比较的钢铁、材料科学及科学文献领域专用模型。以先验模型估值引导后，预测准确度进一步超过先验模型本身及未引导的大语言模型。仅使用训练集中的百分之五高相似度参考数据，效果即可接近使用全部训练集；数据质量比数量更关键。模型自报高置信度也与较小的预测误差显著相关。

Conclusion: 将通用大语言模型与先验数值预测及高质量参考数据结合，可形成准确且具成本效益的材料性能预测方案，有望减少对昂贵领域专用预训练的依赖，助力材料发现。

Abstract: As general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs), such as Gemini and DeepSeek, exhibit increasingly advanced reasoning capabilities, a critical question arises: how do their property prediction abilities compare to those of domain-specific pretrained materials models? In this study, we evaluate predictions for Ti, steels, high-entropy alloy (HEA) and Al, demonstrating that general-purpose LLMs surprisingly outperform SteelBERT, MatSciBERT, and SciBERT. To further enhance performance, we introduce a hybrid approach that utilizes numerical priors from a prior model, such as CatBoost or SteelBERT, to guide the general LLMs (Deepseek V3.1, Gemini-2.5-Flash). By inputting these initial estimates from a prior model as prompts, the LLM acts as a reasoning corrector, achieving accuracy superior to the prior model and "unguided mode". Furthermore, we investigate the impact of data strategy using a similarity-based metric, revealing that data quality dominates quantity; using merely 5% of high-similarity reference data of training set as input yields performance similar to the results of using 100% of training set. Additionally, the LLMs demonstrate excellent uncertainty calibration, where self-reported "high" confidence strongly correlates with minimal deviation from ground truth. Our findings suggest that synergizing general-purpose LLMs with prior model numerical baselines and high-quality data curation creates a cost-effective and accurate paradigm for materials discovery, reducing the need for expensive domain-specific pretraining.

</details>


### [92] [Multi-layer Indexer: Overlapping Selection and Attention in DeepSeek Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03768)
*Serdarcan Dilbaz,Arjun Krishna,Kiran Kamble,Daniel M. Bikel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多层索引器利用相邻层选出的注意力令牌高度相似这一特点，让索引扫描与注意力计算并行执行；与跨层索引复用结合后，可提升长上下文解码速度，同时维持接近原方法的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力虽能减少长上下文推理的计算量，但在深度求索稀疏注意力中，每层的闪电索引器仍需扫描完整缓存并选出得分最高的二千零四十八个令牌。长上下文下，这一串行扫描反而成为解码瓶颈。由于相邻层所选令牌高度相似，论文探索复用前一层的选择，以消除索引器与本层注意力之间的依赖。

Method: 提出多层索引器：每层注意力使用前一索引器层选出的令牌集合，使当前层索引器可与本层注意力并行运行。该方法无需重新训练或增加模型权重；默认实现无需修改算子，可直接运行于生产解码流程，也提供可选的融合解码算子进一步加速。它还能与跨层索引缓存结合：缓存机制减少需要运行索引器的层，多层索引器则隐藏其余索引器的扫描耗时。

Result: 在深度求索V3.2和GLM-5上，以保留索引器层比例二分之一至三十二分之一、上下文长度一万至十九万五千令牌进行评估。相同保留比例下，组合方法相较跨层索引缓存，在二分之一保留率时最高分别快百分之十四点五和百分之九点八，在四分之一保留率时分别快百分之七点九和百分之五点一；上下文越长，收益越明显。在十二万八千令牌上下文下，四分之一保留率达到原模型一点三二倍的解码吞吐，高于跨层索引缓存在八分之一保留率下的一点二八倍。长文本与推理基准上的准确率与跨层索引缓存相差仅数个百分点。

Conclusion: 多层索引器通过利用层间选择相似性，将索引扫描从解码关键路径中移出，并能与已有的跨层索引复用互补。实验表明，它在长上下文场景下可进一步提高吞吐，且无需改动模型参数，准确率损失较小。

Abstract: Sparse attention cuts the cost of long-context inference by making the attention matrix sparse: each query attends only a subset of the sequence, chosen by a fixed pattern or, in recent production LLMs, learned per query. In DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA), the attention of DeepSeek-V3.2 and the GLM-5 family, every layer is preceded by a lightning indexer that scores the whole cache and keeps the k=2048 highest-scoring tokens for the layer to attend. At long context this indexer scan, not the sparse attention it feeds, is where the decode time goes. But adjacent layers select nearly the same tokens, so a layer can attend the selection of the previous indexer layer instead of its own. This removes the dependency: each indexer then runs concurrently with the attention of its layer. The resulting Multi-layer Indexer (MLI) needs no retraining and no new weights. Its default realization changes no kernel, running inside SGLang's production decode graph; an optional fused decode kernel accelerates it further on DeepSeek-V3.2. MLI composes with IndexCache (the cross-layer reuse GLM-5.2 ships as IndexShare): IndexCache drops the indexer from some layers, and MLI hides the scan on the rest. We serve DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5 under IndexCache's protocol at retentions 1/2 to 1/32 (the fraction of layers that keep an indexer) and contexts 10K to 195K. At equal retention the composition decodes faster than IndexCache: up to 14.5% faster on DeepSeek-V3.2 and 9.8% on GLM-5 at 1/2, and 7.9% and 5.1% at 1/4, largest where IndexCache keeps the most indexers and growing with context. At retention 1/r it is faster still than IndexCache at 1/(2r) while keeping twice the fresh selections: at 128K it reaches 1.32x the unmodified model's decode throughput at 1/4, above IndexCache's 1.28x at 1/8. Its LongBench, RULER and LongBench v2 accuracy stays within a few percentage points of IndexCache's.

</details>


### [93] [Probabilistic Steering of Generative Priors for Lab-in-the-Loop Antibody Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03844)
*Anisha Parsan,Sebastian Ober,Calvin McCarter,Nick Bhattacharya,Hunter Elliott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将抗体生成式发现与迭代优化平滑衔接，利用概率代理模型的后验不确定性决定结构先验与实验数据监督的权重；在实验样本较少时提高优化效率，并增加优化分子的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗体研发通常被划分为新型结合分子的发现和候选分子的迭代优化两个阶段，现有方法也往往各自独立。这种分阶段流程可能造成结构模型提供的先验与湿实验数据监督之间衔接不充分，尤其在实验数据有限时，优化效率和分子多样性可能受限。

Method: 提出一种统一发现与优化的流程：从生成式结构模型出发，逐步过渡到基于实验数据的迭代优化；通过概率代理模型的后验不确定性，引导结构先验与实验监督之间的衔接和权衡。

Result: 摘要报告称，该方法在实验样本量较少的条件下提升了优化效率，同时使优化得到的分子具有更高多样性。摘要未提供具体指标或对比数值。

Conclusion: 将生成式发现与迭代优化整合为连续过程，有望改善抗体候选分子的优化，尤其适用于实验数据有限的情形；后验不确定性可作为协调结构先验与实验监督的重要依据。

Abstract: Therapeutic antibody development is often framed as comprising two discrete stages: initial discovery of novel binders followed by iterative optimization to refine these hits into therapeutic-grade molecules. Models and methods often reflect these stages, with generative structure models applied to discovery and probabilistic black-box optimization methods applied to refinement. Here we propose to unify these stages by smoothly transitioning a generative discovery model into iterative optimization, allowing the handoff between the structure-based prior and supervision from wet lab data to be guided by the posterior uncertainty of a probabilistic surrogate. We show that this yields improvements in optimization efficiency in the low-N regime, as well as improved diversity amongst the optimized molecules.

</details>


### [94] [Dr. No: Feature Steering for Abstention in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03862)
*Farid Hasanov,Riccardo Renzulli,Bahram Zonooz,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Decebal Constantin Mocanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需重新训练模型的特征调控方法，通过推理时调整少量内部特征，促使基础模型在证据不足时更恰当地拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型常在视觉或文本证据不足时仍给出答案，尤其在高风险或重视信息可靠性的场景中，缺乏依据的自信回答可能比拒答更有害。论文希望识别并控制这种行为背后的内部机制。

Method: 提出“拒答调控”方法：利用稀疏自编码器发掘与拒答及作答承诺相关的特征，并在提示预填充阶段调节其中少量特征，不对基础模型进行重新训练。研究在多模态拒答基准和语言推理任务上评估该方法，并与多种基于不确定性的拒答基线比较。

Result: 与事后拒答方法相比，特征调控在正确作答与适当拒答之间取得了更好的平衡。提升在多模态问答中较明显，尤其是图像与问题不匹配、问题与答案不相容的情形；在语言推理基准的数学子任务上也有较清晰的迁移效果。对答案缺失识别及依赖检索的多跳问答，效果则较有限。

Conclusion: 稀疏内部特征可作为轻量的推理时控制手段，帮助基础模型形成更能顾及不确定性的行为。但拒答并非由单一、普适的内部信号决定，其控制效果会随证据来源和任务类型而变化。

Abstract: Large foundation models often answer even when the available visual or textual evidence is insufficient to support a reliable conclusion. This failure to abstain makes them hard to trust in high-stakes or information-seeking settings, where a confident but unsupported answer can be worse than no answer at all. It also raises a natural question: can the internal mechanisms behind this behaviour be identified and controlled at inference time? We present Dr. No, a feature-steering approach that mines sparse autoencoder (SAE) features associated with abstention and commitment, then modulates a small set of them during the prefill pass, without retraining the base model.
  Dr. No is evaluated on LLaVA-1.6 Mistral 7B across multimodal abstention benchmarks, including UNK-VQA and MM-UPD, and additionally benchmarked on language reasoning tasks using Qwen3.5-2B on AbstentionBench subsets. Against multiple uncertainty-based abstention baselines, feature steering achieves a more favourable balance between correct answering and appropriate abstention than post-hoc rejection methods. The gains are strongest on UNK-VQA and on MM-UPD cases involving image--question or question--answer incompatibility, and transfer most clearly to mathematical reasoning subsets of AbstentionBench. However, performance is more limited on absent-answer detection and retrieval-heavy multi-hop QA, such as MuSiQue. These results suggest that sparse internal features provide lightweight control handles for shaping uncertainty-aware behaviour in foundation models, while also showing that abstention control is source-dependent rather than governed by a single universal signal.

</details>


### [95] [Auditing Multi-Proxy Explanations of Web User Preferences: Identification Boundaries and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04997)
*Yu Zhang,Zhihan Wang,Yuhang Lu,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一套审计框架，用于判断行为、评分、文本等代理信号能否支持关于真实用户偏好的解释。多数代理在偏差较大的情况下仍可恢复归因方向，但真实数据证据主要说明模型拟合结果与比较基准的一致性，尚不足以证明恢复了潜在偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络系统常以行为、评分、文本或大语言模型生成的用户画像推断偏好，但解释模型可能只揭示代理变量的测量机制，而非真正关心的偏好。论文旨在明确区分何时能够精确识别、只能部分识别，或应当拒绝下结论，并避免把代理信号误当成偏好本身。

Method: 论文构建相对于明确参考分布的审计框架，区分点识别、部分识别与弃答。针对特征相关且方向任意的代理偏差，证明当未受污染代理归因占严格多数时，坐标中位数可恢复潜在归因的支持集与方向；若尺度为外部论据所支持的正值，还可恢复幅度。研究还证明该多数门槛是最优的，并刻画近似清洁、尺度不确定时的最小最大误差、相容目标集合及清洁代理归因误差的传播。实验包括已知真值基准、亚马逊时序分离研究，以及受控真实协变量扰动。

Result: 在预算设定正确时，直接归因张量审计达到百分之百覆盖率，对活跃驱动因素的符号判断准确率为百分之九十五点四零至百分之九十七点零五，且没有错误符号判断。亚马逊研究中，中位数聚合对四个目标均降低了相对于比较基准的条件符号误差；预先设定的八项目标与指标对比中，有六项经邦费罗尼校正的区间排除零。受控扰动实验在四个目标的预设核心指标上均支持中位数方法。不过，相容集合分析显示，当前代理配置很少足以支持严格的符号结论。

Conclusion: 在代理污染严格受控且清洁代理占多数时，中位数聚合能够稳健恢复归因方向；但若污染预算、尺度或基准依据不足，审计应报告部分识别或弃答，而不应断言偏好已被识别。亚马逊实验的比较基准校准较弱，因此证据仅支持模型拟合面与基准相对对齐，不能据此认定已恢复潜在用户偏好。

Abstract: Web systems infer user preferences from behavior, ratings, text, and LLM-generated profiles, yet explaining a proxy may reveal its measurement process rather than the preference of interest. We develop an audit framework distinguishing point identification, partial identification, and abstention relative to an explicit reference distribution. Under feature-dependent, arbitrarily signed proxy bias, a strict majority of uncontaminated proxy attributions lets the coordinatewise median recover latent-attribution support and direction and, with externally justified positive scales, magnitude. We prove this threshold sharp using response-valid indistinguishable worlds, characterize sharp minimax error and exact compatible-target sets under approximate cleanliness and scale uncertainty, and propagate clean-proxy attribution error. A frozen known-truth benchmark spans two response families, four proxy counts, all contamination counts, three bias strengths, and eleven methods, with a focal comparison confirmed on twenty unseen seeds. Under correct budgets, a direct attribution-tensor audit achieved 100% coverage, 95.40%-97.05% active-driver sign decisions, and no false signs. In a temporally separated Amazon study, median aggregation reduced comparator-relative conditional sign error for all four targets; six of eight prespecified target-metric contrasts had Bonferroni intervals excluding zero. A controlled real-covariate perturbation favored the median on the prespecified focal metric for all four targets. Compatible sets showed that the current proxy configuration rarely licensed strict-sign conclusions. Weak comparator calibration limits the Amazon evidence to fitted-surface alignment rather than latent-preference recovery.

</details>


### [96] [ExaTune: Exhaustive Hyperparameter Landscape Exploration at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03867)
*Kevin Dick,Katie Lonsway*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种高性能计算系统，通过穷举离散超参数网格，生成完整的超参数性能景观，并用多种景观指标分析其结构；结果显示，常见优化算法会遗漏完整景观中可见的关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规超参数优化通常只返回一个最优配置，丢弃了配置与模型性能之间的整体关系。由于过去缺少细粒度、完整枚举的数据，进化计算中的景观分析方法难以大规模应用于机器学习超参数空间，也无法系统研究优化算法忽略了哪些结构信息。

Method: 作者开发了一个开源高性能计算系统，对离散超参数网格中的每个配置进行穷举评估，并通过交叉验证汇总结果，形成可复现的基准数据集。随后使用五类指标分析景观：空间自相关、崎岖度、局部最优密度、适应度—距离相关性，以及基于方差的参数重要性。

Result: 研究发布了八张完整景观图，涵盖五类模型、六个分类基准和两个回归基准，共穷举评估二十八万五千零八十种配置。实证分析发现，贝叶斯优化、奥普图纳、超带和随机搜索在常见试验预算下，会遗漏景观中的不稳定区域、次级最优解及参数交互效应。

Conclusion: 完整的超参数景观图能够揭示单一最优配置和常规搜索过程无法呈现的性能结构，为理解模型对超参数选择的敏感性及优化算法的局限提供了新资源。作者认为，这类信息因优化算法的设计目标而无法由其自身完整提供，因此穷举景观基准可补充现有的超参数优化研究。

Abstract: Hyperparameter optimization (HPO) returns a single optimal configuration, systematically discarding the explored performance landscape that encodes how results depend on configuration choices. Fitness landscape analysis comprises a robust collection of methods from evolutionary computation offering ruggedness indices, autocorrelation functions, and fitness-distance correlation that have never been applied to machine learning hyperparameter spaces at scale, given that fine-grained ground-truth enumeration data has not existed. We present ExaTune, an open-source HPC system to produce a new artifact class: the hyperparameter landscape map. ExaTune exhaustively evaluates every point on a discretized hyperparameter grid via $K$-fold cross-validation, aggregates results into reproducible parquet benchmarks, and applies a suite of five formalized landscape metrics: spatial autocorrelation (Moran's $I$), ruggedness, local optima density, fitness-distance correlation (FDC), and variance-based parameter importance. We release a benchmark collection of 8 complete landscape maps (totalling 285,080 exhaustively evaluated configurations) spanning five model families (SVMs, random forests, XGBoost, ridge regression, and $k$-nearest neighbors) across six classification and two regression benchmarks, hosted on HuggingFace. Empirical analysis demonstrates that Bayesian optimization, Optuna, Hyperband, and Random Search at standard trial budgets systematically miss instability regions, secondary optima, and parameter interaction effects that are visible in complete landscapes; information that is structurally unavailable to any optimizer by design.

</details>


### [97] [Embedding Semantic Entropy: NLI-Free Semantic Uncertainty Estimation with Geometry-Aware Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03876)
*Akshiya Raveendran,Praveenasarma Baskarakurukkal,Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Uthayasanker Thayasivam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌入语义熵方法，以语义嵌入空间中的聚类取代基于蕴含判断的硬聚类，并通过动态选择聚类估计大语言模型回答的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能生成流畅但错误的回答，因此需要可靠的不确定性估计。现有语义熵依赖基于自然语言推理蕴含判断的语义聚类，聚类过程较硬，也不易检查；而不确定性分数的质量取决于语义分组是否合理。

Method: 嵌入语义熵在语义嵌入空间中使用中心点聚类，替代基于蕴含判断的聚类，并动态选择聚类，以同时考虑簇内紧密程度和簇间分离程度，再据此估计回答的不确定性。

Result: 该方法在问答与数学推理等任务上，使用多种七十亿参数级模型进行评估，整体取得了有竞争力的受试者工作特征曲线下面积和正确回答覆盖率相关指标。结果表明，以嵌入空间聚类替代自然语言推理蕴含聚类，仅造成有限性能下降，同时使语义分组更直观、可检查。

Conclusion: 嵌入语义熵将不确定性估计从不透明的蕴含判断转化为可检查的聚类问题。显式构造紧凑且彼此分离的语义簇，为理解和改进大语言模型的不确定性估计提供了另一种可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often generate fluent yet incorrect responses, making un- certainty estimation important for reliable deployment. Semantic Entropy (SE) estimates uncertainty by clustering responses based on semantic equivalence, but it relies on hard entailment-based clustering. We propose Embedding Semantic Entropy, which replaces entailment-based clustering with K-Medoids in semantic embedding space and uses dynamic cluster selection to account for intra-cluster compactness and inter-cluster separation. We evaluate the method on TriviaQA, SQuAD, NQ-Open, SVAMP, and BioASQ using Falcon- 7B-Instruct, LLaMA-2-7B, LLaMA-2-7B-Chat, and Gemma-7B. Results show competitive AUROC and AUARC performance, suggesting that NLI-based entailment clustering can be replaced with embedding-space clustering with limited degradation while improving the inspectability of semantic grouping. This matters because semantic uncertainty scores depend on underlying semantic groups; by making these groups explicit, compact, and separable in embedding space, Emb-SE turns uncertainty estimation from a black-box en- tailment decision into an inspectable clustering problem.

</details>


### [98] [The Information Limits of Neural Controlled Differential Equations Under Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03887)
*Rostislav Gusev,Evgenia Romanenkova,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文刻画稀疏观测下神经受控微分方程预测的不确定性，并给出可计算的预测歧义证书及采样复杂度结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经受控微分方程沿观测数据插值得到的连续路径演化隐状态。然而，稀疏观测无法确定观测时刻之间的输入路径；即使不同连续路径符合相同观测，也可能产生不同的终端状态和预测。因此，需要量化这种由路径缺失造成的歧义，并分析增加观测能在多大程度上消除它。

Method: 研究对象为速度有界的平面驱动路径。论文刻画与离散观测相容的终端状态集合，并用由可行有向面积及经传输的李括号构成的 zonotope（中心对称多面体）进行首阶近似。将该集合投影到标量预测方向，得到针对具体预测目标、可计算的歧义证书；同时分析确定性自适应采样策略下剩余不确定性的最优最坏情形半径。

Result: 对于非退化的平面受控微分方程，若仅假设速度有界，使用数量为 N 的观测区间时，最优最坏情形半径按 N 的负一次方衰减；若速度和加速度均有界，则按 N 的负二次方衰减。合成实验通过构造符合观测的连续路径补全来评估歧义证书，并考察路径正则性对结果的影响。

Conclusion: 稀疏观测下的预测歧义不仅取决于观测值，也受未观测路径的有向面积及系统动力学结构影响。论文提供了针对标量预测的定量证书，并证明更强的路径平滑性假设可显著提升采样效率：误差随观测区间数的衰减阶数由负一次方提高至负二次方。

Abstract: Neural controlled differential equations (Neural CDEs) evolve hidden states along continuous paths constructed by interpolating discrete observations. Sparse measurements leave the input between observation times undetermined, and admissible continuous paths consistent with the same data can produce different terminal states. We characterize this ambiguity for planar drivers with bounded speed. The set of compatible terminal states admits a leading-order zonotopic approximation determined by feasible signed areas and transported Lie brackets. Its projection onto a scalar prediction yields a computable, target-specific ambiguity certificate. We also study the number of measurements needed to reduce the remaining uncertainty. For nondegenerate planar CDEs, the optimal worst-case radius over deterministic adaptive sampling policies scales as $N^{-1}$ when speed is bounded and $N^{-2}$ when both speed and acceleration are bounded, where $N$ is the number of observation intervals. Synthetic experiments assess the certificate using admissible continuous completions and examine the role of path regularity.

</details>


### [99] [When Latent Dynamics Meet Data Assimilation: A Benchmark of Koopman-Type Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03916)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Wenxuan Yuan,Xiaoyuan Cheng,Pouria Behnoudfar,Chuanqi Chen,Charlotte Moser,Yuta Tarumi,Jin-Long Wu,Rossella Arcucci,Takemasa Miyoshi,Nan Chen,Marc Bocquet,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统比较多种柯普曼表示方法，发现其效果取决于系统特性、观测信息和同化算法；闭式分析更新在实验中通常更准确、稳健，但矩阵求逆仍是计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柯普曼表示通过在变换空间中以线性演化描述非线性动力学，理论上兼具线性方法的简洁性与广泛适用性。然而，这些优点未必能转化为深度潜变量数据同化中的实际性能提升。论文旨在系统检验不同柯普曼结构对预测、同化及优化性质的影响。

Method: 研究比较七种代表性方法与结构基线，覆盖五类从近似周期到强混沌、状态维度范围较广的动力系统。评估内容包括预测与同化精度、物理状态恢复、潜变量轨迹行为和动力学一致性，并考察高斯误差假设、目标函数凸性及分析解的唯一性。

Result: 结果显示，表示灵活性与优化可处理性之间存在权衡。潜变量线性演化有助于稳定短期预测，但在强混沌和高维系统中可能限制表达能力，且不一定提升同化精度。参数化线性算子虽更灵活，但与编码器、解码器联合优化仍是非凸问题。具有闭式分析更新的方法在实验中总体表现更好且更稳健，但矩阵求逆构成计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: 柯普曼结构的实际收益并非由线性表示本身保证，而取决于观测是否提供足够信息，以及所采用的同化程序。方法设计需要在表达能力、优化难度、同化稳健性与计算成本之间权衡。

Abstract: Koopman representations describe nonlinear dynamics through linear evolution in a transformed space, offering an attractive framework for their linear simplicity and generality. Yet these advantages do not necessarily translate into improved performance in deep latent data assimilation (LDA). We systematically compare seven representative methods and structural baselines across five systems spanning approximately periodic to strongly chaotic dynamics and a broad range of state dimensions. We evaluate forecast and assimilation accuracy, physical state recovery, latent-trajectory behavior, and dynamical consistency, and assess Gaussian error assumptions, objective convexity, and uniqueness of analysis solutions. Our results reveal a trade-off between representational flexibility and optimization tractability. Linear evolution of the latent state can stabilize short-term forecasts but restrict expressiveness in strongly chaotic and high-dimensional systems, without consistently improving assimilation accuracy. Parameterized linear operators offer greater flexibility, yet their joint optimization with the encoder--decoder remains nonconvex. Methods with closed-form analysis updates generally perform better and exhibit greater robustness in our experiments, although matrix inversion remains a computational bottleneck. These findings show that the practical benefits of Koopman structure depend on both sufficient observational information and the choice of assimilation procedure.

</details>


### [100] [Sequential auditing of connected forecast errors under partial observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03928)
*Yuanbo Zhou,Junjie Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何在真实结果缺失时，有选择地查询图中节点的结果，以判断是否存在一个连通群体，其结果都落在各自预测区间的同一侧。提出的方法减少了作出判断所需的平均查询数，但不一定更早发现事件，也不一定降低局部计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图预测系统常用预测区间表达相连位置的不确定性，但评估时真实结果可能只被部分观测。若查询预算有限，审计者需要决定优先检索哪些缺失结果，才能判断是否出现了连通群体共同违反预测区间同一侧的情况。

Method: 研究将审计过程建模为选择性查询：逐步获取缺失的真实结果，直到已观测标签足以确定目标事件，或查询预算耗尽。论文提出一种名为「ＭＯＭＳ」的方法，并与筛选后的随机查询及最短见证策略比较；同时使用直接连通分量遍历复现随机查询轨迹，无须枚举所有连通子集。

Result: 在三种公开图时间序列数据上，当百分之三十的标签被隐藏时，ＭＯＭＳ相较筛选后的随机查询，将作出判断所需的平均查询数降低百分之十九点六至百分之三十八点五。在某交通流数据集的局部遮蔽实验中，最短见证策略在十次查询内发现了更多阳性案例，但效果受节点顺序影响。直接遍历复现了九千六百九十六条筛选随机查询轨迹。

Conclusion: 选择更高效的查询策略可以减少确定事件所需的查询量，但查询量减少并不必然意味着更早发现阳性事件或降低局部计算成本。审计方法的效果还会受到遮蔽方式、节点顺序及计算过程的影响。

Abstract: Graph forecasting systems often rely on prediction intervals to quantify uncertainty across connected locations, but the true outcomes needed for evaluation may be only partially observed. We study how an auditor can selectively retrieve missing outcomes to determine whether there exists a connected group whose outcomes violate the same side of their respective forecast intervals. The audit stops when the observed labels determine the event or the query budget is exhausted. On three public graph time series, MOMS reduces mean queries by 19.6% to 38.5% relative to screened random acquisition when 30% of labels are hidden. Under localized masking on METR-LA, shortest witness finds more positive cases within ten queries, although the difference depends on node ordering. Direct component traversal reproduces all 9,696 screened random traces without enumerating connected subsets. The experiments show that reducing queries to a decision does not necessarily improve early event discovery or reduce local computation.

</details>


### [101] [Accelerating FastDEC Clustering Algorithm with GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03934)
*Mi Li,Eibe Frank,Bernhard Pfahringer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向图形处理器的快速优势估计聚类实现，通过对称近邻搜索、并行优势分量归约及自适应调度，显著加速聚类，同时保持原算法的聚类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速优势估计聚类通过优势关系估计密度，能够获得较高聚类质量，但精确近邻构建和优势分量处理仍有较高计算开销，限制了其处理大规模数据的效率。研究旨在利用现代图形处理器加速这两个关键环节。

Method: 提出对称扭曲近邻搜索内核，利用自近邻搜索的对称性，仅遍历距离矩阵的上三角区域，并由线程束协同完成前若干近邻的筛选；同时设计线程束并行的优势分量归约算法。结合适应内存容量的执行规划和流调度，进一步降低归约开销并避免其成为运行瓶颈。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上，新实现与原快速优势估计聚类在所有评测等价性基准上的聚类输出完全一致。相较于采用精确近邻搜索库的实现，端到端性能提升约一点七至四点九倍，且数据维度越高，优势通常越明显。相较原单线程实现，在极高维案例中最高加速约两万五千倍；论文指出，这一极端结果并不代表通常情况下的加速幅度。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持聚类结果不变的前提下，显著提升了快速优势估计聚类的执行效率，尤其适用于高维数据。其加速来自对近邻搜索和优势分量归约的针对性并行优化，但极端加速比应结合原实现近邻搜索性能退化的具体情境理解。

Abstract: Fast Dominance Estimation Clustering (FastDEC) is an efficient variant of Density Peak Clustering (DPC) that achieves high clustering quality through dominance-based density estimation, but remains constrained by the computational cost of exact k-nearest-neighbor (k-NN) construction and dominance component (DC) processing. We present SymWarp-FastDEC, a GPU-accelerated implementation of FastDEC. At its core is SymTrikNN, an exact self-k-NN kernel that exploits search symmetry using upper-triangular traversal and warp-cooperative top-k selection. We also introduce WP-DC, a warp-parallel DC reduction algorithm designed for modern GPUs. Combined with memory-adaptive execution planning and stream scheduling, WP-DC eliminates DC reduction as a runtime bottleneck. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that SymWarp-FastDEC produces clustering outputs identical to the original FastDEC on all evaluated equivalence benchmarks. Compared with a FastDEC variant using FAISS GpuIndexFlatL2 for exact k-NN search, our approach achieves approximately 1.7x to 4.9x higher end-to-end performance, with the advantage generally increasing with dimensionality. Compared with the original single-threaded Python FastDEC implementation, speedups reach approximately 2.5 x 10^4 in a very high-dimensional case where its k-d-tree-based search degrades; this extreme result is not representative of typical acceleration.

</details>


### [102] [P$^2$-MOPD: Accelerating Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation via Parameter Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03953)
*Sinuo Wang,Jiacheng Lu,Zeyu Zhang,Maojia Song,Rui Sun,Beidi Luan,Yutong Xie,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Qi Wu,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法联合优化学生模型参数与专家参数的混合系数，在在线策略蒸馏中更快、更均衡地恢复多领域能力；十七亿参数模型最终平均恢复率达百分之九十四点七，高于基线的百分之八十点九。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线策略蒸馏能利用学生生成的轨迹整合专家能力，但恢复过程可能较慢或不均衡。任务算术虽无需训练，却受限于冻结的学生与专家参数所张成的范围。论文旨在结合专家参数先验与学生模型的持续适应能力，突破这一限制。

Method: 提出一种联合优化方法，将学生与专家参数的线性混合作为单一可微模型，在同一在线策略蒸馏目标下，同时学习混合系数和学生参数。由于学生参数仍可训练，专家参数提供先验引导，但不会将模型限制在原有参数组合范围内。

Result: 在四个领域、十七亿和四十亿参数规模的实验中，该方法从与基线相同的初始化出发，实现了更快且更均衡的能力恢复；达到基线最终平均恢复水平所需的训练步数减少一半。训练结束时，十七亿参数模型的平均恢复率为百分之九十四点七，基线为百分之八十点九。

Conclusion: 实验表明，将参数混合与在线策略蒸馏联合优化，能够兼顾专家能力迁移和学生模型的自由适应，优于仅依靠固定参数线性组合或常规多教师蒸馏的恢复表现。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation consolidates expert capabilities by supervising student-generated trajectories, whereas task arithmetic offers a training-free alternative through linear parameter combinations. We view the linear combination of student and expert parameters as a single differentiable model and propose P$^2$-MOPD, which jointly optimizes the mixing coefficients and the student weights under the same on-policy distillation objective. Unlike task arithmetic, which stays within the span of the frozen student and expert parameters for any choice of merging coefficients, P$^2$-MOPD keeps the student trainable, so the experts act as parameter priors without confining it to this span. Across four domains at 1.7B and 4B scales, P$^2$-MOPD achieves faster and more balanced recovery than MOPD from the same initialization, surpassing its final average recovery in half the training steps. At the end of training, our 1.7B model reaches 94.7% average recovery, versus 80.9% for MOPD.

</details>


### [103] [Forget What Comes Next: LLM Unlearning via Statistical Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03955)
*Andrii Shkabrii,Claudia Plant,Martin Pawelczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于希尔伯特—施密特独立性准则的机器遗忘方法，旨在减少待遗忘词元与模型预测所用内部表示之间的统计依赖。实验显示，该方法在遗忘效果与模型效用之间取得较好平衡，并能扩展至大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可能记忆并复现训练数据中的片段，带来隐私与法律风险。现有遗忘方法即使完成训练目标，投毒样本仍可能在统计上被检测到，因此需要进一步检验模型是否真正消除了对目标内容的记忆。

Method: 提出一种机器遗忘算法，通过希尔伯特—施密特独立性准则降低待遗忘词元与模型用于预测这些词元的内部表示之间的统计依赖。论文还将该目标与精确记忆建立形式化联系，并给出可计算的上界，以便将训练目标与模型复现目标词元的能力直接关联。

Result: 实验覆盖八种设置，模型规模最高达七十亿参数，并展示了向七百亿参数模型扩展的能力。评估内容包括高斯投毒数据、个人信息、生物学与网络安全领域的危险知识，以及受版权保护的书籍和新闻文章。摘要称，该方法平均取得最优的遗忘效果—模型效用权衡，并且是唯一能使高斯投毒样本无法被检测、同时保持模型效用接近原始水平的方法。

Conclusion: 该研究以降低目标内容与预测表示之间的统计依赖来促进机器遗忘，并通过与精确记忆的理论联系增强了目标的可解释性。实验结果表明，该方法在多类敏感或受保护内容上具有竞争力，并具备扩展到大规模模型的潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can memorize and reproduce fragments of their training data, raising privacy and legal concerns that motivate machine unlearning. Yet poisoned training examples can remain statistically detectable after unlearning, which raises the question of whether current unlearning objectives remove what the model has memorized at all. In this work, we introduce PINT, a novel algorithm for machine unlearning that uses the Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) to reduce the statistical dependence between tokens to be forgotten and the internal representations used to predict them. We formally relate our HSIC-based objective to Exact Memorization through a computable upper bound, connecting the training objective directly to the model's ability to reproduce the forgotten tokens. In extensive experiments, we compare forgetting-utility trade-offs across eight settings with models up to 7B parameters and demonstrate scalability to 70B parameter models. Our evaluations cover unlearning Gaussian poisons, personal information, hazardous knowledge in biology and cybersecurity, and copyrighted books and news articles. PINT achieves the strongest forgetting-utility trade-off on average and is the only method to make Gaussian poisons undetectable while maintaining near-original model utility. Code is available at: https://github.com/shkabrii/PINT

</details>


### [104] [Sampling Interventional Distributions with Orthogonal MMD Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03962)
*Bariscan Bozkurt,Hugh Dance,Zonghao Chen,Alexandre Galashov,Arthur Gretton,Houssam Zenati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于核均值嵌入和粒子流的干预分布采样方法，在高维图像任务中兼具较强效果与较高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多因果推断任务需要获得干预后的完整结果分布，而非仅估计少量统计量。现有方法通常要拟合目标分布的生成模型并估计额外的辅助组件，在高维结果场景下计算开销较大。

Method: 干预最大均值差异流以核均值嵌入表示目标干预分布，并使用具有双重稳健性和奈曼正交性的估计器估计该嵌入；随后通过最大均值差异梯度流推动粒子，从而生成干预分布样本。该方法支持硬干预和软干预，并可借助冻结的编码器处理高维图像结果。

Result: 论文报告了估计与粒子近似方面的理论保证。在图像基准实验中，该方法达到或超过当前先进生成式方法的表现，同时显著提升运行速度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过直接估计并复用干预分布的核均值嵌入，提供了生成完整干预结果分布的一种计算高效方案；理论分析与图像实验共同表明，它适用于高维因果推断任务。

Abstract: Many causal inference problems require sampling from interventional outcome distributions rather than estimating low-dimensional summaries. Existing approaches often rely on fitting generative models of the target distribution together with additional nuisance components, which can be computationally demanding for high-dimensional outcomes. We propose Interventional Maximum Mean Discrepancy Flow (IMMD-Flow), a particle-based method that instead represents the target interventional distribution through its kernel mean embedding. This kernel embedding admits a doubly robust, Neyman-orthogonal estimator with finite-sample guarantees and can be efficiently reused to sample from the interventional distribution via MMD gradient flow. IMMD-Flow accommodates both hard and soft interventions, admits theoretical guarantees for estimation and particle approximation, and extends to high-dimensional image outcomes through frozen encoders. Across image-based benchmarks, IMMD-Flow matches or improves upon state-of-the-art generative approaches while showing significant speed advantages.

</details>


### [105] [Ermakov--Based embeddings as a parameter-free layer for physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03964)
*Roberto Bernal-Jaquez,Roberto Romero Huerta,Alexander Schaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将埃尔马科夫—平内尺度函数ρ作为无额外参数的输入嵌入物理信息神经网络，并比较其在三类时变系数问题中的作用；不同问题需要不同的变换，消融实验至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变系数演化方程本身已决定某些尺度变化，直接训练神经网络会浪费模型容量去学习这些已知结构。论文希望将经典尺度函数ρ直接写入网络输入，以降低学习难度，同时厘清该方法在哪些问题中有效、依赖哪些组成部分。

Method: 利用埃尔马科夫—平内方程的尺度函数ρ构造无额外参数的网络输入嵌入。通过精确的矩恒等式，仅凭初始数据即可闭式求得ρ，无需辅助网络或预先知道解。该构造覆盖三类问题：无势线性扩散（平内常数c=-1、频率ω恒为零）、时变束缚势中的扩散（c=-1、ω不为零），以及时变频率量子谐振子（c=+1）；并分别考察扩散时间重参数化、振幅因子和二次相位因子的作用。

Result: 三类问题的有效机制不同。在线性扩散中，仅扩散时间重参数化就能复现大部分提升，因此该问题不足以单独检验整体方法，必须进行消融实验。时变束缚势扩散中不存在时间重参数化，相关控制反而降低精度，振幅因子则至关重要，带来40.8倍提升。量子振子中绝热近似也失效，关键在于二次相位因子，带来5.7倍提升。若约化后的解不收敛到固定点，方法的外推优势便会消失。

Conclusion: 研究的基础是自相似渐近分析中的经典缩放变量变换；主要新意在于利用矩恒等式，使尺度函数能够直接成为时变系数问题的网络结构输入，并通过跨问题比较揭示各组成部分的实际作用。该方法并非普遍有效，其收益取决于问题结构及约化解的收敛性质。

Abstract: Evolution equations with time-dependent coefficients force a neural solver to spend capacity on a scale that the equation already determines. We show that a single classical object, the Ermakov-Pinney scale function $ρ$, can be written into the input layer of a physics-informed neural network as a parameter-free embedding. One construction covers three problem families through the two signs of the Pinney constant $c$: linear diffusion ($c=-1$, $ω\equiv0$), diffusion in a time-dependent confining trap ($c=-1$, $ω\neq0$), and the quantum oscillator with time-dependent frequency ($c=+1$). An exact moment identity gives $ρ$ in closed form from the initial data alone, so no auxiliary network or knowledge of the solution is needed. Our central finding is comparative: each family requires a different component of the construction and has a different honest control. In family I, the diffusive-time reparametrisation alone reproduces most of the gain, so that family is a poor test of the idea and shows why an ablation is mandatory. In family II no time reparametrisation exists, the control degrades accuracy, and the amplitude prefactor becomes essential (40.8x). In family III the adiabatic approximation also fails, and the quadratic phase factor is what matters (5.7x). The method does not help when the reduced solution does not converge to a fixed point: its extrapolation advantage then disappears. The reduction itself is the classical scaling-variable change of the self-similar-asymptotics literature; what is new is the moment identity that makes it architectural for time-dependent coefficients, and the comparative anatomy above.

</details>


### [106] [Stacking-Based Weighting for Large Language Bayes in M-Open Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03966)
*Anant Bhide,Akarsh Gupta,Edmond Cunningham,Justin Domke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为大型语言模型贝叶斯推断提出基于堆叠的模型加权方法，在模型设定有误时减少预测过度依赖单一候选模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型贝叶斯方法能根据自然语言描述生成多个概率程序，并在各模型内进行推断，再汇总预测。但实际生成的候选模型集可能存在设定错误；基于似然的贝叶斯模型平均可能把权重集中到单一模型，无法充分体现候选模型间的不确定性。

Method: 保留原有的语言模型驱动的候选模型生成及模型内推断流程，将汇总阶段改为堆叠：通过优化预测表现来确定模型权重，而非依据边际似然。针对潜变量模型中留一法预测量难以计算的问题，推导变分下界，并据此构造可计算的证据下界堆叠目标。

Result: 在真实世界的概率推断任务中，该方法取得了与贝叶斯模型平均相当的预测表现，同时产生分布更分散的模型权重，降低了模型设定错误时对单一候选模型的过度集中。

Conclusion: 基于堆叠的汇总方式可在保留大型语言模型贝叶斯推断原有流程的同时，改善候选模型设定不完善时的不确定性表达，并维持有竞争力的预测表现。

Abstract: Large Language Bayes (LLB) transforms a natural language problem description into a collection of candidate probabilistic programs and performs inference within each model, combining their predictions using Bayesian model averaging. While this provides a flexible framework for probabilistic inference without manual model specification, the resulting candidate model set is often misspecified in practice, and likelihood-based aggregation can concentrate on a single model, under-representing uncertainty across the sampled models. We propose a stacking-based aggregation method for LLB that selects model weights by optimizing predictive performance rather than marginal likelihood, while preserving the original language-model-driven model generation and within-model inference pipeline. Because leave-one-out predictive quantities are intractable in latent-variable models, we derive a variational lower bound that yields a tractable ELBO-based stacking objective. Across real-world probabilistic inference tasks, our method achieves comparable predictive performance to Bayesian model averaging while yielding more dispersed model weights, reducing concentration on a single candidate model under misspecification.

</details>


### [107] [One Mistake, Three Layers: Persistence Rules Under Depth Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03968)
*Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Michael Brown,Sanjeda Sara Jennifer,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，按深度曲线中连续若干层超过阈值来判断信息首次可解码，会因相邻层相关性而严重提前估计；作者提出经零假设校准的估计方法，并发现口袋信息在各层均可解码，累积深度更能区分不同熟悉度情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐层探测冻结模型的表示已很常见，但如何从深度曲线判断某种信号何时首次出现，缺乏可靠规则。相邻层的强相关会使连续多层超过阈值并不等同于真实起始，导致错误的提前判断。论文还关注公开共折叠模型检查点与其代码不匹配、可能造成表示掺入随机噪声的问题。

Method: 作者构造零假设轨迹：在每个评估单位内置换标签，并在所有深度层固定同一次置换，以保留层间相关结构；再以逐层顺序统计量设定阈值，并通过校准而非任意指定来确定需要持续超阈值的层数。该方法用于分析冻结共折叠模型主干中的口袋信息，按蛋白质家族熟悉度与口袋熟悉度划分一千八百二十个截止日期之后的靶标，并比较起始深度与预先定义的累积深度。此外，作者检查了公开检查点与配套代码的参数一致性。

Result: 合成数据表明，在类似变换器的深度相关性下，要求连续三层超过零假设阈值会把起始时间提前十九层，而且每次重采样都会报告起始。真实数据中，连续五层规则在零假设轨迹上的触发率为百分之四至七，接近名义百分之五；连续三层规则则为百分之十二至十七，重现了合成实验中的失效。口袋成员信息在四种熟悉度条件下的全部四十八个模块中均超过阈值，因此起始深度无法区分这些条件；累积深度则显示，家族熟悉但口袋结构新颖的情形最晚，且在所有特征集合中均如此。该情形的表现也最弱，并且这一差距在控制序列长度、口袋度量和比对深度后仍然存在。另发现一个广泛使用的公开检查点与其自身代码之间有一百二十八个主干参数缺失，加载时这些参数会被随机初始化。

Conclusion: 判断表示信号的可解码起始点时，必须显式处理深度层之间的相关性，并对持续超阈值规则进行零假设校准；未经校准的连续层规则可能严重夸大信号出现的早晚。对于本文的口袋信息任务，累积深度比起始深度更有区分力。研究还提示，使用公开模型检查点前应核验参数完整性，否则看似正常的表示可能包含随机初始化带来的噪声。

Abstract: Probing frozen representations layer by layer is now standard practice, but reporting when a signal first becomes decodable requires a rule for reading a depth curve, and the obvious rule is wrong. On synthetic curves with known ground truth, requiring a probe score to exceed a null-calibrated threshold for three consecutive stages reports an onset nineteen stages early under transformer-like depth correlation, with every resample returning an onset. The failure follows from correlation between neighboring layers, a hazard known in neuroimaging but not, to our knowledge, checked for depth curves.
  We give an estimator whose error rate is calibrated rather than assumed. Null trajectories are built by permuting labels within each evaluation unit and holding each permutation fixed across depth, preserving the correlation structure the rule must survive; thresholds are per-layer order statistics; and the persistence requirement is calibrated rather than chosen.
  Applied to pocket information in a frozen co-folding trunk, stratifying 1,820 post-cutoff targets by protein-family and pocket familiarity, the five-stage rule fires on 4 to 7 per cent of null trajectories against a nominal 5 per cent and the three-stage rule on 12 to 17: the synthetic failure reproduces on real data. Pocket membership is decodable above threshold at all 48 blocks in all four regimes, so the onset cannot discriminate them; the pre-declared accumulation depth does, and is latest for familiar families with structurally novel pockets in every feature set. That regime is also the weakest, by a margin surviving controls for length, pocket measure and alignment depth.
  Separately, a widely used public co-folding checkpoint loads against its own code with 128 trunk parameters missing; those would be randomly initialized, and every representation extracted would be partly noise while appearing normal.

</details>


### [108] [Rapid Mean-Field Formation Transport with a Shared-Representation Neural Fokker-Planck Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03981)
*Zejian Zhou,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于大规模多智能体编队运输的均场方法，通过离线优化共享参数化模型，降低在线计算成本，并在数值实验中展现较好的运输精度与运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典均场规划通常需要反复求解相互耦合的前向与后向偏微分方程，在线重新规划的计算负担较重。本文旨在降低大规模多智能体系统实现编队运输时的在线计算成本，同时保持运输精度。

Method: 使用同一个参数化非线性映射分别输出标量密度和向量控制。离线优化模型参数时，综合考虑福克–普朗克方程残差、初始条件残差、基于数值模拟闭环粒子轨迹的编队匹配代价以及控制能耗。计算限制在状态空间的有界区域内，并对穿越边界的概率流量施加惩罚。对于周期域模型，进一步证明诱导密度演化的适定性，并将密度近似误差界定为与动力学残差和初始条件残差相关。在线运行时控制器系数保持固定。

Result: 数值实验量化了该方法相较于均场规划器的精度与运行时间权衡；结果显示，其运输误差低于经典反馈基线，并验证了密度与控制采用共同参数化的益处。

Conclusion: 该方法将主要参数优化移至离线阶段，以共享参数模型近似密度和控制，从而减少在线计算需求。理论分析提供了密度演化适定性与近似误差保证，实验则表明该方案在效率、运输精度及参数化设计方面具有优势。

Abstract: This paper develops a mean-field formation transport method with low online computational cost for large-scale multi-agent systems. Classical mean-field planners often require repeated solutions of coupled forward and backward partial differential equations, making online replanning computationally demanding. Density and control are therefore approximated by a common parameterized nonlinear map with separate scalar density and vector control outputs. Parameters are optimized using Fokker--Planck and initial-condition residuals, formation-matching costs on numerically simulated closed-loop particle trajectories, and control effort. Numerical computations are restricted to a bounded region of the state space, with an additional penalty on probability flux across its boundary. For the periodic-domain model, we establish well-posedness of the induced density evolution and bound the density-approximation error in terms of the dynamics and initial-condition residuals. Parameter optimization precedes online operation, during which controller coefficients remain fixed. Experiments quantify the accuracy--runtime trade-off relative to a mean-field planner, show lower transport error than classical feedback baselines, and demonstrate the benefits of the common parameterization.

</details>


### [109] [Physics-Informed Identification of Tensegrity Prestress across Taut-Slack Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04007)
*Sandesh Lamsal,Sumit Kumar,Rubi Bhandari,Subhamoy Sen,Andrzej Rutkiewicz,Leszek Małyszko,Landolf Rhode-Barbarigos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用运动记录反演张拉整体结构预应力及其他构件参数的方法，并在模拟与实测数据上验证其精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预应力决定张拉整体结构的稳定性、刚度和动力特性，却难以从稀疏测量中直接观测，且可能随服役时间下降。强激励还会使拉索松弛，导致动力响应呈现非光滑特征；既有识别方法主要适用于拉索始终绷紧的情况，而物理信息神经网络通常只解决已知参数下的正向响应预测问题。

Method: 研究将参数识别构造成逆问题：在受约束的笛卡尔坐标下，训练神经网络以满足运动方程残差。该表示具有恒定质量矩阵，且力密度以线性形式进入方程。将构件力密度、阻尼和轴向刚度设为可训练参数，同时保留拉索仅受拉的力学规律。另以费舍尔信息分析在反演前判断测量记录能够辨识哪些构件参数。

Result: 在单模块平面结构中，即使加速度含噪且仅测量一半自由坐标，可辨识力密度的恢复误差仍低于百分之一点五；对存在或不存在绷紧—松弛转换的记录均有效，且发生转换的记录能够辨识仅凭始终绷紧记录无法确定的构件。在扩展至五模块、含十六根拉索的双跨桥结构后，力密度误差中位数为百分之四点二。实测数据中，识别得到的弹簧刚度与独立测量值平均相差百分之零点一；全尺寸张拉整体单体在十三个预应力水平下的识别结果，与拉索力传感器读数的均方根误差为百分之二点九。

Conclusion: 该方法将张拉整体参数识别扩展到包含拉索松弛的非光滑动力情形，并能结合可辨识性分析判断数据所含信息。模拟与实测结果表明，该方法可从稀疏运动记录中较准确地反演预应力及相关参数。

Abstract: Prestress governs the stability, stiffness, and dynamic behavior of tensegrity structures, but it is not directly observable from sparse structural measurements, and it may decrease during service life. Under strong excitation, the cables may slacken and the dynamics become nonsmooth. Existing identification methods are limited to the taut regime, and physics-informed networks for tensegrities solve only the forward problem of predicting the response from known member parameters. This study formulates the inverse problem. Neural networks are trained on the equation-of-motion residual in constrained Cartesian coordinates, where the mass matrix is constant and the force densities enter linearly. The force densities, damping, and axial stiffness are trainable parameters, and the tension-only cable law is kept. A Fisher-information analysis shows, before inversion, which member parameters a record can determine. The method is tested on simulated planar tensegrity frames with up to five modules and a three-dimensional prism, and on measured records of a biomimetic leg and a full-scale tensegrity simplex. On the one-module frame, the identifiable force densities are recovered within 1.5% from noisy accelerations at half the free coordinates, on records with and without taut-slack transitions. Records with transitions determine members that taut records leave undetermined. On the five-module frame, a 16-cable two-span bridge, the median force-density error rises moderately, to 4.2%. On measured records, an identified spring stiffness is within 0.1% of its independently measured value on average, and the identified simplex prestress at 13 levels agrees with its cable force transducers to 2.9% root-mean-square.

</details>


### [110] [Decision Transformer with Task-Aware Representations for Offline Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04010)
*Xinbo Zhao,Yuhang Liu,Xin Zhang,Lei Le,Zhaohan Xi,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向离线元强化学习的任务感知决策变换器，通过多种监督信号学习可迁移的任务表示，并借助语言引导的自适应策略提升未见任务上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线元强化学习需要从有限的离线轨迹中准确识别任务，才能生成适合当前任务的策略。若任务表示不充分，模型在新任务上的零样本或少样本泛化便会受限。论文旨在构建紧凑、可迁移且能有效指导策略生成的任务表示。

Method: 提出对比式任务感知决策变换器框架，联合利用四类信息学习任务表示：离线轨迹的对比学习、与自然语言任务描述的对齐、由任务条件世界模型提供的一步监督，以及由任务条件价值函数提供的长期监督。所得任务表示作为策略的条件输入，用于任务感知的元策略学习。此外，设计语言落地的自适应机制，以支持对未见任务的零样本和少样本适应。预训练大语言模型作为共享骨干，同时承担语言编码、世界建模、价值估计和策略学习，以促进参数共享并提升效率与泛化能力。

Result: 论文报告称，在多种离线元强化学习基准上的广泛实验中，该方法整体优于已有方法，尤其在难度较高的零样本泛化场景中表现突出。摘要未提供具体数据或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将轨迹对比学习、语言描述以及短期和长期任务监督结合起来，有助于形成更有效的任务表示；再配合共享模型骨干与语言引导的自适应机制，可增强离线元强化学习对新任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: To enable effective generalization in offline meta-RL, it is essential to accurately infer the underlying task through latent task representations, which provide a compact and transferable summary of task-specific characteristics for guiding policy generation. In this paper, we propose Contrastive Task-Aware Decision Transformer (CoTA-DT), a DT-based offline meta-RL framework that learns task representations by jointly integrating four complementary components: (i) contrastive learning from offline trajectories, (ii) alignment with natural-language task descriptions, (iii) one-step supervision via a task-conditioned world model, and (iv) long-term supervision via a task-conditioned Q function. The learned task representations are used as conditioning signals for a DT-based policy, enabling task-aware meta-policy learning. To further support generalization in both zero-shot and few-shot settings, we design a language-grounded, self-adaptive strategy that enables effective and reliable adaptation to unseen tasks. Notably, in CoTA-DT, the pretrained LLM serves as a shared backbone for language encoding, world modeling, Q-function estimation, and policy learning. This unified design facilitates parameter sharing, improving both efficiency and generalization. Extensive experiments across diverse offline meta-RL benchmarks demonstrate that CoTA-DT consistently outperforms prior methods, particularly in challenging zero-shot generalization scenarios.

</details>


### [111] [Denoising Is Stein: Moment Access and a Resource Hierarchy for Learning Hidden Structure in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04043)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究含隐藏非高斯成分的数据如何通过去噪样本对学习得分函数，并刻画辨认该成分所需的矩阶数、特征规模与样本复杂度；还提出两阶段算法，并给出理论与实验支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当数据含有隐藏的非高斯结构时，去噪学习能否识别该结构，以及识别所需的维度依赖复杂度，尚需明确刻画。若协方差已显露结构，可直接恢复其子空间；若协方差与各向同性基线匹配，学习者就必须利用更高阶的首个区分矩，并从干净样本及其噪声观测中提取信息。

Method: 论文将去噪损失中的相关性解释为相对于基线分布的斯坦差异，并证明对多项式向量场，这些差异恰好给出相应阶数的矩差。对同一干净点取条件独立的新噪声观测并求平均，可构造干净分布上的精确矩查询。在协方差匹配情形下，论文分析满足四阶矩与二阶矩约束的方向无关特征字典、点积核岭模型及单个单位上的小步长在线随机梯度下降；另提出两阶段方法，先利用平滑去噪损失的曲率恢复隐藏方向，再细化该方向并拟合标量读出。在协方差可见情形下，则利用主成分投影降低对环境维数的依赖。

Result: 协方差匹配时，若首个区分矩的阶数为 k*，所需特征数及所述点积核岭模型的样本量具有 d^(k*−1) 量级；单单位在线随机梯度下降在对数因子范围内达到相同维度指数。对于任意偶数 k*，两阶段算法使用 O(d^(k*/2) log d) 个干净点，即可相对高斯基线获得固定的得分增益。协方差可见时，冻结特征的阈值为 d^J/J!；采用主成分投影后，拟合模型、得分风险及生成分布不再依赖环境维数。实验中，部分迹重叠与提出的谱转变相符；宽度为八的网络在重复使用干净点训练时，于维数 64 至 192 的范围呈现近似 d² 的训练集阈值，并保持验证与校准开销固定。

Conclusion: 论文建立了从去噪损失到高阶矩识别的联系，并区分了协方差可见与协方差匹配两种结构学习情形。其理论表明，首个区分矩的阶数决定复杂度的维度指数；两阶段方法可在更低样本量下获得稳定得分改进，实验结果总体支持相关谱转变与样本复杂度预测。

Abstract: We study score learning from denoising pairs when the data contain a hidden non-Gaussian component. If covariance reveals the component, its subspace can be recovered directly. If covariance is isotropic, the first distinguishing moment has order $k^\ast$, and the learner must extract that moment from clean points and their noisy views.
  Correlations in the denoising loss are Stein discrepancies against a baseline; for polynomial fields they determine exactly the corresponding moment differences. Averaging fresh noisy views conditional on a clean point gives an exact moment query on the clean distribution. In the covariance-matched regime, orientation-independent dictionaries satisfying an $L^4/L^2$ condition need order $d^{k^\ast-1}$ features, and the stated dot-product kernel ridge models need the same power in samples. Small-step online SGD on one unit has this exponent up to logarithms. A two-stage learner uses the curvature of a smoothed denoising loss to recover the hidden direction, then sharpens it and fits a scalar readout. For every even $k^\ast$, it obtains a fixed score gain over the Gaussian baseline from $O(d^{k^\ast/2}\log d)$ clean points. In the covariance-visible regime, the frozen-feature threshold occurs at $d^J/J!$, while projection onto principal components makes the fitted-model, score-risk and generated-law distributions independent of ambient dimension.
  In the covariance-matched experiments, the measured partial-trace overlaps are consistent with the proposed spectral transition. A width-8 network trained with clean-point reuse shows a $d^2$-like training-set threshold over $d=64$--$192$, with fixed validation and calibration overhead.

</details>


### [112] [Structured Priors: Towards an algebra of component priors for prior-data fitted networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04056)
*Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出由可组合先验及其代数算子构成的结构化先验框架，并验证其对先验数据拟合网络的影响；混合先验在多数回归基准上表现良好，但基于分量网络的模型平均实验未成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先验数据拟合网络通常依赖单一、人工设计的合成数据生成器。若生成器混合多个组成部分，混合权重往往由人工指定，其对预训练网络的影响只能在训练后观察，缺少清晰、可验证的设计原则。论文旨在以结构化、可分析的先验组合方式取代这种经验式构造。

Method: 论文在测度论框架下定义混合、卷积、幅度缩放、局部变换和投影等算子，并形式化证明这些算子所遵循的代数规律。研究将其实现于软件工具包，并通过实验检验生成器及训练后网络是否体现相应规律；同时评估小型组成先验的混合效果，并提出关于模型平均的理论结果。

Result: 实验表明，所实现的生成器能够在置换检验的分辨率范围内复现已证明的恒等关系，训练后的网络也遵守分配律。由小型分量构成的混合先验在八个开放回归基准中的七个上达到与最佳单一分量相当或更好的表现；在其中两个数据集上，它仅用九分之一的数据预算，便达到通用随机函数先验使用完整预算仍未达到的误差水平。理论上，若各分量网络分别是其先验下的贝叶斯预测器，则这些网络的模型平均是混合先验的贝叶斯预测器；但预注册实验未能验证这一预测，因为训练好的网络很少能识别出数据所来自的生成分量。

Conclusion: 结构化先验为构造和分析先验数据拟合网络提供了具有形式化依据的途径，混合小型先验也显示出实际竞争力。不过，分量网络的模型平均尚未得到实验支持，关键障碍可能在于网络对生成分量的识别能力不足。论文据此倡议进一步研究结构化先验的代数及其对训练后网络行为的影响。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks are typically trained using synthetic data generated by a single, manually constructed generator. In cases where such a generator combines multiple components, e.g., in TabPFN, Mitra, or TabICLv2, this is achieved through a mixture using manually specified weights, the impact of which on the network only becomes apparent after pre-training. Instead of such a hand-built generator, this work proposes a structured prior: a small set of named component priors, linked by operators whose algebraic properties are formally proven and whose influence on the trained network has been validated. Within a measure-theoretic framework, the operators of mixture, convolution, amplitude scaling, local transform, and projection are defined. Their governing laws are proven. These operators are implemented in the macl2l software package. Experiments demonstrate that the implemented generators reproduce the identities within the resolution of a permutation test. Furthermore, it is shown that trained networks obey the distributive law; that a mixed prior composed of small components performs as well as or better than any individual component in seven out of eight open regression benchmarks; and that on two of these tables a structured prior reaches, with a ninth of the data budget, an error that a generic random-function prior does not reach even with the full budget. A theorem on model averaging demonstrates that networks trained on individual components constitute the Bayes predictor for the mixture, provided that the component networks are themselves the Bayes predictors for their respective priors. However, the pre-registered test of this averaged predictor with trained component networks failed, because the networks identify the generating component too rarely. The paper proposes a research program focused on the algebra of structured priors for prior-data fitted networks.

</details>


### [113] [StreamLTT: Risk Certification for Policy Selection under Stream Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04062)
*Jinwen Xu,Qin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 流式学习后检验框架针对存在依赖与非平稳性的部署数据，认证随历史变化的风险，并据此筛选满足风险目标的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习后检验通常依据校准损失估计固定总体风险，但在序贯部署中，损失可能相互依赖且随时间变化，使固定策略的风险会随已观测历史而改变。因此，需要重新界定适用于此类场景的风险目标，并设计相应的有限样本检验方法。

Method: 提出流式学习后检验，将固定总体风险替换为平均可预测风险：在每个结果揭示前，依据当时可获得的信息对该决策的损失进行条件化。通过条件中心化，将经验偏差转化为鞅和，并据此构造能够适应可预测方差的检验，以及有限样本的霍夫丁型替代检验。再结合族错误率控制，同时认证一组策略，供后续部署选择。

Result: 在七个预测数据集和七种部署风险上的实验表明，相较于独立同分布、稳健方差、分块自助法及基于集中不等式的替代方法，该方法得到的认证前沿明显更接近预设风险目标，且实测族错误率与预设水平相符。有限样本消融实验显示，采用可预测风险定义是收紧风险前沿的关键；利用可预测方差进行自适应，则能更有效地使用族错误率预算。

Conclusion: 该方法将风险认证策略的适用范围扩展到具有依赖性和非平稳性的序贯部署数据流。其核心贡献不仅是改进检验方式，也在于明确了此类场景中应认证的风险目标。

Abstract: Learn-then-Test (LTT) provides a general framework for certifying deployment policies against a prescribed risk target, but its standard formulation targets a fixed population risk estimated from calibration losses. In sequential deployment, losses may instead be dependent and nonstationary, so the risk of a fixed policy can evolve with the observed history. We introduce StreamLTT, which replaces this fixed population risk with average predictable risk, defined by conditioning each decision's loss on the information available before its outcome is revealed. Conditional centering turns empirical deviations from this target into a martingale sum, enabling both a test that adapts to predictable variance and a finite-sample Hoeffding alternative. Combined with family-wise error rate control, StreamLTT yields a simultaneously certified policy region that supports subsequent deployment selection. Across seven forecasting datasets and seven deployment risks, StreamLTT places the certified frontier substantially closer to the prescribed risk target than IID, robust-variance, block-bootstrap, and concentration-based alternatives, while empirical family-wise error closely follows the prescribed level. A finite-sample ablation further shows that the predictable-risk formulation drives the tighter risk frontier, while adaptation to predictable variance makes more effective use of the available family-wise error budget. StreamLTT therefore extends risk-certified policy selection to dependent deployment streams by addressing both what risk should be certified and how it should be tested.

</details>


### [114] [TB-Transolver: Parallelized and Coherent Long-Horizon Rollouts via Temporal Bundling for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04063)
*Sidharth S. Menon,Ameya D. Jagtap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出时间捆绑式求解器框架，通过并行处理多个时间快照降低瞬态物理问题的计算开销，并利用时空相干性指标预先确定合适的捆绑宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解器依靠时间条件化或自回归推演处理随时间变化的问题，虽然能保持时间因果关系并发挥其在非结构网格上的空间建模优势，但通常需要按时间步顺序执行，计算成本随预测时域线性增长，限制了其在瞬态物理建模中的效率和应用范围。

Method: 提出时间捆绑式求解器，将多个时间快照组成时间捆绑进行处理，以提升时间依赖偏微分方程求解的效率。论文还提出时空相干性指标，用于量化同一时间捆绑内快照之间的相关程度，并仅凭训练数据预先选择合适的捆绑宽度，从而避免耗费较大的网格搜索。

Result: 在塑性、流体动力学、反应扩散和全球天气预报四类问题上，该方法相较于传统自回归及时间条件化基线，训练速度最高提升二十九倍。对于时空相干性较高的系统，结合多节点、多图形处理器并行后，相较单图形处理器实现最高四十五点六倍的综合加速。

Conclusion: 时间捆绑为求解器处理时间变化问题提供了一种降低顺序计算开销的途径；时空相干性指标则支持依据训练数据选择捆绑宽度。实验结果表明，该框架在多个物理领域具有显著加速潜力，且可进一步受益于分布式并行计算。

Abstract: Transolver has recently gained widespread adoption for solving diverse physical problems on unstructured geometric meshes by leveraging its Physics-Attention mechanism, which seamlessly extends standard softmax attention to handle physical domains at scale. For time-dependent problems, existing literature relies on time-conditioned or autoregressive rollouts to preserve temporal causality while retaining Physics-Attention as the spatial backbone. However, a fundamental limitation of this formulation is its sequential execution model, wherein computational costs scale linearly with the temporal horizon, rendering standard Transolver inefficient for transient problems despite its exceptional spatial efficacy. This bottleneck has historically hindered its broader application in time-dependent physical modeling. To address this limitation, we introduce TB-Transolver (also referred to as Temporally Bundled Transolver), an efficient framework built on temporal bundling that scales Transolver for time-dependent PDEs. Furthermore, we propose \emph{spatiotemporal coherence} (${\rm C(K)}$) as a general metric to systematically quantify snapshot correlation within a temporal bundle, enabling the \emph{a priori} selection of optimal bundle widths using training data alone, thereby avoiding expensive grid searches. We demonstrate the computational efficacy of TB-Transolver across four physically distinct domains, namely, plasticity, fluid dynamics, reaction-diffusion, and global weather forecasting; achieving training speedups of up to $29\times$ over conventional autoregressive and time-conditioned baselines. Finally, these computational gains are further amplified through distributed multi-node and multi-GPU parallelism, yielding aggregated speedups of up to $45.6\times$ at optimal bundle widths for systems exhibiting high spatiotemporal coherence compared to single-GPU implementations.

</details>


### [115] [Graph-Localized Coverage for Conformal Prediction on Inductive Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04066)
*Jinwen Xu,Qin Lu,Georgios B. Giannakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向图数据的在线保形预测方法，在维持整体覆盖率的同时提升不同图邻域中的局部可靠性，并缩小预测集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统保形预测通常保证总体覆盖率，却无法说明错误集中出现在哪些图邻域。不同邻域的查询可能具有不同的不确定性，因此整体覆盖率达标并不代表局部预测同样可靠。

Method: 提出图局部在线保形预测框架，适用于归纳式节点分类和边预测。方法利用基于蒙特卡洛丢弃的预测所估计的互信息，构造可随不确定性调整的非一致性分数；再以全局在线阈值为基础，通过核函数将相似图查询的覆盖反馈传播至局部进行修正。理论上，在不要求样本可交换或过程平稳的条件下，建立了路径上的总体校准误差界；对于精确核函数形式，还给出图局部校准保证。

Result: 在七个节点分类基准和七个边预测基准上的实验表明，相近的总体覆盖率可能掩盖明显不同的局部可靠性。该方法在保持覆盖率接近目标值的同时，持续降低图局部校准误差；不确定性缩放还在各项节点与边数据集上缩小了预测集合，且总体覆盖率基本不变。

Conclusion: 该研究将总体在线校准扩展到图邻域层面的局部校准，并利用不确定性信息调整预测集合。实验与理论结果表明，该框架有望在维持总体覆盖保证的同时，使图上的预测可靠性更均衡、预测结果更紧凑。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides marginal coverage guarantees, but marginal coverage does not determine where prediction errors occur on a graph. Queries from different graph neighborhoods can exhibit substantially different levels of uncertainty even when aggregate coverage remains near its target. We introduce GLocalCP, an online CP framework for inductive node classification and edge prediction that combines marginal and graph-local calibration with uncertainty-adaptive prediction sets. Specifically, GLocalCP puts forth a novel uncertainty-adaptive nonconformality score that relies on the mutual information obtained from Monte-Carlo (MC) dropout based prediction. Then, it augments a global online threshold with a kernel-based local correction that propagates coverage feedback among similar graph queries. On the theoretical horizon, we establish pathwise $O(N^{-1/2})$ marginal calibration error without exchangeability or stationarity and, for the exact-kernel formulation, an explicit $O(N^{-1/2})$ graph-localized calibration guarantee. Experiments on seven node-classification and seven edge-prediction benchmarks show that similar marginal coverage can conceal substantially different local reliability. Our proposed GLocalCP maintains coverage near the target while consistently reducing graph-local calibration error. Uncertainty scaling in GLocalCP further reduces prediction-set size across all evaluated node and edge datasets while leaving marginal coverage essentially unchanged.

</details>


### [116] [Finite-Sample Convergence in Networked Average Reward MARL: Decentralization Pitfalls and Entropy Remedies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04072)
*Yizhou Zhang,Yashaswini Murthy,Laixi Shi,Adam Vierman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究网络化多智能体强化学习中的分散式平均奖励优化，证明局部信息下通常难以保证收敛至全局最优；提出的演员—评论家算法可有限样本收敛至近似驻点，而加入固定状态熵正则化后可获得更强的全局收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络中的智能体只能利用自身及其有限跳邻域的信息进行更新，却需要共同优化全网奖励。各智能体独立优化策略以及对全局状态的部分可观测，使分散式学习面临根本困难，因此需要刻画其可达的收敛保证，并探索改善全局优化性质的方法。

Method: 首先分析局部信息条件下的平均奖励多智能体强化学习，给出无法保证收敛至全局最优联合策略的不可能性结果。随后提出基于线性函数逼近的分散式演员—评论家算法，并分析其有限样本收敛性质；进一步在目标函数中加入固定状态熵正则化，以改变目标的优化景观。

Result: 不可能性结果表明，在所研究的分散式设置中，通常只能为策略优化提供驻点层面的保证。所提出的算法可在有限样本下收敛到梯度范数较小的近似驻点；加入固定状态熵正则化后，算法可获得更强的全局收敛保证。

Conclusion: 局部信息、智能体各自优化以及部分可观测限制了分散式多智能体学习达到全局最优的能力。线性函数逼近的分散式演员—评论家方法能够提供近似驻点保证，而固定状态熵正则化有望克服部分优化障碍，增强全局收敛性。

Abstract: We study the problem of average reward Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) where agents interact within a network. Each agent in the network conducts local updates with information within its $k$-hop neighborhood and collaboratively maximizes the overall reward of the entire network. We first provide impossibility results in achieving convergence to the global optimal joint policies in our setting. These impossibility results highlight the challenges of decentralized learning with local information in multi-agent systems, namely the issues of multiagency, where each agent independently optimizes its policy, and partial observability, where agents have limited visibility into the global state. Given these challenges which indicate that decentralized policy optimization can generally be certified only up to stationarity, we provide finite-sample convergence guarantees for a decentralized actor-critic algorithm with linear function approximation that converges to an approximate stationary point with small gradient norm. To overcome such impossibility results, we further show that adding fixed-state entropy regularization reshapes the objective landscape which leads to even stronger global convergence guarantees for our proposed actor-critic algorithm.

</details>


### [117] [Computer-Use Agents Gain Little Benefit from a World Model When Conditioned on Tutorials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04073)
*Anh Nguyen,Tianyi Xu,Phat Tran,Harry Yu,Huazheng Wang,Stefan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文构建了带有多模态教程的电脑使用智能体数据集，并提出教程条件下的策略—评判器框架。实验发现，已有教程时，加入世界模型生成的未来状态对性能提升有限；主要瓶颈在于评判器对未来状态的推理和任务完成识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期电脑使用智能体结合任务相关教程、世界模型前瞻规划和多模态大语言模型评判器取得进展，但教程、评判器与世界模型各自带来的收益尚未被单独评估。论文旨在通过系统对照，明确世界模型在教程条件下是否仍有独立价值，并找出影响效果的关键因素。

Method: 论文构建了两个包含精选多模态教程的数据集，覆盖日常操作与科学任务；并提出教程条件下的策略—评判器框架：策略生成候选动作，评判器从中进行选择。为隔离世界模型的作用，作者比较不使用世界模型、使用零样本文本描述预测后续状态，以及使用返回真实未来图像的理想预言机等设置。

Result: 实验显示，在提供任务相关教程后，世界模型生成的后续状态对策略—评判器框架帮助很小，即使提供准确的未来图像也未带来明显收益。错误分析表明，主要问题不在于未来状态预测是否准确，而在于评判器能否基于未来状态进行推理，以及能否正确识别任务是否完成。

Conclusion: 教程条件下已有方法的收益，更多来自智能体对教程的理解，以及评判器从候选动作中做出选择，而非世界模型预测的状态轨迹。后续改进不应只关注更准确的后续状态预测，还需提升评判器对预测结果的推理能力和任务完成判定能力。

Abstract: Recent work has improved computer-use agents (CUAs) by combining retrieved task-relevant tutorials and look-ahead planning using world models with MLLM judges. However, the contribution of each component -- tutorials, the judge, and the world model -- to overall improvement has not been evaluated in isolation. To fill this gap, we first construct OSWorld-Tutorial and ScienceBoard-Tutorial: two datasets for CUAs with curated multimodal tutorials, covering everyday and scientific domains. We then propose RAG-PolicyJudge (RAG-PJ), a policy-judge framework for CUAs conditioned on task-specific tutorials: the policy proposes candidate actions, and the judge selects among them. To isolate the effect of world models given tutorials, we compare RAG-PJ with and without common world models: using either zero-shot text-based descriptions of next states or an oracle that returns ground-truth visual observations. We find that RAG-PJ gains little benefit from these rollouts when tutorials are present, even with perfect visual futures. This suggests prior world-model gains in tutorial-conditioned methods come mainly from tutorial understanding and the judge's ability to select the proposed actions, rather than from predicted rollouts. Error analysis reveals that the bottleneck is not rollout fidelity, but the judge's ability to reason over future states and recognize task completion. This highlights the need to improve not just next-state prediction in world models, but also reasoning over their predicted rollouts. Datasets and code are available at https://github.com/realjoenguyen/RAGPJ-official.

</details>


### [118] [RAPID: Increasing Efficiency of Internal Distillation via Randomized Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04081)
*Ryan Swift,Konstantinos Psounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种随机投影式内部蒸馏方法，无需训练额外投影矩阵即可对齐教师与学生的内部表示；在推理任务的后训练实验中，训练效率最高提升百分之二十一点一，性能与现有方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部蒸馏通过让学生模型学习教师模型的内部表示来传递知识，但教师与学生的表示维度往往不一致。现有方法通常训练额外的投影矩阵，将教师表示映射到学生空间；这些参数会增加训练成本，并在训练后被丢弃，造成效率浪费。

Method: 提出随机投影式内部蒸馏方法，依据约翰逊—林登施特劳斯引理构造高效变换，将教师表示压缩至学生的嵌入空间，并在理论上保证表示结构得到较好保留。此外，设计结合指数移动平均与同方差不确定性加权的多目标函数，用于内部蒸馏训练。

Result: 在推理任务的后训练实验中，该方法最高可提升百分之二十一点一的训练效率，同时保持与现有方法相近的性能。摘要未提供具体任务、模型规模或各项指标的详细结果。

Conclusion: 随机投影可替代需要额外训练的表示投影器，减少内部蒸馏的训练开销；结合相应的多目标优化策略，该方法在提升效率的同时能够维持具有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is a technique used to transfer the expertise of large teacher models into small, efficient student models. Recent advances have integrated the distillation of internal activations, more directly training students to learn similar representations as the teacher. One of the core challenges of internal distillation is the dimensional incompatibility between the student and teacher internal representations. Existing methods add learnable matrices to project the larger teacher activations into the smaller representation space of the student. This is a notable point of inefficiency, as learning additional parameters for the projectors increases training costs, only for them to be discarded immediately after training. We introduce RAPID (RAndomized Projection for Internal Distillation) a novel distillation methodology which eliminates the wasteful projection learning by leveraging randomized methods. RAPID utilizes an efficient transform construction based on the Johnson-Lindenstrauss lemma to compress the teacher representations into the student embedding space with strong theoretical guarantees of structural preservation. RAPID also employs a combination of exponential moving average (EMA) and homoscedastic uncertainty weighting in a novel multi-objective function designed for internal distillation. Our experiments in post-training for reasoning demonstrate that RAPID increases training efficiency by as much as 21.1% while maintaining performance comparable to existing methods.

</details>


### [119] [Follow the Features: SAE Feature-Guided Data-Efficient Instruction-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04085)
*Ryan Swift,Konstantinos Psounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“跟随特征”数据筛选方法，利用稀疏自编码器识别训练数据中的重要特征，并据此挑选少量且多样的指令样本；实验显示该方法在百条样本预算下表现有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调往往需要从大量原始数据中筛选出少量有效样本，但关键难点是判断哪些数据特征值得保留。论文希望借助可解释的特征信号，提升小规模数据集的筛选效率，并让筛选过程能够复用于不同的目标模型。

Method: 方法先利用稀疏自编码器提取并表征训练数据特征，再依据特征在整个数据集上的激活统计量为特征排序。随后，根据样本对高排名特征的表达程度为样本评分，并通过特征重采样鼓励所选样本覆盖更多样的特征。用于筛选的语言模型与稀疏自编码器组合独立于后续微调模型，因此可在不同目标模型间复用。

Result: 在两个模型与数据集组合上，固定训练预算为一百条样本、序列长度为二百五十六个词元时，该方法的表现与格雷普和艾斯二艾尔具有竞争力，但不同设置下的相对排名有所变化。在通义千问三代八十亿参数模型使用羊驼数据训练的实验中，该方法在由基于羊驼评测的胜率与开放大语言模型排行榜表现构成的调和平均指标上，比全量数据训练高一点五九个百分点；不过，全量训练的排行榜得分仍更高。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，稀疏自编码器的特征统计量可作为指令微调数据筛选的有效信号，尤其适用于有限训练预算下构建小型数据集。不过，方法效果会随模型和数据集设置变化，且在部分单项指标上并未超过全量训练。

Abstract: Selecting small, effective instruction-tuning datasets requires identifying which aspects of the original data to retain. We introduce Follow the Features (FtF), a data-selection method that uses sparse autoencoder (SAE) features to characterize training data and guide subset construction. FtF ranks features using dataset-level activation statistics, scores samples by their expression of highly ranked features, and incorporates feature resampling to encourage diversity. The LLM-SAE pair used for selection is independent of the model being fine-tuned, enabling reuse across target models. We evaluate FtF on two model-dataset settings at a training budget of 100 samples with a fixed sequence length of 256 tokens. FtF achieves performance competitive with GRAPE and S2L, with relative rankings varying across settings. On Qwen 3 8B trained on Alpaca, FtF exceeds full-dataset training by 1.59 percentage points in the harmonic mean of an AlpacaEval-based win rate and Open LLM Leaderboard performance, although full-dataset training retains a higher Leaderboard score. These results support SAE feature statistics as a useful signal for data-efficient instruction tuning.

</details>


### [120] [ScaleTraj: Scale-Decomposed Human Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04097)
*Yulin Zeng,Yiheng Zhao,Jun Yan,Yuhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，许多个人轨迹生成方法在汇总到群体层面后，无法复现真实出行距离的重尾分布；为此提出按空间尺度预测移动的生成模型，在两个数据集上缩小了这一差距并保持数据逼真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人层面的移动轨迹对用户行为应用至关重要，但现有方法难以同时保证个体轨迹与群体统计规律的真实性。尤其是自下而上的生成方法，常无法在汇总后复现出行距离的截断幂律重尾分布。论文将这种群体层面的出行距离偏差称为“群体出行距离差距”，并试图揭示其普遍性及成因。

Method: 论文系统评估了多种基于模型和数据驱动的轨迹生成方法，以检验上述差距是否普遍存在。随后提出“尺度保真度画像”，按空间尺度分解并量化不同尺度上的出行分配偏差。基于分析结果，论文设计了一种自回归变换器，将位置预测拆分为两个环节：先选择移动尺度，再在该尺度条件下选择目的地网格。

Result: 评估结果表明，群体出行距离差距存在于多种轨迹生成范式中；尺度保真度画像还显示，出行距离分布不匹配伴随着不同空间尺度间出行分配的系统性误差。在两个数据集上的实验中，所提出的方法相较于基于模型和数据驱动的基线方法缩小了该差距，同时保持了生成数据的保真度。

Conclusion: 论文强调，生成逼真的个体轨迹并不必然带来真实的群体移动统计，空间尺度上的出行分配是连接两者的重要因素。显式分解尺度选择与目的地选择，有助于改善群体层面的出行距离分布，为兼顾个体与群体保真度的轨迹生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Individual-level trajectories are essential for applications that rely on user-level mobility behavior. Existing mobility-generation approaches face a fundamental tension between individual-level and population-level statistical fidelity: top-down models reproduce aggregate mobility statistics but struggle to guarantee realistic individual trajectories, while bottom-up generators synthesize individual trajectories yet often fail to preserve key population-level mobility regularities after aggregation. In particular, many bottom-up generated populations fail to reproduce the displacement distribution, whose heavy tail is well summarized by a truncated power law. We formalize this phenomenon as the \textbf{Population-Level Displacement Gap}. To investigate its prevalence, we conduct a systematic evaluation of representative model-driven and data-driven trajectory generators. Our results provide evidence that the Population-Level Displacement Gap is prevalent across diverse generation paradigms.
