To diagnose this gap, we introduce the \textbf{Scale Fidelity Profile (SFP)}, a scale-decomposed metric that quantifies discrepancies in trip allocations across spatial scales. SFP indicates that population-level displacement mismatches co-occur with systematic errors in how trips are allocated across spatial scales. Motivated by this observation, we develop \textbf{ScaleTraj}, an autoregressive Transformer that decomposes location prediction into a Scale Selection Head that decides the movement scale and a Scale-Aware Cell Selection Head that predicts the destination conditioned on the selected scale. Experiments on two datasets show that ScaleTraj reduces the Population-Level Displacement Gap versus model-driven and data-driven baselines, while preserving fidelity. Our code is available at https://github.com/SCU-TrustworthyAI/ScaleTraj.

</details>


### [121] [When Strong Reasoners Join the Pool: Small Union Premiums and Costly Runaway Generations in LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04098)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，在三种模型及固定采样协议下，多模型联合调用带来的“至少一个答案正确”收益仅为0.215至0.588个百分点，明显低于先前研究报告的3.5个百分点；结果高度依赖模型池与评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路由基准常用事后预言式上限评估路由器：只要某个模型曾答对，就视为该题可解。但在随机解码下，这一指标混合了两种不同收益：每题事先选择一个模型所能达到的上限，以及调用多个模型后保留任一正确答案的额外收益。先前小型开源模型研究测得后者为3.5个百分点，但当前推理模型加入后该收益有多大尚不清楚。

Method: 研究者在单张英伟达B300显卡上，按预先固定的协议生成并评测五百道数学题基准的全部题目；对每题、每个模型使用三十个随机种子样本。模型包括一个七百亿参数指令模型，以及英伟达的九十亿参数推理模型和四百九十亿参数推理模型；后者由同一七百亿参数模型经神经架构搜索衍生而来。采用厂商采样设置、六万五千五百三十六个词元上限、固定数学验证评分器，并以追加式记录保存尝试结果，共评估四万五千个回答。

Result: 两种推理模型准确率均为96.1%，高于基线模型的76.2%。所有双模型和三模型组合的经验联合调用收益为0.215至0.588个百分点；相对于始终调用九十亿参数模型，基于经验数据的模型选择上限仅高1.27个百分点。将该模型重复采样三次，其至少一次答对的经验概率与三个模型各调用一次几乎相同。仅有31个回答达到词元上限，其中25个出现严格周期性尾段；不过，这些截断回答在某模型输出长度平方代理指标中的占比最高达60.2%。长文本生成也使原计划加入的第四个模型无法及时完成评测。

Conclusion: 在本研究的模型池与协议下，多模型并行调用带来的额外正确率收益很小，重复采样单个强推理模型已能取得近似效果。但这一结果不能脱离实验条件推广：模型、池规模和协议均与先前研究不同。因此，报告预言式上限差距时，应同时说明模型池、评测协议、每个条件的采样次数及解码预算。

Abstract: Routing benchmarks often judge a router against a hindsight oracle that counts a query as solved whenever any model's recorded answer is correct, but under stochastic decoding that oracle mixes two different objects: the clairvoyant ceiling of committing to one model per query and the premium of calling several models and keeping any correct answer. A companion study measured this premium at 3.5 percentage points (pp) for 11 small open models on MATH-500; how large it is once current reasoning models enter the pool was unknown. We measure it with a fixed-protocol, pre-committed generation on a single NVIDIA B300: all 500 MATH-500 problems, 30 seeded samples per problem, Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and NVIDIA's Nemotron Nano 9B v2 and Nemotron Super 49B v1.5 (a NAS derivative of the same 70B) under vendor sampling, a 65,536-token cap, a pinned Math-Verify scorer, and an append-only attempt ledger, for 45,000 scored responses. The two reasoning models both reach 96.1% accuracy against 76.2% for the 70B baseline. The empirical product-coupling premium is 0.215-0.588 pp across all two- and three-model pools; the empirical plug-in selection headroom over always calling Nano is 1.27 pp; and resampling Nano three times gives practically the same empirical any-correct rate as calling all three models once. Only 31 responses reached the cap, 25 of them with exactly periodic tails, yet the capped responses account for up to 60.2% of a model's output-length-squared proxy, and long generations made a fourth planned model, Llama-3.1-8B, too slow to finish. The premium is far below the companion's 3.5 pp, but models, pool size and protocol changed together; an oracle gap should therefore be reported with the pool, the protocol, the samples per cell and the decoding budget that produced it.

</details>


### [122] [ModelBlaster: Closing the Performance Gap through Learning-Augmented Agents Across Models, Kernels and Hardware Backends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04104)
*Shengjun Kris Dong,Dima Nikiforov,Sahil Modi,Sana Damani,Edward C Lin,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可跨硬件复用优化经验的模型与内核联合优化框架，在英伟达、高通及基于精简指令集的向量处理器上均取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习编译器通常只在预设的调度和微内核空间中搜索；基于大语言模型的优化方法虽能探索不同算法与硬件专用技巧，却往往局限于单一编程接口或加速器，偏重内核优化，缺少模型级变换，也难以跨硬件复用经验。因此，需要一种能够统一优化内核与完整模型、并积累和迁移优化经验的方案。

Method: 提出模型爆破框架，采用记忆增强的上下文强化学习。其核心是可在推理过程中持续更新的知识库，将性能状态与可采取的动作、执行结果及约束关联起来，并通过基于演员—评论家机制的文本梯度下降更新策略。模型级优化器利用内核级知识指导整模型决策。不同硬件共享同一优化循环与知识结构，仅在代码降级和性能测量环节适配目标平台，覆盖英伟达图形处理器、高通图形处理器及精简指令集向量核心。

Result: 在英伟达图形处理器上，相较于深度学习框架基线，框架在内核基准测试第一级、第二级和第三级分别取得一点八五倍、五点五八倍和五点一八倍的几何平均加速，在另一执行基准的第一、二级取得一点六九倍加速。在高通图形处理器上，相较于厂商软件开发套件加速五点八倍，相较于开源机器学习编译器加速一点七倍。在精简指令集向量核心上，针对七十个内核基准问题，相较于带有高性能计算库的轻量级推理框架加速二点七倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将持久化经验记忆、内核级与模型级优化相结合，可在异构硬件上实现有效的代码优化，并提升优化知识跨内核、模型及硬件目标复用的能力。该框架通过统一的优化机制适配多类加速器，展现了超越单一硬件或单一优化层级的潜力。

Abstract: Optimizing accelerator code across hardware architectures is difficult. ML compilers search predefined spaces of schedules and micro-kernels, while LLM-guided optimization can explore alternative algorithms, operator decompositions, and hardware-specific techniques. However, existing LLM agents typically target a single programming interface, toolchain, or accelerator family, focus on kernel-level optimization without model-level transforms, and offer limited reuse of experience across hardware targets.
  We present ModelBlaster, a Memory-Augmented In-Context Reinforcement Learning (MAIC-RL) framework for optimizing data-parallel kernels and whole models across heterogeneous SIMD and SIMT accelerators. Its mutable policy is a persistent knowledge base updated at inference time by Actor-Critic Textual Gradient Descent (AC-TGD). The knowledge base maps performance states to actions, outcomes, and constraints, enabling experience reuse across kernels, models, and hardware targets. A model-level optimizer uses kernel-level knowledge to inform whole-model decisions. The same optimization loop and knowledge schema support CUDA on NVIDIA GPUs, OpenCL on Qualcomm GPUs, and RVV intrinsics on RISC-V vector cores, with only lowering and measurement layers specific to each target.
  Compared with PyTorch, ModelBlaster achieves geometric-mean speedups of 1.85x, 5.58x, and 5.18x on KernelBench Levels 1, 2, and 3, and 1.69x on SOL-ExecBench Levels 1 and 2 on a B200 GPU. On Qualcomm Adreno GPUs, it achieves 5.8x over the vendor SDK and 1.7x over Apache TVM; on RISC-V vector cores, it achieves 2.7x over ExecuTorch with XNNPACK across 70 KernelBench problems.

</details>


### [123] [Structure, Dimensionality, and Distribution Shape - What Makes Pretraining Data Transferable, and Why the Input Pipeline Decides Which](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04106)
*Sangyoon Bae,Jiook Cha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“迁移不变性原则”：预训练语料哪些属性影响迁移，取决于模型如何变换输入，而非数据属于哪种模态。研究据此构建可控合成数据生成器，并在时间序列与神经信号任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实预训练数据可能稀缺、昂贵或涉及隐私，但合成数据能否替代真实数据，取决于合成语料应保留哪些特征。论文指出，结构、维度和数值分布形状并非总是同等重要；模型的输入变换会决定哪些属性对迁移有影响，因此需要在训练前明确应合成什么。

Method: 论文提出迁移不变性原则，并构建“迁移锻造”生成器，可分别设定合成数据群体的结构、维度和数值分布形状。作者通过两种输入处理方式进行检验：一种对每个输入窗口标准化，另一种不进行标准化；随后比较不同合成语料与真实语料的预训练效果。

Result: 在对输入窗口进行标准化的时间序列任务中，数值分布形状基本不起作用，结构设定决定迁移效果；同等规模下，合成预训练可匹配真实语料，扩大规模后则能超过真实语料。在不进行标准化的钙荧光神经信号任务中，数值分布形状成为关键约束，分布匹配的合成语料能够替代真实预训练数据，且无需使用任何真实神经活动数据。固定模型与数据、仅切换输入标准化方式后，分布形状操控的影响变化达五点三倍。

Conclusion: 决定合成语料应匹配哪些属性的，不是数据模态本身，而是模型的输入处理流程。该原则为预训练前设计合成数据提供了依据，也表明在真实数据难以获取的场景中，可针对模型需求制造有效的预训练语料。

Abstract: What makes a pretraining corpus transferable? We describe a corpus by three properties: its structure, its dimensionality, and the distribution shape of each unit's values. We propose the Transfer-Invariance Principle: the same property can be irrelevant to transfer under one input transform and decisive under another, so the model's input transform, not the data modality, decides which properties can matter. To test it we build TransferForge, a generator that places a synthetic population at any prescribed structure, dimensionality, and distribution shape. KernelSynth, the synthetic prior of Chronos, cannot, because it draws independent series. When the model standardizes each input window (PatchTST on UCR), the distribution shape is inert and the structural setting governs. Synthetic pretraining then matches a real corpus of the same size and exceeds it once scaled. When the model encodes calcium fluorescence signals without normalization (POYO Plus), the shape becomes a governing constraint, and a shape-matched synthetic corpus replaces real pretraining with zero real neural activity. Flipping only the input normalization, with the model and data fixed, changes the effect of a shape manipulation by a factor of 5.3, so the pipeline, not the modality, sets the switch. The principle yields a recipe that chooses what to synthesize before any training. Pretraining data then becomes something to manufacture for the model, most valuably where real data is scarce, expensive, or private.

</details>


### [124] [Separating Common and Unique Directions for Data-Free Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04108)
*Shiting Wang,Yingjie Zou,Ke li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需数据的模型合并预处理方法：将任务向量分解为跨任务共享的共同方向与任务特有方向，分别处理后再合并，从而提升多种算术合并方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算术合并方法直接在参数空间处理任务向量，并对所有方向使用同一种逐元素规则，忽略了不同方向的可合并性差异。研究发现，任务向量在共享方向基底中呈现两类结构：跨任务共享、离散度较低的共同主体，以及由特定任务主导、离散度较高且能量占比突出的尾部。

Method: 提出共同—独特子空间分解方法。该方法在合并前将各任务向量拆分为共同方向成分和独特方向残差；共同成分交由下游算术合并算子处理，独特残差则分别保留。整个过程无需额外训练或数据。

Result: 实验涵盖十一种基础模型、四个模型系列及四种算术合并算子。在四十四组模型与算子组合中，该方法提升了四十二组，最高较对应基线提升百分之二十二点五。

Conclusion: 任务向量包含可区分的共同与任务特有结构，统一处理所有方向可能限制模型合并效果。显式分解并分别处理这些成分，能广泛提升算术模型合并的性能。

Abstract: Model merging combines multiple fine-tuned models without additional training, and arithmetic methods have emerged as a simple yet effective way to merge models without data. However, existing arithmetic methods treat task vectors in parameter space and apply a single element-wise rule to directions that can differ in mergeability. In a shared directional basis, we find a consistent two-regime structure: a low-dispersion common bulk shared across tasks and a high-dispersion task-dominated tail that carries disproportionate energy. These findings motivate Common--Unique Subspace Decomposition (CUSD), a data-free pre-merging method that separates task vectors into common and unique directional components, merges the common components with downstream arithmetic operator, and retains unique residuals separately. Experiments across $11$ base models, four model families, and four arithmetic-based merging operators show that CUSD improves 42 of 44 model--operator combinations and achieves up to 22.5% improvement over the corresponding baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/stwang666/CUSD

</details>


### [125] [Decomposing Rank Reversals under Temporal Nonstationarity: Alignment, Aggressiveness, and Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04116)
*Deying Song,Xueying Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文建立统一理论框架，解释时间分布漂移下模型排名为何随训练窗口变化，并指出误校准的预测激进程度是排名反转的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间非平稳会使不同模型对历史记忆长度的敏感性各异，导致模型在不同训练期限下的表现排名反转。既有研究分别讨论自适应回看窗口、有效模型复杂度和预测评估，缺少解释排名反转结构机制的统一框架。

Method: 将样本外决定系数分解为目标对齐、预测激进程度的校准误差和预测不稳定性三部分；据此刻画模型复杂度的阶段边界，推导局部漂移条件下岭回归的最优记忆长度闭式方程，并通过替代界将理论保证推广至复杂模型架构。研究还在大规模金融面板数据上，对十四种线性、树模型和神经网络架构进行基准比较。

Result: 理论分析与实证基准测试表明，模型排名反转与误校准的预测激进程度可预测地激增有关；该框架为时间漂移下的泛化表现提供了机制性解释。摘要未报告具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 模型在时间漂移环境中的优劣取决于其对历史记忆的敏感性及预测激进程度是否校准。所提出的分解与最优记忆分析，为理解和诊断随训练期限变化的模型表现提供了统一理论工具。

Abstract: Under temporal nonstationarity, model performance rankings reverse across training horizons due to heterogeneous sensitivities to memory. While existing literature addresses adaptive lookbacks, effective model complexity, and empirical forecast evaluation in isolation, no unified framework explains the structural mechanics driving these rank reversals. We bridge this gap by deriving a model-agnostic decomposition of out-of-sample $R^2$ into target alignment, miscalibrated aggressiveness, and predictive instability. Within this framework, we mathematically characterize complexity phase boundaries, establish closed-form optimal-memory equations for ridge regression under local drift, and extend these guarantees to complex model architectures via surrogate bounds. We validate these theoretical dynamics by benchmarking 14 linear, tree-based, and neural architectures across large-scale financial panels. In doing so, our framework demonstrates that empirical model rank reversals are driven by predictable surges in miscalibrated aggressiveness, providing a rigorous mechanistic diagnosis of generalization under temporal shift.

</details>


### [126] [LoopKV: Loop-Aware KV Cache Compression for Serving Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04137)
*Tianle Zhou,Yang Zhang,Tianyu Wu,Zhuohan Wang,William Nixon,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种按循环与层动态分配键值缓存精度的方法，在减少缓存占用的同时提高模型服务吞吐量，并基本保持原始精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式变换器通过反复使用同一模块提升准确率，却会为各循环分别保存键值缓存。缓存占用限制了可同时处理的请求数，降低重复读取模型权重所带来的收益。统一量化精度或按循环使用固定规则，无法适应不同循环、不同层对量化和缓存共享的敏感度差异；跳过低注意力值则可能使误差逐轮累积。

Method: 方法先离线分析每个循环与层的缓存敏感度及共享误差，再为各单元选择四位存储、二位存储，或复用最后一轮的键值缓存。规划器在预测损伤和存储量预算内尽量减少读取量。解码时先读取四位值的高两位，仅在该循环的注意力权重要求较高精度时再读取低两位。论文还将执行流程整合为一个融合算子，并接入推理服务框架。

Result: 在单张图形处理器上运行十四亿参数的乌罗模型时，面向内存优化的方案比统一四位量化少存储百分之三十五的键值缓存。在最大长度为一万六千个词元、且受延迟服务目标约束时，其有效吞吐量达到统一四位量化方案的一点七至二点二倍。在六项公开基准测试中，该方法的平均表现均未显著低于半精度基准。

Conclusion: 研究表明，循环与层级别的离线精度规划能够更有效地权衡缓存存储、读取量与输出损伤。结合按需读取低位信息的解码机制，可在基本维持模型质量的前提下显著提升内存效率与服务吞吐量。

Abstract: Looped transformers apply a shared block several times per token to improve accuracy without adding parameters. For models such as Ouro that keep a separate key-value (KV) cache per loop, cache memory limits the batch size that amortizes repeated weight reads. Uniform-bit quantization and fixed rules per loop do not allocate precision according to each layer's sensitivity in each loop. Measurements on Ouro show that quantization sensitivity varies across (loop, layer) cells, and the sum of individual cell damages approximates the overall output damage. Sharing damage also varies by cell, with later loops generally tolerating sharing better. Skipping low-attention values to save reads produces errors that compound across loops. LoopKV uses a one-time offline profile to choose 4-bit storage, 2-bit storage or reuse of the final loop's KV for each cell. The planner minimizes reads under budgets for predicted damage and storage. During decoding, each loop reads the high 2 bits of each 4-bit value and fetches the low bits only where its own attention weights require 4-bit precision. A single fused kernel in vLLM executes the plan. Serving Ouro-1.4B on one RTX PRO 6000, the memory-optimized LoopKV-M plan stores 35% less KV than uniform 4-bit quantization (KIVI-4) on the same fused kernel and CUDA graph. It sustains 1.7-2.2x KIVI-4's goodput under a latency service-level objective with a 16K-token maximum length. None of its averages on six public benchmarks falls significantly below BF16's.

</details>


### [127] [RGDM: Robust Graph Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04146)
*Yancheng Wang,Ping Li,Dongfang Sun,Yingzhen Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向受对抗攻击图数据的稳健图扩散模型，可生成高质量的带标签节点与边结构，并通过扩充图提升半监督节点分类的抗攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有节点级图扩散方法能够在图中合成节点和边，但并未针对对抗污染图学习稳健的生成器，因此难以可靠地产生干净、可融入原图的合成结构。

Method: 稳健图扩散模型由稳健图自编码器和潜空间扩散模型构成。方法采用边筛选机制，并对潜在特征施加低秩正则化，以降低对抗污染的影响，生成可融入给定图的带标签节点与边结构。

Result: 实验表明，将生成的合成结构与受攻击的原图合并后，用扩充图训练的图神经网络在半监督节点分类任务中，对多种图对抗攻击表现出显著增强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合稳健图表示学习与潜空间扩散生成，可从受对抗攻击的图中合成高质量结构，并用于改善图神经网络的抗攻击性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models represent a powerful class of generative models known for their solid theoretical foundations and remarkable performance across diverse tasks and domains. Existing node level diffusion methods synthesize nodes and edges within a graph, but are not designed to learn a robust generator from adversarially corrupted graphs. In this study, we introduce the Robust Graph Diffusion Model (RGDM), designed to generate labeled synthetic graph structures consisting of nodes and edges that integrate seamlessly into a given graph. The RGDM consists of a Robust Graph Autoencoder (RGAE) and a Latent Diffusion Model (LDM). Leveraging an edge selection mechanism and an innovative low-rank regularization on the latent feature, the RGDM produces clean and high-quality synthetic graph structures, even when trained on graphs subject to adversarial attacks. Comprehensive experimental evaluations reveal that Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) trained on the augmented graph, which is formed by merging the original attacked graph with the synthetic graph structures, exhibit significantly improved robustness against various graph adversarial attacks in the context of semi-supervised node classification. The code of the RGDM is available at https://github.com/Statistical-Deep-Learning/RGDM.

</details>


### [128] [On the Feature Evolution of Temporal Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04158)
*Shangzhe Li,Dechen Zhang,Difan Zou,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究时序差分学习如何在有限宽度神经网络中形成并传递预测特征。理论分析表明，同时更新隐层与读出层优于仅更新读出层；即使后续阶段的价值估计不完善，也能为更早阶段提供有效监督，并据此得到有限预算下的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序差分学习使用由不断变化的价值估计生成的自举目标，但在神经网络更新不完整时，预测特征如何形成、跨阶段传播并最终改善策略表现，仍缺乏清晰理解。论文旨在连接神经表示学习的机制与强化学习中的有限样本、有限计算预算表现。

Method: 作者在一类具有未知二次结构、观测维度为 d、时域为 H 的平滑随机马尔可夫决策过程中，分析有限宽度的两层整流线性单元价值网络。首先研究一次具有信息量的时序差分更新，考察隐层权重与读出层同步更新相对于仅更新读出层的效果；随后追踪多个阶段之间耦合的神经网络更新，每次目标复制只执行一步随机梯度更新，不假设中间的贝尔曼回归已被完全求解。最后，在总回报有界的带符号动作问题族上，分析先探索、后承诺的算法。

Result: 理论结果显示，有信息量的时序差分更新能够捕获二次型贝尔曼信号，并形成具有有界预测读出的表示。同步更新相较于匹配的仅读出层更新，在有限样本预测风险上严格更优，其总体二次响应大 2(d+1) 倍。即使学得的后续阶段价值估计并不完美，也能为更早的零奖励阶段提供有效监督；预测误差暂态以 exp[-Ω(K/(Hd))] 的速度衰减，另有与动作无关的偏移、采样误差及有限宽度误差。在构造的问题族上，使用 K=Õ(Hd) 次全局更新，算法可在 T 个回合内达到 Õ(d³H^(1/3)T^(2/3)) 的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 论文从理论上说明，时序差分自举不仅能训练价值读出层，也能在隐层与读出层并行更新时塑造有预测能力的表示；这种表示还可通过不完全的阶段间更新向前传播，并转化为有界计算预算下的策略性能保证。结论目前建立在特定构造的问题族与分析设定之上，适用范围仍需进一步检验。

Abstract: Temporal-difference (TD) learning trains neural representations using bootstrapped targets generated by evolving value estimates, yet how predictive features emerge and propagate under incomplete neural updates remains poorly understood. We investigate this problem using finite-width two-layer ReLU critics on a constructed family of smooth stochastic horizon-$H$ MDPs with unknown quadratic structure and $d$-dimensional observations. We first show that an informative TD update acquires the quadratic Bellman signal and yields a representation admitting a bounded predictive readout. The simultaneous hidden-weight and head update achieves a strict finite-sample prediction-risk improvement over matched head-only SGD, with a $2(d+1)$-fold larger population quadratic response. We then establish that an imperfect learned continuation can already provide informative supervision to an earlier zero-reward stage. By tracking coupled neural updates with one SGD step per target copy, without requiring intermediate Bellman regressions to be solved, we obtain a uniform prediction-error transient $\exp[-Ω(K/(Hd))]$, up to action-independent shifts and explicit sampling and finite-width errors. On the constructed signed-action MDP family whose total reward is bounded by 1, we show that an exploration-then-commit algorithm can achieve $\widetilde{O}(d^3H^{1/3}T^{2/3})$ regret over $T$ episodes, using $K=\widetilde{O}(Hd)$ global updates. Our analysis connects bootstrapped feature acquisition, concurrent neural refinement, and finite-budget policy performance.

</details>


### [129] [Say the Same, Act Differently: Diagnosing and Steering Text-Action Divergence in Reasoning VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04166)
*Zihao Feng,Qingzhao Zhang,Chunyu Xia,Bo Yu,Z. Morley Mao,Ramesh Govindan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视觉扰动可在不改变模型生成理由文本的情况下显著改变其物理动作；因此，理由原文不变并不能证明动作安全，安全监测必须直接检查预测动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能系统常借助自然语言理由供监督者监控安全，但既有攻击通常会篡改理由文本，留下可见迹象。本文关注更隐蔽的风险：视觉输入可能改变实际动作，却让理由逐字不变，从而使仅依赖文本的监测失效。

Method: 作者在驾驶与操作任务模型共享的文本／动作接口上，提出“文本中性动作子空间”诊断，寻找对动作敏感、同时与文本损失梯度近乎正交的隐状态方向；两种模型中的梯度重叠分别仅为百分之一点八七和百分之五点二八。随后沿该方向引导隐状态或视觉扰动，并与文本梯度方向及未引导攻击进行比较，同时评估动作变化、文本漂移和碰撞情况。

Result: 在相同隐状态扰动幅度下，沿文本中性方向的干预对动作的影响比文本梯度方向大四点六四至四十七点八倍；文本梯度方向造成的文本漂移则大二十一点二至二百七十七倍。驾驶任务的154个带障碍物标注场景中，文本中性引导攻击在全图扰动和局部前向摄像头区域扰动下，分别有百分之五十二点六和百分之三十五点一引发新的碰撞，未引导攻击仅为百分之四点五五。相较文本保持基线，引导方法将理由逐字保留率从百分之八十三提高到百分之八十九点五，并在碰撞率相近时使教师强制文本漂移减少百分之六十八点四。操作任务中，文本漂移减少百分之四十点五，百分之八十五的样例保留完全相同的回答，同时动作片段偏移一點七三个单位。

Conclusion: 视觉输入中的扰动能够绕开理由文本，直接操纵模型动作；因此，理由看似合理且逐字未变，并不足以作为安全凭证。面向具备推理能力的视觉语言动作系统，监测机制应直接验证预测动作，而不能只审查生成的理由。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language-action (VLA) models pair physical control with natural-language rationales that supervisors can read to monitor embodied safety. Prior attacks on VLA reasoning corrupt the rationale itself and leave visible evidence in the text. We show that visual perturbations can instead alter the physical action while keeping the generated rationale word-for-word identical. At the shared text/action interface of Alpamayo for driving and InstructVLA for manipulation, our text-neutral action-subspace (TNAS) diagnostic shows that the locally most action-sensitive direction is nearly orthogonal to the active text-loss gradients, with 1.87% and 5.28% norm overlap. At equal hidden-state perturbation norms across both models, interventions along this text-neutral residual shift actions 4.64x to 47.8x more than text-gradient perturbations, whereas text-gradient perturbations induce 21.2x to 277x more text drift. Steering visual perturbations along this direction reaches the same decoupling from the image. Across 154 obstacle-annotated driving scenes in Alpamayo, TNAS-guided attacks induce a new collision under a word-for-word identical rationale in 52.6% of scenes under full-image perturbations and 35.1% under a localized forward-camera patch, compared with 4.55% for unguided action attacks. Relative to a text-preserving baseline, TNAS guidance raises exact rationale preservation from 83.0% to 89.5% and cuts teacher-forced text drift by 68.4% at a comparable collision rate. In InstructVLA, TNAS guidance cuts text drift by 40.5% and preserves the exact response on 85.0% of examples alongside a 1.73-unit action-chunk shift. An unchanged rationale therefore cannot certify a safe action, so monitors for reasoning VLAs must verify the predicted action channel directly.

</details>


### [130] [What Does 4-Bit Microscaling Cost a Wearable Activity Recognizer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04185)
*Megan Healy,Hossein Valavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较两种四位块缩放格式在可穿戴运动识别模型中的影响。结果显示，权重和激活均量化为四位时，NVFP4的精度明显优于MXFP4；主要差异来自缩放因子的选择方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备上的动作识别需要减少模型存储与内存访问，因此低精度计算具有吸引力。尽管MXFP4与NVFP4已在通用深度学习和大型语言模型中受到评估，其对小型可穿戴传感器模型的代价尚不清楚。

Method: 研究使用包含159,492个参数的一维卷积神经网络，根据手腕加速度数据识别四类篮球动作。采用按球员分组的五折交叉验证，确保测试球员未出现在训练中，并逐折比较数值格式。除实测的半精度浮点与八位整数模型外，还模拟四位权重量化及权重、激活同时量化，并通过两种块大小和三种缩放规则进行消融分析。

Result: 全精度模型的宏平均F1为69.26%；实测半精度浮点和八位整数模型的下降均不超过0.16个百分点。仅量化权重时，MXFP4下降1.45个百分点，NVFP4下降0.72个百分点；权重和激活均量化为四位时，二者分别下降5.14和0.75个百分点，NVFP4在全部五个测试折中领先。该优势在两种替代模拟器设置下仍然存在，领先幅度为3.49至4.40个百分点。消融分析表明，缩放因子选择是MXFP4精度损失的主要来源：向上取整缩放因子可恢复3.18至3.63个百分点，改用八位浮点缩放因子又可提升0.84至1.20个百分点；块大小的影响较小。

Conclusion: 在这项小型可穿戴动作识别任务中，NVFP4比MXFP4更能保留模型精度，尤其是在权重和激活均采用四位表示时。研究指出，MXFP4的损失主要与向下取整导致缩放因子过小、进而使较大数值被截断有关；改进缩放策略可能显著缩小差距。

Abstract: Wearable sports sensors can recognize actions on the device itself, where lower-precision numbers reduce model storage and memory traffic. Two 4-bit block-scaled formats, MXFP4 from the Open Compute Project, introduced in 2023, and NVFP4 from NVIDIA, introduced in 2025, are drawing interest. Published evaluations cover general deep-learning benchmarks and large language models; to our knowledge, nobody has yet measured what they cost a small wearable-sensor model.
  We train a 159,492-parameter 1D CNN to recognize four basketball actions from wrist-accelerometer data in Hang-Time HAR and test it on players it has never seen, using five-fold cross-validation grouped by player. Every comparison between number formats is made fold by fold.
  In full precision the model reaches 69.26\% macro-F1. Measured TFLite FP16 and INT8 models lose at most 0.16 percentage points. In simulation, 4-bit weights cost 1.45 points with MXFP4 and 0.72 points with NVFP4. When activations are quantized to 4 bits as well, MXFP4 loses 5.14 points and NVFP4 only 0.75, and NVFP4 comes out ahead in all five held-out-player folds.
  The ranking survives two alternative simulator settings, with NVFP4 leading by 3.49 to 4.40 points. A $2 \times 3$ ablation with 4-bit weights and activations (two block sizes and three scale rules) points to scale selection as the main source of MXFP4's loss. The reference MXFP4 conversion we simulate rounds the scale down, which can pick scales that are too small, so many blocks clip their largest values. Rounding the scale up recovers 3.18 points with 32-value blocks and 3.63 points with 16-value blocks, and switching to an 8-bit floating-point scale, as NVFP4 does, adds another 0.84 and 1.20 points. Block size matters less. Under this round-down rule, 16-value blocks score 0.44 points below 32-value blocks in all five folds; under the other two rules there is no consistent effect.

</details>


### [131] [Where Did the Update Go? Certifying Declared Change Envelopes for Model Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04192)
*Muhammad Usama,Muhammad Saad Qadir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“声明变更范围”框架，用未标注部署流量检验模型更新是否仅在预先声明的输入范围内改变决策。常见微调方法往往在范围外产生大量变化；通过蒸馏旧模型在部署格式下的决策，可让部分更新以少量样本获得范围外泄漏率不超过百分之五的统计证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型持续更新，监管也越来越接受事先声明的更新，但证明更新只在声明范围内改变行为，与衡量整体变化幅度是不同问题。有效更新本来就应改变某些决策，因此整体分歧率或回归界限无法证明变化符合声明。论文旨在量化并认证范围外的行为变化，即“泄漏”。

Method: 开发者在训练前承诺一个输入区域，将区域外发生的决策变化定义为泄漏。由于泄漏通过比较新旧模型即可衡量，论文使用可随时有效的统计检验，在未标注流量上认证泄漏率，并推导所需样本量的上下界；同时将各次更新的证书组合起来，约束所有已声明范围之外的长期漂移。实验考察低秩适配、全参数微调、偏好优化、强化学习和知识编辑，并评估以部署格式提示蒸馏旧模型决策的锚定训练方法。

Result: 在参数规模为十五亿至七百二十亿的模型上，狭窄范围的更新普遍影响范围外决策：领域与政策类低秩适配中，更新改变的决策仅约百分之七至三十六落在声明范围内。共二百五十六个训练变体中，范围外泄漏与更新对范围外输入造成的相对熵变化高度相关，斯皮尔曼相关系数为零点九四。所测试的常见补救方式，都无法在保留大部分预期变化的同时使规则型更新获得认证。相较之下，部署格式下的旧模型决策蒸馏，使锚定低秩适配、组相对策略优化，以及低学习率全参数微调，能够用数百至数千条未标注样本认证百分之五的泄漏率；结果也在留出流量和书面回答上复现。认证仅适用于类似决策的输出；开放式聊天中，仅重新分批就会改变百分之十一至二十一的回答，因而没有任何更新获得认证。

Conclusion: 声明变更范围为评估模型更新是否越界提供了不同于整体回归测试的可统计认证框架，并可累积约束长期漂移。实验表明，常规微调很容易产生广泛的范围外影响，而蒸馏旧模型在部署格式下的决策是较有效的控制手段。不过，证书目前只适用于决策型输出，不能直接推广到开放式对话。

Abstract: Deployed language models are updated continually, and regulators increasingly accept changes that were declared in advance. Showing that an update changed behavior only where it was declared to is a different statistical problem from bounding how much it changed: a useful update is meant to change some decisions, so global disagreement and regression bounds cannot certify it. We formalize the problem with declared change envelopes. Before training, the developer commits to a region of inputs, and every decision that changes outside it counts as leakage. Leakage compares two models, so it can be certified from unlabeled traffic with anytime-valid tests whose sample cost we bound from above and below, and per-update certificates compose into a bound on lifetime drift outside all declared scopes. We measure leakage for LoRA and full fine-tuning, preference optimization, reinforcement learning, and knowledge editing on models from 1.5B to 72B parameters. Narrow updates leak widely: domain and policy LoRA updates keep only about 7-36% of the decisions they change inside the declared scope. Across 256 trained variants, leakage tracks the update's KL divergence to the incumbent on out-of-scope inputs (Spearman 0.94). None of the customary remedies we tested makes a rule-like update certifiable without giving up much of its intended change. Distilling the incumbent's decisions on unlabeled prompts in the deployment format does: anchored LoRA, GRPO, and (at a small learning rate) full fine-tuning updates certify 5% leakage from a few hundred to a few thousand unlabeled samples, and the certificates replicate on held-out traffic and on written answers. The certificates cover decision-like outputs only; on open-ended chat, re-batching alone changes 11-21% of responses and no update certifies. Code: https://github.com/Usama1002/declared-change-envelopes

</details>


### [132] [Zero-Gated Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01116)
*Basavaraj R M,Manav Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种零初始化的归纳式门控交叉注意力桥接模块，在训练初始阶段严格保持冻结语言模型的原有行为，并逐步引入多模态信息；实验显示，该方法降低了留出集损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向冻结语言模型注入非文本模态时，训练早期常出现优化不稳定，使架构改进的效果难以与短暂的训练波动区分。论文旨在设计一种能稳定起步、又可逐渐实现多模态条件化的连接机制。

Method: 提出归纳式门控交叉注意力模块，并将其接入冻结的猎鹰四百亿参数、开放羊驼三百亿参数和布鲁姆七十亿参数模型的六个解码器层。模块采用零初始化，使初始输出与原模型完全一致；训练时则通过门控逐渐调节跨注意力信息。训练期间使用的可训练适配器参数为四百零四亿零四百九十六万一千二百八十八个。

Result: 在猎鹰四百亿参数模型上训练五万九千五百步后，训练损失从三点九四零三降至零点零零零七。留出评估中，最佳门控开启检查点的损失为二点二二零一，门控关闭基线为四点三四六九，降低百分之四十八点九；平均留出损失从四点三四二三降至二点二四九八。平均门控幅度为零点零七一三，且初始化时最大输出差异为零。

Conclusion: 结果表明，零初始化门控交叉注意力能够在不扰动预训练模型初始行为的前提下逐步引入多模态条件，并在所报告的留出评估中显著降低损失。不过，摘要未说明数据集、模态类型及评估设置，且训练损失极低，因此仅凭这些结果尚不足以判断泛化能力或训练过程的普遍稳定性。

Abstract: Injecting non-text modalities into frozen language models often introduces early optimization instability, making it difficult to separate architectural gains from transient training dynamics. We introduce Inductively Gated Cross-Attention (IGCA), a zero-initialized cross-attention bridge that preserves pretrained model behavior at initialization while enabling gradual multimodal conditioning. Integrated across six decoder layers of frozen Falcon-40B, open_llama_30b, and Bloom-7b1 backbones (with 40,404,961,288 trainable adapter parameters used during training), IGCA acts as an exact algebraic no-op at initialization with a maximum output discrepancy of 0.00e+00. Over 59,500 training steps on Falcon-40B, the training loss decreases from 3.9403 to 0.0007 (with final ten step losses of [0.0001, 0.0003, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0005, 0.0011, 0.0005, 0.0006, 0.0005, 0.0007]). On held-out evaluation, the best gate-on checkpoint achieves a loss of 2.2201 compared to 4.3469 for the gate-off baseline -- a 48.9% reduction (2.1268 nats) -- while average held-out loss improves from 4.3423 to 2.2498 (1.9075 nats) with a mean gate magnitude of $\vert{}\tanh(α)\vert{} = 0.0713$.

</details>


### [133] [An Information Budget for Label-Noise Memorization, and What Statistical Complexity Tracks: A Study with Multilayer Perceptrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04199)
*Nancy Baygorrea C.,Otávio da Fonseca Martins Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究神经网络何时、在多大程度上记住标签噪声，并检验统计复杂度能否刻画这种现象。结果显示，归一化记忆信息能反映训练样本上的噪声记忆，但统计复杂度作为停止训练指标的效果有限，且结论受噪声类型影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练准确率或记忆比例虽可描述模型拟合噪声的程度，却未必能区分真正学到的信号与对错误标签的记忆。论文因此考察统计复杂度能否补充对模型预测的刻画，并识别其在训练过程中的变化规律。

Method: 研究对象为多层感知机。作者依据标准恒等式，将预测信息分解为信号、记忆和异质性，并在噪声与真实类别满足独立条件时，推导记忆项在总体层面为零；另以费诺型界联系记忆信息与训练准确率。主要实验分析手写数字数据集上的八十九次训练，预先检验十项假设；扩展实验则涵盖时尚物品数据集、成对翻转噪声及四种训练变体。研究同时比较统计复杂度的峰值时序、早停表现与识别受污染样本的能力。

Result: 在主要实验中，归一化记忆信息随训练样本上的记忆比例上升，在使用新鲜噪声标签的留出样本上则接近零，且接近费诺型界。与原始记忆比例不同，它在所述独立条件下具有为零的总体基准；机会校正也大体消除了两者差异。即使所有标签都被替换为错误类别，测试准确率降至百分之零点五，预测仍保留了关于真实类别的信息。噪声率不高于百分之四十时，平均统计复杂度通常在记忆阶段之前达到峰值；噪声率更高时，峰值落在记忆阶段内，接近训练置信度达到零点六的时期，二者难以区分。作为早停规则，它仅在低噪声下与既有规则表现相当；识别受污染样本的能力不如简单的无标签评分。扩展实验在均匀噪声下得到相似结果，但在成对翻转噪声下，复杂度峰值很早出现，通常在两轮训练之内。

Conclusion: 归一化记忆信息比单纯记忆比例更能反映预测中对标签噪声的记忆，并具有明确的总体零基准；统计复杂度则可揭示训练动态，但不能普遍充当可靠的早停或噪声样本检测指标。结论仅适用于所研究的网络结构、两个小型数据集及合成噪声，尤其需要谨慎推广至不同噪声机制。

Abstract: How much label noise has a network memorized, and when? We study a multilayer perceptron and what statistical complexity adds to a description of its predictions. Standard identities decompose predictive information into signal, memorization, and heterogeneity. The memorization term vanishes in the population under the stated noise-independence condition. A Fano-type bound relates it to training accuracies, but the memorized fraction M provides no upper bound. Across 89 MNIST runs with ten pre-specified hypotheses (five supported, four unsupported, one not testable), normalized memorization information rose with M on training samples, remained near zero on held-out samples with fresh noisy labels, and was close to its Fano bound. Unlike raw M, it has a zero population null under the independence condition and chance correction largely removed this difference. When every label was replaced by an incorrect class, test accuracy fell to 0.5%, yet predictions retained information about the true class. Mean statistical complexity peaked before memorization at corruption rates up to 40%. At higher rates, its peak fell within the memorization phase, near the epoch when mean training confidence reached 0.6 and the two could not be separated. As a stopping rule, it matched established rules only at low noise and detected corrupted samples less well than simple label-free scores. An exploratory extension of 280 runs covering Fashion-MNIST, pair-flip noise, and four training variants yielded similar results under uniform noise, but not pair-flip noise, where complexity peaked within two epochs. Conclusions are limited to this architecture, two small datasets, and synthetic noise.

</details>


### [134] [TRIAGE: Reliability- and Load-Conditioned Escalation for Two-Tier LLM Serving with a Semantic Evidence Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01174)
*Basavaraj R M,Aryan Bharat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一种依据生成后的可靠性状态与负载变化的图形处理器成本来决定是否升级模型的两级服务方法。实验发现，升级成本随请求负载显著上升，但核心可靠性信号未能识别被污染的证据；后续语义证据评估器效果较好，却不能保证所有模型组合都受益。整体端到端收益很小，主要结论是：上线前的简单门槛测试未能揭示这一无明显收益的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两级大语言模型服务可先用小模型回答，再将部分请求交给大模型，以平衡回答质量与成本。但既有基于提示词的路由器无法判断检索到的证据是否真正支持答案，也通常把升级成本视为固定值，忽略服务负载变化。本文旨在检验能否结合生成后的可靠性信息与动态计算成本，更合理地决定是否升级请求。

Method: 作者提出名为“分诊”的升级策略，以生成后的可靠性状态和随负载变化的图形处理器价格作为决策依据，并在单张加速卡上使用七十亿参数与三百二十亿参数模型进行服务测试。研究预先注册了可靠性状态识别证据污染的假设，并进一步考察语义证据评估器对错误答案的检测能力；此外，还在不同模型组合上评估级联效果、成本与端到端分配表现。

Result: 在每秒一至十个请求的负载区间，升级成本上升了二十一点九倍，但与键值缓存余量并无单调关系。预先注册的可靠性状态检测证据污染假设未获支持，检测指标仅为零点五七一；提示词专用路由器则按构造只能达到零点五。事后加入语义证据评估器后，污染检测指标升至零点八四六，在从未服务过的样本上为零点九二六；该评估器对小模型错误答案的预测能力也较强。在“较小模型到较大模型”的闭源模型组合中，级联效果仅比大型模型低约三个百分点，但接口成本降低百分之七十九点五；然而，对价格差距为二至三倍的开放模型组合，级联表现甚至低于随机路由。七十亿到三百二十亿参数模型的端到端测试仅有二点八八个百分点的留出集差距，因此分配器合理地选择不升级。

Conclusion: 本文的主要结论并非提出了普遍有效的升级策略，而是揭示了其适用边界：动态成本确有必要考虑，语义证据判断也可能改善可靠性评估，但收益取决于模型组合与成本结构。预设可靠性信号未通过关键检验，且整体端到端优势有限；上线前的门槛测试未能提前发现这一无明显收益的结果。

Abstract: Two-tier LLM serving escalates selected requests from a small model to a large one, but prompt-based routers cannot see whether retrieved evidence supports an answer, and they treat the GPU cost of an escalation as constant. We study TRIAGE, which conditions escalation on a post-generation reliability state and on a load-dependent GPU price, and we report both what held and what did not on one A40 serving Qwen2.5-7B and 32B (AWQ) under VLLM. Escalation cost rose 21.9x from 1 to 10 requests/s but showed no monotone dependence on KV-cache headroom, and a pre-registered claim that the reliability state detects corrupted evidence (AUROC>=0.70) was falsified at 0.571, although any prompt-only router scores exactly 0.500 by construction. Post hoc, a semantic evidence judge raised detection to 0.846 (0.926 on never-served items) and predicted wrong small-model answers at AUROC 0.90-0.96 on RGB and three RouterBench model pairs; the resulting cascade stayed within about 3 pp of GPT-4 at 79.5% lower API cost for GPT-3.5->GPT-4, yet fell below random routing for open-model pairs with 2-3x price ratios. End to end, the 7B->32B pair showed only a +2.88 pp held-out gap, so the allocator rightly declined to escalate and the headline is a null that a small pre-launch gate failed to expose.

</details>


### [135] [LENS: Reliability-Aware Stock Prediction via LLM-Extracted Priors and News-Driven Semantic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04217)
*Hao Zhou,Tiejun Ma,Luis Felipe Costa Sperb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可靠性感知的静态—动态图框架，融合大语言模型提取的公司关系与新闻语义关系，提升股票收益预测和排序表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络方法常把提取出的关系边视为同等可信的事实，忽略不同关系来源和边本身的可靠性差异；同时，静态公司关系难以反映市场环境变化，新闻覆盖也不均衡。论文旨在更细致地建模关系可信度与动态市场信息。

Method: 框架包含两类图：静态侧利用大语言模型提取多关系公司图，并通过多次提取结果的置信度加权聚合，降低一致性较低关系的影响；动态侧从新闻构建语义图，采用指数衰减进行时间聚合，以刻画变化中的市场语境。随后，使用依赖上下文的门控融合模块，按股票和日期动态调整两类图的权重，用于收益预测与股票排序。

Result: 在标普五百指数成分股上，相较最强基线，前五名收益指标提升百分之十六点四，前五名夏普比率指标提升百分之二十二点六；在纳斯达克一百指数成分股上，两项指标分别提升百分之十五点五和百分之二十点九。消融实验分别检验了各组件的贡献，人工关系核验也支持所提关系聚合具有事实依据。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对大语言模型提取的关系进行可靠性建模，并与新闻驱动的动态语义图结合，能够利用互补信息改善图式金融预测。论文的核心贡献是将关系可靠性纳入从图构建到融合的整个预测流程。

Abstract: Predicting stock returns requires modeling persistent company relations and time-varying market signals. Graph Neural Network (GNN) approaches combine static priors with dynamic relational graphs, but treat every extracted edge as a trustworthy binary fact, ignoring heterogeneous reliability across relational sources. We propose LENS (LLM-Extracted priors and News-driven Semantics), a static--dynamic graph framework for stock return prediction and ranking that models relational reliability as a first-class signal across the pipeline. On the static side, we extract a multi-relational company graph from a Large Language Model and aggregate it over multiple runs with confidence weighting to downweight low-consistency edges. On the dynamic side, we build a news-driven semantic graph with exponential-decay temporal aggregation to capture changing market context under uneven coverage. A context-dependent gated fusion module rebalances the two graphs at the per-stock, per-day level. On S&P 500, LENS improves AR@5 by 16.4% and SR@5 by 22.6% over the strongest baseline, and on NASDAQ 100 it achieves comparable improvements (15.5% and 20.9% respectively). Ablations isolate each component's contribution, and a human-validation study of sampled relations supports that the aggregation is grounded, providing evidence that reliability-aware LLM relations and news-based semantic graphs carry complementary information for graph-based financial prediction.

</details>


### [136] [Coarse Semantic Blocks for Efficient Training-Free Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04313)
*Sosuke Kobayashi,Shoichiro Yamaguchi,Seiya Tokui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的稀疏注意力方法：为每个提示构建键索引，将语义相关但位置不连续的词元聚成大块，再由查询选择并处理这些块。在长上下文下，该方法显著加速预填充与解码，同时在较大模型上基本维持基准模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文注意力的计算与内存开销高，密集注意力难以高效处理数十万乃至百万词元；现有稀疏方法还可能因索引或分组开销，无法同时兼顾预填充和解码速度。本文旨在以无需训练的方式减少注意范围，并控制稀疏化带来的准确率损失。

Method: 针对每个提示构建键索引，将键向量聚类为较大的语义块，块内词元可以来自不连续位置。每个查询通过块质心评分选择相关块，随后仍对选中块中的原始键和值执行注意力。选择相同块的查询会被合并处理，以提升预填充效率。较大的块降低质心评分与分组处理开销，使每个查询在相近时间预算内可访问更多词元。

Result: 在单张英伟达赫二百显卡上，计入索引构建和使用开销后，百万级上下文下，预填充相较密集注意力提速约二点三至二点四倍，解码提速约一点七至二倍；百万词元时，预填充提速约二点九至三倍。三百五十亿参数、约三十亿参数激活的混合模型，在五项长上下文任务、长文本综合基准以及最高二十五万六千词元的多轮检索任务中，表现接近密集注意力。四十亿参数模型的准确率损失主要出现在多键检索，但在三项基准上均优于延迟相近的密集二十亿参数模型。

Conclusion: 该方法通过语义分块与查询分组，在无需训练的前提下兼顾长上下文预填充和解码加速。实验显示，大块及较高词元预算可同时带来速度与准确率优势；不过，小模型在多键检索上的退化表明，效果仍受模型规模和任务类型影响，结论也主要适用于文中测试的模型与硬件。

Abstract: We introduce a training-free sparse attention method that builds a prompt-specific key index to accelerate both prefill and decoding. The method clusters the keys into large semantic blocks that group tokens from noncontiguous positions. Each query position selects blocks by scoring their centroids and attends to the original keys and values within them. Queries that select the same block are processed together, so prefill stays fast even though each query chooses its own blocks. Large blocks make centroid scoring and this grouped processing cheap, so each query can attend to more tokens in the same time. Larger blocks with a larger token budget can thus be both faster and more accurate on long-context tasks than smaller blocks with a smaller budget. We evaluate two hybrid LLMs, Qwen3.5-4B and 35B-A3B, on one NVIDIA H200. Including the cost of building and using the index, whole-model wall-clock speedups over dense attention are 2.3-2.4x for prefill and 1.7-2.0x for decoding at 512K tokens, and 2.9-3.0x for prefill at 1M. The 35B-A3B model stays close to dense attention on five RULER tasks through 1M, on LongBench-v2 overall, and on MRCR through 256K. The 4B model loses accuracy mainly on multi-key retrieval, yet outperforms dense Qwen3.5-2B, whose latency is similar at 512K, on all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [137] [LIRA: A More Interpretable Dynamic Loss-Informed Rank Allocation for Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04239)
*Sihao Wang,Bozheng Li,Xuemiao Xu,Haoxin Yang,Yujie Xu,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种依据局部损失变化动态分配低秩适配秩的方法，使秩调整更具可解释性，并在特定基准设置下取得优于若干对照方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准低秩适配通常为各层固定秩配置，难以反映不同层和训练阶段的适配需求。已有动态秩方法多依赖启发式或一阶信号，与局部损失行为联系间接，因此难以解释每次秩重分配的依据。

Method: 提出损失知情秩分配方法。它在二阶近似下，根据近期低秩适配因子移动所对应的局部损失扰动估计各层分数，并以逐项可解释的依据指导秩调整。方法定期采用基于位移的损失轨迹分配算法，在总预算匹配的条件下生成离散有效秩，同时兼容标准适配器结构。摘要还提及使用轨迹指数移动平均进行估计。

Result: 在罗伯塔基础模型的通用语言理解基准及指定实验协议下，该方法优于所报告的均匀秩低秩适配和自适应秩基线。轨迹指数移动平均的表现与固定点费舍尔方法相当，但估计成本更低。

Conclusion: 该研究将局部损失扰动估计用于动态秩分配，为秩调整提供更直接、可解释的依据。实验结果显示，该方法在所测设置中兼具竞争力与较低估计成本；其结论目前应限定于摘要所述模型、基准和实验协议。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for large pretrained models. However, standard LoRA typically assigns a fixed rank profile despite layer- and stage-dependent adaptation needs, while recent adaptive-rank variants often rely on heuristic or first-order signals that provide only an indirect link between rank reallocation and local loss behavior. This leaves the rationale behind each rank reallocation insufficiently interpretable. Motivated by this gap, we propose LIRA (Loss-Informed Rank Allocation), a dynamic-rank LoRA method designed to make online rank reallocation more interpretable. LIRA scores each layer by the estimated magnitude of the local loss perturbation associated with its recent LoRA-factor movements under a second-order approximation, making the basis for each rank change interpretable term by term. By periodically applying DELTA (Displacement-Evaluated Loss-Trajectory Allocation), a displacement-based algorithm for LoRA rank reallocation, LIRA produces discrete effective ranks under matched budgets while remaining compatible with standard (A,B) adapters. On RoBERTa-base GLUE under the La-LoRA/LoRA protocol, LIRA outperforms the uniform-LoRA and adaptive-rank baselines reported under this protocol; Trajectory EMA attains performance competitive with Fixed-point Fisher at lower estimation cost.

</details>


### [138] [Sketchpad: certified exact arithmetic with a small vision-language model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04434)
*Mostafa 'Neo' Mohsenvand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“草稿纸”计算框架：让语言模型每步只读取外部工作纸上的有限局部视图，并由固定运行时移动视图、更新状态，从而把任意长度计算的正确性转化为有限个局部情形的可验证性质。作者形式化证明了这一保证，并展示模型可执行多种精确算术及多项式运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在长算术题上的准确率通常来自有限测试样本，无法保证对未见输入或任意长度输入都正确；调用外部工具虽可获得精确结果，却把计算移出了模型。论文希望兼顾模型内部执行计算的能力与可证明的全输入正确性。

Method: 模型在渲染的工作纸上逐步执行算术程序：每次前向计算只读取三个单元格，并输出一个步骤，包括数字、进位或借位信息及下一操作。固定运行时负责移动读取视图，不直接写入结果数字。作者在 Lean 证明框架中证明：当每步只依赖有界局部视图时，模型对任意长度输入都正确，当且仅当它对运行时可能呈现的有限视图全部正确；若步骤还读取先前步骤，则不存在足以保证正确性的有限测试集。证明还涵盖有界数值扰动及程序组合。系统另以符号运行时构建多项式求解、展开和因式分解，并通过适配器上的乘法门使工作纸未启用时精确恢复基础模型。

Result: 部署设备上的全部 2,446 种局部视图均通过检查。经认证的模型可处理任意长度的有符号加减、长乘法、长除法、平方根和最大公因数。模型在浏览器中通过预先登记的 200 道题；另在 220 道测试中全部答对，其中包括最多 1,000 位数的加法及最多 20 位数的乘法。十个对比模型中表现最强者答对 217 道。基于这些程序的符号运行时还可执行多项式求解、展开和因式分解，且其中的数值计算均由模型完成。

Conclusion: 论文表明，通过限制模型每一步可读取的外部状态范围，可以把任意长度计算的正确性归约为有限、可检查的局部条件，并以形式化证明支持这一保证。该方法为兼顾语言模型计算能力、部署效率与可靠性提供了新路径；其结论依赖于所设定的局部读取和运行时机制。

Abstract: Language models are accurate on most long-arithmetic problems, but their accuracy is estimated on samples and carries no guarantee for an unseen input; tool use is exact but moves the computation outside the network. We show that exactness at all input lengths becomes a finite, checkable property of a network when each of its steps reads only a bounded view of an external working memory. In Sketchpad, Qwen3.5-0.8B with a low-rank adapter executes arithmetic procedures on a rendered sheet. Each forward pass reads a three-cell view and emits one step: a digit, a carry or borrow, and the next operation. A fixed runtime moves the view and writes no digit of a result. We prove in Lean that the model is then exact on all inputs of any length if and only if it is exact on the finite set of views the runtime presents, that no finite test suffices when steps also read earlier steps, and that the guarantee tolerates bounded numerical perturbation and extends to compositions of procedures. All 2,446 views pass on the deployment device, a laptop GPU running the model in a web browser. The certified model performs signed addition and subtraction, long multiplication and division, square roots and greatest common divisors at arbitrary length. It solved 200 of 200 pre-registered problems in the browser, and 220 of 220 sums of up to 1,000 digits and products of up to 20 digits, of which GPT-6.1 Sol, the strongest of ten other models, solved 217. A symbolic runtime built on these procedures solves, expands and factors polynomials with every number computed by the model, and a multiplicative gate on the adapter recovers the base model exactly when the sheet is not in use.

</details>


### [139] [OmniST: Topology-Invariant Spatiotemporal Forecasting with Guaranteed-Coverage Uncertainty at Unseen Sensor Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04256)
*Praveg Vashishtha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种不依赖传感器数量或固定图结构的时空预测模型，并结合保形校准，为训练中未出现的传感器提供具有有限样本覆盖保证的预测区间；面对时间分布漂移时，滚动自适应校准可恢复覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时空图卷积网络依赖固定大小的邻接矩阵，模型参数与训练时的传感器数量绑定，因此难以适应传感器增删，也无法直接预测训练中未见位置。已有插值或图模型虽能处理新位置，却缺乏有限样本的区间覆盖保证。此外，静态保形校准在时间分布漂移下可能严重低估不确定性。

Method: 提出拓扑不变预测模型奥姆尼时空：依据地理坐标生成节点表示，在每次前向计算时按坐标构建近邻注意力，并对各节点分别使用双向门控循环单元处理时间序列，使计算不依赖节点总数。再将该模型与归纳式分割保形校准结合，为任意位置生成预测区间，并在节点增删时维持覆盖保证。针对时间漂移，采用滚动自适应保形估计器动态校准区间。

Result: 在德里空气质量数据上，模型对三十天后的细颗粒物浓度预测平均绝对误差为每立方米三十一微克；移除百分之四十的站点时，性能下降不超过百分之一点一。对未见站点的预测优于最近邻插值约百分之十二、优于学习型克里金图网络约百分之二十一。跨越空气质量、天气和交通领域的四个数据集，在站点减少百分之三十至五十时，覆盖率仍达到或超过百分之九十；在拥有三百二十五个传感器的交通数据上也保持保证。静态校准在后期的覆盖率约为百分之五十，而滚动自适应校准使总体覆盖率达到百分之八十九、后期达到百分之九十六，但区间会更宽。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，坐标驱动且不依赖固定拓扑的时空模型能够适应传感器数量变化，并将预测扩展到从未观测的位置；结合保形校准后，可提供有理论覆盖保证的预测区间。若数据随时间漂移，滚动自适应校准比静态校准更可靠，但需接受区间变宽的代价。

Abstract: Standard spatiotemporal graph convolutional networks need a fixed-size adjacency matrix, coupling model weights to a specific sensor count N; they cannot produce any output at a sensor absent from training. We present OmniST, a topology-invariant forecaster whose computation has zero structural dependency on the number of nodes, and which, composed with inductive split-conformal calibration, gives distribution-free, coverage-guaranteed prediction intervals at arbitrary (including never-seen) sensor locations, preserving that guarantee as sensors are added or removed. Topology invariance follows from coordinate-based tokenization, k-nearest-neighbor attention from geographic coordinates at each forward pass, and per-node temporal processing via a bidirectional GRU. On real Delhi air-quality data (37 stations, 2019-2023), OmniST attains a 30-day PM2.5 MAE of 31.0 ug/m3 and degrades at most 1.1 percent when 40 percent of stations are removed. Its inductive forecasts at unseen stations beat nearest-neighbor interpolation (about 12 percent) and a learned kriging GNN (IGNNK, about 21 percent), and, unlike either, carry a finite-sample coverage guarantee. The guarantee holds at 90 percent or above under 30-50 percent node dropout across four real datasets over three domains (Delhi and Beijing air quality, NOAA GHCN weather, Caltrans PeMSD7-M traffic), and still holds at 325 real sensors (full PeMS-BAY). We also fix a temporal-drift failure: static conformal under-covers over time (about 0.50 late), but a rolling adaptive-conformal estimator restores target coverage (0.89 overall, 0.96 late) at the cost of wider intervals.

</details>


### [140] [Near-Zero Token Embeddings Silently Stall Training in Gated Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04449)
*Xin Liu,Boyuan Sun,Yuxin Wang,Haoran Song,Mingjian Zhao,Mengyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 部分未绑定权重的门控及混合模型存在近零输入嵌入；在门控序列模型中，这可能引发极端梯度放大、梯度裁剪溢出和训练更新丢失。论文提出分组裁剪与门控下限两种补救方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解释一种训练与微调中的异常现象：某些词元的输入嵌入范数接近零时，门控序列模型可能产生极端大的梯度。此类词元可能来自预训练中未出现的稀有词元或模板词元；对于输入、输出嵌入未共享的模型，零输入梯度与权重衰减可使其嵌入逐渐缩小。作者进一步关注这类梯度如何导致单批次更新被整体丢弃，以及如何避免这一问题。

Method: 作者调查了二百九十一份开放权重语言模型仓库中的嵌入表，并在多种门控序列模型配置下分析近零输入的梯度传播机制。分析发现，零输入会使当前词元的读出因子消失，抑制历史状态读出；连续层中的残差均方根若低于归一化下限的平方根，预归一化便会逐层放大反向梯度。针对已发布模型，作者提出分组裁剪：将输入嵌入梯度单独裁剪，并以六十四位精度或重缩放范数计算。针对预训练，作者提出门控下限：在西卢门控输出上添加正偏置，使零输入时门控仍保持为正。

Result: 调查发现，在未绑定权重的门控及混合模型检查点中，二十个样本（共三十八个）存在可访问且范数低于十的负六次方的输入嵌入行；绑定权重模型中则未发现此现象。在门控序列模型中，近零输入可使嵌入梯度放大超过三十个数量级，最高达三十五个数量级。若梯度元素超过约一点八四乘以十的十九次方，常见的单精度全局范数裁剪可能溢出并将当前批次梯度置零。在一项泰卢固语持续预训练实验中，约百分之二十的训练步因此丢失更新，触发问题的嵌入行也持续冻结。分组裁剪可消除更新丢失，并在一步内修复受影响的嵌入，实测端到端开销可忽略。门控下限在最高三亿七千万参数的实验中，将近零梯度降低约二十八个数量级，且困惑度与基线相当。

Conclusion: 近零输入嵌入与门控结构、预归一化及单精度全局范数裁剪相结合，会形成严重的梯度放大与更新丢失风险。分组裁剪适用于修复已发布检查点，门控下限则可从预训练阶段抑制问题；两种方法分别提供了推理后修复与训练时预防的途径。

Abstract: Among 291 surveyed open-weight language model repositories (280 distinct embedding tables), 52.6% of untied gated and hybrid checkpoints (20/38) contain reachable input embeddings with norms below $10^{-6}$, compared with 0% of weight-tied models. Such near-zero rows can arise during pretraining when unseen rare or template tokens receive zero input gradients while AdamW weight decay shrinks untied embeddings. In tested Transformer configurations, non-initial positions or a BOS token prevent this failure mode. In gated sequence models (Mamba, Gated DeltaNet, xLSTM), a near-zero token can amplify input-embedding gradients by more than 30 orders of magnitude (up to 35). At zero input, current-token readout factors (SiLU gates or linear queries) vanish, suppressing historical-state readout. Residual RMS values can then fall below the square root of the RMSNorm floor across consecutive layers, causing Pre-RMSNorm to amplify backward gradients by up to $10^3$ per layer. When a gradient entry exceeds $1.84 \times 10^{19}$, FP32 global-norm clipping in PyTorch, Hugging Face Trainer, and FSDP2 can overflow to infinity and zero the current batch's gradients. In Telugu continual pretraining of Nemotron-3-Nano-4B, this discards updates on approximately 20% of steps and leaves triggered rows frozen. We propose two remedies. For released checkpoints, GroupClip decouples input-embedding clipping using a 64-bit or rescaled norm; its rescaled implementation eliminates update loss and heals triggered rows in one step with negligible measured end-to-end overhead. For pretraining, GateFloor ($\mathrm{SiLU}(z) + δ$, $δ\in [0.01, 0.1]$) keeps the gate positive at zero input, reducing near-zero gradients by 28 orders of magnitude in experiments up to 370M parameters, with perplexity comparable to the baseline.

</details>


### [141] [Deep Learning Compilers: Architecture, Optimization, and Empirical Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04273)
*Jinghao Wen,Ruixuan Wang,Xun Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理深度学习编译器的架构、流程与优化技术，并通过实验发现，编译收益取决于工作负载：适合融合的计算图最高可加速八点二一倍；动态形状编译可使形状切换的累计成本降低约百分之七十，但会略微降低稳定运行时的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型日益复杂，硬件平台也更加多样，因而编译器对提升计算效率和支持规模化部署至关重要。然而，编译器的架构设计、各阶段优化效果及实际部署成本仍需系统分析。本文旨在结合架构梳理与实证评估，厘清编译器的能力边界及其适用条件。

Method: 将深度学习编译器划分为前端、中端和后端，重点分析中端如何连接高层模型表示与面向特定硬件的优化；梳理代表性编译器及各阶段优化技术；并在英伟达图形处理器上，使用一种主流深度学习框架及其编译器开展实验，评估计算图优化效果、编译开销和动态形状处理能力。

Result: 实验显示，编译器的性能收益与工作负载特征密切相关：适合算子融合的计算图最高获得八点二一倍加速，其他工作负载的提升则有限。编译开销可能需要经过数千次执行才能摊销。动态形状编译可将形状切换的累计成本降低约百分之七十，但代价是稳定状态下的性能略有下降。

Conclusion: 深度学习编译器的实际价值不能仅依据峰值加速来判断，还需综合考虑计算图特征、编译成本、执行次数及形状变化。本文通过系统架构分析和定量实验，为编译器设计、优化策略选择与实际部署提供了参考。

Abstract: As deep learning (DL) models become increasingly complex and hardware architectures grow more heterogeneous, DL compilers play a critical role in optimizing computational efficiency and enabling scalable deployment. This paper presents a systematic analysis of DL compilers, covering their architectural evolution, compilation workflows, optimization techniques, and emerging research directions. We organize DL compiler architectures into frontend, middle-end, and backend stages, emphasizing the role of the middle-end in connecting high-level model representations with hardware-specific optimizations. We further examine representative DL compilers and recent optimization techniques across different compilation stages. Beyond architectural and technical analysis, we conduct an empirical evaluation of compiler behaviors to investigate the effectiveness of graph optimization, compilation overhead, and dynamic-shape handling. Experiments using PyTorch/TorchInductor on an NVIDIA RTX A6000 demonstrate that compiler benefits depend strongly on workload characteristics, with speedups of up to 8.21\times for fusion-friendly computation graphs, while other workloads exhibit limited improvements. Our results further reveal that compilation overhead can require thousands of executions to amortize, and dynamic-shape compilation reduces cumulative shape-transition costs by approximately 70\% at the expense of modest steady-state performance degradation. By combining systematic architectural analysis with quantitative experimental evidence, this work provides insights into the design, optimization, and practical deployment of modern DL compilers.

</details>


### [142] [Reciprocity Can Erase Direction:tructural Boundaries and Controlled Utility of Magnetic Graph Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04295)
*Dawei Han,Zicheng Wang,Yaoxing Ren,Yi Lv,Rongqian Wu,Fumin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示磁图网络中的方向信息可能在预测前被图结构抹去，并给出相应的理论刻画与实验检验；结果表明，连续相位在所测机制任务中保留了方向信息，但尚无证据证明其具有临床任务上的普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁图网络通常借助相位编码边的方向，但学习得到的图结构可能在模型预测前就消除这一方向机制。因此，论文旨在明确方向信息何时会被结构性抹去，并区分“方向信息被保留”与“方向信息对具体任务确有帮助”这两个不同问题。

Method: 论文针对基于支撑结构的磁拉普拉斯算子推导精确恒等关系，将虚部能量及相位偏离零值的程度与按边权幅度加权的互反性联系起来；进一步证明，当观测节点数满足 m≤k+1 时，前 k 个近邻的选择会导致完全塌缩，并为稠密支撑结构中的互反弧比例给出下界。作者还构造节点特征和边权幅度相同、标签相反的一对传播实例，证明抹去方向的表示至多达到随机猜测水平。实验包括 72 组固定神经网络拟合、无方向信息的对照任务，以及保留公开临床影像数据集开发研究中的验证分析。

Result: 在合成机制测试中，支撑相位模型在单向环上的测试受试者工作特征曲线下面积为 1.0000，但在互反环和完全支撑结构上降至 0.5000；连续分数相位模型和实值有向对照模型在所有这些结构上均为 1.0000。所有模型都能解决不含方向差异的对照任务。在公开临床影像数据集的开发研究中，连续相位确实参与了计算，但其相对于零相位的稳健性差异仅为 +0.0036，描述性 95% 区间为 −0.0014 至 +0.0095；该分析基于用于检查点选择的验证患者，不能据此确立临床效用。

Conclusion: 本文提供了区分“方向信息已被结构抹去”与“任务效用尚未得到证实”的理论和实验框架，并指出在特定条件下磁相位能够保留有用方向信息。研究结果不支持磁相位在所有图结构或临床任务上普遍优越，也不应被解读为已经验证了临床方向信息的价值。

Abstract: Magnetic graph networks encode direction through phase, but a learned graph can erase the intended directional mechanism before prediction. We characterize this failure for support-based magnetic Laplacians. Exact identities relate imaginary energy and distance from zero phase to amplitude-weighted reciprocity. Top-$k$ selection forces complete collapse when the observed-node count satisfies $m\leq k+1$, and dense support imposes a lower bound on reciprocal-arc fraction. We then construct paired propagation examples with identical node features and symmetric amplitudes but opposite labels, giving a provable chance-level limit for direction-erasing representations. In 72 fixed neural fits, support phase achieved test AUROC 1.0000 on a unilateral ring but 0.5000 on reciprocal ring and complete supports; continuous score phase and a real directed comparator achieved 1.0000 throughout. All models solved the direction-free control. These are oracle-direction mechanism tests, not evidence of learned clinical direction. In the preserved public DeepDRiD development study, continuous phase was active but its robustness difference from zero phase was only $+0.0036$ (descriptive 95\% interval $-0.0014$ to $+0.0095$) on validation patients used for checkpoint selection. The contribution is a structural and experimental framework for distinguishing erased direction from unestablished task utility. It provides a precise circumstance in which magnetic phase preserves useful information, without asserting universal or clinical superiority.

</details>


### [143] [Self-Privileged Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04632)
*Yangrong Hu,Xueyu Zhou,Yue Wu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析了监督微调中令牌级加权的作用，并提出利用特权信息构造先验分布的方法，使更能预测正确答案的令牌获得更大权重；数学推理与代码生成实验显示，该方法优于标准监督微调及其他基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有令牌级加权方法通常依据模型在各位置的输出分布调整训练权重，但这类权重会诱导怎样的目标分布、为何有效，尚未得到充分解释。论文希望厘清不同加权策略的优化特性，并改善静态加权对先验质量的依赖。

Method: 论文从优化目标出发，刻画令牌级加权所诱导的目标分布：动态加权会使分布显著尖锐化，静态加权则拟合数据分布与冻结先验分布的乘积。在此基础上，论文提出自特权微调：将初始模型冻结后，输入特权信息（如数学题的标准答案）形成先验，用于指导微调中的令牌加权。通过贝叶斯分解，论文进一步说明，该方法会依据目标令牌对预测正确答案的有效信息量重新加权。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务的主要实验设置中，自特权微调均优于标准监督微调及其他基线。摘要未提供具体数值或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 令牌加权方式会改变监督微调所拟合的目标分布。静态加权具有较稳定的训练目标，但效果取决于先验质量；利用特权信息构造先验，可突出对正确答案更具预测力的令牌，并提升多个任务上的表现。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a key component of language model post-training, enabling effective task adaptation and serving as an important foundation for subsequent reinforcement learning. Recent methods seek to improve SFT by introducing token-level weights into the training objective, where these weights are typically derived from a model's output distribution at each token position. To better understand how such weighting schemes affect SFT, we analyze their optimization objectives and theoretically characterize the target distributions they induce. Our analysis shows that dynamic weighting methods, such as Dynamic Fine-Tuning (DFT), induce a heavily sharpened distribution, whereas static weighting methods fit a target distribution proportional to the product of the data distribution and a frozen prior. These findings suggest that static weighting provides a more stable training target, while its effectiveness critically depends on the quality of the prior distribution. Building on this observation, we propose Self-Privileged Fine-Tuning (SPFT), which uses a frozen copy of the initial model conditioned on privileged information (such as ground truth for mathematical questions) as a prior to provide guidance for token-level weighting during fine-tuning. Through a Bayesian decomposition, we further show that SPFT reweights the standard static prior according to how informative each target token is for predicting the ground-truth outcome, thereby assigning larger weights to more predictive tokens. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation show that SPFT improves over standard SFT and other baselines across the main settings.

</details>


### [144] [What Is the Influence of Task Order in Continual Learning? An Empirical Study and Order-Aware Evaluation for Motion Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04309)
*Zirui Li,Yunlong Lin,Haohan Yang,Xiaocong Zhao,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 持续学习算法的总体排名高度依赖任务顺序，尤其受起始任务影响；评测应公开顺序分布，并报告按顺序与起始任务拆分的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习算法的汇总分数通常隐含了任务顺序分布，但该分布很少被明确说明。因此，算法间的比较可能混合训练随机性与任务顺序造成的差异，导致排名难以解释或复现。

Method: 研究将算法比较形式化为对预先声明的任务顺序分布求期望，并在道路布局运动预测任务上进行评估：使用四个任务，遍历全部二十四种任务顺序，比较四种算法，每种设置进行三次训练重复，共计二百八十八次运行。分析当前任务误差、后向迁移、起始任务及任务统计特征，并进一步考察暖启动结果与不同顺序加权方式。

Result: 任务顺序解释了当前任务误差和后向迁移变异的百分之六十五至百分之九十一；其中百分之九十至百分之九十九的顺序效应来自起始任务身份，最小任务在冷启动条件下难以学好。首阶段之后，顺序效应与训练变异相当。多数顺序下，当前任务误差和后向迁移的领先算法与第二名的差距不超过一次训练重复的标准差。后向迁移主要通过其参考项受顺序影响，而该项对首个任务赋予最大权重，因此相对改进和最终误差可能选出不同的领先算法。依据任务统计量构造的转移方向描述指标能够反映最小任务的位置，但按该指标对任务顺序加权并未改变算法排名。

Conclusion: 持续学习基准不应只报告汇总分数，而应明确声明任务顺序分布，并同时提供按任务顺序、起始任务拆分的结果，以及暖启动和最终误差。这样才能区分训练波动与顺序效应，使算法比较更可靠、透明。

Abstract: Aggregate scores for continual learning algorithms rest on a distribution over task orders that is rarely stated. We formulate algorithm comparison as an expectation over a declared order distribution, which separates training variation from order variation, and apply it to motion forecasting with four road-layout tasks from the INTERACTION dataset, all 24 task orders, four algorithms, and three training replicates (288 runs). Task order accounts for 65\% to 91\% of the variation in current-task error and backward transfer, and 90\% to 99\% of this order effect comes from the identity of the starting task: the smallest task cannot be learned from a cold start. Beyond the first stage, the order effect is comparable to training variability. In most orders, the leading algorithm in current-task error and backward transfer is within one replicate standard deviation of the runner-up. Backward transfer depends on the order mainly through its reference term, which weights the first task most heavily, so relative improvement and final error identify different leading algorithms for displacement error. A transition-direction descriptor computed from task statistics tracks the position of the smallest task, and weighting orders by it leaves the algorithm ranking unchanged. These results call for benchmarks that declare their order distribution and report order-resolved, starting-task-resolved, warm-start, and final errors alongside aggregate scores. The code and data are available \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/task-order-cl-4f7b/}{here}.

</details>


### [145] [Anchor: Augmented Cross-Layer Recurrent State for Long-Context Retrieval in Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04327)
*Vimal William,Yuanzhe Li,Danella Zhao,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 锚点方案只在少数关键层设置循环记忆，并让多个层共享同一份记忆、通过各自的读取映射提取信息，从而以更小状态提升长上下文检索与推理表现，并降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练自回归变换器线性化的一种方法，是结合滑动窗口注意力与固定大小的循环记忆。但并非每一层的循环状态都同样有助于长上下文检索：研究发现，检索能力集中在少数关键层，其余层保存的信息相似，只是经过各层特有的变换。因此，为每层分别配置记忆可能造成冗余。

Method: 提出锚点方案：仅在少数关键层配置测试时训练记忆；记忆由跨层共享的循环状态构成，每段信息只写入一次，再由多个层通过各自学习的读取映射读取。这些映射可对共享信息进行层特有的拉伸与旋转。该方法还展示了适用于其他混合注意力结构的潜力。

Result: 在两种主流开源语言模型上，与既有线性化方法相比，锚点方案的召回率提高二点三至三点一倍，多任务语言理解基准准确率提高六点七至八点九个百分点，同时循环状态缩小至原来的十分之一。其准确率保留了教师模型的百分之九十一至九十五；相较于软最大注意力，端到端延迟最高降低至原来的约四分之一点三。

Conclusion: 长上下文检索所需的循环记忆在网络深度方向上具有稀疏性，且多个层可通过不同读取变换共享同一信息。利用关键层与跨层共享记忆，能够减少状态冗余，并在降低延迟和记忆规模的同时改善检索及基准表现。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient linearization turns a pretrained autoregressive Transformer into an efficient model by hybridizing sliding-window attention (SWA) with a constant-size recurrent memory. But does every layer's recurrent state contribute to long-context retrieval? Investigating an SWA and Test-Time Training (TTT) hybrid, we find that retrieval is sparse across depth, concentrated in a few anchor layers, and that the remaining states hold similar information expressed through layer-specific transformations. We exploit both observations in Anchor: TTT memory placed only at the anchor layers, and a cross-layer recurrent state written once and read by many layers, each through its own learned read map that realizes a layer-specific stretch and rotation, along with demonstrated generalization to other hybrid attentions. On Llama-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B, Anchor delivers 2.3-3.1x higher recall and 6.7-8.9 points higher MMLU accuracy than LoLCATs with a 10x smaller recurrent state, retains 91-95% of the teacher's MMLU accuracy, and cuts end-to-end latency by up to 4.3x relative to softmax attention.

</details>


### [146] [Proper Agnostic Learning of Affine Halfspaces under Log-Concave Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04344)
*Zhijie Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对全维对数凹分布的仿射半空间无关学习算法，在任意二元标签下输出单个仿射半空间，误差至多为最优误差的常数倍加精度项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标签可能含噪、且边缘分布的均值与协方差未知时，仍希望高效地学习仿射半空间，并获得相对于最优分类器的保证。关键困难是：为了处理不平衡的少数类而对样本进行条件化，会放大标签噪声，因此需要将条件分布上的学习结果可靠地推广回整个空间。

Method: 算法从独立的带标签样本出发，稳健估计少数类截断区域的均值与协方差，近似最小化经典的区域深度对偶目标，并扩张一个超水平集，直到达到边缘分布的指定分位数。核心几何结论表明：当少数类半空间的质量为某个小概率，且平衡限制上的条件分歧足够小时，全局分歧与该概率乘以条件分歧在常数因子范围内相当，从而抵消条件化造成的噪声放大。证明结合对数凹分布的四阶矩平方和界，以及将平衡仿射半空间学习转化为齐次半空间学习的平移归约。

Result: 算法输出一个仿射半空间，其分类误差不超过最优可能误差的通用常数倍再加上精度参数。样本量及实数运算复杂度关于维度和精度倒数为多项式，并关于失败概率倒数的对数为多项式；样本复杂度还可对该置信度对数因子呈线性。算法不需要预先知道边缘分布的均值或协方差。

Conclusion: 该工作通过少数类区域上的稳健估计与新的分歧几何比较，解决了条件化学习中的噪声放大问题，实现了对全维对数凹边缘分布的高效、常数因子无关仿射半空间学习。

Abstract: We give a proper constant-factor agnostic learner for affine halfspaces under full-dimensional log-concave marginals. For arbitrary binary labels, the algorithm uses independent labeled samples and returns a single affine halfspace with error at most $C\mathrm{OPT}+\varepsilon$. Its sample and real-arithmetic complexity are polynomial in dimension, inverse accuracy, and the logarithm of the reciprocal failure probability. The mean and covariance of the marginal need not be known, and the sample complexity can be made linear in this logarithmic confidence factor. The main geometric result compares global disagreement with disagreement on a balanced restriction: for a minority halfspace of mass $p$, global disagreement is within universal constant factors of $p$ times conditional disagreement, provided the latter is sufficiently small. To construct the restriction from noisy labels, we estimate the minority cap's mean and covariance robustly, approximately minimize the classical zonoid-depth dual objective, and expand a superlevel set to a marginal quantile. The disagreement comparison cancels the noise amplification caused by conditioning and allows the learned halfspace to be used on the entire space. The analysis combines degree-four sum-of-squares moment bounds for log-concave laws with a translation-based reduction from balanced affine learning to homogeneous halfspace learning.

</details>


### [147] [Aligning Training and Rollout Policies for End-to-End FP4 RL on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04376)
*Hei Yi Mak,Hoang Le,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Huan Lin,Zhengzhi Tang,Zhexu Liu,Yao Wang,Yaoyuan Wang,Anandharaju Durai Raju,Tanzila Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，混合专家模型的低精度强化学习后训练必须对训练与推理两侧采用对称的量化恢复方法；据此提出的莫专家量化恢复方案可改善两侧对齐，并显著缩小与全精度模型的准确率差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有低精度强化学习恢复方法能弥补稠密模型在激进量化下的精度损失，但直接用于混合专家模型时，相比朴素低精度基线并无改善。论文旨在查明这一差异的原因，并解决混合专家模型后训练中的量化恢复问题。

Method: 作者分析训练与推理策略对齐情况，重点考察路由器的专家选择及其对低精度恢复方式的敏感性，并以训练、推理概率之间的皮尔逊相关系数和路由选择匹配率衡量对齐程度。基于分析，提出莫专家量化恢复方案，要求训练侧与推理侧采用对称的恢复修正。

Result: 实验分析表明，混合专家模型的离散路由决策使训练与推理策略对齐对恢复方式是否对称格外敏感。采用非对称方案时，包括仅修正训练侧的稠密模型方案，训练与推理概率相关性及路由选择匹配率都会明显下降。所提方案恢复了两侧对齐，并显著缩小了模型准确率与全精度模型之间的差距。

Conclusion: 混合专家模型的低精度强化学习后训练不能简单照搬稠密模型的恢复配方。训练侧与推理侧对称地实施量化恢复，是维持策略对齐、弥补精度损失的关键原则。

Abstract: In this work, we study end-to-end FP4 reinforcement learning (RL) post-training for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models. Recent work on FP4 RL has introduced effective recovery recipes that restore accuracy under aggressive low-precision quantization for dense models, but we find that the same approach yields no improvement over the naive FP4 baseline in the MoE setting. We conduct an in-depth study to understand why. Our analysis identifies the root cause in the routing mechanism unique to MoE: because the router makes discrete expert-selection decisions, training-inference policy alignment becomes acutely sensitive to whether the same recovery technique is applied symmetrically on the training and inference sides. When an asymmetric recipe is used-including the dense-model approach, which corrects only the rollout-training-inference alignment deteriorates sharply, as measured by the Pearson correlation coefficient between training and inference probabilities, and router selection matching rates. Building on this finding, we propose MoeResQ, a quantization-recovery recipe for MoE RL post-training that enforces symmetry between the training- and inference-side corrections. MoeResQ restores training-inference alignment and substantially closes the accuracy gap to full precision, establishing symmetric recovery as the key principle in FP4 RL for MoE models.

</details>


### [148] [LoopVQ: Sub 1-bit KV Cache Quantization for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05304)
*Minsoo Cheong,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向循环式变换器的键值缓存联合向量量化方法，通过跨循环步联合编码并微调码本，大幅压缩缓存，同时保持模型精度并提升推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式变换器通过重复使用同一组层，减少了模型参数和参数存储需求，但每个循环步仍分别保存键和值，导致键值缓存随循环步数线性增长，成为主要内存瓶颈。研究还发现，缓存内容在循环步之间存在较高冗余，不同循环步对量化的敏感度也显著不同，因此统一处理各循环步并不理想。

Method: 提出循环向量量化方法，对多个循环步的通道进行联合编码，以利用循环维度上的冗余；随后微调量化码本，使量化模型的输出尽可能接近全精度模型。该方法可与现有键值缓存量化技术结合，并已集成到推理服务框架中。

Result: 在每元素0.5比特的设置下，该方法在通用任务、长链式推理和长上下文检索任务上，均优于面向标准大语言模型的现有键值缓存向量量化方法。在每元素0.25比特时，与另一种量化方法结合后仍能保留大部分脑浮点精度模型的准确率。服务测试显示，在3.2万令牌上下文下，解码延迟接近减半；与脑浮点精度基线相比，1.4亿参数和26亿参数的欧罗模型峰值服务吞吐量最高分别提升4.3倍和6.1倍。

Conclusion: 循环步间的缓存冗余和量化敏感度差异，为压缩循环式变换器的键值缓存提供了有效机会。所提出的方法能以极低比特数显著降低缓存开销，并兼顾准确率与推理效率，且可与其他量化方案互补。

Abstract: Looped transformers are a parameter-efficient alternative to standard transformers for language modeling. By reapplying the same layers across multiple loop steps, recent looped language models match the performance of non-looped models several times their size. While this architecture effectively reduces parameter memory, each loop step still stores its own keys and values, so the key-value (KV) cache grows linearly with the number of loop steps and becomes the main memory bottleneck. We observe that the KV cache of looped models is highly redundant along the loop axis and that the loop steps differ substantially in their sensitivity to quantization. Based on these observations, we propose $\textbf{LoopVQ}$, a KV cache vector quantization method for looped transformers that codes channels of multiple loop steps jointly, and fine-tunes the codebooks to match the output of the full-precision model. At 0.5 bits per element, LoopVQ outperforms existing KV cache vector quantization methods for standard LLMs on general, long chain-of-thought reasoning, and long-context retrieval tasks with Ouro and Huginn models. Its design is orthogonal to these methods, and combined with TaSQ, it retains much of the BF16 accuracy even at 0.25 bits per element. We also implement LoopVQ in SGLang, where it nearly halves the decode latency at a 32K-token context and raises the peak serving throughput by up to $4.3\times$ and $6.1\times$ on the 1.4B and 2.6B Ouro models compared to the BF16 baseline.

</details>


### [149] [SaMA: Morpho Adaptation via Asymmetric Expansion of Kronecker Product](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04462)
*An Nguyen,Anh Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可扩展形态适配方法，旨在改善低秩适配的优化特性，并以更少参数获得更高有效秩；摘要称其在常识与算术推理任务上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩适配虽能以较少参数微调模型，但存在两项局限：其不变性流形会在损失景观中形成平坦方向，给自适应优化器带来优化瓶颈；有效秩随参数预算仅线性增长，限制了适配能力。

Method: 提出可扩展形态适配，基于对克罗内克积的系统推广，放宽完美洗牌分解中的共享块约束，使表示能力可从低秩延伸至满秩。作者还指出，该方法的不变性流形收缩为对角群结构，因而在结构上更利于优化。

Result: 摘要报告，该方法在常识推理和算术推理基准上持续优于多种强参数高效微调基线，同时具有更高的参数效率；其实际秩可随参数预算呈二次增长，并可达到满秩。

Conclusion: 该方法通过扩大秩增长能力并改善参数化结构，针对低秩适配的表达能力和优化问题提出了统一方案。摘要中的实验结果显示其具有竞争力，但未提供具体指标、模型规模或计算成本，尚无法据此比较提升幅度。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has attracted significant attention in the parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) landscape. However, its design suffers from two fundamental limitations. First, its invariance manifold induces flat directions in the loss landscape, creating an optimization bottleneck for adaptive optimizers. Second, its effective rank scales only linearly with the parameter budget. In this paper, we propose Scalable Morpho Adaptation (SaMA), a PEFT method grounded in a principled generalization of the Kronecker product. By relaxing the shared-block constraint in the perfect-shuffle factorization, SaMA spans from low to full rank and achieves a practical rank that scales quadratically with the parameter budget, up to full-rank. Furthermore, we show that its invariance manifold collapses to a diagonal group, making it structurally more optimizer-friendly. Empirically, SaMA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines on commonsense and arithmetic reasoning benchmarks while being more parameter-efficient.

</details>


### [150] [Aligning Language Models with General Preferences: An Empirical Study of Direct Nash Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05373)
*Austin Tsai,Saurav Nanda,Ganapathy Parthasarathy,Bhuvnesh Kumar,Parivesh Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文独立复现直接纳什优化的实用流程，并发现三轮迭代可显著提升两种开源指令模型的偏好评测表现；但结果来自单次实验，且教师与评测者相同，需谨慎解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接纳什优化旨在通过近似双人偏好博弈的纳什均衡，使语言模型适应一般偏好函数。论文关注其实用版本能否在开放权重模型上有效运行，以及流程中哪些组成部分重要、是否必须依赖闭源评审模型，并评估其成本及其与理论方法的差异。

Method: 研究者独立复现实用版流程，并在拉马三点一八八十亿参数指令模型与米斯特拉尔七十亿参数指令模型上进行实验。每轮先按当前策略采样，再由评审模型对样本及教师提供的标准回答评分；随后筛选偏好差距较大的回答对，并采用类似直接偏好优化的对比式更新。实验使用闭源强模型或同系列较大开源模型进行评分，并通过消融实验考察标准回答、历史数据回看、偏好对筛选方式、采样数量及差距阈值的作用。

Result: 经过三轮迭代，长度控制胜率方面，拉马模型对闭源强模型的胜率由百分之三十一点七升至百分之四十五点三；米斯特拉尔模型由百分之六点八升至百分之十八点四。调优后的八十亿参数模型在所有报告的胜率指标上均超过七百亿参数的同系列指令模型。采用同系列开源评审模型时，拉马和米斯特拉尔分别保留了闭源评审所带来胜率增益的百分之六十二和百分之八十二。消融实验中，移除标准回答、取消历史数据回看或只接受教师偏好的回答对，表现低六至十个长度控制胜率百分点；每提示采样数从一条增至七条、或单独调整偏好差距阈值，对胜率的影响则较小，分别不超过二点一个和二点四个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该实用优化流程能提升所测开源模型的偏好表现，且评分环节不必依赖闭源模型；标准回答、历史数据回看和偏好对选择是较关键的设计。不过，所有结果均为单次运行，教师与评测者又是同一闭源模型，因此提升可能部分反映与该模型偏好的契合，尚不能据此确认对其他评测者或真实用户偏好的普遍改善。

Abstract: Direct Nash Optimization (DNO) aligns a language model to a general preference function by approximating the Nash equilibrium of a two-player preference game. Its practical variant, DNO-Prct, iterates on-policy sampling, LLM-based rating of the samples together with a teacher's gold response, large-margin preference-pair selection and a DPO-style contrastive update. We re-implement DNO-Prct independently and study it on two open-weight instruct models, Llama 3.1 8B Instruct and Mistral 7B Instruct v0.1, with GPT-4o or a larger open-weight model of the same family as the annotator. On AlpacaEval 2.0 with GPT-4o as judge, three iterations raise the length-controlled (LC) win rate against GPT-4o from 31.7% to 45.3% for Llama and from 6.8% to 18.4% for Mistral, and the tuned 8B model scores above Llama 3.1 70B Instruct on every reported win rate. With same-family open-weight annotators, the runs retain 62% (Llama) and 82% (Mistral) of the LC gain achieved with GPT-4o as annotator, so the rating step does not require a proprietary model. In a separate ablation configuration, variants that remove the gold responses, remove the data look-back, or accept only teacher-preferred pairs score 6 to 10 LC points below the full procedure, whereas varying the number of on-policy samples per prompt (1 to 7) moves the LC win rate by at most 2.1 points, and a margin sweep (1 to 5) run in its own pipeline by at most 2.4 points. We also report the cost of the pipeline and where the practical algorithm departs from the theory it approximates. All results are single runs, and GPT-4o is both the teacher and the evaluation judge; we discuss what this implies for the reported gains.

</details>


### [151] [Theoretical Analysis of Muon on Transformers for Next-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04465)
*Shengwei Wu,Yixuan Yang,Gao Huang,Song Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析 Muon 优化器在变换器下一词预测训练中的作用，刻画其两阶段训练过程、收敛性质及泛化能力，并通过实验验证理论结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 在训练基于变换器的大语言模型时表现突出，但其优化动态缺乏充分的理论解释。本文旨在揭示它如何推动模型学习下一词预测所需的关键信息，并解释其训练效率与泛化表现。

Method: 将训练过程划分为两个阶段：预处理阶段学习输出值分量，主阶段学习键与查询分量。对预处理阶段，精确刻画优化轨迹，并证明每次更新后归一化谱间隔均达到最优；对采用软最大注意力的主阶段，推导训练损失与归一化间隔差的非渐近收敛速率。此外，利用学习到的权重结构建立对未见上下文的泛化保证，并在下一词预测任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析表明，预处理阶段每次更新都能使归一化谱间隔最优；主阶段的训练损失和归一化间隔差具有非渐近收敛保证。学习得到的权重结构还可支持对未见上下文的泛化。实验结果验证了这些理论洞见。

Conclusion: 研究为 Muon 在变换器训练中的优化动态提供了理论解释：其更新规则可能帮助模型高效识别对预测重要的词元，并由此解释其在大语言模型训练中的良好表现。

Abstract: Muon has recently shown strong empirical performance in training Transformer-based large language models (LLMs), yet its optimization dynamics remain poorly understood from a theoretical perspective. In this paper, we analyze the training dynamics of Muon for next-token prediction (NTP) in Transformers. The training procedure consists of two phases: the preprocessing phase learns the output-value component, while the main phase learns the key-query component. For the preprocessing phase, we characterize the optimization trajectory exactly and show that the normalized spectral margin is optimal after each update. For the main phase, we establish nonasymptotic convergence rates for the training loss and the normalized margin gap under softmax attention. These results suggest that Muon's update rule can help models efficiently learn which tokens matter for prediction, offering a possible explanation for its strong performance in large language model training. The learned weight structure also yields a generalization guarantee for unseen contexts. Furthermore, we validate our theoretical insights with experiments on next-token prediction tasks.

</details>


### [152] [SKILL2STEER: One-Pass Hypernetwork for Mapping Natural-Language Skills to Low-Rank Post-Block Activation Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04497)
*Ran Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现，编译后的技能偏移主要提供领域通用的行为先验，而非保留技能文档的具体内容；据此提出一种轻量级方法，将技能文档转为可部署的激活偏移，在问答基准上优于冻结模型和参数量更大的权重适配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言技能编译进模型参数，可减少提示词长度并便于技能复用。但既有方法普遍假设编译产物承载了技能内容，且采用不同方式将其注入模型。论文旨在检验这一关键假设，并探索更轻量、易部署的技能编译方案。

Method: 作者在检索智能体上设计三项对照实验：比较正确与错误技能文档、单一平均技能包与逐技能路由，以及训练所得方向与同范数随机方向。随后提出一种单次前向的超网络方法：读取技能文档一次，将其映射为低秩激活偏移，并加到每个解码器模块的输出端。基座模型权重保持冻结，生成器仅在编译阶段运行；偏移可按检索步骤调度，也可合并为无需路由的单一技能包。

Result: 对照实验显示：错误技能文档的表现与正确文档相当；单一平均技能包在双侧等效性检验中与逐技能路由相当；同范数随机方向则退化至不注入基线，说明训练得到的方向仍发挥关键作用。该方法每项技能仅需二百三十六万参数，比权重空间适配器少九点二五倍。在搜索问答任务及七数据集基准上，其精确匹配率为百分之三十七点九，比冻结模型高九点八个百分点，比每项技能参数量多九点二五倍的权重空间方法高二点三个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，编译技能偏移的作用更接近领域通用行为先验，而非技能文档内容的忠实编码。提出的方法以较低参数开销实现了有效的技能注入和免路由部署；不过，实验结果也提示，技能编译的收益不能简单归因于保留了原文中的具体知识。

Abstract: Compiling a natural-language agent skill into model parameters removes the skill text from the prompt and leaves behind a small artifact that can be distributed and reused. Existing methods differ in where they inject the compiled quantity, and all of them assume that the artifact carries the content of the skill. We test that assumption. Through three controls on a retrieval agent, we find that a compiled skill shift carries a domain-general behavioural prior, not the content of the skill. A wrong skill document performs on par with the correct one, a single averaged bundle matches per-skill routing under a two-sided equivalence test, and a same-norm random direction collapses to the no-injection baseline, which shows that the trained direction stays load-bearing. We then introduce SKILL2STEER, a one-pass hypernetwork that reads a skill document once and maps it to low-rank activation shifts added at the output of every decoder block. Each skill compiles to 2.36M parameters, 9.25 times fewer than a weight-space adapter, while the base weights stay frozen and the generator runs only at compile time. The shift can be scheduled across retrieval steps and deployed as one routing-free bundle. On SearchQA, a seven-dataset benchmark, SKILL2STEER reaches 37.9 exact match, 9.8 points above the frozen model and 2.3 points above a weight-space method that ships 9.25 times more parameters per skill.

</details>


### [153] [Sample-Grained Approximate Unlearning with Provable Per-Sample Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04501)
*Yaxin Xiao,Yulin Jin,Li Tang,Haoyang LI,Huadi Zheng,Qingqing Ye,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向单个样本的机器遗忘优化框架，通过对齐输出分布，使近似遗忘结果更接近完整重训练，并降低遗忘效果对超参数的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似机器遗忘方法通常以数据集整体指标评估效果，但整体指标可能掩盖单个样本上的偏差。因此，即使总体表现良好，模型对某些样本的输出仍可能与完整重训练后的模型差异显著。论文旨在评估并改善样本级遗忘效果，同时保留模型在保留集上的效用。

Method: 论文提出样本粒度似然对齐框架，将近似遗忘过程扩展到单样本层面，引导每个样本的输出趋向其正确的目标分布；并提出样本粒度分布差距指标，用于衡量近似遗忘与完整重训练在各样本输出分布上的差异。理论分析证明，该框架能够缩小这一差距，并保证样本级偏差有界。此外，论文设计了低成本方法来估计成员与非成员的目标分布，使框架更具实用性。

Result: 在分类和生成任务上的实验表明，将该框架与现有近似遗忘基线结合，样本粒度分布差距最高可降低百分之八十六点六七；同时，模型对遗忘超参数的鲁棒性也有所提升。

Conclusion: 论文指出，机器遗忘的评估与优化不应只关注数据集整体指标，还应检验单个样本的输出是否接近完整重训练结果。所提出的样本级分布对齐框架兼具理论保证与实验效果，为提升近似遗忘的精细度和稳定性提供了可插拔方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to erase the influence of a designated forget set from a trained model while preserving strong utility on the retain set.
