Approximate unlearning methods efficiently achieve this goal without full retraining, but their effectiveness is primarily assessed using dataset-level aggregate metrics. These aggregate metrics can mask deviations from retraining at the level of individual samples. In this work, we focus on per-sample unlearning effectiveness. We introduce SALA (Sample-grained Likelihoods ALignment), a plug-in optimization framework that enables approximate unlearning methods to operate at the level of individual samples by steering each example toward its correct target distribution. To quantitatively assess this behavior, we further propose the sample-grained distribution gap, a metric that captures per-sample discrepancies between approximate unlearning and exact retraining output distributions. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that SALA contracts this gap and yields a bounded per-sample discrepancy to retraining. To make this framework practical, we develop a low-cost estimation method for the member and non-member target distributions. Extensive experiments on classification and generation tasks demonstrate that SALA reduces the sample-grained distribution gap by up to $86.67\%$ when paired with existing approximate unlearning baselines, while also improving robustness to unlearning hyperparameters.

</details>


### [154] [Variational Learning with Lie Group Exponential Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04513)
*Eren Mehmet Kıral,Thomas Möllenhoff,Koichi Tojo,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Kenichi Bannai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将李群方法推广到一般李群，建立其与自然梯度变分学习及贝叶斯学习规则之间的联系，并据此构造高效的变分学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的变分贝叶斯学习面临两项实现难题：变分分布参数受到约束，以及梯度估计方差较高。已有李群方法可利用参数空间自身的运算结构缓解这些问题，但此前主要针对乘法群或仿射群，适用范围有限；其与标准自然梯度方法的关系也需要进一步厘清。

Method: 将李群学习框架推广至一般李群，并引入与李群表示相关的谐波指数族，作为构造算法的基础。作者系统分析该框架与自然梯度变分学习方法（包括贝叶斯学习规则）的关系，并证明对李群算法作线性近似，可得到贝叶斯学习规则的推广形式。

Result: 论文建立了通用李群算法与标准自然梯度变分学习方法之间的明确联系，证明线性近似能够导出贝叶斯学习规则的广义形式，并提出一种利用李群表示系统构造可高效实现的变分学习算法的方法。

Conclusion: 李群及其谐波指数族为变分学习提供了统一的几何框架，有望同时处理参数约束与梯度估计问题。该框架不仅涵盖既有的若干群结构方法，也将其与自然梯度学习联系起来，为设计更广泛的高效算法提供了系统路径。

Abstract: Variational Bayesian learning for deep neural networks offers many potential benefits. The difficulty in implementation arises from constraints on the parameters of the variational distributions, as well as the need for low-variance gradient estimators. Our recent work proposes Lie group methods for multiplicative or affine groups that naturally overcome such difficulties. Lie groups are essentially parameter spaces with a binary operator such as addition or multiplication, which allows for natural updates within the parameter space. In this article, we streamline and extend our Lie group framework to any general Lie group, and give an explicit relation to standard natural-gradient variational learning methods such as the Bayesian learning rule (BLR). In particular, we prove that linear approximations of the Lie group algorithm give generalizations of the BLR. The key ingredient is the harmonic exponential family, a general class of Lie group exponential families associated to representations of Lie groups. Our work provides a systematic method to construct from such representations efficiently implementable algorithms for variational learning.

</details>


### [155] [The Price of List Replicability: Tight Bounds for Bias Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04532)
*Michael Chen,Atieh Bastan,A. Pavan,N. V. Vinodchandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究估计多个硬币偏差时，列表可复现算法所需的样本数；给出了首个非平凡下界及匹配算法，确定了不同列表大小下的最优样本复杂度（相差至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知列表大小为 d+1 的算法需要约为 d³/ε² 的样本，但该问题没有非平凡的样本复杂度下界，因此尚不清楚维度带来的代价是否可以降低，也无法判断已有算法是否接近最优。

Method: 论文从理论上分析列表可复现估计任务：建立样本复杂度下界，并设计改进算法，使其上界与下界在对数因子范围内匹配。摘要未进一步说明下界证明和算法构造的具体技术。

Result: 当列表大小为 d+1 时，总样本复杂度为约 d²/ε²，精确地说为 ̃Θ(d²/ε²)。更一般地，对任意 k∈[d+1,2ᵈ]，上下界均为 ̃Θ(d²/(ε² log k))。因此，列表大小增大可按 log k 因子降低样本需求；在列表大小为 d+1 时，二次维度依赖在对数因子范围内不可避免。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为此问题提供非平凡样本复杂度下界，并通过匹配算法确定了多种列表大小下的最优样本复杂度，表明已有算法的维度依赖可以显著改善，但二次维度代价仍是内在的（忽略对数因子）。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of list replicable algorithms for estimating the biases of $d$ coins. An algorithm is $(k,\varepsilon,δ)$-list replicable if for every bias vector $\mathbf{p}\in[0,1]^d$, there is a set of outputs $L_{\mathbf{p}}\subseteq \mathbb{R}^d$ of at most $k$ elements such that every $\mathbf{y}\in L_{\mathbf{p}}$ satisfies $\lVert\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{p}\rVert_\infty\leq\varepsilon$, and the algorithm output is in $L_{\mathbf{p}}$ with probability at least $1-δ$. Prior work gave a $(d+1,\varepsilon,δ)$-list replicable algorithm with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{d^3}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$, but no nontrivial sample complexity lower bound was known.
  We establish the first non-trivial sample complexity lower bound for this problem and give an improved algorithm that matches it up to polylogarithmic factors. Together, these results determine the optimal sample complexity for list size $d+1$ to be $\widetildeΘ\left(\frac{d^2}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$, showing that the quadratic dependence on dimension is inherent up to polylogarithmic factors. More generally, for every $k\in[d+1,2^d]$, we establish matching upper and lower bounds of $\widetildeΘ\left(\frac{d^2}{\varepsilon^2\log k}\right)$ on the total sample complexity.

</details>


### [156] [CANDO: Conservative Action-Noise Directional Optimization for Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04543)
*Zeyu Gao,Chengkai Hou,Chaoyue Li,Zhuangzhe Wu,Jiaming Liu,Yao Mu,Peng Jia,Li Zhang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种保守的噪声方向优化方法，并将其与动作空间残差结合，在保留预训练生成策略结构的同时提升在线强化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练生成策略能提供有价值的机器人行为先验，但在线强化学习若调整不当，可能破坏离线示范中学到的结构。现有噪声空间残差方法依赖固定的潜变量可行区域，可能排除有益的噪声调整；动作空间残差虽较省样本，仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出保守噪声方向优化：冻结生成策略，围绕高斯潜变量样本学习依赖状态与噪声的调整方向，并以软保守约束控制适应过程。该约束结合保守价值估计与门控执行，不再预设潜变量可行区域。进一步提出保守动作—噪声方向优化，在噪声空间结构化改进的基础上加入动作空间残差，实现局部动作修正。

Result: 实验显示，噪声方向方法缩小了噪声空间与动作空间残差学习的差距；结合两者的方案进一步提升效果，达到七十六点一的归一化曲线下面积和百分之九十一点四的最终成功率，较最佳单空间方法分别提高六点六和四点一个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过以保守价值评估和门控执行替代固定潜变量边界，噪声空间策略能够在保护预训练行为结构的同时进行更灵活的在线改进；进一步融合动作空间残差，可获得优于单一空间优化的表现。

Abstract: Pretrained generative policies provide strong behavior priors for robot control, but adapting them efficiently with online reinforcement learning remains difficult. A central challenge is how to improve a frozen generative policy without destroying the structure learned from offline demonstrations. Noise-space residual methods address this by modifying the policy's input noise, but existing noise-space methods typically regulate adaptation through fixed latent feasible regions, which may exclude valuable noise edits. In contrast, action-space residual methods are more sample-efficient, but their efficiency can still be further improved. We propose Conservative Noise Directional Optimization (CNDO), a noise-space residual RL method that improves a frozen generative policy by learning state- and noise-conditioned directions around Gaussian latent samples. CNDO removes predefined latent feasible regions and instead regulates noise-space adaptation through the soft conservative constraint, which consists of conservative value estimation and gated execution. We further introduce Conservative Action-Noise Directional Optimization (CANDO), which adds an action-space residual, combining structured latent-space improvement with local action correction. Experiments show that, once CNDO narrows the gap between noise-space and action-space residual learning, CANDO further improves over single-space optimization, achieving 76.1 NAUC and a 91.4% final success rate, surpassing the best single-space results by 6.6 NAUC and 4.1% success rate. More demos and info are available on https://cando-rl.github.io/.

</details>


### [157] [Curriculum unlearning in humans and neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04565)
*Jirko Rubruck,Alice Zhang,Andrew Saxe,Christopher Summerfield*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，由简单到复杂的课程能帮助人类和神经网络克服对简单规则的偏好及其造成的表征压缩，从而学会更适合任务的规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由简单到复杂的学习课程常能提升学习效果，但其背后的计算机制尚不清楚。研究旨在解释为何先学简单规则、再学例外的安排有助于学习者。

Method: 研究采用经典行为任务，结合人类实验与神经网络建模，考察学习者的规则偏好、内部表征变化，以及课程顺序对学习的影响；并检验预先接触简单任务、初始表征压缩程度等因素带来的预测。

Result: 人类和神经网络在学习中都表现出简单性偏好，倾向于学习虽简单却无法充分拟合数据的规则。在神经网络中，这种偏好伴随神经表征压缩。由简单到复杂的课程可帮助两类学习者克服压缩，形成维度更适合任务的表征与策略。预先接触简单任务、初始表征更压缩的学习者，确实更能从这类课程中获益。

Conclusion: 课程的作用不只是逐步增加难度，还可能帮助学习者修正或摆脱先前形成的简单规则。研究为由简单到复杂课程的有效性提供了计算解释：它促使学习者建立足够丰富的表征，以适应任务中的复杂规则和例外。

Abstract: During learning, humans often benefit from curricula that teach simple rules before exceptions. However, we lack a computational explanation for this phenomenon. Here, using a canonical behavioural task and modelling with neural networks, we develop and test a theory for why simple-to-complex curricula succeed. We show that during learning, both humans and neural networks show simplicity biases, a tendency to learn simple rules that are underfit to the data. In neural networks this bias is accompanied by compression of neural representations. Simple-to-complex curricula help both types of learners overcome this compression, developing representations (and policy) with appropriate dimensionality for the task. We test and confirm a further prediction from this theory: learners that have been pre-exposed to simple tasks and with more compressed initial representations benefit more from curricula. These findings suggest that curricula help with ``unlearning'' of simple rules.

</details>


### [158] [Ground-Wing: A Parametric 3D Wing-in-Ground-Effect Dataset for Benchmarking Neural Surrogate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04571)
*Andrea Cenzato,Lukas Karch,Andre Luckow,Yoran Pichon,Francesco Pasqua,Abouzar Ghasemi,Rishikesh Ranade,Ira Shokar,Julia Kowalski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文构建了一个针对地面效应三维机翼的计算流体力学基准数据集，并以六类神经网络评估其预测机翼气动分布及整体升阻性能的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面效应会显著改变机翼周围的流动与气动力，但现有机翼和翼型数据集缺少这一场景，难以充分检验神经网络替代模型对赛车应用中复杂气动条件的预测能力。论文旨在弥补这一基准数据缺口。

Method: 研究采用雷诺平均纳维—斯托克斯方程开展七百七十次数值模拟，覆盖一百九十四种独特机翼构型。构型变化包括二十七种翼型、四种离地高度，以及后掠角、弦长、锥度、迎角和翼尖扭转。随后比较六类神经网络架构，涵盖卷积网络、图网络、变换器类网络和联合嵌入预测架构；预测目标为表面压力系数与壁面剪切应力分布，并据此计算整体阻力和升力系数。

Result: 论文提供了用于神经网络气动替代模型评测的数据集，并设计了跨多种架构的比较任务。摘要未报告各模型的具体误差、性能排名或优胜模型，因此无法据此判断哪种架构表现最佳。

Conclusion: 该数据集以具有代表性的赛车机翼构型和地面效应工况，补充了现有气动基准的不足，可用于评估替代模型对复杂三维流场及整体气动力的预测能力。其价值主要在于提供具有挑战性的统一基准，而非摘要中所述的某个模型性能结论。

Abstract: This paper presents a CFD dataset of a highly parametrized 3D wing operating in ground effect, designed for benchmarking neural surrogate models for aerodynamic prediction, using a wing shape and flow conditions typically encountered in motorsport applications. The dataset comprises 770 RANS simulations of 194 unique wing configurations, sampled across 27 airfoil profile variants and 4 distances from the ground, spanning variations in wing sweep, chord, taper, angle of attack, and tip twist. Six neural network architectures representing distinct paradigms --- convolutional (DoMINO), graph-based (X-MeshGraphNet), transformer-based (AB-UPT, GeoTransolver, GAOT), and JEPA-based (AeroJEPA) --- are evaluated on their ability to predict surface pressure coefficient and wall shear stress distributions, from which integral drag and lift coefficients are recovered. The dataset is intended to be used as a challenging benchmark for surrogate CFD models, due to the uniqueness of the ground effect being absent from other wing and airfoil datasets.

</details>


### [159] [SCANO: Restoring Boundary-Condition Completeness in Sampled Meshes with a Sliced-Cross-Attention Neural Operator for 3D Thermal Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04603)
*Tianyu Li,Zhiwei Cao,Qingang Zhang,Ruihang Wang,Binyang Song,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出两种边界条件处理模块，缓解网格抽样造成的边界信息失真，使神经算子在局部区域和完整网格上的预测更一致，并显著提升精度与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万单元网格会带来显存压力，因此神经算子通常在抽样网格上训练，再用于完整网格或局部区域。但抽样可能遗漏或扭曲边界信息，导致预测精度下降，尤其使局部区域预测偏离完整网格预测。论文旨在明确这种边界条件何时保持一致，以及如何汇总和注入边界数据。

Method: 论文将解表示为边界数据与类型相关测度的积分，并据此归纳抽样造成的三类失真：边界覆盖不完整或全局状态依赖样本、边界面按数量而非面积加权、边界输入被压缩为低维向量。由此提出四项可在训练前检验的一致性条件，并设计两个与骨干网络无关的模块：边界求积注意力模块对所有边界面按类型和面积加权汇总为切片标记，供各层查询点读取；锚定全局状态模块则利用不依赖当前抽样点的锚点估计全局状态。两者与可扩展的消息传递和切片注意力骨干结合，构成模型。」

Result: 在受控基准、公开基准及包含百万单元网格的数据中心热模拟数据集上，边界求积注意力模块提升了所有受测骨干网络的表现，在完整网格预测中最高将误差降低至原来的十五分之一。锚定全局状态模块使局部区域预测与完整网格预测更一致；在公开基准的多数骨干网络上，区域预测误差至少降低十倍。两个模块结合后，在两个基准上都使原有最佳骨干网络的完整网格误差接近减半。模型预测房间尺度场耗时不到一秒，比对整个房间进行锚点自注意力计算快一百倍以上。

Conclusion: 边界信息的覆盖、面积权重、表示方式及全局状态是否受抽样影响，是维持抽样训练与完整网格推理一致性的关键。论文提出的两种模块能有效改善边界条件处理，并兼顾预测精度与计算效率，为大规模网格上的神经算子部署提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Training neural operators on million-cell meshes can exceed GPU memory limits. Models are therefore trained on subsamples and deployed on whole meshes or selected regions. However, subsampling can distort boundary information, degrading accuracy. We ask when boundary conditioning under sampling stays consistent with whole-mesh training, and how to aggregate and inject boundary data. Representing the solution as an integral of boundary data against type-dependent measures, we show that subsampling distorts this conditioning in three ways: incomplete boundary coverage or a sample-dependent global state, face weighting by count rather than area, and compression of boundary inputs into a low-dimensional vector. This analysis yields four measurable consistency conditions checkable before training. We satisfy them with two backbone-agnostic modules. Boundary Quadrature Attention (BQA) aggregates all boundary faces, typed and area-weighted, into slice tokens that every query point reads at every layer. Anchored Global State (AGS) estimates the global state from anchors independent of the sampled points. Adding both to a scalable message-passing and slice-attention backbone yields SCANO. We evaluate the approach on controlled and public benchmarks and a data-centre thermal simulation dataset (DCTS) containing million-cell meshes. On both benchmarks, BQA improves every backbone, reducing whole-mesh error up to 15-fold. On all three datasets, AGS aligns regional with whole-mesh predictions, cutting regional error at least tenfold for most backbones on the public benchmark. Together, the modules nearly halve the best original backbone's whole-mesh error on both benchmarks. SCANO predicts a room-scale field in under one second, over 100 times faster than whole-room anchor self-attention.

</details>


### [160] [CAPSkip: Cancellation-Aware Prefix Skipping for Selective Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04611)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种考虑梯度抵消的选择性反向传播方法：在控制末层梯度变化的前提下跳过部分样本；在所述实验中，相比近似等时长的密集训练，准确率提高零点二五个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逐步选择性反向传播方法常依据损失或梯度大小给样本排序，但单个样本梯度的大小无法反映不同梯度向量之间的抵消。因此，被跳过样本的梯度总和可能远小于各自梯度大小所暗示的数值，标量评分难以准确衡量跳过一组样本的影响。

Method: 提出“抵消感知前缀跳过”方法。每个小批次仍对所有样本执行前向计算；在模型已正确分类的样本中，按末层梯度范数从小到大排序，寻找最长前缀，使该前缀梯度向量之和相对于整个批次的末层梯度仍处于给定的相对误差预算内，并跳过这些样本的反向传播。由于累计偏差不一定随前缀长度单调变化，方法会扫描全部前缀，并利用因子化格拉姆矩阵降低扫描开销。该方法复用前向计算中的秩一贡献，并通过仅对选中样本的行执行反向传播来节省计算。

Result: 在对数据集图像分类任务中，使用该方法对小型数据集上的视觉变换器进行一百零三轮微调，耗时为一百零三轮密集训练的零点五七六倍。与耗时近似匹配的五十九轮密集训练相比，准确率高零点二五个百分点；三次实验的区间为高零点零八至零点四二个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法利用样本梯度之间的抵消关系，在控制原始末层梯度变化的条件下减少反向传播计算，并在所述微调实验中展现出时间效率和准确率优势。不过，理论界限仅适用于精确算术下原始末层梯度的变化，不适用于更深层梯度、优化器更新或完整训练轨迹；因此不能据此保证这些量也受到相同程度的控制。

Abstract: Per-step selective back-propagation forwards every example of a mini-batch but back-propagates only some of them. Most rules rank examples by loss or gradient magnitude, but such scalar scores ignore cancellation between per-example gradients: the gradient removed by a set can be much smaller than its individual magnitudes suggest. We introduce CAPSkip (Cancellation-Aware Prefix Skipping). Among the examples the model already classifies correctly, CAPSkip skips the longest prefix, in increasing order of last-layer gradient norm, whose vector sum stays within a relative budget $b$ of the batch's last-layer gradient. In exact arithmetic this bounds the change of the raw final-layer gradient, using rank-one contributions already available from the forward pass; the bound does not extend to deeper layers, optimizer updates, or the training trajectory. Because the cumulative distortion is not monotone in the prefix length, CAPSkip scans all prefixes; a factorised Gram matrix makes the scan cheap and keeps it on the device. With activation reuse and selected-row backward, CAPSkip fine-tunes DeiT-S on CIFAR-100 for 103 epochs in 0.576x the time of 103-epoch dense training, and is +0.25 pp [+0.08, +0.42] above an approximately time-matched 59-epoch dense run (n=3).

</details>


### [161] [Policy Network Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04614)
*Jiate Liu,Deheng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出扩散式条件策略网络参数生成方法，可根据任务生成强化学习策略网络参数，并在多种环境及未见环境中展现良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型已能生成高质量图像与视频，但能否生成强化学习策略网络参数，以及此类参数是否具备跨环境泛化能力，仍有待探索。既有方法在生成网络规模等方面也存在限制。

Method: 提出扩散式条件策略网络参数生成框架，由基于变换器的自编码器和任务条件扩散模型组成。自编码器通过将网络参数划分为片段进行编码，以高压缩率表示参数；扩散模型则根据环境任务条件生成对应的策略网络参数。

Result: 单一统一模型能够为经典控制、雅达利游戏和穆乔科等不同领域的多种任务合成高质量策略，并可生成参数量超过既有方法所能处理规模的网络。在未见环境中，生成的参数还能带来更快收敛，并取得有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 研究表明，扩散模型不仅可用于生成内容，也有望用于生成强化学习策略网络参数。该方法在多任务适用性、网络规模和未见环境泛化方面展现潜力，但摘要未提供具体量化结果或对适用边界的详细说明。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown remarkable progress in AI-generated content, including images and videos. In this paper, we explore whether diffusion models can generate the parameters of policy networks in reinforcement learning (RL), and whether the generated policies exhibit generalization capabilities. To achieve this, we propose Diffusion-based Conditional Policy Network Parameter Generation (DPPG), which consists of a Transformer-based autoencoder and a task-conditioned diffusion model. The autoencoder achieves a high compression rate by patchifying network parameters, while the task-conditioned diffusion model generates corresponding parameters conditioned on different environments. Our experiments demonstrate that, with a single unified DPPG model, we can synthesize high-quality policies across a diverse set of tasks from different environment domains, including Classic Control, Atari games, and MuJoCo. Additionally, our method can generate networks with a larger number of parameters compared to previous approaches. Furthermore, we find that in previously unseen environments, DPPG can generate parameters that achieve faster convergence and reach competitive performance, showcasing the potential future applications of parameter generation via diffusion.

</details>


### [162] [Does LinUCB Need Valid Confidence Sets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04641)
*Ahmed Ben Yahmed,Marc Abeille,David Janz,Clément Calauzènes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，即使线性上置信界算法采用任意固定的置信宽度参数，只要动作集合具有适当曲率，仍可实现次线性遗憾；曲率促使算法探索各个方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性上置信界算法通常依赖有效的置信集合与乐观选择来保证性能。本文探讨这种置信宽度是否不可或缺，并研究动作集合的几何性质能否在缺乏乐观保证时仍驱动有效探索。

Method: 将算法的置信宽度替换为任意固定的正数，并分析具有光滑性和强凸性的动作集合。作者提出一种带对数上限的谱势函数，限制已充分探索方向对势函数的贡献，据此推导设计矩阵最小特征值的下界，并分析动作集合曲率如何促使算法探索所有方向。

Result: 作者证明该算法具有高概率次线性遗憾界，因此有效的置信集合并非算法成功的必要条件。当置信宽度取标准量级、约为维度平方根乘以对数时间平方根时，轮次为「Ｔ」、维度为「ｄ」的遗憾上界为「大Ｏ（光滑性与强凸性参数之比 × ｄ × 根号（Ｔ乘以对数Ｔ））」，另有迭代对数因子；相较经典保证，时间跨度依赖改善了对数时间平方根因子。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了一种不同于乐观原则的探索机制：动作集合的曲率本身即可推动算法覆盖各个方向，从而支持次线性遗憾。新的谱势分析工具也带来更紧的遗憾界，尤其改善了标准置信宽度下对轮次数的依赖。

Abstract: We show that replacing LinUCB's usual confidence width $β$ by any fixed $α>0$ preserves sublinear regret when the action set is $M$-smooth and $m$-strongly convex. Our high-probability regret bound for this generalisation, $α$-LinUCB, establishes that valid confidence sets are not necessary for the success of LinUCB. We identify the mechanism behind this result: action-set curvature drives $α$-LinUCB to explore every direction, even when optimism is not guaranteed. To analyse this mechanism, we introduce a logarithmically capped spectral potential that, unlike the standard log-determinant potential, caps contributions from well-explored directions. This yields lower bounds on the smallest eigenvalue of the design matrix. At the standard confidence width $α=β\asymp\sqrt{d\log T}$, our $T$-round regret bound in $d$ dimensions is $O\bigl(\frac{M}{m}d\sqrt{T\log T}\bigr)$, up to iterated-logarithmic factors, improving the horizon dependence of the classical LinUCB guarantee by a factor of $\sqrt{\log T}$.

</details>


### [163] [Exploiting Biases in Attention for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04657)
*Charles Herr,Gabriele Spadaro,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现视觉变换器中不同令牌对注意力计算的影响并不相同，并提出在注意力公式中加入输入相关偏置，以估计令牌的重要性并据此裁剪注意力图，从而降低计算开销，同时保留大部分分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力矩阵中各列对最终计算的贡献存在差异，而且这种差异会随网络深度增加而更加明显。这说明令牌可能具有不依赖具体查询的整体重要性；若能识别这些令牌，便可减少不必要的注意力计算。

Method: 在查询与键的点积中加入依赖输入的偏置项。该偏置为每个令牌提供一个标量，用来估计其在注意力过程中对其他令牌的预期影响，并将这一估计作为裁剪注意力图的依据。

Result: 在多种规模的变换器模型上，该方法显著减少了注意力计算量和推理延迟，同时保留了原有的大部分分类准确率。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模令牌的整体影响力，可以更有针对性地裁剪注意力计算，在较少损失分类性能的情况下提升视觉变换器的计算效率。

Abstract: Attention is known to be sparse in vision transformers. In particular, we analyze the unequal relevance of attention-matrix columns to the final computation and show that this phenomenon becomes increasingly pronounced with network depth. This can be interpreted as saying that tokens have an absolute importance for the attention process, independently of queries. Inspired by this, we propose a reformulation of the attention formula that makes this phenomenon explicit by adding an input-dependent bias to the query-key dot product. We show that this new term gives each token a scalar value indicating its anticipated influence on other tokens during the attention process, and can thus serve as a proxy for pruning attention maps. Across multiple transformer model sizes, this allows us to substantially reduce the computational cost of attention, in terms of both FLOPs and inference latency, while retaining most of the original classification accuracy. We publicly released the code and models at https://github.com/EIDOSLAB/AttentionWithBias.

</details>


### [164] [Leakage-free evaluation of residual graph convolutional networks for network intrusion detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04660)
*Zahid Iqbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在严格控制数据泄漏的实验中，图神经网络未比普通多层感知机带来可测的准确率提升；树模型整体更强，而图模型在特征受噪声干扰时更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有图神经网络入侵检测研究可能因测试流量参与训练图构建、重复流量跨越训练与测试集，或仅报告单次划分而高估性能。本研究旨在排除这些乐观偏差后，衡量残差图卷积网络的真实增益，并与较简单模型公平比较。

Method: 研究使用两个入侵检测数据集，按标签清理完全重复的流量记录，其中一个数据集删除了百分之六十九的流量。图只根据训练流量构建；验证与测试流量预测时再连接至最近的训练流量。研究比较两层残差图网络、普通多层感知机、树集成及其他模型，并使用三个不同随机种子重新划分数据；另进行消融、留一攻击类别族测试和特征噪声测试。

Result: 残差图网络在两个数据集上的二分类F1值分别为百分之九十八点一一和百分之九十点五七，与不使用图结构的多层感知机及其他图网络表现相当。树集成在第一个数据集上高出一点四八至一点五七个F1百分点，并在两个数据集的精确率—召回率曲线下面积上领先；其多分类宏平均F1值为百分之九十三点零八，而残差图网络仅为百分之六十四点五一，差距主要来自后者将良性流量误分为少数攻击类别。各项消融，包括改为传导式建图，均未带来超出随机种子波动的变化。特征加入标准差为零点五的高斯噪声后，残差图网络F1值仍为百分之九十六点一六，而梯度提升树降至百分之零点六七。

Conclusion: 在防止数据泄漏的评估设置下，流量图上的消息传递没有显示出相较普通感知机的准确率优势，树集成在总体检测表现上仍更强。图模型的潜在价值主要体现在对特征噪声的鲁棒性，而非常规条件下提升准确率。

Abstract: Graph neural networks are increasingly proposed for network intrusion detection, yet many reported results come from protocols in which test flows shape the graph used for training, duplicated flows sit on both sides of the split, or a single split is reported. This study measures what a residual graph convolutional network gains over simpler models once these sources of optimism are removed. CICIDS2017 and NF-UNSW-NB15-v2 were cleaned with label-aware removal of exact duplicates, removing 69% of NF-UNSW-NB15-v2 flows. Flows were linked in a nearest-neighbour graph built from training flows only, and validation and test flows were attached to their nearest training flows at prediction time. A two-layer residual network was compared with eight models under the same settings, including a nearest-neighbour vote over the same graph, gradient-boosted trees and a random forest, over three seeds that each draw a new split, with ablations, leave-one-family-out and feature-noise tests. On CICIDS2017 the residual network reached a binary F1 of 98.11% (SD 0.18) and on NF-UNSW-NB15-v2 90.57% (0.38), indistinguishable from a multilayer perceptron without the graph and from other graph networks. Tree ensembles were 1.48 to 1.57 F1 points higher on CICIDS2017 and led on the area under the precision-recall curve on both datasets; their CICIDS2017 multiclass macro-F1 reached 93.08% against 64.51%, a gap mainly from benign flows the residual network assigned to small attack classes. No ablation, including transductive graph construction, changed F1 beyond seed variation. Under Gaussian feature noise (SD 0.5), the residual network kept an F1 of 96.16%, while XGBoost fell to 0.67%. Under a leakage-controlled protocol, message passing over flow graphs adds no measurable accuracy over a perceptron, and tree ensembles remain stronger on both; the advantage of graph models lies in robustness to feature noise.

</details>


### [165] [Best-of-Depth: Learning Exploratory Dynamics in Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04663)
*Shota Takashiro,Takeshi Kojima,Masanori Koyama,Kohei Hayashi,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“深度择优”训练方法，让循环变换器沿不同计算深度产生的答案都成为候选解，从而提升解的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环变换器可以通过重复更新隐状态增加推理计算，但只用最终深度的答案训练，并不会鼓励模型在中间深度探索有用的替代解。现有中间监督方法也可能依赖特定深度的标签，限制了训练方式。

Method: 深度择优将模型在多个深度上的输出视为候选集合，并以跨深度的预测损失进行训练；不同深度可以匹配不同的有效目标，无需修改模型结构或提供按深度区分的标签。论文还通过精确匹配分析，说明该目标与提升解的覆盖率相关。

Result: 在数独式皇后摆放问题和图着色任务中，该方法的解覆盖率优于仅训练最终深度及中间监督基线。皇后摆放任务的覆盖率达到百分之九十一点零三，而最终深度训练为百分之四十五点七五。实验还显示，模型能发现超出训练深度的解，并在熟悉输入上找回教师候选集中未提供的解。对预训练循环语言模型进行微调后，在各固定深度分别生成程序的评估设置中，深度择优也取得了更高的汇总代码生成成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，循环计算的训练不应只关注轨迹末端，也可以把整条计算轨迹用于发现多种解。深度择优在多类任务中提升了解的覆盖能力，并展现出超越训练深度及既有教师候选的潜力。

Abstract: Looped Transformers extend inference-time computation through repeated latent-state updates, but final-depth training does not directly encourage useful intermediate alternatives. We introduce Best-of-Depth (BoD), which trains a recurrent trajectory as a candidate set by minimizing prediction loss across depths. Different valid targets can be matched at different depths without architectural changes or depth-specific labels. An exact-match analysis connects this objective to solution coverage. On N-Queens completion and graph coloring, BoD improves coverage over final-depth and intermediate-supervision objectives. N-Queens coverage reaches 91.03%, versus 45.75% for final-depth training. Further experiments show discovery beyond the training depth and recovery of solutions withheld from teacher pools on familiar inputs. Fine-tuning a pretrained looped language model with BoD yields higher pooled code-generation success than final-depth training when programs are generated separately at evaluated fixed depths. These results support training recurrent computation for solution discovery along its trajectory.

</details>


### [166] [Feature-Based and Deep Learning Methods for Astronomical Light Curve Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04664)
*V. Baymakanov,Kh. Khamitov,A. Raimova,A. Isaeva,R. Taran,M. Zedan,M. Kornilov,M. Demianenko,K. Malanchev,M. Hushchyn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较了基于特征的机器学习与端到端深度学习在变星分类中的表现；在约四万个、类别分布不均衡的天体样本上，传统模型表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习有望直接从观测数据中学习表示，但其在天文时间序列分类中的实际效果仍不明确。研究旨在评估其与传统特征工程方法的差异，并分析性能差距的可能原因。

Method: 使用兹威基瞬变巡天的恒星嵌入数据集，对七个类别的变星进行分类。比较以具有物理依据的特征训练的传统机器学习模型，与直接输入原始光变曲线训练的端到端深度学习模型。

Result: 基于特征的传统模型持续优于深度学习模型。有限的标注数据是性能差距的原因之一；此外，光变曲线具有多变量、不规则采样和形态多样等特点，也增加了深度模型学习与泛化的难度。

Conclusion: 在现有训练数据规模下，当前深度学习模型尚未能有效泛化。研究为后续工作建立了定量基线，并指出扩大标注数据集、利用大型未标注巡天数据进行自监督预训练，以及改进模型方法，是发挥深度学习潜力的重要方向。

Abstract: We compare feature-based machine learning and end-to-end deep learning for variable star classification using the Zwicky Transient Facility (ZTF) StarEmbed dataset, which contains about 40,000 objects across seven imbalanced classes. Classical models trained on physically motivated features consistently outperform deep networks trained directly on raw light curves. We find that limited labeled data contributes to the performance gap, while the multivariate, irregularly sampled, and morphologically diverse nature of light curves poses additional challenges for deep learning models. While deep learning holds promise for learning representations directly from observations, our results indicate that current models fail to generalize with the available training set size. These findings motivate further investigation of the benefits of scaling labeled datasets and self-supervised pretraining on large unlabeled surveys. This work establishes a quantitative baseline for future research and highlights the need for further innovations to unlock the potential of deep learning in astronomical time series analysis.

</details>


### [167] [FedCSGA: Client Selection Based on Genetic Algorithm for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04667)
*Ghani Haider,Majid Kundroo,Leo Zhang,Jinchul Choi,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于遗传算法的联邦学习客户端选择方法，通过同时考虑客户端资源状况、数据差异及历史参与情况，提升模型精度并加快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构边缘环境中的客户端在数据分布和计算、通信能力方面存在差异，都会影响联邦学习的收敛效率与模型效果。许多现有方法分别处理统计异质性或系统异质性，未能充分考虑两者在多轮训练中的耦合和动态影响，也可能造成客户端长期被偏向选择。

Method: 提出基于遗传算法的客户端选择方法，将客户端子集编码为染色体，并通过非线性适应度函数评估候选子集。该函数联合纳入资源感知指标与历史参与权重，以捕捉客户端组合层面的相互作用，减轻持续选择偏差，并平衡数据效用与系统效率。

Result: 在时尚商品图像、彩色物体图像、百类别图像和微型图像等数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多种异构设置下优于对比基线，准确率最高提升百分之七，并以更少的通信轮次实现更快收敛。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持在客户端选择中联合建模统计因素与系统因素的有效性。该方法能够提升异构联邦环境下训练的精度、效率与稳健性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training of machine learning models across distributed clients while preserving data privacy. In practical deployments, FL relies on selecting a subset of clients in each communication round to reduce communication cost, mitigate stragglers, and improve training efficiency under heterogeneous data and system conditions. In heterogeneous edge computing environments, both statistical heterogeneity and system heterogeneity significantly affect convergence efficiency and model performance. However, existing client selection methods often address these factors independently, overlooking their coupled and dynamic impact across communication rounds. To address this limitation, we propose FedCSGA, a Client Selection method for Federated learning based on a Genetic Algorithm. FedCSGA encodes client subsets as chromosomes and evaluates these subsets through a non-linear fitness function that jointly integrates resource-aware metrics and history-aware participation weighting. This design captures subset-level interactions, mitigates persistent selection bias, and balances statistical utility with system efficiency. Extensive experiments on FMNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR- 100, and Tiny-ImageNet demonstrate that FedCSGA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse heterogeneity settings, achieving accuracy improvements of up to 7.0% and faster convergence with fewer communication rounds. These results validate the effectiveness of jointly modeling statistical and system factors to achieve robust and efficient learning in heterogeneous federated environments.

</details>


### [168] [Sheaf-MPNN: A Library for Sheaf Neural Networks as Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04670)
*Alessio Borgi,Mario Severino,Luke Braithwaite,Steve Azzolin,Rémi Bourgerie,Olga Zaghen,Emanuele Mule,Riccardo Ali,Leo Minh Kustermann,Edoardo Coppola,Antonio Purificato,Stefano Fiorini,Christopher Irwin,Filippo Spiga,Fabrizio Silvestri,Michael Bronstein,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出剪切消息传递神经网络库，以局部传输与聚合实现剪切扩散，避免显式构造全局稀疏拉普拉斯矩阵，并提供统一模型实现、基准测试和理论等价性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图神经网络通常假设相邻节点的特征处于相同空间，这一假设会导致过平滑，并削弱模型在异配图上的表现。剪切神经网络通过为节点和边设置各自的向量空间，并学习邻接特征间的限制映射来缓解这一问题。然而，现有实现往往显式构造全局稀疏分块拉普拉斯矩阵，带来额外的内存和计算开销，限制了可扩展性；不同架构也缺少统一、可复现的比较框架。

Method: 论文提出剪切消息传递神经网络库，将剪切扩散重新表述为沿局部关联关系进行的特征传输与聚合，从而无需构造全局拉普拉斯矩阵。该库在统一的消息传递框架下实现十五类剪切神经网络，支持同质与异质图、有向与无向图，以及对角、一般、正交和低秩限制映射，并支持基于注意力的映射预测器。论文还构建涵盖节点级、图级、异质图学习与计算效率的基准测试套件，并分析局部消息传递形式与原有方法的理论等价性，以及保持传播动力学所需的架构机制。

Result: 论文建立了所提出的消息传递实现与对应原始剪切扩散方法之间的理论等价关系，并指出实现等价传播所需的关键架构机制。摘要未报告具体任务上的数值性能提升或效率增幅；其主要成果是提供统一实现与系统化基准评测基础。

Conclusion: 该工作将剪切神经网络的实现从全局矩阵运算转向局部消息传递，有望降低实现中的内存与计算负担，并使多种剪切架构能够在标准图学习框架中统一实现、比较和扩展。

Abstract: Standard Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) implicitly assume that neighboring nodes share the same feature space, which contributes to oversmoothing and poor performance on heterophilic graphs. Sheaf Neural Networks (SNNs) remove this assumption by giving each node and edge its own vector space and learning restriction maps that transform features as they move between neighbors. These maps define a sheaf Laplacian that drives diffusion over the graph, but existing implementations often assemble it explicitly as a global sparse block matrix, which adds memory and computational overhead and can hinder scalability. In this paper, we introduce Sheaf-MPNN, a PyTorch Geometric library that reformulates sheaf diffusion as local incidence-wise transport and aggregation, eliminating the need to construct the global Laplacian. Sheaf-MPNN implements fifteen families of SNNs under a unified message-passing framework, each supporting both homogeneous and heterogeneous graphs, and directed and undirected settings, with diagonal, general, orthogonal, and low-rank restriction maps and attention-based predictors. We further provide a unified benchmarking suite covering node- and graph-level tasks, heterogeneous graph learning, and computational efficiency, enabling systematic and reproducible comparisons across architectures. We establish theoretical equivalence between these message-passing formulations and their original counterparts and identify the architectural mechanisms needed to preserve their propagation dynamics. This unified perspective provides a principled foundation for implementing, comparing, and extending sheaf-based architectures within standard graph learning frameworks. Source code is available at https://github.com/alessioborgi/sheaf-mpnn.

</details>


### [169] [Uncertainty-Aware Prototype Learning for Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04689)
*Jinwu Hu,Langyu Bian,Jian Sun,Dongjin Yang,Chao Shuai,Yufeng Wang,Kai Zhou,Bin Xiao,Mingkui Tan,Hongmin Cai,Wei Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向开放集测试时自适应的轻量级方法，通过不确定性区分已知类与未知类样本，并结合动态原型和对比式熵优化，减少未知样本对适应过程的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放集测试时自适应需要处理同时出现的分布偏移已知类样本与分布偏移未知类样本。现有方法多采用闭集假设，缺少可靠区分两类样本的机制，可能将未知类样本纳入提升置信度的适应过程，产生有害梯度并损害已知类识别性能。

Method: 提出可插拔方法“面向开放集测试时自适应的不确定性感知原型学习”。首先，结合分类器—原型得分与经小批次归一化的证据不确定性得分，区分已知类和未知类样本。其次，利用缓冲的已知类特征定期更新各类别的目标原型，使其逐步贴近估计的类别方向。最后，对已知类样本最小化熵、对未知类样本最大化熵；同时通过原型级对比学习，使已知类特征靠近对应目标原型，并降低未知类特征与已知类原型的最大相似度。

Result: 在多个CIFAR基准和Tiny-ImageNet-C上，UAPA优于最强基线；其平均开放集分类率（OSCR）分别提升2.08和1.28个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法通过样本筛分、动态原型更新及差异化熵与对比优化，降低未知类样本对测试时适应的负面影响，并提升开放集场景下的适应表现。

Abstract: Open-set test-time adaptation (TTA) aims to adapt source-trained models to unlabeled test streams where covariate-shifted in-distribution (csID) samples and covariate-shifted out-of-distribution (csOOD) samples may coexist. Existing TTA methods are typically designed under a closed-set assumption and therefore lack explicit mechanisms for reliably distinguishing csID from csOOD samples. Consequently, csOOD samples may be mistakenly included in confidence-enhancing adaptation, producing harmful gradients that degrade known-class performance. To address this, we propose Uncertainty-Aware Prototype Learning for Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation (UAPA), a plug-and-play method for reliable open-set adaptation. Specifically, we first introduce Uncertainty-Driven ID/OOD Separation, which combines a classifier-prototype score with a mini-batch-normalized evidence-derived uncertainty score to distinguish csID from csOOD samples. We then propose a Dynamic Prototype Refinement strategy that periodically refines class-specific target prototypes using buffered csID features to progressively follow the estimated class directions. Lastly, we introduce Contrastive Prototype Entropy Adaptation, which applies entropy minimization to csID samples and entropy maximization to csOOD samples, while prototype-level contrastive learning aligns csID features with their corresponding target prototypes and reduces the maximum similarity between csOOD features and known-class prototypes. Empirically, UAPA outperforms the strongest baselines on the CIFAR benchmarks and Tiny-ImageNet-C, improving average OSCR by 2.08 and 1.28 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [170] [Surrogate Models for Stochastic Al-Cu Phase-Field Solidification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04695)
*Simon Savukoski,Tatu Pinomaa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种条件扩散桥模型，用于快速预测二维铝铜合金凝固演化；在长时程预测准确性、枝晶细节保持和计算速度方面优于或快于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场模型能够解析快速合金凝固中的微观组织演化，但计算开销较大，限制了长时间模拟、随机集成研究以及不同工艺条件的系统筛选。研究旨在以较低成本获得可靠的后续状态预测。

Method: 提出方差保持型去噪扩散桥模型，以当前相场、浓度场和给定温度场为条件，预测下一时刻的相场与浓度场，并通过自回归方式连续推进预测。模型针对二维铝铜合金凝固过程进行训练与评估。

Result: 在两个未参与训练的热梯度条件下，该模型在长时程预测中，对全域平均相变量、全域平均铜浓度和枝晶尖端位置的误差均低于确定性卷积网络基线。它还能在长时间滚动预测中保留细致的枝晶结构，而确定性编码器—解码器模型的形貌会逐渐变得平滑。相较相场求解器，该模型预测相场和浓度演化约快七十三倍。

Conclusion: 该模型兼具较快推理速度与较好的长时程形貌保真度，有望用于加速工艺条件筛选及基于多次模拟的微观组织研究。摘要所述结果聚焦于二维铝铜体系及两个留出热梯度，应用到其他材料与条件仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Phase-field models resolve microstructural evolution during rapid alloy solidification, but their computational cost limits long trajectories, stochastic ensembles, and studies across process conditions. We present a variance-preserving denoising diffusion bridge model (VP-DDBM) for state-conditioned prediction of two-dimensional Al-Cu solidification. Given the current phase, concentration, and prescribed temperature fields, the model predicts the subsequent state and is applied autoregressively. Across two held-out thermal gradients, VP-DDBM produces lower long-horizon errors than deterministic U-Net and SI-ConvNeXt baselines in domain-mean phase, domain-mean Cu concentration, and leading-tip position. It also retains fine dendritic structure over long rollouts, whereas the deterministic U-Net progressively smooths the morphology. VP-DDBM predicts the phase and concentration evolution approximately $73\times$ faster than the phase-field solver, showing potential for faster process-condition screening and ensemble-based microstructure studies.

</details>


### [171] [TAPES: Task-Aligned Joint Predictive Pretraining for Transaction Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04747)
*Ruobing Zuo,Weiyu Han,Huilin He,Haoyu Jiang,Ze Zhao,Jie Sheng,Xiao Tan,Shuai Chen,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向下游任务效用的交易序列联合预测预训练框架，通过筛选预测目标并建模目标间的条件共现，提升多类金融任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交易记录可构造数值、变化量及相对于历史事件的关系等不同预测目标，而不同目标对历史表示的要求并不相同。仅凭目标本身的可预测性，无法判断其学得的表示是否有利于下游任务；此外，逐目标计算预测损失也不能显式捕捉多个目标之间的条件共现关系。

Method: 框架先依据可用字段与运算符构造有限候选目标集，再在预算相同的短期联合预训练试验中比较各目标集所学表示的下游效用，并据此选定目标集。随后重新初始化编码器，使用共享离散潜变量参数化的联合似然，对选定目标进行联合预训练，最后通过有监督适配完成下游任务。

Result: 在真实交易风险场景以及涵盖多种下游任务的公开金融基准上，该方法优于具有代表性的基线。消融实验显示，预测目标筛选和联合建模均对性能提升有所贡献。

Conclusion: 研究表明，交易序列预训练不应只关注目标的可预测性，还应依据下游效用选择目标，并建模目标间的依赖关系。该框架将这两点结合起来，为面向多样金融任务的交易表示学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Transaction event sequences support diverse prediction tasks in online commerce and digital financial services. The same transaction records can yield prediction targets with different semantics, including values, changes, and relations to past events. These targets impose different constraints on history representations, but their predictability alone does not establish the downstream utility of a target set. Moreover, target-wise prediction losses do not explicitly model conditional co-occurrence among targets. To address these challenges, we propose TAPES, a task-aligned joint predictive pretraining framework for transaction sequences. TAPES constructs a finite collection of candidate target sets from admissible field-operator combinations. It selects a target set according to the downstream utility of representations learned through short joint pretraining trials under matched budgets. It then reinitializes the encoder and pretrains it on the selected targets through a joint likelihood parameterized by a shared discrete latent variable, before downstream supervised adaptation. Experiments in a real-world transaction-risk setting and on multiple public financial benchmarks spanning diverse downstream tasks demonstrate that TAPES outperforms representative baselines. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of predictive target selection and joint modeling.

</details>


### [172] [What Does It Mean to Remove an Edge? Intervention-Aware Fidelity for Graph Neural Network Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04751)
*Rocco Mattia Di Mauro,Marco Grassia,Giuseppe Mangioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 图神经网络解释的删除保真度取决于删除操作如何作用于模型计算，因此不能只根据边排序评价解释效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见评估会删除排名靠前的边，再观察模型输出变化；但消息传递网络中的“删除边”并无唯一含义。结构删除会移除图中的边并重新计算度归一化、注意力等量；消息抑制则保留原有结构，只阻断该边传递的信息。若不说明干预方式，删除保真度的含义就不明确。

Method: 研究形式化定义了结构删除与消息抑制，并推导两者完全等价的充分条件：对于系数不依赖边集合的加性消息传递，两种操作等价；若聚合过程受零消息是否存在影响，或删边会改变其余消息的权重系数，则两者可能不同。研究据此分别构造针对两种干预的边归因排序，并交叉使用两种干预评估排序效果。

Result: 在九个数据集、不同深度的图同构网络、图卷积网络和第二代图注意力网络上进行实验。九个图同构网络对照均符合理论预测。以目标对数几率为评估量时，在可报告的图卷积网络和第二代图注意力网络实验单元中，有十个单元的两种排序相对保真度会随评估干预方式改变；其中九个单元里，某一排序在与其构造方式相同的干预下具有更大的删除曲线下面积。

Conclusion: 删除保真度不是边排序本身固有的属性，而是排序与干预方式共同决定的。评估时应明确报告干预方式、输出函数、解释目标和扰动单位；当科学问题无法唯一确定干预语义时，应比较不同干预方式下的结果。

Abstract: Graph neural network explanations are often evaluated via deletion fidelity, i.e., by deleting their top-ranked edges and measuring the resulting change in model output. Yet, in a message-passing GNN, "deleting an edge" does not specify a unique intervention. Structural deletion removes the edge from the graph and recomputes quantities such as degree normalization and attention, whereas message suppression leaves this structural context intact while blocking the message carried by the edge. The meaning of deletion fidelity therefore depends on what the intervention targets in the model computation. We formalize this distinction and derive sufficient conditions for exact equivalence. The two interventions coincide for additive message passing with edge-set-independent coefficients, but can diverge when aggregation depends on the presence of zero messages or when removing an edge changes the coefficients applied to surviving messages. We then construct intervention-specific edge attributions and evaluate each resulting ranking under both interventions, testing whether their relative fidelity changes with the evaluation intervention. Across nine datasets and a panel of GIN, GCN, and GATv2 models at depths 2--5, all nine GIN controls satisfy the equivalence predicted by the theory. On the target logit, the relative fidelity of the two rankings depends on the evaluation intervention in 10 of 11 reportable GCN and GATv2 cells, and in nine of them each ranking has the larger deletion area under the intervention it was built with. Deletion fidelity is thus not a property of an edge ranking alone. Evaluation protocols should explicitly report the intervention, output functional, target, and perturbation unit, and test alternative intervention semantics when the scientific question does not uniquely determine one.

</details>


### [173] [Non-Interfering Asymmetric AdamW Updates for Constrained Exploration of Equifinality in Hybrid Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04756)
*Ekaterina Gikalo,Zavud Baghirov,Christian Reimers,Markus Reichstein,Martin Jung,Marco Körner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种优化方法，在不损害观测拟合的前提下，有意识地扩大模型对未观测内部过程的解释范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合模型通常根据总通量或总储量等观测量拟合，但这些观测未必能约束内部过程的分配方式。因此，多个模型可能同样符合观测，却对机制给出显著不同的解释。仅靠随机初始化形成的模型集，可能无法充分展现这种不确定性。以蒸散为例，总蒸散量可观测，但其中蒸腾与蒸发的比例不可观测。

Method: 作者训练多个模型，并加入针对蒸腾量的辅助排斥目标，以扩大成员间的分配差异，同时要求观测拟合不退化。为解决优化干扰问题，作者提出非干扰非对称网络优化方法：在适配带动量预条件的优化器时，使更新对主损失的一阶影响与仅优化主目标相同；限制辅助更新的幅度，并将排斥作用集中在主梯度未充分利用的参数方向。

Result: 摘要指出，单靠随机种子得到的模型集仅覆盖较窄的蒸腾与蒸发分配范围。所提出的方法旨在扩大符合观测数据的分配范围，同时维持主任务拟合；摘要未提供具体数值或定量比较结果。

Conclusion: 对于内部过程不可观测的混合模型，观测拟合良好并不能唯一确定机制解释。通过受控的辅助优化，可以更有意识地揭示这种等效解所包含的内部差异；而确保辅助更新不干扰主目标，必须考虑实际优化器产生的更新方向，而不能只检查原始梯度是否正交。

Abstract: Hybrid models let neural networks supply the unresolved parameters of a process-based simulator, and are fitted to observations that constrain aggregate fluxes and storages but not the internal partitioning that produces them. Such a fit is equifinal: configurations indistinguishable in the observed aggregates can disagree substantially about the unobserved internals, and therefore about the mechanism they attribute to the same data. We study evapotranspiration, whose total flux is observed while its split into transpiration and evaporation is not. A model ensemble built from random seeds alone spans only a narrow band of the partitionings the data admit. We produce the spread deliberately, training members under auxiliary repulsion on transpiration, subject to the fit to observations not degrading. Non-interference is usually arranged by making the auxiliary gradient orthogonal to the main one, but under AdamW the applied step is a preconditioned momentum direction, so orthogonality between raw gradients no longer implies it. We introduce Non-interfering Asymmetric Network Optimization (NANO), an update whose first-order effect on the main loss is exactly that of the main objective alone, whose auxiliary step is bounded, and which concentrates repulsion in the coordinates the main gradient leaves idle.

</details>


### [174] [Koopman Generative Operators for Efficient Probabilistic Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04760)
*Raz Marshanski,Liran Nochumsohn,Mayank Jauhari,Boris N. Oreshkin,Omri Azencot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KGO 将 Koopman 动力学、流匹配与自适应不确定性建模结合，实现高精度、单步快速生成的概率时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率时间序列预测需要兼顾时间结构建模、不确定性表达能力与推理效率。现有潜变量动力学模型的生成机制较受限制，而扩散和流匹配等生成方法通常依赖迭代采样，计算开销较大。

Method: 提出 Koopman 生成算子（KGO），将预测视为结构化不确定性的演化。其包含三部分：Koopman 分块嵌入（KoPE）用于保持时间一致性的潜在轨迹外推；Koopman 流匹配（KoFM）在 Koopman 潜在空间中通过闭式矩阵指数实现单步生成，免去迭代采样；自适应不确定性门控（AUG）针对不同变量和预测步长调整不确定性，以学习方式分解偶然不确定性，无需人工调参。

Result: 在 ProbTS 的 17 项基准任务中，KGO 在 13 项上取得 CRPS 最优，在 16 项上取得 NMAE 最优。与迭代式生成模型相比，其推理时间至少降低 25 倍。

Conclusion: KGO 在所评测基准上兼顾了预测精度与推理效率，为概率时间序列预测提供了一种具有结构化动力学基础、能表达自适应不确定性且支持快速单步生成的方法。

Abstract: Probabilistic time-series forecasting requires models that simultaneously capture structured temporal dynamics, expressive uncertainty, and efficient inference, yet existing approaches fall short of this goal: latent dynamical models impose structure but rely on restrictive generation mechanisms, while modern generative methods such as diffusion and flow matching achieve flexibility at the cost of iterative and computationally expensive sampling. We introduce the Koopman Generative Operator (KGO), a probabilistic forecasting method that conceptualizes prediction as the evolution of structured uncertainty. KGO integrates three core components: (1) Koopman Patch Embedding (KoPE) for temporally consistent latent trajectory extrapolation; (2) Koopman Flow Matching (KoFM), which enables fast, single-step generation through a closed-form matrix exponential in a Koopman latent space, bypassing iterative sampling; and (3) an Adaptive Uncertainty Gate (AUG), which provides probabilistic forecasts by adapting uncertainty per-variable and per-horizon, corresponding to a learned decomposition of aleatoric uncertainty without manual tuning. By unifying these components, KGO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on 13/17 ProbTS benchmarks in CRPS and 16/17 in NMAE. Further, by eliminating iterative sampling, KGO delivers at least a 25$\times$ reduction in inference time compared to iterative generative models. These results position KGO as a practical and principled framework that advances the accuracy-efficiency trade-off in probabilistic time-series forecasting, combining leading accuracy on the evaluated benchmarks with fast, single-step generation.

</details>


### [175] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning with Submodular Trajectory Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04774)
*Yasaman Zolfimoselo,Nikolas Geroliminis,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对具有解耦动力学和单调次模轨迹奖励的合作多智能体强化学习，提出有限历史策略与贪心算法，在降低计算复杂度的同时提供近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非加性奖励使最优策略可能依赖所有智能体的完整历史，精确优化的复杂度会随时间范围和智能体数量呈指数增长。论文希望限制策略所需的历史信息，同时量化这种限制造成的性能损失，并保留理论保证。

Method: 提出预窗口曲率，用来衡量早于最近若干步的状态如何削弱当前的边际收益。依据该曲率，分析将每个智能体的策略限制为仅依赖最近若干步状态所带来的误差；再利用智能体动力学相互解耦的性质，依次采用贪心方法优化各智能体的局部策略。

Result: 当历史窗口长度固定时，算法在估计误差之外可达到近似比（1−预窗口曲率）／（2−预窗口曲率），计算时间关于智能体数量和时间范围为多项式级。对于时间可加奖励，算法恢复已知的二分之一近似保证；对于单智能体情形，其近似因子不低于已知的总曲率界。论文还将该框架用于多智能体交通监测，并进行了实验评估。

Conclusion: 通过以预窗口曲率刻画有限历史策略的代价，论文将难以计算的完整历史决策转化为具有理论保证、计算更可扩展的局部贪心优化，为多智能体次模奖励问题提供了一种兼顾效率与近似性能的方法。

Abstract: We study cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning with decoupled dynamics and a monotone submodular reward function over joint trajectories. Due to the non-additive reward, an optimal policy may depend on the history of all agents. This dependence makes exact optimization exponential in the horizon and the number of agents. To overcome this challenge while retaining theoretical guarantees, we introduce pre-window curvature $c_m$, which quantifies how states visited more than $m$ steps earlier reduce current marginal gains. Using this curvature to control the error from restricting the policy of each agent to depend only on its last $m+1$ states, we propose a greedy algorithm that exploits the decoupled dynamics to optimize local policies sequentially. For fixed $m$, the algorithm achieves a $\frac{1-c_m}{2-c_m}$ approximation guarantee up to estimation error, and its computation time is polynomial in the number of agents and the horizon. We recover the known $1/2$-factor approximation for time-additive rewards and obtain an approximation factor no smaller than the known total-curvature bound for a single agent. We formulate multi-agent traffic monitoring as an example of this framework and evaluate our algorithm on it.

</details>


### [176] [Density-Aware Reward Scaling: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Reward-Proportional Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04792)
*Ria Shekhawat,Abhijeet Sinha,Yingtao Zhu,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种轻量级奖励处理方法，通过降低高密度结果区域的奖励权重，提升强化学习对多样化高回报结果的覆盖能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大化期望回报的强化学习目标容易将概率集中到少数高奖励结果上。当任务存在多个不同且都理想的解时，这种模式坍缩会限制结果多样性。论文旨在改善多模态结果空间中的覆盖度，同时保留标准策略梯度训练流程。

Method: 密度感知奖励缩放在每轮策略采样得到的结果组内估计结果的局部密度，并按密度的倒数缩放奖励，使较稀疏区域的结果获得相对更大的训练权重。该方法无需学习密度模型、额外目标函数或价值评估器，也不改变底层策略梯度目标；作者将其用于组相对策略优化。

Result: 在受控的多模态导航任务中，该方法避免了基线组相对策略优化出现的单一模式坍缩，并较好地恢复了目标模式分配。在文本生成图像提示词适配任务中，该方法在多个数据集、策略语言模型及奖励设置下，持续提升图像和提示词的多样性，同时保持良好的语义一致性。

Conclusion: 密度感知奖励缩放是一种可直接集成到现有强化学习流程中的轻量方法，能够在不改变标准策略梯度训练框架的前提下，促进对多样化高回报结果的探索与覆盖。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning objectives that maximize expected return can concentrate probability mass on a small subset of high-reward outcomes, limiting diversity when multiple distinct solutions are desirable. We introduce Density-Aware Reward Scaling (DARS), a simple reward-processing mechanism that promotes reward-proportional sampling in continuous outcome spaces while retaining standard policy-gradient training. DARS estimates the local density of outcomes within each on-policy rollout group and inversely scales rewards by this density. The method requires no learned density model, auxiliary objective, critic, or modification to the underlying policy-gradient objective. In a controlled multimodal navigation task, DARS-GRPO avoids the single-mode collapse exhibited by GRPO and closely recovers the target mode allocation. Applied to text-to-image prompt adaptation, DARS-GRPO consistently improves image and prompt diversity across datasets, Policy LMs, and reward settings while maintaining good semantic alignment. These results show that density-aware reward scaling provides a lightweight way to improve coverage of diverse high-reward outcomes within existing reinforcement-learning pipelines.

</details>


### [177] [Training Specialist LLMs on Your Phone: Forward-Only Optimization via Block-Spectral Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04797)
*Liyan Tan,Yequan Zhao,Ziyue Liu,Yupeng Su,Ruijie Zhang,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种仅依赖前向计算、无需用户标注数据的端侧个性化训练系统，可用极少量参数将共享模型适配为专用助手，并显著提升手机任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在个人设备本地运行大语言模型有助于保护用户数据，但设备内存有限，难以部署能力全面的大模型。多数个性化任务只需专门能力，因此可将共享端侧模型适配为轻量专才；然而，反向传播内存开销过高，用户也通常不会提供标注数据。论文旨在以低资源方式训练模型，并利用可自动核验的任务结果作为学习信号。

Method: 论文提出移动专才系统，以前向计算为基础，根据可验证奖励强化学习中的任务结果进行训练。其核心算法富拉斯只训练预训练权重的分块谱坐标，即各列块的奇异值。算法独立缩放这些列块，改变它们对权重矩阵的相对贡献，从而旋转奇异向量；这类扰动更接近可验证奖励强化学习产生的权重变化。由于更新可改写为层输入变换，该方法也能适配四位量化模型而不直接修改其权重。

Result: 生成的专才模型仅占一至五兆字节，更新参数比例为百分之零点零一五至百分之零点零五。用于手机操作智能体时，任务成功率从百分之六至百分之二十三提升至百分之八十九至百分之一百；研究还在一部手机上用一夜训练出八十亿参数的个性化模型。在可验证奖励强化学习基准上，该方法与全参数进化策略表现相当；用于四位量化模型时，优于基于低秩适配器的进化策略和量化进化策略。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过训练权重的分块谱坐标，可以在不使用反向传播、且只更新极少参数的条件下，有效实现端侧个性化适配。该方法兼顾设备内存限制、量化模型适配和任务表现，为在个人设备上训练轻量专用模型提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) on personal devices keep user data local, but limited memory restricts their general capability. Since most user-specific tasks do not require the breadth of frontier models, a practical solution is to adapt a shared on-device model into lightweight specialists. However, backpropagation is prohibitive in memory, and users rarely provide labeled data. We present MobileSpec, a system that trains specialists with forward passes alone and learns from automatically checked task outcomes, as in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Because forward-only updates are built from random perturbations, training is efficient only when these perturbations resemble the weight changes of RLVR, which largely preserve the singular values of pretrained weights and rotate their singular vectors. The core algorithm of MobileSpec, FuRA-S, produces such perturbations while training only the block-spectral coordinates of pretrained weights, namely the singular values of their column blocks. As we show theoretically and empirically, rescaling these blocks independently shifts their relative contributions to each weight matrix and thereby rotates its singular vectors. Since this update can be rewritten as a transformation of the layer input, FuRA-S also adapts INT4 models without modifying their weights. With specialists of only 1-5 MB that update 0.015-0.05% of the parameters, MobileSpec raises the success rate of a phone-use agent from 6-23% up to 89-100% and trains a personalized 8B model on an iPhone 16 Pro overnight. On RLVR benchmarks, FuRA-S is competitive with full-parameter evolution strategies (ES) and outperforms ES over LoRA adapters and quantized ES on INT4 models.

</details>


### [178] [On the Complexity of Finite-Sum Nonconvex-Strongly-Concave Minimax Optimization with Exact Proximal Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04806)
*Yuxing Peng,Zhiqing Tang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明：即使算法能够自适应地查询各分量的精确函数值、梯度和近端响应，有限和非凸—强凹极小极大问题的最坏情形查询复杂度仍有严格下界；仅使用分量梯度的方法可在对数因子内达到该界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这类有限和非凸—强凹极小极大问题出现在对抗训练和分布鲁棒学习中。已有复杂度下界只适用于线性张成算法、零守恒算法或受限的近端查询协议，尚未说明更强的精确分量近端查询是否能利用梯度无法揭示的方向，从而降低求解成本。

Method: 作者构造困难实例，研究具有 n 个分量、联合均方光滑常数为 L、条件数为 κ 的问题，并对初始点的原始间隙和联合梯度范数作出约束。下界针对可随机化、自适应地查询精确分量函数值、梯度及近端响应的任意算法。构造中，各分量使用相同的近端步长；某分量的近端响应只能揭示隐藏方向，直至遇到第一个归属于其他分量的方向。

Result: 当 ε²/(LΔ) 小于某个普适常数时，任何上述算法在最坏情形下都至少需要 Ω(n + min{√nκ, n^(3/4)√κ}LΔ/ε²) 次分量查询，才能找到价值函数的 ε-驻点。仅使用分量梯度的方法可在至多 log²(2+κ) 的因子内达到这一复杂度，且该保证包含初始化成本。另有同期工作在零守恒一阶方法范围内证明了相同的复杂度率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，精确分量近端访问和不受限的自适应性都不能改善该问题的多项式级最坏情形复杂度；因此，分量梯度方法在复杂度率上已近乎最优。论文也将下界覆盖范围从以往受限的算法类别扩展到了具有精确近端查询能力的算法。

Abstract: Finite-sum nonconvex-strongly-concave minimax problems arise in adversarial training and distributionally robust learning. Their known lower bounds cover only linear-span algorithms, zero-respecting algorithms, or a restricted proximal protocol. They leave open the power of exact component proximal steps, whose responses are global saddle points of regularized component problems and can expose directions that gradients do not reveal. We show that neither unrestricted adaptivity nor exact proximal access improves the polynomial complexity. We consider $n$ components with joint mean-squared smoothness $L$ and condition number $κ$, started from a point with primal gap at most $Δ$ and joint gradient norm at most $\sqrt{2LΔ}$. For $\varepsilon^2/(LΔ)$ below a universal constant, every randomized adaptive algorithm with exact component values, gradients, and proximal responses needs $Ω(n+\min\{\sqrt n\,κ,n^{3/4}\sqrtκ\}LΔ/\varepsilon^2)$ component queries in the worst case to find an $\varepsilon$-stationary point of the value function. A method using only component gradients attains this rate up to a factor $\log^2(2+κ)$, including initialization. Zhou (arXiv:2610.04944) concurrently proves the same rate for zero-respecting first-order methods. In our hard instances, every component admits proximal steps with the same step sizes, and a response for a component reveals hidden directions only up to the first one assigned to another component.

</details>


### [179] [Sleep-Disordered Breathing Detection with Quantum Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04818)
*Alejandro Mayorga-Redondo,José Daniel Viqueira,Diego Alvarez-Estevez,Eduardo Mosqueira-Rey,Andrés Gómez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究变分量子循环神经网络用于睡眠呼吸暂停与正常呼吸事件的时间序列二分类，发现其整体表现可与经典循环神经网络相当，并在小规模数据集上表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍事件识别是重要的时间序列分类任务。研究旨在探索量子循环神经网络能否在参数量和量子比特数量较少的条件下，成为经典循环神经网络的有效替代方案。

Method: 提出一种结合经典计算与量子计算的变分架构，用于区分呼吸暂停事件和正常呼吸片段，并在多个复杂程度不同的真实数据集上与经典循环神经网络进行比较。

Result: 经典模型与量子模型总体表现相近。量子模型在最小的数据集上表现更优，在最复杂的场景中略逊一筹；尽管参数量和量子比特数量显著较少，仍保持了有竞争力的表现，并展现出较好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 变分量子循环模型在当前硬件与算法仍有限的情况下，已显示出用于时间序列分类的潜力。随着量子计算技术发展，其应用价值有望进一步提升，但在复杂任务中的性能仍需改进。

Abstract: This work investigates the application of Quantum Recurrent Neural Networks to the binary classification of sleep-disordered breathing events in time-series data, aiming to distinguish between apneic and normal breathing episodes. A variational classical--quantum architecture is proposed and evaluated against classical Recurrent Neural Network models. Experiments were conducted on multiple real-world datasets with varying levels of complexity to assess the strengths and limitations of the quantum approach. In the comparative analysis, both classical and quantum models showed comparable performance. The quantum model achieved superior performance on the smallest dataset, while yielding slightly weaker results in the most complex setting. However, the quantum approach exhibited notable strengths, such as competitive performance despite a drastically reduced parameter count and low-qubit regime, as well as good generalization capabilities. These results suggest that, despite current hardware and algorithmic limitations, variational quantum recurrent models represent a promising alternative for time-series classification as Quantum Computing technologies continue to mature.

</details>


### [180] [Anomaly Detection on Resource-Constrained Devices with Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04835)
*Marco Angioli,Mathias Persson,Hiroto Tamura,Hadi Banaee,Amy Loutfi,Gouhei Tanaka,Alberto Giaretta,Denis Kleyko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文优化了基于储备池状态马氏距离的无监督时序异常检测方法，使其更适合边缘设备；在保持检测效果的同时，显著降低运算量、模型大小、能耗与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备的计算能力和存储空间有限，因此异常检测模型不仅要有较好的检测性能，还需控制计算与内存开销。论文旨在评估基于储备池状态马氏距离的方法在资源受限设备上的适用性，并进一步降低其部署成本。

Method: 论文提出两项优化：将随机储备池连接替换为循环移位连接；利用最终储备池状态计算整段时间序列的异常分数，而不必逐状态处理。此外，利用储备池的时间记忆优化输入预处理。研究者在微型设备异常检测基准上比较该方法与参考自编码器及其他方法，并将推理内核部署到微控制器上，测量延迟、估算能耗及统计内存占用。

Result: 在所评估的方法中，只有该储备池方法和自编码器达到基准性能目标，而储备池方法所需运算更少、模型更小。采用优化预处理后，该方法的受试者工作特征曲线下面积为0.869，模型大小为24千字节，单次推理估算能耗为24微焦耳，延迟为0.32毫秒；相较采用标准预处理的自编码器，速度提升75.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过简化储备池连接、利用最终状态计算异常分数并优化预处理，基于储备池状态马氏距离的方法能够在保持竞争性检测性能的同时显著降低边缘部署开销。结果表明，该方法适合计算与存储资源受限的微控制器设备。

Abstract: Anomaly detection at the edge requires the deployment of models that achieve competitive performance, while minimizing computational cost and memory footprint. In this article, we investigate the suitability of the Mahalanobis Distance of Reservoir States (MDRS), an unsupervised time-series anomaly detection method, for resource-constrained devices and propose two optimizations to reduce its computational requirements. Specifically, we replace random reservoir connectivity (MDRS-Rand) with a circular shift (MDRS-Circ) and show that the anomaly score for an entire time series can be computed from the final reservoir state alone. Furthermore, we show that exploiting the reservoir's temporal memory enables more efficient input preprocessing, further reducing computational cost. We evaluate the MDRS variants on the MLPerf Tiny anomaly detection benchmark against the reference autoencoder and other anomaly detection methods, and deploy their inference kernels on a NUCLEO-F401RE microcontroller to assess latency, estimate energy consumption, and measure memory footprint. Among the evaluated methods, only MDRS and the autoencoder meet the benchmark's performance target, with MDRS requiring fewer operations and smaller models. With optimized preprocessing, MDRS achieves an ROC AUC of 0.869, a model size of 24 KB, an estimated energy consumption of 24 uJ per inference, and a latency of 0.32 ms, corresponding to a 75.9x speedup over the autoencoder with standard preprocessing.

</details>


### [181] [Found by Search, Broken by a Shortcut: Transfer and Tampering of Searched Intrinsic Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04838)
*Chase J. Metoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过搜索通用内在驱动奖励，发现一种奖励能迁移到未见任务并优于若干基线；但任务专属奖励在该任务上更强，而且搜索未能使智能体抵抗搜索时未出现的重置漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究关注内在奖励能否在不依赖具体任务设计的情况下，帮助强化学习智能体学习、迁移到训练中未见的任务，并抵御搜索阶段未包含的漏洞。核心问题是，自动搜索得到的通用驱动是否比人工设定或随机选择的奖励更有效、更稳健。

Method: 每种候选奖励由四类通用事件构成：发现新视角、拾取、放下和切换；每类事件均设有权重、满足后的奖励缓解量及奖励补充速率。研究预注册了三个阶段，并在任何搜索结果产生前，将搜索方案锁定于公开且带时间戳的记录中。研究使用协方差矩阵自适应进化策略和等预算随机搜索，共进行一千五百六十次近端策略优化训练；最终评估与篡改测试另使用三百四十次训练。比较对象包括任务专属强化学习、回合内新颖性奖励及随机搜索选出的奖励，并在训练任务、留出任务和加入免费重置动作的漏洞测试中评估。

Result: 在留出任务“钥匙走廊三号”上，进化策略选出的奖励在三项预注册比较中均胜过任务专属强化学习、回合内新颖性奖励和随机搜索选出的奖励，且均达到预注册的实际意义标准。它是所测试通用奖励中迁移表现最好的。不过，使用任务专属事件（钥匙与锁门）的饱和奖励在该任务上明显更强：二十个种子全部完成任务，而进化策略奖励仅四个完成；此结果为描述性比较。在“解锁拾取”任务上，进化策略奖励的平均曲线下面积比使用通用事件的饱和奖励高零点二七六，也属于描述性结果。在训练任务上，进化策略奖励与随机搜索奖励之间没有达到预注册的差异标准。加入搜索时不存在的免费重置动作后，进化策略奖励在“门钥匙八乘八”任务上的平均曲线下面积从零点八二九降至零点一三六，且描述性比较显示其表现低于任务专属强化学习。

Conclusion: 搜索得到的通用内在驱动在所有受测任务上都提供了帮助，并能迁移到留出任务；但这并不意味着通用奖励优于针对任务设计的奖励，也不意味着搜索过程能自动带来稳健性。尤其是，进化策略选出的奖励未能抵御搜索阶段未包含的免费重置漏洞。

Abstract: Can a search over innate drives find intrinsic rewards that help a reinforcement-learning agent learn, transfer to a task it never saw, and resist an exploit that was absent during the search? Each candidate reward combines four generic events (a new view, a pickup, a drop and a toggle), each with a weight, a relief and a refill rate. We ran three pre-registered stages; the search protocol was locked in a public, timestamped commit before any search result existed. CMA-ES and an equal-budget random search used 1,560 PPO training runs, and the final evaluation and tamper test used 340. On the held-out task, KeyCorridorS3R2, the CMA-ES-selected reward (E) beat task-only PPO, episodic novelty and the random-search-selected reward (R) in all 3 registered comparisons, all of them meaningful by the registered size rule. Among these generic rewards E transferred best; the earlier paper's satiation reward, which on this task is given hand-wired key and locked-door events, did better (20 of 20 seeds solved against 4 of 20; descriptive). On UnlockPickup, where that reward uses generic events, E's mean area exceeded it by 0.276 (descriptive). On the training tasks there was no registered difference between E and R: both E-over-R tests missed. A free reset action, absent during the search, collapsed E's learning on DoorKey-8x8 (mean area 0.829 without it, 0.136 with it; registered) to below task-only PPO (descriptive). The searched drives helped on every task tested, but a reward given task-specific events did better on the held-out task, and selection gave E no protection against an exploit absent from the search.

</details>


### [182] [DLER: Dual-Logit Experience Replay for Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04885)
*Huiwei Lin,Shanshan Feng,Kong Aik Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双逻辑经验回放方法，通过结合余弦逻辑与点积逻辑，缓解在线持续学习中的预测偏置和灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线持续学习需要模型在数据不断变化且只能单次训练的条件下持续学习。经验回放虽能缓解灾难性遗忘，但当前数据与历史数据不平衡，容易使预测偏向当前类别。已有偏置校正方法仍需适应在线学习场景。作者指出，点积分类结果可分解为角度因素与范数因素：角度因素善于学习新知识，却容易受预测偏置影响；范数因素则有助于保留历史知识。

Method: 提出双逻辑经验回放，同时用于模型优化与记忆管理。模型学习当前样本时采用余弦逻辑；回放历史样本时加入点积逻辑，以缓解预测偏置。为在有限回放记忆下加强双逻辑学习，方法加入辅助监督式对比目标，促使同类样本在角度空间中更加紧凑，使少量回放样本学到的校正效果能够推广至同类其他样本。记忆更新时，优先保留点积预测对正确类别的置信度高于余弦预测的样本，使样本选择与双逻辑训练目标保持一致。

Result: 在三个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法有效；在记忆预算有限时，效果尤其突出。

Conclusion: 通过针对新旧样本采用不同逻辑形式，并将训练目标与记忆选择相协调，双逻辑经验回放能够兼顾新知识学习与历史知识保留，从而缓解在线持续学习中的预测偏置和遗忘问题。

Abstract: Online continual learning aims to continuously train neural networks on a non-stationary data stream in a single pass. Rehearsal-based methods alleviate catastrophic forgetting by replaying stored samples, but the imbalance between current and previous data often leads to predictions biased toward current classes. Many approaches have been proposed to overcome the forgetting problem by correcting the bias; however, they still need to be improved in an online fashion. By decomposing the dot-product logits into an angle factor and a norm factor, our analysis reveals that the angle factor efficiently captures novel knowledge but is particularly susceptible to prediction bias, whereas the norm factor helps preserve historical knowledge. Based on these observations, we propose Dual-Logit Experience Replay (DLER), which exploits dual-logits for both model optimization and memory management. Specifically, DLER learns current samples using cosine logits and incorporates additional dot-product logits when replaying previous samples to mitigate prediction bias. To strengthen dual-logit learning under limited replay memory, an auxiliary supervised contrastive objective promotes angular compactness within each class, extending corrections learned from limited replay samples to other samples of the same class. Meanwhile, we introduce a memory update strategy that preferentially retains samples for which dot-product prediction assigns a higher probability to the correct class than cosine prediction, aligning sample selection with dual-logit training. Extensive experiments on three datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of DLER, particularly under limited memory budgets.

</details>


### [183] [Fault-tolerant shallow recurrent decoders for sparse sensing with missing sensor measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04893)
*Matteo Romilio Pasqual,Matteo Tomasetto,J. Nathan Kutz,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向稀疏、异步及缺失传感器数据的容错状态重建方法，在不增加推理开销的前提下提升高维状态估计的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实传感系统中的传感器可能故障、间歇断连、采样不同步并受到噪声影响，导致观测数据不规则、不完整且异质。传统实时状态重建方法通常假设传感器持续工作且同步采样，因此难以适用于实际运行环境。本文旨在放宽这一假设，实现对不可靠传感器观测的有效利用。

Method: 研究以浅层循环解码器为基础，比较朴素数据填补方法与容错循环时序编码器，并提出四种基于变换器的掩码机制，将二元数据可用性掩码纳入模型，使其显式感知各传感器是否提供观测。此外，设计多步解码训练目标，以改善潜在状态轨迹的平滑性并增强预测鲁棒性。

Result: 在一项合成基准测试和三个水动力学及海洋建模真实问题上，实验表明：基于变换器的掩码机制优于常规数据填补方法和容错循环架构；多步解码训练目标在不同缺失模式下提升了重建精度与鲁棒性；上述改进保留了原方法的计算效率，推理阶段无需额外开销。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模传感器数据的可用性，并采用多步解码训练，浅层循环解码器能够更可靠地处理稀疏、异步和缺失观测，实现高维状态重建。该方法兼顾精度、容错能力与计算效率，因而更适用于现实环境中的传感系统。

Abstract: Real-world sensing systems are inherently imperfect: sensors may fail, intermittently disconnect, operate with asynchronous sampling rates, and be subject to noise, leading to measurement streams that are irregular, incomplete, and heterogeneous. Nevertheless, sensing strategies for real-time state reconstruction typically assume synchronous and continuously active sensors, a constraint that substantially limits their applicability in operational environments. In this work, we introduce fault-tolerant sparse sensing strategies in the context of shallow recurrent decoders (SHRED) to enable high-dimensional state estimation from sparse, asynchronous, and missing sensor measurements. Specifically, we consider naive imputation strategies, fault-tolerant recurrent temporal encoders, and we introduce four different transformer-based masking mechanisms to make SHRED explicitly aware of sensor availability through the binary data-availability mask. Moreover, we further introduce a multi-step decoding training objective for SHRED, resulting in smoother latent trajectories and more robust predictions. Throughout a synthetic benchmark and three real-world problems in hydrodynamic and ocean modeling, we show that (i) transformer-based masking mechanisms outperform standard imputation techniques and fault-tolerant recurrent architectures, (ii) the multi-step decoding training objective enhances reconstruction fidelity and robustness in the presence of heterogeneous missing-data patterns, while (iii) the computational efficiency of SHRED is preserved, with no additional inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [184] [Attending to Memory Writes Reduces Seed Variability in Recurrent Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04902)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究一种有界差分注意力机制：在循环模型中利用因果注意力整合更新提案，并以有界修正参与状态更新。实验显示，它在部分设置下可降低不同随机种子间的性能波动，并改善长序列外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环记忆需要学会有效更新，但辅助学习的注意力机制也可能损害长度泛化。论文因此关注如何将注意力放在更新层面，同时限制其对循环更新的影响，并考察这种设计是否能提升长序列表现的稳定性。

Method: 提出有界差分注意力：先对尚未校正的更新提案进行因果注意力计算，再将有界校正纳入循环更新。其实现将加性相位递归与并行扫描结合，并在并行扫描前计算注意力。实验采用前缀监督的模五任务，在长度一至四十上训练，并在长度八十上测试；同时比较注意力位置、移除校正以及阻断注意力输入梯度等变体。

Result: 长度八十时，上层差分注意力的准确率为百分之七十点八一±八点二六，局部注意力循环基线为百分之六十九点二一±二十二点四二；前者的种子间标准差降低百分之六十三点二，平均准确率相近。上层状态注意力也降低波动，准确率为百分之七十四点七五±六点二四；移除上层差分校正后，准确率仍为百分之七十点六二±八点六零。阻断差分注意力输入梯度后，准确率为百分之六十五点二五±十二点四零，标准差增加百分之五十点二；达到百分之九十九验证准确率的平均时间变晚，但达到百分之九十五阈值的平均时间在七个种子上反而提前。下层差分注意力的准确率为百分之六十二点六零，而下层状态注意力仅为百分之十九点四零，基线为百分之七十一点零六；移除下层差分校正后，准确率降至百分之二十二点五六。其相对下层状态注意力的优势在阻断输入梯度后仍存在。

Conclusion: 结果表明，有界更新级注意力可作为循环学习的辅助机制：在上层设置中，它能在平均表现相近时显著降低种子间波动；在下层设置中，它比状态注意力表现出更轻的外推退化。校正移除实验也说明，有界校正对下层优势尤其重要。不过，实验限于特定任务、训练长度和测试长度，尚不足以证明其对其他任务普遍有效。

Abstract: Recurrent memory requires learning useful updates, but attention added to assist learning can also compromise length generalization. We study bounded delta attention: causal attention over uncorrected update proposals whose bounded correction enters the recurrent update. Its implementation in TraceRelay combines additive phase recurrence with attention computed before a parallel scan. On prefix-supervised modulo-5, trained at lengths 1--40, three findings characterize its behavior at length 80. First, upper delta attention attains $70.81\pm8.26$% accuracy across seven seeds, versus $69.21\pm22.42$% for the local-attention recurrent baseline (mean $\pm$ sample SD): a 63.2% reduction in SD with a similar mean. Upper state attention also reduces variation, reaching $74.75\pm6.24$%, so this benefit is not unique to update-level attention. Disabling the upper delta correction retains $70.62\pm8.60$%. Second, blocking gradients at the delta-attention inputs changes accuracy to $65.25\pm12.40$% in the matched seven-seed comparison, increasing SD by 50.2%. It also delays a 99% validation threshold on average among the same six seeds that reach it; the 95% threshold is reached earlier on average with input-gradient blocking across all seven seeds. Third, lower delta attention exhibits substantially less extrapolation degradation than lower state attention: 62.60% versus 19.40%, against a 71.06% baseline. Removing its correction reduces accuracy to 22.56%; its advantage over lower state attention also persists under input-gradient blocking. These observations motivate bounded update-level attention as a recurrent learning scaffold, combining reduced seed variation with less severe degradation in a placement where state attention fails.

</details>


### [185] [Cool Graphs: Active Property Search Towards Quantum Nano-Refrigerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04907)
*Sofia Blyufer,Amir Kleiner,Uri Peskin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出结合图变分自编码器与不确定性驱动主动学习的搜索框架，用较少量子化学模拟高效筛选能够从热环境吸收能量的纳米电子结点配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能够从周围热环境吸收能量的纳米器件有望充当量子制冷机，但寻找合适配置十分困难：多体状态空间随器件规模指数增长，而评估每种候选配置都需要昂贵的量子化学计算。

Method: 框架将格点能量、耦合矩阵和温度等物理参数映射到平滑的低维潜在空间，以区分制冷与耗散参数区域；再利用蒙特卡洛丢弃法估计模型不确定性，通过主动学习反复挑选模型最不确定、接近分类边界的候选配置进行模拟，并据此更新模型。

Result: 在留出配置上的分类判别能力为百分之九十二点零，误差范围正负百分之零点四；对未见过的外推拓扑，判别能力为百分之八十九点五，误差范围正负百分之一。经过三十二轮主动学习，在所有测试拓扑中，该策略发现吸能系统的效率均持续高于随机搜索；各拓扑中具有目标性质的配置仅占百分之一点九至百分之八点四。潜在空间还呈现出与微观模型参数相关、具有物理可解释性的组织结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为组合规模庞大的纳米器件设计空间提供了数据高效的搜索途径。研究结果提示，可能存在能够以低维且可解释的物理量区分制冷与非制冷区域的“制冷坐标”，有望为纳米级电子信息处理器件的有效制冷及降低高性能计算的环境负担提供帮助。

Abstract: Nanodevices that absorb energy from their thermal environment can act as quantum refrigerators. Finding suitable configurations, however, is a needle-in-a-haystack problem: the many-body state space scales exponentially with the size of the device, where each candidate configuration requires an expensive quantum-chemical computation. We introduce a search framework that couples a Graph Variational Autoencoder (GVAE) with an uncertainty-driven Active Learning (AL) loop to efficiently identify energy-absorbing configurations in electronic junctions with a 2D "working material" to be optimized. The GVAE maps physical parameters - site energies, coupling matrices, temperature - into a smooth latent space that distinguishes cooling from dissipative parameter regimes, achieving a classification discriminability of $0.920 \pm 0.004$ on held-out configurations and $0.895 \pm 0.010$ on unseen extrapolation topologies. An AL pipeline based on Monte Carlo dropout then targets the decision boundary, iteratively selecting the candidates where the model is least certain for simulation. Over 32 AL cycles, the uncertainty-driven strategy discovers energy absorbing systems at rates consistently exceeding the random baseline across all tested topologies (where only 1.9-8.4% of configurations exhibit the desired property), providing a data-efficient pathway for navigating a combinatorically large design space. The learned latent space organizes configurations along physically interpretable axes, that correlate with underlying microscopic model parameters. This hints at the existence of "cooling coordinates" - low-dimensional, physically interpretable quantities separating cooling from non-cooling regimes. Our framework, thus, paves a new way towards effective cooling of nanoscale electronic information processing devices, to reduce the environmental footprint of high performance computing.

</details>


### [186] [Modulating Merging Strengths via Joint Loss Estimation for LoRA-based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04931)
*Kun Gu,De Cheng,Zhipeng Xu,Lingfeng He,Di Xu,Nannan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向持续学习的低秩适配器合并方法，通过逐元素调节新任务更新，并模拟任务间干扰，以更好地兼顾新旧任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于低秩适配器的合并方法通常用单一系数缩放新任务更新，再将其合入已有模型。但更新中的不同元素对新旧任务的影响可能不同，统一缩放难以充分控制遗忘与新知识学习之间的权衡。

Method: 方法由两部分组成。其一，利用海森信息引导自适应合并：在同时考虑旧任务与当前任务的联合目标下，直接求解目标合并更新，并由此得到对当前任务低秩适配器更新进行逐元素调节的解析解。其二，采用任务模拟扰动，通过模拟不同任务之间的干扰，提升更新在后续合并过程中的稳定性。

Result: 论文摘要称，在多个基准测试中，该方法持续优于已有的基于参数高效微调的持续学习方法；摘要未提供具体数据或性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该工作指出，持续学习中的适配器合并不宜只依赖全局缩放系数。结合海森信息进行逐元素调节，并通过任务干扰模拟增强稳定性，有望改善新旧任务之间的知识保留与迁移。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to adapt models to new tasks while preserving previously learned knowledge. Recently, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), a representative Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) method, has gained increasing attention in CL due to its efficiency and scalability. Several LoRA-based merging methods maintain a single inference model by scaling the newly learned LoRA update with one coefficient and merging it into the previous weights after each task. However, they usually control the whole update with a shared scalar factor, although different update elements may affect previous and current tasks in different ways. To address this issue, we propose Merging Strength Modulation via Joint Loss Estimation (M2LE). It consists of Hessian Information-guided Adaptive Merging (HIM) and Task Simulation Perturbation for Merging (TSP). Instead of searching for a global merge coefficient, HIM directly solves for the target merged update under a joint objective over previous and current tasks. Solving this objective gives an analytic solution that naturally takes the form of element-wise modulation over the current task LoRA update. To improve the stability of the learned update under subsequent merging, we introduce TSP which simulates different tasks interference. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that M2LE consistently outperforms existing PEFT-based continual learning methods. Code is available at https://github.com/bbdeeer/M2LE_NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [187] [Deep Neural Operator Learning for stochastic heat equations and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04955)
*Hengrong Du,Qi Feng,Weinan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了深度神经算子可普遍逼近高维半线性随机热方程的解算子，并将方法应用于随机纳维—斯托克斯方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经算子逼近理论较少覆盖空间维数至少为二、且受分布值高斯噪声驱动的随机偏微分方程。论文旨在建立适用于这类随机方程的普适逼近理论，并给出可量化的误差与网络规模控制。

Method: 首先用光滑傅里叶截断将噪声输入压缩为有限个测量值，再借助贝索夫空间与舍弗估计控制截断造成的解误差。随后以整流线性单元构成分支网络，并配合基于网格的主干函数逼近有限维解映射；由此得到所需噪声测量数、主干函数数目的显式界，以及任意有限阶随机矩意义下的逼近结果。线性方程情形下，该降维对应于截断第一维纳混沌。最后将理论用于随机纳维—斯托克斯方程，分别对初始条件和噪声进行有限测量，建立高概率的局部时间逼近结论。

Result: 论文建立了空间维数至少为二的半线性随机热方程解算子的普适逼近定理，并给出显式的测量数量与主干函数数量界。逼近误差可在任意有限阶随机矩意义下控制；对随机纳维—斯托克斯方程，也得到具有显式感知速率的高概率局部时间逼近结果。

Conclusion: 研究将神经算子普适逼近理论扩展到由分布值高斯噪声驱动的随机偏微分方程，并展示了有限测量、正则性估计与神经网络逼近相结合的有效性。线性情形与第一维纳混沌截断相联系，非线性流体方程案例则说明该框架具有进一步应用潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a universal approximation theorem for solution operators of semilinear stochastic heat equations in spatial dimension \(d\ge2\), extending deep neural operator approximation results to SPDEs with distribution-valued Gaussian forcing. A smooth Fourier cutoff reduces the forcing to finitely many measurements, while Besov and Schauder estimates control the resulting solution error. The finite-dimensional solution map is then approximated by ReLU branch networks together with grid-based trunk functions, yielding explicit bounds on the numbers of forcing measurements and trunk functions and approximation in every finite stochastic moment. For linear equations, the reduction coincides with truncation of the first Wiener chaos. Lastly, as an application, we prove a high-probability local-in-time approximation theorem for stochastic Navier--Stokes equations, with finite measurements of both the initial condition and forcing and an explicit sensing rate.

</details>


### [188] [ZEMA: confidence-calibrated signed descent with only one state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04987)
*Ali Dadsetan,Domenic Rosati,Frank Rudzicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于梯度置信度的优化方法：利用批内逐样本梯度估计各参数方向的可信程度，再平滑更新；实验显示其效果可与亚当优化器竞争，但单步计算耗时明显增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究将亚当优化器解释为受梯度不确定性调节的符号下降。作者据此希望直接利用批内梯度信息衡量每个参数更新方向的支持强度，并探索一种只需维护较少优化器状态的替代方法。

Method: 该方法对每个参数，根据当前批次的逐样本梯度计算标准分数，将其转换为梯度符号的概率，再对得到的更新量施加单一指数移动平均。由于置信度是在平滑之前计算的，每个参数只需维护一个状态，而非亚当优化器的两个状态。逐样本梯度方差还可通过批次中的一小部分样本估计。

Result: 在微调和预训练实验中，该方法表现可与亚当优化器竞争，并降低验证损失的波动；在部分设置下，它也能更稳定地恢复全数据集梯度的符号。代价是单步耗时约为亚当优化器的四点六倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用逐样本梯度估计更新方向置信度，是一种有潜力的优化思路，且可减少每个参数所需维护的状态。后续可将置信度自适应扩展到符号几何之外的方法，例如穆恩优化器，并探索其在差分隐私训练中的应用。
