However, integrating NVFP4 into RL degrades rollout quality and amplifies cross-engine training-inference mismatch (TIM), destabilizing training. We show that these failures are concentrated rather than uniform - severely mismatched tail tokens concentrate in a few outlier sequences, while policy weights remain largely intact and quantization error concentrates in a few high-variance activation channels. Guided by this, we present FourSight, an algorithm-system co-design with two targeted solutions: TIM-aware sequence filtering that excludes drifted trajectories from policy updates, and sparse precision compensation that keeps only top-variance channels in BF16 while retaining native NVFP4 elsewhere. We further design a fused SpliceGEMM kernel with specialized prefill and decode datapaths to minimize overhead. FourSight sustains near-BF16 accuracy and strong FP4 deployment quality across models and engines, while improving step-to-accuracy by 1.76-2.60x and staying within 2.3% of FP4 step time up to 120B-scale models.

</details>


### [328] [Artifact-to-Capability: Diagnosing Breaks in Capability Formation of Self-Evolving Agents in a Controlled Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04952)
*Ziqian Ban,Haoxiang Yang,Yanfei Zhang,Shixin Liang,Haoran Yu,Xu Lin,Shaobin Zhao,Xuran Li,Zhiyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，经验产物不断累积、加载后行为发生变化、指标上升，并不能证明智能体真正形成了可复用能力；作者据此提出分层诊断框架，并以五子棋实验揭示三种方法各自的能力形成缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化智能体会将交互经验写入产物，并在后续任务中复用。现有研究通常以产物累积、加载后行为改变和指标提升作为能力进步的代理信号，但这些信号究竟代表什么尚未明确定义，因此无法可靠判断能力是否真正形成。

Method: 作者将基于产物的能力形成界定为五项具有先后依赖关系的性质，其中两项构成能力形成的检查点；并提出三尺度诊断，分别在状态、对局和演化谱系层面检验这些性质。实验在自由规则五子棋中，使用同一基础模型考察反思型方法、航行者方法和第三种进化方法。

Result: 三种方法都呈现了产物累积、加载后行为变化和指标提升这三类信号，但各自存在不同断裂：反思型方法的通过率提升仅源于合法落子增多；航行者方法中，单独可靠的组件组合后却错过全部二十个可立即获胜的机会；第三种进化方法通过检查点后，后续版本只丢失原本通过检查的状态。

Conclusion: 代理信号无法单独确定能力是否已经形成。评估自我进化智能体需要明确能力形成的判据，并追踪其在不同尺度及演化过程中的表现，区分表面指标改善与稳定、可组合且可保留的真实能力。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents write interaction experience into artifacts and reuse them in later tasks. Most work supports such methods with three proxy signals: artifacts accumulate, behavior changes once they are loaded, and metrics rise. We call this signal-based evaluation. What these signals are meant to indicate, however, has not been defined. To fill this gap, we define capability formation from artifacts as five sequentially dependent properties, two of which form a checkpoint for capability formation, and introduce three-scale diagnosis, which judges these properties at the state, game, and lineage levels. In freestyle Gomoku, Reflexion, Voyager, and DGM evolve with the same base model and all show the three signals, yet each exhibits a distinct capability-formation break. Reflexion shows a legalization gain: its pass rate rises only because more of its moves are legal. Voyager shows a compositional override: components that are sound on their own combine to miss all 20 available one-move wins. DGM shows loss-only drift: after passing the checkpoint, later versions only lose passing states. These counterexamples show that signal-based evaluation cannot determine whether a capability has formed.

</details>


### [329] [ALBI: LLM-Guided Inductive Logic Programming via Iterative Generate-Test-Constrain Refineme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05039)
*Aboubaker Seddiq Benterki,Jüri Vain,Gert Kanter,Leelo Aino Jürgenstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出阿尔比，一种以大语言模型生成假设、以逻辑编程系统验证假设的神经符号归纳逻辑编程框架。在三十个基准任务及语义匿名化测试中，三种前沿模型均达到百分之百成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统“从失败中学习”范式依赖基于答案集编程的假设生成器。本文旨在以大语言模型替代该生成器，同时保留形式化验证能力，并检验模型是否真正进行符号归纳，而非仅依赖谓词名称等表面线索。

Method: 阿尔比采用迭代式“生成—测试—约束”流程：大语言模型根据结构化提示提出候选假设，普洛格逻辑编程系统对其进行测试，再利用诊断反馈引导模型修正。作者还提出语义匿名化这一变形测试方法，通过改变谓词名称来评估模型的符号推理是否稳健。

Result: 在三十个标准归纳逻辑编程基准任务上，三种前沿大语言模型均实现百分之百成功率，表现与波珀相当，平均仅需一点四至一点七轮迭代。语义匿名化后，所有模型仍保持百分之百成功率，迭代次数适度增加，为其具备稳健符号推理能力提供了证据。

Conclusion: 研究表明，大语言模型可以承担归纳逻辑编程中的假设生成工作，并与形式化验证器结合形成有效的神经符号框架。语义匿名化测试进一步显示，模型的高成功率不太可能仅由谓词名称的表面匹配所致。

Abstract: We introduce ALBI (Agent for Learning By Induction), a neuro-symbolic ILP framework that replaces the ASP-based hypothesis generator in the Learning from Failures (LFF) paradigm with a Large Language Model (LLM), while retaining SWI-Prolog as a formal verifier. ALBI implements an iterative generate-test-constrain loop: the LLM proposes candidate hypotheses from structured prompts; Prolog tests each candidate; and diagnostic feedback drives refinement. We further introduce semantic anonymization, a metamorphic testing methodology, to distinguish structural symbolic induction from surface-level pattern matching on predicate names. Experiments on 30 standard ILP benchmarks show that ALBI achieves 100% success across three frontier LLMs (Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT 5.4 Pro, Claude Opus 4.6), matching Popper while requiring only 1.4-1.7 iterations on average. Under anonymization, all models maintain 100% success with a moderate iteration increase (1.36-1.67 times), providing evidence of robust symbolic reasoning.

</details>


### [330] [LatentTriad: Pre-Action Risk Representations for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05088)
*Zhe Yu,Yi Liu,Ying Zhang,Simin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需执行技能代码的筛查方法，利用模型预填充阶段的内部状态识别技能内容与用户任务是否相符，在多来源、多模型评测中取得较高准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复用技能可能包含不可信内容，并诱使智能体执行超出用户任务范围的操作。仅靠可疑模式匹配难以判断这种依赖上下文的风险；动态分析则需要实际运行技能，带来成本与潜在风险。因此，论文希望在采取行动之前，以低成本识别任务与操作之间的不一致。

Method: 研究发现，模型在预填充阶段形成的内部状态能够反映任务与操作的一致性，在受控评测中也适用于未见过的表达形式。对操作相关状态进行匹配干预，会影响后续的边界相关状态，并使固定风险读出偏向干预来源的标签。基于这些发现，论文提出“潜在三元组”方法，融合任务边界特征、经过组别校正的操作方向，以及与良性状态关系的偏离。所有检测分支共用一次预填充，不生成输出词元、不调用工具，也不执行技能代码。

Result: 在来自五个来源的五千八百三十个技能包和七种模型骨干上，该方法的汇总精确率、召回率综合指标为百分之九十九点零至百分之九十九点二，按来源宏平均为百分之九十三点零至百分之九十四点六。单独测量的紧凑实现，其预填充与检测中位耗时为二百五十九至二百八十四毫秒。

Conclusion: 研究表明，行动前的模型内部状态不仅可读出任务与操作的一致性，也会对后续边界状态产生功能性影响；利用这些信息，可以在不执行技能的情况下筛查上下文相关风险。结果支持其作为技能部署前安全检查的潜在方案，但摘要未提供真实部署环境中的长期效果或更广泛的外部验证结论。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly extended with reusable skills, but untrusted skill content can induce operations outside the user's task. Detecting this contextual risk requires more than suspicious-pattern matching, while dynamic analysis incurs the cost of skill execution. We find that hidden states formed during prefill contain readable information about task-operation consistency, including across held-out expression forms in controlled evaluations. Matched Operation-state interventions also affect later Boundary states and shift fixed risk readouts toward the donor label. Based on these findings, we introduce LatentTriad, which combines contextual Boundary features, group-adjusted Operation directions, and departures from benign state relationships. All branches share one prefill: detection generates no output tokens, invokes no tools, and executes no skill code. Across 5,830 skill packages from five sources and seven backbones, LatentTriad achieves 99.0-99.2% pooled F1 and 93.0-94.6% source-macro F1. A separately measured compact implementation completes prefill and detection in a median of 259-284 ms. These results connect pre-action risk readability and functional influence to execution-free skill screening.

</details>


### [331] [The Platonic Brain: Representational Convergence in EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05097)
*Nicolas Waehner,Enzo Tagliazucchi,Jacobo Sitt,Demian Wassermann,Giovanni Franco Gabriel Marraffini,Trinidad Borrell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，脑电表征的可解码性越强，不同脑电模型之间的表征通常越相似；但跨模态趋同取决于模态与比较指标，并非模型参数越多就越趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柏拉图表征假说认为，随着规模和性能提升，人工神经网络会趋向共享表征。本文检验这一假说能否推广到人脑表征：脑电基础模型在更擅长解码脑电信息时，是否也会与其他脑电模型及视觉、语言模型形成更相似的表征。

Method: 研究使用六名参与者观看电视剧时采集的脑电记录，并将其与对应的视觉和语言内容对齐。研究者评估脑电基础模型对一组信息性脑电指标的解码能力，并比较模型表征在局部邻域相似性和整体结构上的跨模型、跨模态对齐程度，同时考察模型参数规模的作用。

Result: 解码能力更强的脑电模型，在局部邻域相似性和整体结构两项指标上，通常表现出更强的脑电模型家族间对齐；这种趋势并未随参数规模稳定增强。跨模态结果则有所不同：解码能力提升与脑电表征和语言模型之间更强的整体结构对齐相关，但与视觉模型的整体对齐没有稳定关联；局部对齐也未呈现一致关系。

Conclusion: 结果仅部分支持柏拉图表征假说：表征趋同与解码性能有关，但并非由模型规模简单决定，也不普遍适用于不同模态和相似性指标。局部表征可能更受低层感觉特征影响，而整体几何结构或许反映较弱但共享的语义结构。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) predicts that artificial neural networks converge on shared representations as scale and performance increase. We test whether these predictions hold for representations of the human brain with EEG foundation models. Using EEG recordings from six participants watching a television series, together with aligned visual and language content, we tested whether EEG foundation-model representations converge within and across modalities as their embeddings become better at decoding an informative set of EEG markers. We found that EEG models with greater decodability showed stronger cross-family alignment in both local neighbourhood similarity and global structure, not consistently increasing with model parameter count. Cross-modal convergence was modality and metric dependent: greater EEG decodability was associated with stronger global alignment to language, but not consistently to vision models, while local alignment showed no consistent association. This dissociation may reflect local sensitivity to low-level sensory features, whereas global geometry may capture weaker shared semantic structure.

</details>


### [332] [From AutoResearch to Continual AutoResearch: Do Agents Learn Across Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05108)
*Jeremy Qin,Ruizhi Deng,Mohamed Osama Ahmed,Frederick Tung,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出持续自主研究评测环境，用于比较智能体在连续任务中保留经验与每次重置的表现。实验发现，持续状态能让五种前沿智能体更快找到较强方案，但不一定提升最终解的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究评测通常在每个新问题上重启智能体，成本较高，也无法判断智能体能否记住并迁移先前任务中的经验。研究旨在系统评估持续积累经验是否有助于适应新任务，并区分经验迁移与会话连续性带来的影响。

Method: 构建开放式的持续自主研究环境，在匹配任务序列、数据和时间预算的条件下，对比同一智能体的持续运行版本与重置版本。评测涵盖九类任务，包括合成任务、符号回归、时间序列预测和表格自动化机器学习；使用五种前沿智能体，并考察首次解质量、研究效率和最终留出集表现。此外，通过记忆消融实验改变智能体可保留的历史信息，以分析经验迁移的作用。

Result: 五种智能体均能从持续状态中获益，在研究过程中更有效率，也能更早找到较强方案。然而，效率提升并未稳定转化为更好的最终解，说明积累经验与有效利用经验并非同一能力。

Conclusion: 持续保留状态有助于提升智能体的研究效率，但仅有记忆并不足以保证最终性能提高。评估和改进自主研究智能体时，应分别考察其保存经验的能力与将经验迁移、应用到新任务的能力。

Abstract: Autoresearch has shown that agents can autonomously write code, run experiments, and iteratively improve machine learning solutions. Yet most evaluations start a new agent session from scratch for each new problem, making them costly and leaving unclear whether agents can retain and apply prior insights when adapting to new tasks. We introduce CONTINUAL AUTORESEARCH, an open-ended environment that compares persistent and reset instances of the same agent under matched task streams, data, and time budget. Continual AutoResearch covers nine task families, spanning synthetic tasks, symbolic regression, time-series forecasting, and tabular AutoML. Across five frontier agents, we measure first-solution quality, research efficiency, and final held-out performance. To distinguish insight transfer from session continuity, we run a memory ablation that vary what information agents retain from prior tasks. We show that persistent state improves research efficiency of all five agents, allowing them to find stronger solutions sooner. However, these gains do not consistently translate into better final solutions. Overall, our findings suggest that retaining experience and utilizing it effectively are distinct capabilities.

</details>


### [333] [From Opinions to Commitments: Unstable Assignments and Silent Choices in AI-Mediated Proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05135)
*Sachit Mahajan,Javier Argota S{á}nchez-Vaquerizo,Arthur Capozzi,Dirk Helbing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，语言模型生成的集体提案会因随机种子、意见顺序和提案目标而改变；单一提案可能掩盖可选行动及未被呈现的观点，因此应公开差异并说明提案目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型正被用于将公民意见转化为集体提案，其中可能包含行动主体、任务、预算和期限等具体安排。但同一批意见重复处理时，安排可能发生变化；若只发布一份提案，参与者便可能看不到其他可行选择，也难以察觉哪些观点被排除。

Method: 研究评估了四种语言模型调解系统，包括三种开放权重系统和一种商业系统，并使用两个公民协商数据集。每项任务都从三十条意见生成五份提案，分别改变随机种子或意见排列顺序。研究还比较了四种降低差异的措施，并由三名人工标注者核验模型对差异的判断。

Result: 在开放权重系统的五次运行组中，百分之十七出现了部分提案包含具体安排、部分提案不包含的情况；商业系统中这一比例为百分之二十。仅改变意见顺序，就几乎与换用其他公民的意见一样频繁地改变安排。人工标注确认运行间差异确实存在，但语言模型评判者夸大了差异出现的频率。即使采用零温度、商业系统、筛选提案或合并提案，仍存在显著变化。此外，追求共识的提示词对少数立场的覆盖明显少于要求描述观点差异的提示词。

Conclusion: 语言模型生成的单一提案可能同时掩盖安排上的替代方案和被忽略的观点。研究建议对提案进行审计，展示不同运行产生的替代安排，并披露生成提案所依据的目标，以提高集体决策过程的透明度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being used to turn citizens' opinions into collective proposals. These proposals can make assignments: who should act, what must be done, how much one should spend and by when. However, if the assignments change when the same opinions are processed again, publishing a single proposal can hide alternatives that participants never get to see. We examined this issue in four LLM mediators (three open-weight and one commercial) using opinions from DeliberationBank and Polis. For each task, a mediator drafted a proposal from thirty opinions five times, varying either the random seed or the order of the opinions. In 17% of five-run sets from the open-weight mediators and 20% from the commercial one, some runs contained assignments while others did not. Reordering the opinions changed the assignments almost as often as replacing them with opinions from different citizens. Three human annotators confirmed that the detected differences between runs were real, although the LLM judge overstated how often they occurred. Substantial variation remained under four safeguards: temperature zero, a commercial mediator, draft selection, and draft merging. The mediator's objective also shaped representation: prompts seeking agreement covered minority positions much less often than prompts asking to describe differences. A single proposal can therefore obscure choices in both the assignments it proposes and the views it excludes. We propose audits that make alternative assignments visible and disclose the objective behind each proposal.

</details>


### [334] [Meta$^2$: Precomputing Reusable Knowledge for Online Harness Evolution in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05139)
*Lingzhi Yuan,Yiguang Cai,Weize Liu,Siqi Yang,Feijiang Han,Yongkyun Lee,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种离线学习、在线适应的智能体框架：从训练任务中学习可复用的工具链演化经验，再在新任务运行过程中依据轨迹证据进行局部调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线优化通常为所有任务部署同一套工具链，容易过拟合训练任务，也无法响应新运行中才出现的信息。在线演化虽能适应单次任务，却可能每次都从头推理，或依赖测试时通常不可用的验证器反馈。论文旨在兼顾跨任务复用与单任务在线适应。

Method: 元二框架在离线阶段对比同一任务智能体在三种条件下的运行轨迹与结果：不使用演化器、由当前元技能指导演化器，以及使用训练阶段特权验证器信息的演化器。系统汇总这些对比，迭代形成文本形式的可复用演化知识，指导何时调整工具链、何时保持不变以及应避免哪些改动。在线阶段，演化器结合该知识与当前轨迹证据，选择保持原状或进行任务局部编辑；编辑仅影响该次运行后续过程，不重置环境或已有进度，且测试时不使用验证器反馈。

Result: 在两个长时程基准测试中，使用指定模型的元二在所比较方法中表现最佳：终端基准二点一的成功率为百分之八十一，深度软件工程一点一为百分之四十四点四。消融实验支持对比式演化指导和将在线编辑限制在单次任务内的设计价值。

Conclusion: 该方法将离线优化对象从统一工具链转为可迁移的演化知识，并在在线阶段按任务局部调整工具链，从而结合跨任务经验复用与运行时适应能力。实验结果表明，这种设计在所测长时程任务中具有优势。

Abstract: Harness optimization improves long-horizon agents by refining the prompts, tools, memory, and hooks around a frozen model. Offline methods can learn from completed trajectories, but deploy one shared harness that may overfit training tasks and cannot react to evidence revealed during a new run. Online evolution can adapt before, during, or after one run through an evolver, but may have to reason about every intervention from scratch or rely on verifier feedback that is usually unavailable at test time. We introduce Meta$^2$, which moves offline optimization one level up: rather than optimizing a shared harness, it learns reusable knowledge for harness evolution and applies it within each task online. Offline, Meta$^2$ learns a textual Meta-Skill $M$ by contrasting training rollouts of the same task agent under three configurations: without an evolver, with an evolver guided by the current version of $M$, and with an evolver given privileged verifier information available only in training. Contrasts in trajectories and outcomes are aggregated across tasks to refine $M$ into reusable guidance on when and how to evolve, when to leave the harness unchanged, and which interventions to avoid. Online, an evolver combines the frozen $M$ with emerging trajectory evidence to decide whether to KEEP the harness unchanged or EVOLVE it through a task-local edit. Each edit applies to the rest of the same run without resetting its environment or progress. Thus, evolution guidance transfers across tasks while harness edits remain task-local, and held-out verifier feedback is never available to the evolver. On two long-horizon benchmarks, Terminal-Bench 2.1 and DeepSWE 1.1, Meta$^2$ achieves the highest performance among evaluated methods with GPT-5.6-Terra, reaching 81.00% and 44.44%, respectively. Ablations support the value of contrastive evolution guidance and keeping online harness edits task-local.

</details>


### [335] [Creating Power Flexibility in Datacenters via Query Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05148)
*Shivam Patel,Bingqing Chen,Jared Fernandez,Guannan Qu,Gauri Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出通过动态调整查询在不同语言模型实例间的分配，在不延迟请求的情况下调节数据中心功耗；仿真显示可提供最高约百分之十六的总功耗灵活性，并能在约十秒内实现目标降功耗的百分之九十。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型推理的能耗持续增长，数据中心需要在保障交互式服务质量的同时，灵活调整用电以支持电网安全运行。传统上可能通过延迟请求来调节负荷，但这会影响服务体验，因此论文探索无需推迟请求的查询路由机制。

Method: 论文构建通用分析框架，将查询到达率和序列长度分布与令牌迭代时间、请求驻留时间、模型实例服务能力及功耗联系起来。基于该框架，分析可行的查询路由分配及其对应的功耗调节能力，从而选择满足目标功耗变化的路由策略。研究在四种图形处理器平台、参数规模为十亿至七百亿的语言模型上验证框架，并通过真实轨迹驱动的仿真评估一座配备一万张高端图形处理器的数据中心。

Result: 该框架对可实现功耗灵活性的估计平均绝对百分比误差为百分之二点八。仿真中，查询路由带来的灵活性最高可达数据中心总功耗的百分之十六；在约十秒内即可实现预期降功耗幅度的百分之九十。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在不同模型实例间动态路由查询，是一种无需延迟请求即可调节数据中心功耗的有效机制。所提出的分析框架还可用于数据中心层面的规划与编排，帮助运营者评估并实现所需的负荷灵活性。

Abstract: The growing energy requirements of large language model inference highlight the need for datacenters to support safe grid operation by providing power demand flexibility, while maintaining service for interactive workloads. This work investigates query routing as a mechanism to enable such capability by dynamically allocating queries across different language model instances without deferring requests. We develop a general analytical framework that connects query arrival rates and sequence length distributions to token iteration time, request residence time, serving capacity, and power consumption of model instances. Leveraging this framework, we characterize the feasible query routing allocations and the corresponding power demand flexibilities, enabling the selection of routing strategies to achieve desired power consumption behavior. We validate our framework across four GPU platforms on language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, obtaining 2.8% mean absolute percentage error in estimating achievable power flexibility. We evaluate our approach on a real trace-driven simulation of a 10,000 H100 GPUs datacenter, exhibiting flexibility up to 16% of total power consumption, with 90% of the desired power reduction achieved within approximately 10 seconds. These results establish query routing as an effective mechanism for datacenter power demand flexibility and demonstrate the broader applicability of our proposed framework to datacenter level planning and orchestration.

</details>


### [336] [Catalyzing Interactive HTML Generation Capabilities via High-Fidelity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05151)
*Xujia Wang,Yixuan Liu,Shaohan Huang,Furu Wei,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出网页交互强化学习框架「网页催化剂」，通过更可靠的视觉评估与执行轨迹归因，提升模型生成网页小游戏的功能性和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型虽然能生成可渲染的网页，却常在实际交互中出现功能不完善、体验不佳等问题。既有方法多依赖特定网页任务的监督数据，且浅层交互难以充分揭示交互体验、奖励信号和贡献归因方面的不足。

Method: 以小游戏作为高难度测试场景，提出包含三个部分的浏览器交互强化学习框架：游戏级图形界面评估器用于持续交互和评价；视觉锚点用于抑制视觉语言模型奖励机制被投机利用；动作轨迹归因则利用运行时执行轨迹，将训练反馈落实到具体生成令牌。

Result: 在请求量不足一千、且主要涉及小游戏的训练条件下，该方法使模型在「曲奇基准迷你游戏」上的得分从六十八点八升至七十五点三，在「网页生成基准」上的准确率从四十八点八升至五十六点九，并在所评估指标的平均排名上超过「克劳德奥普斯四点七」。分析还显示，视觉锚点可避免美观度下降，基于运行轨迹的归因可加快功能改进。

Conclusion: 研究表明，提升反馈信号的真实性与归因精度，是显著改善交互式网页生成的关键。该框架在少量训练请求下取得跨基准提升，说明以高要求交互任务训练模型具有潜力。

Abstract: Generating HTML from natural-language specifications is an important application of code large language models (LLMs). Despite producing renderable pages, current models often fail to ensure functionality and visual quality during actual interaction. Existing methods typically target task-specific webpage supervision, leaving demanding interactive artifact optimization underexplored. Using mini-games as a challenging testbed, we expose limitations in interaction experience, reward signal, and credit assignment fidelity that are obscured by shallow webpage interactions. We introduce WebCatalyst, a browser-interaction reinforcement learning framework with three complementary components: a game-level GUI assessor for sustained interaction and evaluation, Visual Anchor for mitigating VLM reward hacking, and Action-Trace Credit for grounding token-level credit assignment in runtime execution traces. Trained on fewer than 1K requests, predominantly for games, WebCatalyst raises the Cookie-Bench Mini Games score from 68.8 to 75.3 and also the WebGen-Bench accuracy from 48.8 to 56.9 on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, outperforming Claude Opus 4.7 in average rank across evaluated metrics. Further analysis shows that Visual Anchor prevents aesthetic degradation, while runtime-grounded credit assignment accelerates functional improvement. Together, our results show that improving feedback fidelity is the key to substantially advancing interactive HTML generation.

</details>


### [337] [Learning Behaviorally Relevant Dynamics in Multimodal Neural Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05167)
*Parastoo Azizeddin,Eray Erturk,Maryam M. Shanechi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出一种融合多种神经信号与行为数据的非线性动态模型，能够区分与行为相关的神经动态和剩余动态，并适应不同步或信号缺失的记录情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经元放电、局部场电位和行为数据分别反映大脑活动的不同空间与时间特征。以往区分行为相关变化与其他神经变化的模型通常只使用一种神经信号，难以充分利用多模态数据，也难以全面刻画脑活动与行为之间的关系。

Method: 提出一种非线性多模态动态模型，在推断过程中融合具有不同统计特性和时间分辨率的多种神经信号及行为数据，并通过结构化学习，将神经动态划分为行为相关部分与剩余部分。

Result: 在非人灵长类动物执行二维伸手任务时同步采集的神经元放电和局部场电位数据上，该模型能够更准确地分离并学习两类神经动态。相较于对照方法，其行为解码、神经信号预测和潜在表征与行为的对齐效果更好；在神经信号不同步或部分缺失时仍保持这些能力。

Conclusion: 该模型为跨时间与空间尺度研究行为相关的神经计算提供了多模态分析工具，并针对真实神经记录中常见的信号异步和缺失问题展现出适用性。

Abstract: Multimodal neural data can enable a more complete understanding of brain dynamics underlying behavior. Modalities such as spiking activity, local field potentials (LFPs), and behavior capture diverse spatiotemporal aspects of brain processes, yet prior neural-behavioral models that separate sources of variability rely on a single neural modality. By leveraging these complementary strengths, multimodal neural fusion can provide a unified, rich representation of brain-behavior processes and address the limitations of single-modality analyses. Here we develop BREM-NET, a nonlinear multimodal dynamical model that integrates behavior with multiple neural modalities having distinct statistical characteristics and temporal resolutions. BREM-NET performs multimodal neural fusion during inference while separating behaviorally relevant and residual neural dynamics via a structured learning framework. In a public multimodal non-human primate dataset with simultaneous spiking and local field potential recordings during a 2D reaching task, BREM-NET separates these two types of dynamics and learns them more accurately, as reflected in better behavior decoding, better neural prediction, and stronger latent alignment with behavior, and it retains these capabilities when neural modalities are asynchronous or suffer dropouts, which are major challenges in real-world recordings. This framework provides a new tool for studying behaviorally relevant neural computations across spatiotemporal scales of brain activity.

</details>


### [338] [AgentAM: An AI Agent for Spatter- and Defect-Aware Process Optimization in Laser Powder Bed Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05171)
*Olabode T. Ajenifujah,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出智能体增材制造系统，使工艺工程师能用自然语言调用激光粉末床熔融过程代理模型；其预测与优化表现良好，并通过基准测试验证了智能体的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光粉末床熔融的预测模型虽可辅助工艺决策，但通常需要编程技能和特定模型知识，因而难以在生产现场使用。研究旨在降低模型使用门槛，同时帮助工程师在减少飞溅等目标下避开缺陷边界和工艺波动风险。

Method: 针对不锈钢三一六升，研究以二百七十九组单道熔融流体计算模拟训练多项式代理模型，预测熔池尺寸和飞溅体积，并依据几何特征判定钥孔、未熔合、球化或稳定等缺陷状态。系统通过模型上下文协议连接大语言模型智能体，由智能体选择并配置预测、工艺窗口扫描及约束优化工具；另引入考虑安全裕度的优化方法，并以自然语言提示基准测试评估智能体。

Result: 熔池尺寸预测的决定系数不低于零点九五，飞溅体积测试集决定系数为零点八三；几何判定与模拟所得状态在交叉验证中有百分之九十三点四一致。约四十至五十组分布良好的模拟即可复现缺陷图。安全裕度优化在假设百分之二工艺漂移时，将估计保持良好状态的概率从零点九四提高至一点零零；加入联合抽样的几何残差后，则从零点七二提高至零点九一。主基准测试共七百二十五次运行：输入解析通过率为百分之九十八点五，智能体准确报告预测值和缺陷类别，并在六十五个可行优化任务中满足全部约束，结果与穷举最优值的中位差为百分之一。以上概率为模型估计值，并非实测生产结果。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了以自然语言智能体连接工艺代理模型的可行性，可让非编程用户开展预测、工艺窗口分析和约束优化。结果也表明，优化时显式考虑工艺漂移及模型残差有助于远离缺陷阈值、提升稳健性；不过，结论仍受模拟数据、训练范围及假设条件限制，实际生产应用还需进一步验证。

Abstract: Predictive models for laser powder bed fusion (LPBF) are rarely used on the shop floor because querying them requires programming and model-specific knowledge. We present AgentAM, a large language model (LLM) agent that gives process engineers natural-language access to CFD-trained surrogates of spatter and melt pool geometry for SS316L through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Polynomial surrogates trained on 279 single-track simulations predict melt pool dimensions ($R^2 \geq 0.95$) and spatter volume ($R^2 = 0.83$ on test data) at any power-velocity point in the trained range, and geometric criteria assign each point a geometry-derived defect regime (keyhole, lack of fusion, balling or stable) that matches the regime obtained from the simulated geometry in 93.4% of tracks under cross-validation. The agent selects and parameterizes tools for prediction, process-window scans and constrained optimization. Minimum-spatter optima often sat within 10 $μ$m of the lack-of-fusion boundary; a margin-aware optimizer moved them away from all thresholds and raised the estimated probability of remaining classified Good from 0.94 to 1.00 under an assumed 2% process drift, and from 0.72 to 0.91 when jointly sampled cross-validated geometry residuals were added. About 40-50 well-spread simulations sufficed to reproduce the defect map. To evaluate the agent layer, we introduce a 145-prompt benchmark scored against direct tool calls, plus eight prompts (40 runs) on margin-aware requests. Over the 725 runs of the main benchmark the agent passed the intended inputs in 98.5% of parsing prompts, reported the scored spatter values and defect classes faithfully and met every stated constraint in the 65 feasible optimization runs, within a median 1.0% of the brute-force optimum.

</details>


### [339] [JHiMA-WebRL: A Joint Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Long-Horizon Web Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05263)
*Shanka Subhra Mondal,Jiayun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种联合训练任务分解器与执行器的分层多智能体强化学习方法，使较小规模模型在长程网页操作任务中取得有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实网页任务通常需要跨越多个步骤进行规划、状态导航、进度跟踪和视觉操作。单一策略难以同时处理这些要求；已有模块化方法虽将规划与执行分开，却往往没有在强化学习过程中同步更新规划器。论文因此研究：联合训练规划与执行能否更有效地支持长程网页导航。

Method: 作者提出联合分层多智能体强化学习框架，将网页任务分解器与执行器结合。首先，利用强教师模型从多模态图形界面交互轨迹中生成结构化子任务标注，并据此微调分解器和以子任务为条件的执行器。随后，作者扩展组相对策略优化方法，在交互式网页环境中通过分层轨迹分组和针对不同角色的优势估计，联合优化分解器与执行器。

Result: 在网页竞技场精简版第二版基准上，该方法的总计一百四十亿参数模型（由两个各七十亿参数的模块组成）取得百分之三十七点二的平均成功率，高于同一训练任务和评测协议下、经强化学习微调的三百二十亿参数基线模型的百分之二十四。消融实验还显示，强化学习阶段同步更新分解器优于只更新执行器。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联合学习规划与执行的角色分工有助于提升长程网页任务表现；与单纯扩大单体模型规模相比，采用较小模型并进行协同训练，也可以成为有竞争力的方案。

Abstract: Multimodal web agents have shown promising capabilities for interacting with websites through graphical user interfaces (GUIs), yet realistic web tasks often require long-horizon, multi-step planning that remains challenging for monolithic policies. Such agents must jointly infer high-level plans, navigate through intermediate states, track progress over extended interaction histories, and execute visually grounded actions. Although prior work has explored modular multi-agent systems with a separate planner, the planner is typically not updated jointly with the actor. We investigate whether explicitly separating and jointly training planning and execution provides a stronger inductive bias for long-horizon web navigation. We introduce JHiMA-WebRL, a joint hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for web agents that couples a task decomposer with an actor. To train the decomposer, we use a strong teacher model to construct structured subtask supervision from multimodal GUI trajectories, and use these annotations to fine-tune both the decomposer and the subtask-conditioned actor. We then extend Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to jointly optimize the decomposer and actor in the interactive WebArena environment through hierarchical rollout grouping and role-specific advantages. On WebArena-Lite-v2, JHiMA-WebRL-14B, consisting of a 7B actor and a 7B decomposer, achieves an average success rate of 37.2%, compared with 24% for a ScaleCUA-32B agent that we fine-tune with RL on the same training tasks and evaluate under the same protocol. Ablations show that jointly updating the decomposer during RL outperforms updating the actor alone. These results suggest that jointly learned role factorization with smaller models can be a competitive alternative to scaling monolithic agents for long-horizon web tasks.

</details>


### [340] [The Geometry of Ignorance: Detecting Suppressed Knowledge in LLMs through Activation Fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05272)
*Adar Peleg,Dvir Alsheich,Tomer Ashuach,Avi Mendelson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于隐藏状态几何结构的方法，用于判断模型是否真正遗忘知识，并在不执行攻击的情况下评估遗忘的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭模型是否回答问题，难以判断其是否真正遗忘某项知识：拒答或压制输出可能掩盖模型内部仍可恢复的知识。因此，论文希望找到一种不依赖输出行为、能够直接反映内部知识状态及遗忘稳健性的指标。

Method: 比较模型处理概念相关查询及其匿名化改写时的隐藏状态差异，称为“匿名向量”，并分析这些向量的几何一致性。论文以聚类紧密度、熵及碎片化等指标区分已知与未知概念，并进一步按网络层分析几何特征与不同攻击方式下知识恢复情况的关系。

Result: 在来自三个模型系列的十个指令微调模型中，各项碎片化指标均能可靠地区分已知概念与未知概念。对五种遗忘算法的评估显示，遗忘后的几何碎片化与四类对抗攻击下的知识恢复情况相关，但相关方向取决于攻击方式。分层分析还发现，不同攻击会利用不同深度的几何特征；这些攻击特异性关联无法通过行为评估观察到。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态的几何结构可作为一种单阶段、无需实施攻击的遗忘稳健性代理指标，揭示输出层面指标难以捕捉的参数化知识信息。不过，几何碎片化与攻击恢复之间的关系受攻击方法影响，因此评估时需要结合具体攻击机制解释指标。

Abstract: Evaluating whether a large language model (LLM) genuinely knows a concept remains a fundamental problem, since behavioral metrics can be fooled by suppression: a model that refuses to answer may still harbor recoverable knowledge. The framework measures the geometric coherence of \textit{anon-vectors}, the differences in hidden states between a concept-specific query and its anonymized paraphrase, and shows that genuine knowledge forms a tight, low-entropy cluster while ignorance forces geometric fragmentation. Across ten instruction-tuned LLMs from three model families, all fragmentation metrics reliably separate known from unknown concepts, and post-unlearning, fragmentation geometry correlates with adversarial recovery across four attack types and five unlearning algorithms, though the direction of that relationship is shown to depend on attack methodology. Furthermore, a layer-resolved analysis reveals that different adversarial mechanisms exploit this geometry at varying depths, with high attack-specific correlations that are invisible to behavioral evaluation. Together, these results constitute a single-phase, attack-free proxy for unlearning robustness that captures aspects of parametric knowledge inaccessible to output-level metrics. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/AdarPeleg/geometry-of-ignorance

</details>


### [341] [You don't need long context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05306)
*Peter Belcak,Saurav Muralidharan,Jindong Jiang,Yonggan Fu,Wonmin Byeon,Hanrong Ye,Ryan Angilly,Ali Taghibakhshi,Shih-Yang Liu,Siru Ouyang,Greg Heinrich,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，频繁压缩上下文可显著降低生成成本，同时在多类长程任务中几乎不损失准确率，这对超长上下文模型的必要性提出质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成越靠后的词元，成本通常越高；在前沿开放权重模型中，成本差异最高可达约七倍。因此，减少模型需要持续处理的上下文长度，可能大幅提升长程任务的效率。研究旨在检验频繁压缩上下文是否会损害任务表现，以及长上下文模型是否确有必要。

Method: 研究在多种长时程任务和长输入任务中频繁压缩模型上下文，并评估任务准确率；还考察软件工程任务中每个上下文至多执行一次操作时的表现。基于这些观察，作者进一步提出语义状态空间模型的概念。

Result: 在所测试的任务范围内，保持较短上下文几乎不损害任务准确率。即使每个上下文至多采取一次操作，模型在长时程软件工程任务中的表现也没有崩溃。摘要指出，后续词元的生成成本可能比前面的词元高出约七倍。

Conclusion: 长程任务未必需要持续维持极长上下文；通过频繁压缩上下文，模型可能以较低成本保持良好表现。研究因此质疑长上下文语言模型的必要性，并提出语义状态空间模型作为值得探索的方向。

Abstract: Later tokens are costlier to generate than earlier tokens. On frontier open weight models, the disparity in cost may rise to as much as 7x. Through frequent compaction, we show across a broad range of long-horizon and long-input tasks that frontier language models sacrifice little to no task accuracy when maintaining short contexts. We further demonstrate that LLM performance does not collapse on long-horizon software engineering tasks even when taking at most one action per context, and based on that we outline the concept of semantic state space models (SSSMs). Our findings question the need for long-context language models.

</details>


### [342] [Adversarial Speculative Reasoning Improves Long Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05345)
*Hongyin Luo,Wei Fang,Lijie Yang,Owen Stepan,Daniel Suit,Jeff Krapf,Dana Wensberg,Jack O'Brien*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，推测解码中的数值噪声会在长程推理中累积并影响智能体表现；通过训练草稿模型在关键推理位置主动被拒绝，可让目标模型以较低噪声生成这些关键内容，从而提升复杂任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码通常被视为不改变模型行为的加速技术，因此草稿模型训练主要追求更高的接受率。但在长程智能体任务中，验证窗口内的数值噪声可能逐步累积；思考过程中的草稿接受情况在约三十步后即可改变输出分布，并使推理准确率产生明显且方向不定的变化。研究旨在控制这种噪声，提升长程推理的可靠性。

Method: 研究者从智能体执行轨迹中识别关键推理位置，并训练草稿模型采用对抗性偏好，使其在这些位置倾向于提出会被目标模型拒绝的草稿。推理时，草稿模型与目标模型在关键决策点分离并重新开始验证，使关键思考词元由目标模型采样，并在噪声较低的条件下编码。该方法不修改目标模型，也不改变其经验证的整体词元分布。

Result: 在复杂智能体编码基准上，该方法提高了端到端任务成功率。训练所需参数仅占托管参数的百分之零点零六至百分之零点二八，无需改动目标模型。

Conclusion: 推测解码不仅能加速生成，也可以作为长程上下文精度的主动分配机制。通过让草稿模型在关键推理位置被拒绝，可降低噪声对重要思考内容的干扰，使其在长程推理中更稳定、持久，进而改善智能体准确率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has been treated purely as a systems optimization that speeds up large language model decoding without changing the model behavior. Under this view, the growing effort to train better draft models has been focused solely on acceptance rate. We argue that this view does not hold true for long-horizon agent reasoning. Measuring frontier open-weight models on complex agentic tasks with different speculative decoding approaches, we found that numerical noise grows within verification windows, and the draft acceptance results of the thinking process can change the output distribution of the model after 30 steps, thus noticeably influencing the reasoning accuracy of long-horizon agents in random directions. In this work, we improve the reasoning capability of target models by controlling such noise. While we cannot change the overall token distribution verified by the target model, we can encode critical reasoning with highest precision by training the draft model to be rejected. We identify these points in agent traces and train the draft model to favor adversarial preference. At inference the trained draft and the target part ways precisely at these decision points, verification restarts, so that critical thinking tokens are sampled from the target model and encoded under its least noisy conditions. This approach makes critical reasoning better indexed and more durable over a long horizon. On complex agentic coding benchmarks, our approach raises end-to-end success rate with no change to the target model or its output distribution. Our results recast the speculative decoding mechanism from a pure accelerator into an active allocator for context precision that improves the accuracy of long-horizon agents by training 0.06% to 0.28% of hosted parameters.

</details>


### [343] [Preserving the Privacy of Reward Functions in MDPs through Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09809)
*Shashank Reddy Chirra,Pradeep Varakantham,Praveen Paruchuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究在决策行为可被观察时如何保护智能体的奖励函数隐私，提出基于“模拟欺骗”的强化学习规划方法，在保障期望奖励的同时提升隐私保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在野生动物巡护等场景中，智能体的行动可能暴露其偏好或奖励结构，使观察者能够通过逆向强化学习推断敏感信息。现有奖励函数差分隐私方法既不能保证最低期望奖励，其理论隐私保障对这类推断攻击也不够充分。

Method: 论文借鉴欺骗理论，将欺骗分为隐藏真实信息的“隐匿”和展示错误信息的“模拟”。作者首先从理论上分析并揭示现有隐匿式方法存在显著隐私泄露；随后提出一种基于强化学习的规划算法，通过模拟方式引导可观察的行动呈现误导性偏好，同时保证期望奖励。

Result: 在多个基准问题上的实验表明，该方法在保护奖励函数隐私方面优于已有方法。摘要未提供具体数值或详细的性能权衡。

Conclusion: 对于容易受到逆向强化学习推断的决策隐私，仅隐藏真实奖励信息可能不足。通过模拟来主动塑造外部可观察的行为，并兼顾期望奖励保障，是一种更有效的隐私保护思路。

Abstract: Preserving the privacy of preferences (or rewards) of a sequential decision-making agent when decisions are observable is crucial in many physical and cybersecurity domains. For instance, in wildlife monitoring, agents must allocate patrolling resources without revealing animal locations to poachers. This paper addresses privacy preservation in planning over a sequence of actions in MDPs, where the reward function represents the preference structure to be protected. Observers can use Inverse RL (IRL) to learn these preferences, making this a challenging task.
  Current research on differential privacy in reward functions fails to ensure guarantee on the minimum expected reward and offers theoretical guarantees that are inadequate against IRL-based observers. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel approach rooted in the theory of deception. Deception includes two models: dissimulation (hiding the truth) and simulation (showing the wrong). Our first contribution theoretically demonstrates significant privacy leaks in existing dissimulation-based methods. Our second contribution is a novel RL-based planning algorithm that uses simulation to effectively address these privacy concerns while ensuring a guarantee on the expected reward. Experiments on multiple benchmark problems show that our approach outperforms previous methods in preserving reward function privacy.

</details>


### [344] [On Discovering Algorithms for Adversarial Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00922)
*Shashank Reddy Chirra,Jayden Teoh,Praveen Paruchuri,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由大语言模型引导进化搜索的对抗模仿学习方法，自动发现奖励分配函数，并在未见环境及不同策略优化算法中展现出良好的泛化与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗模仿学习通常依靠判别器估计专家与策略的状态—动作占用密度比，再将其转换为训练奖励。以往研究主要关注密度比估计，对奖励分配如何影响训练动态和最终性能关注不足；现有奖励函数多由人依据散度目标设计，可能限制性能与稳定性。

Method: 提出发现式对抗模仿学习框架，将奖励分配函数的设计视为数据驱动的搜索问题，以模仿策略的实际表现作为依据，并利用大语言模型引导的进化框架高效探索函数空间，从而元学习得到奖励分配方法。

Result: 摘要报告称，该方法能够泛化到未见环境和不同的策略优化算法，并优于当前由人工设计的先进基线；研究还分析了其训练稳定性提升的原因。摘要未提供具体实验数值。

Conclusion: 奖励分配函数是影响对抗模仿学习稳定性与性能的重要因素，不应仅视为密度比估计后的固定转换步骤。自动化、数据驱动地发现奖励函数有望减少人工设计依赖，并提升方法的泛化能力与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Adversarial Imitation Learning (AIL) methods, while effective in settings with limited expert demonstrations, are often considered unstable. These approaches typically decompose into two components: Density Ratio (DR) estimation $\frac{ρ_E}{ρ_π}$, where a discriminator estimates the relative occupancy of state-action pairs under the policy versus the expert; and Reward Assignment (RA), where this ratio is transformed into a reward signal used to train the policy. While significant research has focused on improving density estimation, the role of reward assignment in influencing training dynamics and final policy performance has been largely overlooked. RA functions in AIL are typically derived from divergence minimization objectives, relying heavily on human design and ingenuity. In this work, we take a different approach: we investigate the discovery of data-driven RA functions, i.e, based directly on the performance of the resulting imitation policy. To this end, we leverage an LLM-guided evolutionary framework that efficiently explores the space of RA functions, yielding \emph{Discovered Adversarial Imitation Learning} (DAIL), the first meta-learnt AIL algorithm. Remarkably, DAIL generalises across unseen environments and policy optimization algorithms, outperforming the current state-of-the-art of \emph{human-designed} baselines. Finally, we analyse why DAIL leads to more stable training, offering novel insights into the role of RA functions in the stability of AIL. Code is publicly available: https://github.com/shshnkreddy/DAIL.

</details>


### [345] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对某公司为期一年的真实大语言模型生产服务记录进行全局与纵向分析，并公开完整记录，以支持后续研究和基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型服务负载研究通常覆盖时间较短、范围有限，且难以观察用户在生产环境中如何与模型交互。因此，它们无法充分呈现负载随时间的演变，也难以揭示用户与模型的互动如何塑造实际流量。

Method: 研究团队分析某公司一年的生产服务记录，从总体、时间变化、模型和用户四个层面考察负载特征。该记录涵盖多种模型和用户，包括热门模型与长尾模型；研究还将完整记录公开，以供后续研究使用。

Result: 研究揭示了仅凭总体统计难以发现的负载演变规律及用户—模型关系结构。摘要未提供具体的定量结果或各项发现的详细数值。

Conclusion: 该研究以覆盖面广、时间跨度长的真实生产记录，补充了对大语言模型服务负载的认识。公开完整记录有望帮助后续工作开展生产行为分析与服务系统评测，并减少对抽样或合成负载的依赖。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic. In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from CompanyX. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we publicly release the full one-year trace, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads. The trace is available at https://github.com/HarvardMadSys/chutes_workload.

</details>
